Evaluating multimodal reasoning has historically been difficult because existing medical datasets rarely pair different image modalities, such as radiographs and external clinical photographs. To address this gap, we introduce Trick-or-Treat, an adversarial multimodal medical benchmark that pairs real chest radiographs with synthetic clinical photographs to challenge models' cross-modal reasoning. For each of a wide range of conditions, we created two pairs of images, where the pairs share a single chest radiograph but contain different external photographs. In matched pairs, the chest radiograph and photograph both contain findings suggesting the same disease (e.g., varicella pneumonia suggested by lung opacities and crusted skin lesions seen in chickenpox). In mismatched pairs, the photograph depicts an alternative clinical finding that would not lead to the same unifying diagnosis (e.g. replacing crusted skin lesions with ordinary acne). Synthetic photograph generation allows clinical findings to be varied while the person's features and photographic setting stay consistent, reducing confounding. In an investigation of three frontier models, we found that models hallucinated clinically significant photograph findings in 45-57.5% of mismatched pairs, up from 25-37.5% for the same photographs shown alone. When faced with mismatched pairs, models tended to ignore mismatches and instead hallucinated findings that better matched the radiograph (e.g. misreading acne as crusted skin lesions to justify diagnosing chickenpox). Trick-or-Treat both demonstrates concerning error pattern in frontier models' medical reasoning and provides a proof of concept for the use of synthetic images in adversarial multimodal evaluation.

</details>


### [104] [Uncertainty-Driven Efficient Communication for UAV-Based Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06635)
*Hao, Jiuwu,Wan, Yuting,Sun, Liguo,Zhang, Zao,Wu, Yueyang,Lv, Pin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UCP：面向无人机协同感知的不确定性驱动通信框架，用观测不确定性与判别不确定性分别生成请求/供给空间图以节省带宽，并用不确定性引导的 U-Fusion 交叉注意力聚合信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机协同感知受带宽与算力限制；现有空间选择方法只关注前景区域以减少漏检，忽略感知模糊区域，导致误检无法解决。

Method: 以观测不确定性刻画传感器受限区域、以判别不确定性刻画决策边界附近的模型级歧义；据此构建空间请求图与供给图引导智能体间通信；接收信息经不确定性引导的交叉注意力模块 U-Fusion 融合。

Result: 在3D目标检测与轨迹预测两类任务、三个基准上取得更优的性能—带宽权衡，并具备较强可解释性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 将不确定性作为协同价值的显式度量，可同时缓解漏检与误检，是通信高效的V2X/无人机协同感知可行范式。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-based collaborative perception enables multiple agents to share complementary perceptual information, overcoming limitations such as occlusion and limited sensing range. Communication efficiency is a critical bottleneck, as UAVs operate under stringent bandwidth and computational constraints. However, existing spatial selection methods focus predominantly on foreground regions to reduce missed detections, neglecting perceptually ambiguous regions and leaving false detections unresolved. We propose UCP, an uncertainty-driven framework that characterizes collaborative value through observational uncertainty, identifying sensor-limited regions, and discriminative uncertainty, capturing model-level ambiguity near the decision boundary. These estimates drive spatial request and supply maps for efficient inter-agent communication, and the received messages are aggregated via U-Fusion, an uncertainty-guided cross-attention module. Experiments on three benchmarks across 3D object detection and trajectory prediction demonstrate a superior performance-bandwidth trade-off with high interpretability and strong generalizability.

</details>


### [105] [Human Motion Prediction via Continual Prior Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06637)
*Tang, Jianwei,Hu, Jian-Fang,Liang, Tianming,Lin, Xiaotong,Sun, Jiangxin,Zheng, Wei-Shi,Lai, Jianhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出时序持续学习框架CPC/CPC++，将不同时间尺度的动作预测拆分为子任务分阶段训练，并用可学习的先验补偿因子（FGPCF）显式度量先验知识遗忘，可即插即用于多种HMP骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HMP把不同未来时刻的预测视为单一任务同时学习，长时（远期）预测的学习会干扰并抑制短时（近期）预测的性能，存在任务间冲突。

Method: 将不同时间段的预测划分为多个子任务，采用多阶段渐进式训练（时序持续学习）；引入可学习随机变量先验补偿因子PCF显式建模先验知识损失，并理论推导其可通过最小化目标函数的合理上界与模型参数联合优化；进一步扩展为CPC++，用细粒度FGPCF对各子任务的先验信息损失分别估计。框架与骨干解耦，可插拔。

Result: 在三个HMP基准数据集上，结合PGBIG、siMLPe、MotionMixer、LTD四个SOTA骨干进行实验，验证了框架的有效性与灵活性。

Conclusion: 以持续学习视角重构HMP训练流程、用可学习补偿因子缓解渐近训练中的遗忘，是一条通用且易于集成的改进路径，适用于多数据集与应用场景。

Abstract: Human Motion Prediction (HMP) aims to predict future human poses at different moments according to observed past motion sequences. Previous approaches mainly treated the prediction of different temporal moments as a single prediction task and learned the predictions of varied moments simultaneously, which would encounter a main limitation: the learning of short-term predictions (referring to ``near-future'' prediction) could be hindered by the predictions of long-term (referring to ``far-future'' prediction) motions. In this paper, we develop a novel temporal continual learning framework called Continual Prior Compensation (CPC) to progressively train HMP models, in which we divide the prediction task of motions corresponding to varied temporal moments into several subtasks and train the model in a multi-stage manner. To mitigate the prior information forgetting in the progressive training, we further introduce a learnable random variable Prior Compensation Factor (PCF) to explicitly measure the prior knowledge loss. We theoretically show that the PCF can be efficiently learned together with the model parameters by minimizing a reasonable upper bound of the objective function. The proposed CPC is further enhanced to estimate the prior information loss for each subtask and a new framework called Continual Prior Compensation++ (CPC++) with Fine-Grained Prior Compensation Factor (FGPCF) is finally developed. Our CPC and CPC++ frameworks are quite flexible and can be easily integrated with different HMP backbone models and adapted to various datasets and applications. Extensive experiments on three HMP benchmark datasets using multiple SOTA HMP backbones (PGBIG, siMLPe, MotionMixer, and LTD) demonstrate the effectiveness and flexibility of our frameworks.

</details>


### [106] [Dynamical State-Space Diffusion for Spatiotemporal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06652)
*Lee, Woojun,Kim, Hankyeol,Kang, Pilsung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DSSD 用时对角状态空间模型的有阻尼振荡模态构造时间核，以模型平稳协方差的 Cholesky 因子作为因果、可逆、方差保持的时间算子（等价于 Kalman 新息形式），使扩散的加噪过程本身具备多时间尺度与振荡相关性，去噪器结构不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列扩散预测对每帧施加独立噪声，把全部时间依赖交由去噪器学习，加噪过程本身不携带时序结构；而直接在有限片段上运行状态空间加噪又缺失首帧前状态，零初始化会导致早期帧噪声偏小、偏离扩散调度。

Method: 用稳定对角 SSM 的阻尼振荡模态构建时间卷积核，学习衰减率与频率；为避免不可观测的初始状态问题，改用该模型平稳协方差的 Cholesky 因子作为时间算子，并证明其因果性、精确可逆、逐帧噪声方差与标准扩散一致、在干净端退化为单位阵，且正是 SSM 的 Kalman 新息形式；去噪器改预测时间相关噪声，采样每步先去除再重新施加时间混合。

Result: 在科学领域与机器人视频数据集上，DSSD 优于共享同一去噪器的扩散基线；训练中学习模态的演化表明时间算子能自适应不同数据集的时序特性。

Conclusion: 将时序结构从去噪器转移到加噪过程是一种即插即用、理论可控的改进路径，但实验证据限于时序预测精度与模式演化分析，未在决策、控制或下游机器人任务中验证。

Abstract: Diffusion models for temporal prediction typically corrupt each future frame with independent noise, leaving temporal dependence entirely to the denoiser. We propose Dynamical State-Space Diffusion (DSSD), which learns the temporal structure of the corruption process itself. DSSD builds a temporal kernel from the damped oscillatory modes of a stable diagonal state-space model, whose learned decay rates and frequencies let the corruption capture multiple timescales and oscillatory correlations, while the denoiser architecture remains unchanged. Running this model on a finite clip, however, requires the state before the first frame, which is unobserved; starting from a zero state leaves the early frames with less noise than the diffusion schedule prescribes. We avoid this by using the Cholesky factor of the model's stationary covariance as the temporal operator. We prove that this operator is causal, exactly invertible, keeps each frame's noise variance equal to that of standard diffusion, and reduces to the identity at the clean end of the process, and that it is exactly the Kalman innovations form of the state-space model. The denoiser is trained to predict the temporally correlated noise, and sampling removes and reapplies the temporal mixing at each step. On scientific-field and robot-video datasets, DSSD outperforms diffusion baselines that share its denoiser, and the evolution of its learned modes during training shows that the temporal operator adapts to each dataset.

</details>


### [107] [CardioDrift: Automatic Cardiac Motion Tracking on a 3D Atlas Mesh via Shape-aware Coherent Point Drift with Slice Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06664)
*Movahed, Reza Akbari,Rezaee, Abuzar,Alamri, Shatha,Ho, Edmond S. L.,Deligianni, Fani,Gooya, Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CardioDrift，一种全自动、基于图谱的概率框架，将受试者心脏运动表征在具有全局对应点的3D图谱网格上，通过泛化的相干点漂移（CPD）联合配准多区域图谱表面到分割轮廓，并嵌入切片位移校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心脏运动追踪方法分为两类：无跨受试解剖对应（限制受试者间运动评估），或全局对应点但需耗时易错的半自动图谱拟合。作者希望实现全自动、具备解剖对应且高精度的运动表征。

Method: 将相干点漂移（CPD）概率配准框架推广到多心脏区域（LV、Myo、RV）图谱表面的联合配准，目标为心动周期各时相cine CMR分割图提取的解剖对应轮廓；并将切片位移（slice-shift）校正嵌入同一概率模型，逐切片估计平移以抑制错位伪影。

Result: 在1301名随机抽取的UK Biobank受试者及M&M数据集上评估，LV、Myo、RV的DSC分别为92.23±8.70%、82.11±7.56%、88.69±4.13%，在两个数据集的所有距离与重叠度指标上均优于最强基线。

Conclusion: CardioDrift以全自动方式实现了基于图谱的跨受试者对应心脏运动追踪，避免了半自动图谱拟合，并在大规模数据上取得一致的重叠度与距离指标提升。

Abstract: Automatic cardiac motion tracking from cine cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) imaging is essential for quantifying myocardial motion abnormalities associated with cardiovascular disease. Existing methods fall into two groups: those that represent motion without anatomical correspondence across subjects and therefore limit inter-subject motion assessment; and those that represent motion with globally corresponding points, which typically require a time-consuming and error-prone semi-automatic step to fit a cardiac atlas to the subject space. To address these limitations, this study proposes CardioDrift, a fully automatic, atlas-based probabilistic framework that represents subject-specific cardiac motion on a 3D atlas mesh with globally corresponding points. The Coherent Point Drift (CPD) formulation is generalised to jointly register the atlas surfaces of multiple cardiac regions to the anatomically corresponding contours extracted from cine CMR segmentation maps throughout the cardiac cycle. Slice-shift correction is embedded in the same probabilistic model, estimating slice-wise translations that suppress the misalignment artefact limiting atlas-fitting accuracy. Evaluation on 1301 randomly selected UK Biobank subjects and the M&M dataset, CardioDrift attains Dice similarity coefficients (DSCs) of $92.23\pm8.70\%$, $82.11\pm7.56\%$, and $88.69\pm4.13\%$ for the left ventricle (LV), myocardium (Myo), and right ventricle (RV), exceeding the strongest baseline on every distance and overlap metric of both datasets.

</details>


### [108] [Sekai: Incremental Event Memory for Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06685)
*Liu, Zhengtao,Xu, Jinrong,Kao, Shiu-Hong,Sun, Huixin,Li, Yicong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出广义推理视频分割（GRVS）新任务：目标集合随视频中已观测到的、与查询相关的事件累积而动态演化（可从空集变为单/多目标）；构建因果标注的 Isekai 诊断基准（60 视频、197 查询、11 类时序查询、12,569 个打分点），并提出在线框架 Sekai，含事件记忆推理器 EMR 与记忆条件解码器 MGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线推理视频分割（Online RVS）假设整段视频中目标集合固定，忽略了流式推理的本质属性：随着新事件发生，满足查询的对象会随时间变化。需要形式化并评测这种目标集合动态演化的设定。

Method: 提出 GRVS 任务定义：目标集合依赖于已观测视频历史中累积的查询相关事件；模型需持续维护目标有效性并在当前帧定位当前可见的有效目标。方法 Sekai 由两部分组成：(1) Event Memory Reasoner（EMR）在结构化记忆中维护查询相关事件与演化的目标状态；(2) Memory-Grounded Decoder（MGD）从该记忆中判别有效目标并在当前帧定位。评测侧构建 Isekai 基准，按时间对目标集合做因果标注。

Result: 在 Isekai 上 Sekai 显著优于现有在线 RVS 方法，取得 50.33 J&F，较最强基线提升 3.2 个点。代码、示例标注与评测脚本已开源。

Conclusion: GRVS 揭示了在线推理式视频分割中目标集合时序演化这一被忽视的核心问题，Sekai 通过结构化事件记忆 + 记忆条件解码提供了可行方案，但该工作属于视频理解与分割领域，与 Physical AI/机器人/3D 重建主线关联较弱。

Abstract: Online reasoning video segmentation (Online RVS) requires a model to segment query-referred objects as a video streams in, using only the frames observed so far. Existing formulations largely assume a fixed target set throughout the video, overlooking a fundamental property of streaming reasoning: as new events occur, the objects that satisfy a query may change over time. To address this, we propose a new task, Generalized Reasoning Video Segmentation (GRVS), where the target set depends on query-relevant events accumulated over the observed video history. As new evidence accumulates, the target set can evolve from empty to one or multiple objects, requiring a model to continuously maintain target validity while localizing the valid objects currently in view. To evaluate GRVS, we build Isekai, a diagnostic benchmark of 60 videos and 197 queries across 11 temporal query families, with 12,569 scoring points whose target sets are causally annotated over time. We further propose Sekai, an online framework with two complementary components: an Event Memory Reasoner (EMR) that maintains query-relevant events and evolving target states in structured memory, and a Memory-Grounded Decoder (MGD) that identifies valid targets from this memory and localizes them in the current frame. Experiments show that Sekai significantly outperforms existing online RVS methods, achieving 50.33 J&F and improving over the strongest baseline by 3.2 points. Code, example annotations, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/zenastangbi/Sekai.

</details>


### [109] [Agent-Driven Generative Priors for Pathology Image Synthesis and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06701)
*Yang, Sicheng,Tang, Rui,Zhou, Haipeng,Wang, Hongqiu,Xing, Zhaohu,Ma, Jianda,Dai, Lie,Yang, Guang,Zhu, Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段病理图像框架：智能体质检筛选构建TCGA-200K，训练生成模型并迁移其表征做语义分割，另发布PathSeg-380K标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理深度学习受限于像素级标注稀缺且昂贵；现有合成数据流程缺乏可靠质量控制，且与下游任务耦合弱。

Method: 1) 病理智能体系统评估图像质量并过滤样本，构建217,030个patch的TCGA-200K；2) 在筛选数据上训练生成模型，其骨干学习可迁移的结构与语义表征；3) 用轻量任务头将预训练生成骨干适配到语义分割，并给出约38万细胞、12类别的PathSeg-380K分割数据集。

Result: 在多个病理数据集上取得SOTA分割性能，代码开源。

Conclusion: 自动化数据筛选+生成式表征预训练+轻量下游适配的组合，可在标注稀缺的病理场景中有效提升分割性能，并提供了可复用的数据集与流程。

Abstract: Deep learning methods for pathology are constrained by the limited availability and high cost of large-scale pixel-level annotations. Synthetic data can alleviate this limitation, but existing pipelines often lack reliable quality control and remain weakly connected to downstream analysis. To address these issues, we propose a three-stage framework that integrates automated data screening, generative representation learning, and efficient adaptation to downstream tasks. First, we develop a pathology agent system to assess image quality and filter samples, resulting in TCGA-200K, a dataset containing 217,030 pathology patches selected from the public TCGA repository. Second, we train a generative model on the screened data. In addition to image synthesis, its backbone learns structural and semantic representations that can be transferred to downstream tasks. Third, we adapt the pretrained generative backbone to semantic segmentation using a lightweight task-specific head. We further introduce PathSeg-380K, a semantic segmentation dataset with expert annotations for approximately 380,000 cells across 12 cell types. Experiments on multiple pathology datasets show that the proposed framework achieves state-of-the-art segmentation performance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/script-Yang/Agent-GenPath.

</details>


### [110] [Turns Matter: Advancing Streaming Video RL via Turn-Level Credit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06728)
*Dong, Shuai,Tian, Kanghui,Chen, Linghan,Shao, Wenqi,Wu, Hongrui,Hou, Haowen,Zhu, Yongfu,Wang, Xuehui,Wang, Ziyue,Liu, Wenpu,Xie, Weichu,Xu, Yuqi,Wang, Congcong,Tuo, Kaiwen,Ma, Caoyuan,Wu, Taiqiang,Zhao, Ji,Chang, Wenzheng,Wang, Dianyi,Wang, Siyuan,Wei, Zhongyu,Qiao, Yu,Wang, Jiaqi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurnWise：面向流式视频理解的轮次级GRPO框架，用"silence-completed prefix scoring"做轮级信用分配，并用轮内平均+跨轮平均的损失归一化给"回答/等待"决策等权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解要求模型在每一时刻决定"回答"还是"继续观察"。现有将GRPO用于该场景的方法侧重奖励设计，却仍沿用轨迹级（trajectory-level）共享优势，导致早期成功后的冗余回复被强化、早期失败后的有益追问被抑制——轨迹级反馈与轮级交互不匹配。

Method: 1) 轮级信用分配：对每个决策点，用补全沉默（silence）的prefix计算剩余回报，构造group-relative优势，使该轮学习信号与先前奖励解耦；2) 轮均值策略优化：先在每轮内对token损失取平均，再跨轮取平均，使回答与等待决策权重相同、与输出长度无关；无需learned critic或额外rollout。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在流式视频理解任务上优于既有RL方法。

Conclusion: 将RL优化的信用粒度与决策粒度对齐（轮级优势+轮级损失归一化）是改进流式视频理解策略学习的关键，且实现代价低（无critic、无额外采样）。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to repeatedly decide whether to respond or continue observing. Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has been applied to this setting, with existing approaches emphasizing reward design while retaining a single trajectory-level advantage across turns. Such shared credit can reinforce redundant replies after earlier successes and discourage useful follow-ups after earlier failures. We propose TurnWise, a turn-level GRPO framework that aligns learning with individual streaming decisions. TurnWise addresses the mismatch between trajectory-level feedback and turn-level interaction, promoting informative responses and reducing redundancy. Specifically, turn-level credit assignment uses silence-completed prefix scoring to construct group-relative advantages from each decision's remaining return, separating its learning signal from earlier rewards. Turn-mean policy optimization averages token losses within each turn and then across turns, giving response and waiting decisions equal weight regardless of output length. These complementary designs improve streaming policy learning without a learned critic or additional rollouts. Extensive experiments show our method outperforms prior RL approaches for streaming video understanding.

</details>


### [111] [Coupled Scene Graph Anticipation: When With-Constraint Recall Hides Structural Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06738)
*Nongmaithem, Zeno,Srivastava, Anushttha,Sinha, Anshit,Sisodia, Apoorv,Chowdhury, Suman,Mukherjee, Prerana,Barnwal, Rajesh P.*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出CoupledSGA，用关系交互图＋图注意力嵌入Neural ODE向量场，使共享实体的关系在积分过程中互相影响，并加入基于互斥谓词矩阵的结构一致性损失；同时证明现有With-Constraint评测协议无法反映该类约束的收益，建议报告No-Constraint指标与矛盾率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间SGA中每个subject-object pair用独立Neural ODE演化，共享实体的关系无法相互影响，且无法阻止同一对出现"坐在…上"与"站在…上"等互斥谓词；此外该问题暴露出评测协议本身的缺陷。

Method: CoupledSGA：以Relationship Interaction Graph＋图注意力量化关系间交互并嵌入ODE向量场；引入结构一致性损失L_SC（基于互斥谓词矩阵）。理论分析：(1) 证明With-Constraint下R@K/mR@K在组内胜出谓词及其分数固定时不变，无法捕捉L_SC针对的组内违规；(2) 分析L_SC梯度依赖预测分数而非GT，二谓词规则下放大模型已有排序，全等分时更强抑制出现在更多互斥规则中的谓词。

Result: Action Genome上，两轮warm-up后启用L_SC使R@20从42.21%降至35.18%，但No-Constraint矛盾率由45.92%降至4.07%；等预算比较R@20为40.48%(CoupledSGA) vs 44.78%(SceneSayerODE)。

Conclusion: CoupledSGA被定位为测试平台而非SOTA；With-Constraint召回只能体现强制结构一致性的代价而非收益。建议对施加谓词共激活约束的方法同时报告No-Constraint结果与矛盾率。

Abstract: Scene Graph Anticipation (SGA) predicts object relationships in future video frames. Continuous-time SGA models evolve each subject-object pair with an independent Neural ODE, so relationships sharing an entity cannot influence one another during integration, and nothing prevents mutually exclusive predictions, such as "sitting on" and "standing on", for the same pair. We introduce CoupledSGA, which links such relationships through a Relationship Interaction Graph with graph attention inside the ODE vector field, and adds a Structural Consistency Loss ($\mathcal{L}_{SC}$) based on a matrix of mutually exclusive predicates. Developing CoupledSGA revealed a limitation of the evaluation protocol. With-Constraint evaluation keeps one predicate per group for each subject-object pair, so its R@K and mR@K cannot capture the within-group violations targeted by $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$: we prove they are unchanged whenever each group's winning predicate and its score are held fixed. We further show that the gradient of $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$ depends on predicate scores rather than ground-truth labels: for two-predicate rules it amplifies the ordering the model already holds, and when all scores in a group are equal it suppresses predicates in more exclusion rules more strongly, explaining why the penalty can lower recall while improving consistency. On Action Genome, activating $\mathcal{L}_{SC}$ after two warm-up epochs reduces R@20 from 42.21% to 35.18% while lowering the No-Constraint contradiction rate from 45.92% to 4.07% (matched-budget R@20: 40.48% CoupledSGA vs. 44.78% SceneSayerODE). CoupledSGA is a testbed, not a state-of-the-art claim: With-Constraint recall captures the cost of enforcing structural consistency but not the improvement. We recommend reporting No-Constraint results alongside a contradiction rate for methods that constrain predicate co-activation.

</details>


### [112] [TailSteer: Trajectory-Tail Steering for Drift-Resistant Multi-turn Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06741)
*Li, Ruikang,Chen, Zixuan,Wu, Jiarui,Yao, Mingde,Zhang, Yiyuan,Wang, Yujin,Deng, Hanming,Lu, Lewei,Xue, Tianfan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TailSteer：一种末段训练框架，通过仅在去噪轨迹尾部施加保持性校正、使用编辑器自身on-policy rollout与未改源内容作参考，抑制多轮编辑中的累积视觉漂移，同时保留基座生成与单轮编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮编辑性能强不代表多轮可靠：编辑输出被反复复用时，微小非预期改动会累积成严重视觉漂移。现有缓解手段（多轮数据/跨轮rollout训练、专用推理时机制）要么改变预训练先验，要么难以扩展到基础模型。

Method: 末段训练（final-stage training）：保持早期去噪动态与基座模型接近，把保持性校正集中在轨迹尾部以压制细粒度偏差；校正从编辑器自身的on-policy去噪rollout中学习，以未改动源内容作为保持参考，无需人工标注的真值编辑对。

Result: 在FLUX、SenseNova、Qwen系列上，15–20轮连续编辑的累积漂移显著降低，同时在大量文生图与单图编辑基准上基本保持基座模型性能。

Conclusion: 长程编辑鲁棒性可通过后训练"解锁"而非重学已有能力；尾段校正范式对基础模型可扩展，具备实用价值。

Abstract: Strong single-turn editing performance does not guarantee reliable multi-turn interaction: as edited outputs are repeatedly reused, small unintended changes can accumulate into severe visual drift. This long-term drift is often mitigated through training on multi-turn data or cross-turn rollouts, potentially altering pretrained priors, or through specialized inference-time mechanisms that are difficult to scale to foundation models. We introduce TailSteer, a final-stage training framework that improves multi-turn robustness while retaining the generative and editing capabilities acquired through earlier training. TailSteer follows a simple principle: keep early denoising dynamics close to those of the base model and concentrate preservation corrections near the trajectory tail, where fine-grained deviations can be suppressed. These corrections are learned from the editor's own on-policy denoising rollouts, using unchanged source content as a preservation reference without requiring curated ground-truth editing pairs. Experiments across the FLUX, SenseNova, and Qwen families show that TailSteer substantially reduces cumulative drift over 15-20 editing turns while largely preserving base-model performance on extensive text-to-image and single-image editing benchmarks. These results demonstrate that long-horizon editing robustness can be unlocked in foundation models after TailSteer, rather than by relearning their existing capabilities.

</details>


### [113] [EvoWorld: Towards Out-of-Sight World Evolution in Generative Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06748)
*Bie, Yequan,Jiang, Dengyang,Chen, Zhixuan,Liang, Xiaozhuan,Che, Haoxuan,Liu, Quande,Chen, Xi,Chen, Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"视野外因果演化"(Out-of-Sight Causal Evolution)新任务与EvoWorld框架：VLM作为"世界大脑"维护世界状态并推理事件后果，分段自回归视频渲染器负责视觉实现，配套EvoWorld-Data与EvoWorld-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式视频模型只是"像素渲染器"，缺乏显式世界状态与事件条件后果推理机制，导致输出视觉逼真但行为静态或因果不一致；需将这一推理缺口形式化为可测量任务。

Method: brain-renderer协作框架：视觉语言模型观察生成片段、维护演化世界状态、推理合理状态转移并决定跨出画外的后续演化；分段自回归视频渲染器将推理结果可视化。构建训练数据EvoWorld-Data与评测基准EvoWorld-Bench，覆盖事件实现、因果反应、重访一致性三项指标。

Result: 实验显示EvoWorld在事件条件因果演化上显著优于近期模型，同时保持视觉质量与一致性。

Conclusion: 以显式状态推理+生成渲染解耦的范式，为超越当前视野的推理引导自主世界演化提供参考基线；但属基线方案，尚无真机/真实世界证据，评测仍限于自建基准。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly used as visual world simulators, yet their primary strength lies in rendering plausible pixels rather than reasoning about how a world should evolve. Coherent world evolution requires more than visual fidelity: when an event occurs, a model must infer its consequences for the entities in the world and then render the resulting state. For example, after a wild beast appears, the model should not only depict the wild beast, but also infer whether a nearby person should flee, take cover, or fight back based on the realized scene. Existing diffusion-based video models lack an explicit mechanism for maintaining world state and reasoning over such event-conditioned consequences, often producing visually plausible but behaviorally static or causally inconsistent outputs. To make this reasoning gap concrete and measurable, we propose a new problem, Out-of-Sight Causal Evolution, which requires a model to render an event, reason about its consequences for the evolving world state, and reveal a causally plausible outcome as the world continues to evolve, even out of sight. As a baseline reference solution, we introduce EvoWorld, a brain-renderer collaboration framework in which a vision-language model serves as the world brain: it observes generated segments, maintains an evolving account of the world, and reasons about plausible state transitions, while a segment-autoregressive video renderer realizes them visually. We further introduce EvoWorld-Data and EvoWorld-Bench for training and evaluating event realization, causal reactions, and revisit consistency. Experiments show that EvoWorld substantially improves event-conditioned causal evolution over recent models while preserving visual quality and consistency, establishing a reference point for reasoning-guided, autonomous world evolution beyond the current field of view.

</details>


### [114] [Route First, See Later: Enabling Vision Agents to Reason Scientifically by Separating Routing Policy from Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06760)
*Mukherjee, Shubhabrata,Donatelli, Jeffrey,Ushizima, Daniela*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZETA 将科学图像分析智能体的"路由决策"与"视觉工具执行"显式解耦：先用显式路由策略解析意图，再调用专用视觉工具并做确定性几何测量；在 51 条零样本科学意图查询上，路由与执行可作为独立失效模式分别评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多工具智能体架构把意图理解与视觉执行混在一起，失败时无法判断是"路由错误（推理问题）"还是"工具执行失败（感知问题）"，缺乏可审计性，难以用于高风险科学自动化。

Method: 提出可扩展架构 ZETA：在执行前先解析科学意图，再由专门视觉工具执行；将测量定义为基于显式几何的确定性操作而非生成式输出；固定合法动作接口、仅隔离路由策略规范，构成首个受控的路由消融研究；执行轨迹使路由决策与下游产物可分别观测。

Result: 零样本基准（51 条查询）显示显式路由语义显著提升路由准确率，尤其在语义相邻的动作边界；Qwen 3.8 上分割路由由 6.2%→75.0%，检测路由由 60.0%→100%；但提升幅度因模型而异。

Conclusion: ZETA 提供了区分意图选择错误与视觉执行失败的基础设施，是部署可靠科学视觉智能体的必要前提；其实质为智能体路由/可审计性研究，缺少新视角重建、生成或机器人交互的贡献，属相邻背景类工作。

Abstract: Scientific image-analysis workflows require agents to select among diverse capabilities like description, detection, counting, and segmentation. Current multi-tool architectures conflate intent interpretation with visual execution, masking whether a failure arises from flawed reasoning (misrouting) or failed perception (invalid tool execution). This lack of auditability renders agents unreliable for high-stakes scientific automation. We introduce ZETA, an extensible architecture that operationalizes the decoupling of routing policy from action execution. ZETA resolves scientific intent before invoking specialized visual tools, treating measurement as a deterministic operation grounded in explicit geometry rather than generative output. This architecture exposes the "black box" of agent decision-making, allowing routing correctness and downstream execution to be evaluated as independent failure modes. To our knowledge, we provide the first controlled study in scientific vision-agent routing that isolates explicit routing-policy specification while holding the legal action interface fixed. Evaluation on a zero-shot benchmark of 51 scientific-intent queries shows that explicit routing semantics can substantially improve routing accuracy, particularly at semantically adjacent action boundaries, although improvements vary across models. For Qwen 3.8, explicit routing-policy specification improves segmentation routing accuracy from 6.2% to 75.0% and detection routing accuracy from 60.0% to 100%. ZETA's execution traces make routing decisions and downstream artifacts separately observable, providing a basis for distinguishing intent-selection errors from visual execution failures. ZETA establishes the necessary infrastructure to isolate execution errors from interpretive ones, a critical requirement for deploying reliable scientific vision agents.

</details>


### [115] [Benchmarking Fine-Grained Visual Reasoning for Remote Sensing in Urban Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06770)
*Zhang, Jiawei,Cai, Rizhao,Guan, Dayan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UMBench——基于无人机影像的细粒度城市遥感基准（交通监测、城市建设、环境质量、灾害预警），评测显示现有MLLM依赖场景级先验、忽视细粒度视觉证据；进而提出免训练的"证据优先"思维链方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在城市管理遥感中的细粒度视觉推理能力尚未被充分评测，现有模型易陷入场景级先验而忽略关键局部证据。

Method: 构建UAV图像的多任务细粒度城市遥感基准UMBench；提出evidence-first chain-of-thought，引导模型先检验细粒度视觉证据再推理，且无需训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法能提升开源与商用MLLM在UMBench上的表现。

Conclusion: 细粒度证据驱动的推理对城市遥感MLLM有效，基准与方法可推动该方向评测与改进。

Abstract: Fine-grained visual reasoning is essential for remote sensing in urban management, yet remains underexplored in the evaluation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). We introduce UMBench, a fine-grained urban remote sensing benchmark built from unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) images for multiple urban management tasks such as traffic monitoring, urban construction, environmental quality, and disaster warning. Evaluations on UMBench reveal that current MLLMs often rely on scene-level priors and overlook fine-grained visual evidence. To address this issue, we further propose a novel evidence-first chain-of-thought method that directs models to inspect fine-grained visual evidence in a training-free manner. Experiments show that our method can improve the performance of both open-source and commercial MLLMs on UMBench.

</details>


### [116] [Semantic Residual Vector Quantization for Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06781)
*Liu, Chang,Han, Ke,Biondi, Niccolò,Isotton, Gloria,Ricci, Elisa,Talon, Davide,Sebe, Nicu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SRVQ将手语生成(SLG)重构为"语义→运动学"两级过程：单个残差矢量量化器(SRV Quantizer)的第一层残差编码手语内容、后续层细化姿态/手型/轨迹/身体协调；配合词感知条件化的掩码语义生成器与运动学生成器，输出完整SMPL-X序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于姿态的系统与"仅重构"的运动tokenizer把"签什么(语义选择)"与"怎么签(细粒度关节表达)"纠缠在一起，生成器缺少显式的、文本对齐的中间目标；常需独立语义表示或外部规划阶段，结构冗余且难以分层控制。

Method: 提出语义残差矢量量化(SRVQ)：单一量化器内构建层级，首级残差量化码承担词级语义，后续残差级逐步编码姿态、手型、轨迹与身体协调；采用词感知(word-aware)条件进行层级码生成，先由掩码语义生成器预测语义码，再由运动学生成器补全残差码，最终合成SMPL-X运动。

Result: 在CSL-Daily与Phoenix-2014T上，R@1分别达82.3与70.3，较此前SOTA提升30与46个点；评估采用共享文本-运动嵌入空间中的检索与FID，并辅以标准几何指标。

Conclusion: 将语义选择与运动学实现解耦为分层码本，可无需外部规划模块即获得显式的文本对齐中间表示，显著提升手语生成的检索一致性。属人体动作/手语生成领域，与具身智能、3D重建等核心方向关联有限。

Abstract: Sign language generation (SLG) maps written sentences into signing motion while preserving both lexical content and fine-grained articulation. This requires a model to decide what signs should be expressed and to realize them through precise handshape, trajectory, facial expression and body coordination. Existing pose-based systems and reconstruction-only motion tokenizers often entangle these two decisions, leaving the generator without an explicit text-aligned intermediate target for what to sign before refining how to sign it. To address this issue, we introduce Semantic Residual Vector Quantization (SRVQ), a framework for SLG that frames generation as a two-level process moving from semantics to kinematics. The newly introduced SRV Quantizer learns a hierarchy in which the first residual level encodes sign content and subsequent levels refine pose, handshape, trajectory, and body coordination, all within a single quantizer and without separate semantic representations or external planning stages. Through word-aware conditioning for hierarchical code generation, a masked semantic generator predicts semantic codes and a kinematic generator completes the residual codes for full SMPL-X motion synthesis. We evaluate using retrieval and FID in a shared text-motion embedding space, alongside standard geometric metrics. On CSL-Daily and Phoenix-2014T, SRVQ achieves R@1 of 82.3 and 70.3, outperforming the previous SOTA by 30 and 46 points, respectively.

</details>


### [117] [VA-TDM: A Unified View for Improving Few-Step Diffusion Models by Velocity Adversarial Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06796)
*Luo, Yihong,Pang, Tianyu,Hu, Tianyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Velocity Adversarial Distillation (VAD) 用学习到的 critic 比较学生与教师的速度场，将二者分歧转为学习反馈，统一了 score distillation、Fisher 匹配与矩匹配；其扩展 VA-TDM 沿学生采样轨迹做轨迹分布匹配，实现高质量少步（4步）扩散/流模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏虽高效，但仅用极少采样步数难以保持生成质量。基于 score distillation 的分布匹配方法依赖准确追踪学生不断变化的分布，而在少步采样的中间步骤中学生 score 难以估计，常用估计器所描述的分布可能与更新学生时所用分布不一致。

Method: 利用 flow matching 提供的灵活性，从学生采样过程构造速度场进行监督。引入 VAD：以学习到的 critic 比较学生与教师速度场，把不一致性转化为反馈信号；理论上统一 score distillation、Fisher 匹配与矩匹配，并解释已有启发式 MMD 更新、导出新训练目标。进一步提出 VA-TDM，沿学生轨迹做轨迹分布匹配，为部分去噪步骤提供监督，并结合过渡感知的在线速度学习与学生中间转移上的对偶形式 VAD 监督。

Result: 在文生图与文生视频任务、多种骨干网络上取得四步生成的 SOTA 性能，并可有效扩展至 33B 的 MiniMax-H3；VA-TDM-H3 显著优于少步 Fast-H3，人工偏好更接近原始多步 H3。

Conclusion: 以速度场对抗匹配为核心的 VAD/VA-TDM 为少步扩散与流生成器提供了统一、可扩展到超大规模模型的高质量蒸馏框架。

Abstract: Few-step distillation offers efficient generation with diffusion and flow models, but preserving quality with only a few sampling steps remains challenging. Distribution-matching methods via score distillation provide a strong foundation, yet they rely on accurately tracking the student's changing distribution. At intermediate generation steps in few-step sampling, student-score estimation can be difficult, and commonly used estimators may describe a different distribution from the one used to update the student. Flow matching allows a more flexible approach to supervision through velocity fields constructed from the student's sampling process. Building on this perspective, we introduce Velocity Adversarial Distillation (VAD), which uses a learned critic to compare student and teacher fields and turn their disagreement into feedback for learning. VAD unifies score distillation, Fisher-based matching, and moment matching, also explaining existing heuristic MMD updates and delivering new training objectives. We further develop VA-TDM, which follows trajectory distribution matching: providing supervision for partial denoising along the student trajectory. Beyond this, VA-TDM combines transition-aware online velocity learning and dual-form VAD supervision across intermediate student transitions. Extensive results show that VA-TDM is powerful for learning few-step generators, achieving state-of-the-art four-step performance in both text-to-image and text-to-video generation across various backbones and scaling effectively to 33B MiniMax-H3. In particular, our VA-TDM-H3 notably outperforms the few-step Fast-H3 and is closer to the original many-step H3 according to human preference.

</details>


### [118] [Open-HumanWAM: An Open Recipe for Scaling Human and Robot Experience for Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06817)
*Ma, Juncheng,Li, Jiaxin,Tu, Jiankai,Liang, Bo,Lv, Guodong,Deng, Yufan,Tang, Junzhe,Yang, Liangzhu,Bian, Chen,Gong, Shukai,Zhang, Yintianrun,Cao, Zicong,Zhu, Baohao,Bi, Jianxin,Yan, Tengfei,Zhang, Yinyuan,Zhang, Kewei,Chen, Xinglong,Chen, Kaiqi,Wang, Duomin,Tang, Xiaotian,Tan, Liang,Li, Li,Xu, Yuan,Yang, Haosen,Wang, Yuqi,Xia, Jingfei,Huang, Songyan,Dou, Zhiyang,Dong, Zhen,Xie, Enze,Chua, Tat-Seng,Zhou, Daquan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open-HumanWAM 提出首个完全开源的大规模自我中心人类视频预训练方案：构建 Open-HumanWAM-50K（5 万小时、带手部运动与自然语言标注的语料），揭示预训练时长与下游机器人成功率的经验性对数线性标度律；据此训练纯人类数据的世界-动作模型 HumanWAM-Vanilla，并提出人-机混合数据预训练的视觉目标条件世界-动作模型 HumanWAM-Goal，在 RoboTwin 2.0 与 RoboDojo 上取得 SOTA，并在真机上展现零样本泛化与无需参数更新的具身上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心人类视频是可扩展的操作先验来源，但从"原始数据规模"到"可泛化的机器人策略"之间缺乏严格的数据清洗与训练配方；现有工作缺少对纯人类预训练是否有效、以及数据量与迁移效果定量关系的系统研究。

Method: 1) 构建 Open-HumanWAM-50K 语料：5 万小时自我中心视频，密集标注手部运动与自然语言；2) 通过闭环仿真评测系统研究纯人类预训练，拟合出"预训练小时数—下游成功率"的对数线性标度律；3) HumanWAM-Vanilla：仅用 5 万小时人类视频预训练的世界-动作模型，再做机器人后训练；4) HumanWAM-Goal：以视觉目标为条件的世界-动作模型，进行人-机联合/混合数据预训练以扩展零样本泛化。

Result: HumanWAM-Vanilla 后训练后在 RoboTwin 2.0 取得 92.08%（Clean2Clean）/68.88%（Clean2Random），RoboDojo 平均成功率 14.17%、平均进度 19.00；HumanWAM-Goal 进一步提升至 92.70%/84.60%（Clean2Random 大幅跃升），真机上表现出对未见视觉目标的零样本自适应（无参数更新的具身 in-context learning）。已开源语料、数据清洗与预训练流水线、预训练/后训练权重、推理代码与仿真评测套件。

Conclusion: 人类自我中心视频预训练具备可量化的标度收益，纯人类预训练已具竞争力，而人-机混合数据预训练可显著增强零样本与随机化鲁棒性；开源全流程为具身智能的可复现规模化研究提供基础设施。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable source of manipulation priors for robot learning, yet translating raw data scale into generalizable robot policies demands rigorous data curation and training recipes. We introduce Open-HumanWAM, the first fully open recipe for scaling egocentric pretraining to 50,000 hours, anchored by Open-HumanWAM-50K---a curated egocentric video corpus densely annotated with hand motion and natural language. Leveraging this corpus, we systematically investigate pure human pretraining via closed-loop simulation evaluations and uncover an empirical log-linear scaling law linking pretraining data hours to downstream robot success rates. Grounded in these findings, we develop HumanWAM-Vanilla, a world--action model pretrained exclusively on 50k hours of egocentric human video. Upon robot post-training, HumanWAM-Vanilla reaches 92.08% success on RoboTwin 2.0 Clean2Clean and 68.88% on Clean2Random, alongside a 14.17% mean success rate and a 19.00 mean progress score on RoboDojo. To push the capability frontier further, we explore joint human--robot pretraining with HumanWAM-Goal, a visual-goal-conditioned world--action model. Hybrid data scaling unlocks broad zero-shot generalization across both simulation and physical benchmarks, propelling post-trained RoboTwin 2.0 performance to new state-of-the-art levels: 92.70% on Clean2Clean and a substantial leap to 84.60% on Clean2Random. On physical robots, HumanWAM-Goal further exhibits emergent embodied in-context learning, dynamically adapting its manipulation behavior to unseen visual goals without parameter updates. To support reproducible scaling research in embodied AI, we open-source the Open-HumanWAM-50K corpus, data curation and pretraining pipelines, alongside the pretrained and post-trained HumanWAM checkpoints, inference code, and simulation evaluation suites.

</details>


### [119] [Context-Aware Autoregressive Image Generation for Emerging Reasoning Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06838)
*Ying, Jixuan,Liu, Haoyu,Yang, Timing,Zhu, Tingyu,Neskovic, Predrag,Bhattad, Anand,Xie, Cihang,Zheng, Zeyu,Yuille, Alan,Wang, Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出上下文感知自回归图像生成框架：模型依据全局上下文与当前局部信息动态决定下一token的生成位置，而非固定或随机序，显著提升结构鲁棒性与指令跟随推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归图像生成的生成顺序多为预设或启发式（逐patch、随机序、特定尺度一步生成），导致模型忽略图像中的重要结构依赖，限制结构鲁棒性与推理能力。

Method: 上下文感知自回归生成框架：由全局上下文+当前局部信息动态预测下一token位置，使生成顺序自适应图像结构与语义依赖；可推广至masked AR与scale-wise AR等范式。

Result: 在不同图像AR范式（masked AR、scale-wise AR）上均显著提升结构鲁棒性，并观察到涌现的推理特性——更好理解复杂语义指令、更忠实遵循引导生成。

Conclusion: 生成顺序是图像自回归建模中关键但被低估的因素，上下文感知解码可释放更强的推理型视觉生成能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive image generation has recently emerged as a competitive visual generation paradigm, yet its generation order is often predefined or heuristic, such as next-patch prediction, random-order prediction, or one-step generation for specific scales. We observe that these strategies can cause models to overlook important structural dependencies in images, thereby limiting both structural robustness and reasoning capability. In this work, we propose a context-aware autoregressive generation framework, where the model dynamically determines the next token location based on global context and current local information. Rather than following a fixed or random order, our method allows the generation process to adapt to image structure and semantic dependencies. Extensive experiments show that context-aware generation significantly improves structural robustness across different image AR paradigms, including masked autoregressive and scale-wise autoregressive models. Beyond quantitative improvements, we further observe emerging reasoning properties: the model can better understand complex semantic instructions and generate images that more faithfully follow the given guidance. These findings suggest that generation order is a critical yet underexplored factor in image autoregressive modeling, and that context-aware decoding can unlock stronger reasoning-aware visual generation capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/Hpesojyjx/Saccades-AR.

</details>


### [120] [Same Layout, Different Count: What Number Words Do Past Ten in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06840)
*Yue, Liang,Vance, Eric A.*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: The paper probes how far five open text-to-image models actually follow number words past ~10 objects, holding object identity and starting noise fixed, and finds a model-specific "reach": beyond it, the coarse latent noise fixes layout but not count, and the number's effect has no consistent direction.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To causally isolate what number words contribute to text-to-image generation in crowded scenes, and to show that standard exact-match / relative-error numeracy metrics cannot distinguish a model that follows counts loosely from one that ignores them.

Method: Controlled single-factor ablations in five open models (SD3.5-M, FLUX.1-dev, FLUX.2-klein, Qwen-Image and others): fix object and initial noise, vary only the requested count; human counting of 3,088 images by raters blind to the prompt; decompose the starting latent into coarse vs. fine (below 1/8 resolution) noise and re-draw only the fine part; probe whether the count token reaches the denoiser in early steps; compare text conditioning against encoding the count as a noised grid in the initial latent; and propose a same-noise paired test across counts.

Result: Every model follows small counts up to a per-model reach; SD3.5-M and FLUX.1-dev saturate just past ten, while FLUX.2-klein and Qwen-Image track loosely to twenty-five. Beyond reach, resampling only sub-1/8-resolution noise preserves layout as well as changing the number yet shifts the count two-thirds as much as a new number does. The number token is still consumed by the denoiser in early steps, but no intervention produces a directional effect. Count shown as a noised grid in the latent is recovered by humans in 59% of images versus 4% for text-only; exact-match and relative-error metrics can mislead (a constant guess of sixteen can even be preferred).

Conclusion: Number-word control over object count is a limited, non-directional mechanism that saturates at a model-specific reach; coarse noise dominates layout while fine noise carries count information. Numeracy should be evaluated with same-noise, paired, human-verified protocols rather than exact-match scores.

Abstract: Ask SD3.5-Medium for twelve of an object and then, from the same starting noise, for twenty-five. The picture keeps its layout, but the count changes about as much as it would under a new random seed, and in no consistent direction. We study what number words do once a request passes about ten objects, in five open text-to-image models. Holding the object and the starting noise fixed, we change one thing at a time, and people who never see the request count 3,088 of the images. Every model follows the number for small crowds, but only up to a point, its reach: SD3.5-M and FLUX.1-dev stop following it soon after ten, while FLUX.2 [klein] and Qwen-Image follow it loosely up to twenty-five. Beyond SD3.5-M's reach, the coarse part of the starting noise fixes the composition of the picture but not how many objects it holds: redrawing only the noise finer than an eighth of the image keeps the layout as well as a new number does, and already moves the count two-thirds as far. The number word still reaches the denoiser and acts in its first steps, but nothing we tried gives it a clear direction. Shown instead as a noised grid in the starting latent, the count is drawn: people find it in 59% of images, against 4% when it is only told. Past ten, an exact-match score barely separates a model that follows the number loosely from one that ignores it, and a relative-error score can even prefer a constant guess of sixteen. A same-noise test with a few requests per object tells them apart.

</details>


### [121] [Where to Connect? Boosting MLLMs via Dynamic Gated Pathways across ALL ViT and LLM Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06844)
*Yan, Yingying,Tang, Jiaqi,Wei, Wei,Wang, Qianzhou,Zhang, Lei,Chen, Jianmin,Xia, Yuyang,Geng, Botong,Wu, Jinjian,Chen, Qifeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DGP提出一种全连接动态门控路由：每个LLM层根据自身语义状态对全部ViT层视觉特征做门控加权，实现ViT层级与LLM层级间的自适应跨层交互，在LLaVA-1.5-7B/13B上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流MLLM将单一层视觉表示注入LLM并把所有LLM层视为同质消费者，忽视了不同层对视觉粒度需求不同；现有多层融合方法在编码器侧压缩特征或依赖预定义的稀疏/层级连接，无法建模ViT与LLM层级间的自适应跨层交互。

Method: Dynamic Gated Pathways：在每个ViT层与每个LLM层之间建立all-to-all连接；每个LLM层以自身语义状态为条件，对视觉层偏好进行动态门控（routing机制），从而自适应选择视觉粒度。基于LLaVA-1.5-7B/13B实现。

Result: 相对LLaVA-1.5-7B基线一致提升：SQA^I +4.2、MM-Vet +3.3、VizWiz +3.1、LLaVA^W +3.1，并在多数基准上超过现有多层视觉融合方法。层间路由分析显示不同LLM层确有选择不同视觉粒度的倾向。

Conclusion: 全连接式动态门控路由可有效取代固定单层注入/预定义连接，兼顾性能与可解释性；但方法面向通用多模态理解基准，未见面向具身感知、机器人操作或3D/世界模型任务的验证。

Abstract: A central yet under-explored question in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) is where to connect---how visual information from a hierarchical vision encoder should be wired into the layer-wise semantics of a large language model. Mainstream MLLMs inject a fixed single-layer visual representation into the LLM and treat all LLM layers as a single consumer, ignoring that different layers demand different visual granularities. Recent multi-layer fusion methods either compress hierarchical features on the encoder side or rely on predefined sparse or hierarchical layer-to-layer connections, and therefore stop short of modeling adaptive cross-layer interactions between the ViT and LLM hierarchies. We address this gap with DGP (Dynamic Gated Pathways), a routing mechanism that establishes all-to-all connections between every ViT layer and every LLM layer, with each LLM layer dynamically gating its preference over visual layers conditioned on its own semantic state. Built on top of LLaVA-1.5-7B and 13B, DGP delivers consistent gains over the LLaVA-1.5-7B baseline (e.g., +4.2 on SQA$^{I}$, +3.3 on MM-Vet, +3.1 on VizWiz and LLaVA$^{W}$) and surpasses prior multi-layer visual fusion methods on most benchmarks. Layer-wise routing analyses further show that different LLM layers do select different visual granularities on demand, supporting both the effectiveness and interpretability of the proposed dynamic gated pathways. The code is available: https://github.com/YingyingYan1/DGP.

</details>


### [122] [Trainspotting: Learning Object-Centric 3D Reconstruction from Found Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06849)
*Chen, Hanyu,Snavely, Noah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Trainspotting 数据集（12,068 个物体、311,886 张 Wikimedia Commons 照片），并按“固定于单点 / 跨点移动 / 同设计多实体”三种模式分类，揭示前馈重建模型在野外物体中心照片上的失效模式，并通过微调 VGGT-Ω 显著提升位姿、深度与几何质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型主要在单次会话采集的数据上训练，图像仅在视角上变化而物体与环境基本固定；而公共照片档案中同一物体/设计的照片由不同人在数年、不同环境、不同相机、甚至不同物理个体上拍摄，构成差异显著的多视图分布，导致 SOTA 模型在野外物体中心场景下表现差。

Method: 构建 Trainspotting 数据集：从 Wikimedia Commons 收集 12,068 个物体、311,886 张照片；对每个物体重建以获取相机位姿、多视图立体深度与物体掩码；将物体划分为三类（固定于一处、在多地之间移动、被多个物理个体共享的设计）。在此基础上评测近期前馈重建模型，并用该数据集微调 VGGT-Ω。

Result: 前馈重建模型在三类中的后两类（跨场地移动物体、多物理个体共享设计）失效最严重；在 Trainspotting 上微调 VGGT-Ω 后，在留出物体上的相机位姿、深度与三维几何显著提升；在独立的 NAVI 野外基准（每物体处于不同环境）上也观察到类似提升；传统基准上性能基本不变。

Conclusion: 数据分布（而非模型容量）是野外物体中心重建的关键瓶颈；以“发现式照片”为核心的数据集可有效提升前馈重建的跨环境泛化能力，且不牺牲标准基准性能。

Abstract: State-of-the-art feedforward reconstruction models perform poorly on in-the-wild object-centric photo collections. They are trained largely on single-session object captures whose images vary in viewpoint while the object and its environment remain nearly fixed. Public photo archives contain a different form of multi-view data. They hold found photos of the same object or design: photos taken independently by many people across years, environments, cameras, and sometimes physical units. In this work, we introduce Trainspotting, a dataset of 12,068 objects and 311,886 photos from Wikimedia Commons. We reconstruct each object to recover camera poses, multi-view stereo depth, and object masks. We categorize the objects into three regimes: objects fixed at one site, objects that move between sites, and designs shared by many physical units. Evaluating recent feedforward reconstruction models on Trainspotting shows that they fail most severely in the last two regimes. Fine-tuning VGGT-$\Omega$ on Trainspotting significantly improves camera pose, depth, and 3D geometry on held-out objects. We observe similar improvements on the independent NAVI in-the-wild benchmark, which contains photos of each object in different environments. Performance on conventional benchmarks remains largely unchanged.

</details>


### [123] [Visual Token Routing: Learning to keep tokens that matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06854)
*Aggarwal, Pranjal,Welleck, Sean*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTR 提出可联合训练的稀疏视觉 token 表示：轻量时空路由器逐区域判定当前及上一帧截图的视觉 token 是否进入语言模型，并以自适应损失在 token 数与性能间折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Computer-use agents 每张截图编码为数千视觉 token，长交互历史导致上下文膨胀，推理开销高，难以用于长程任务与实时场景（如视频游戏）。观察到多数视觉 token 与下一步动作语义无关或在时间上冗余。

Method: VTR（Visual Token Routing）联合训练 VLM 与轻量 spatio-temporal router：router 检查当前帧与前一帧的各图像区域，决定其视觉 token 是否送入 LLM；使用自适应损失最小化 token 数量同时控制性能退化；仅 2000 训练步。

Result: 在 OSWorld、CUA-World 与视频游戏动作预测上与全分辨率模型持平，视觉 token 减少 2.5–5.8 倍，推理成本最多降 3.3 倍，prefill 最多提速 2.9 倍；较降分辨率及 token 丢弃基线在 OSWorld 上最高高 12.8 分。

Conclusion: VTR 让 CUA 只处理必要视觉 token，以极低计算成本支撑长程任务与实时应用；属通用 agent 系统效率优化，不含机器人/3D 重建实证，非本方向核心。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently achieved superhuman performance on several long-horizon benchmarks. However, their computational cost limits their use in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains such as video games. A key bottleneck is representing each screenshot as thousands of visual tokens, which accumulate into long contexts as agents reason over extended interaction histories. We observe that most of these visual tokens are either semantically irrelevant to the agent's next action or temporally redundant across screenshots. Building on this observation, we introduce Visual Token Routing (VTR), a joint training framework for learning sparse visual representations that preserve the information needed for action prediction. In VTR, we train the vision-language model together with a lightweight spatio-temporal router that examines each image region in the current and previous screenshots and decides whether its visual token enters the language model. We optimize the router using an adaptive loss to keep the number of visual tokens as small as possible while minimizing performance degradation. With just 2,000 training steps, VTR matches the performance of the full-resolution model on OSWorld, CUA-World, and video game action prediction, while using 2.5-5.8x fewer visual tokens. These savings reduce inference cost by up to 3.3x and speed up prefill by up to 2.9x. VTR also outperforms lowering the image resolution and other baselines for dropping tokens, by up to 12.8 points on OSWorld. Overall, VTR enables computer-use agents to process only the visual tokens they need, allowing them to be used in long-horizon tasks and real-time domains at a fraction of the computational cost. Code and models are available at https://pranjal2041.github.io/vtr

</details>


### [124] [LongBanana: An Expert-Verified Benchmark for Long-Context Multi-Reference Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06856)
*Cao, Haoxiang,Zhang, Yuxuan,Du, Penghui,Li, Bo,Cao, Jiajiong,Fan, Yingying,Wu, Zimeng,Luo, Yifu,Zheng, Mingzhe,Miao, Junwen,Zhang, Huaisong,Yao, Yang,Wang, Yu,Jiang, Dongfu,Nie, Ping,Chen, Wenhu,Wang, Chaoqun,Yu, Changqian,Allen, Kelsey R.*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出专家设计并核验的长上下文多参考图像合成基准 LongBanana（90 任务、4,670 条原子评估项、实体-关系图接地评估），并配套 LongBanana-Gen 智能体框架，表明 ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect 流程可带来依赖 harness 的证据接地与修复增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文、多参考图像的图像合成缺乏专家设计且经专家核验的可审计基准，以衡量智能体式图像生成中的证据检索、干扰项拒斥、实体-关系绑定与全局一致性。

Method: 构建 LongBanana 基准：90 个任务、3×3 分类法，每任务约 32 张参考图、20 条约束、50 个评估项，共 4,670 条专家指定的原子评估项；采用图接地（graph-grounded）评估设计，将实体-关系图的节点、边与全图义务映射为 Content Fidelity、Relational Binding、Global Constraint Satisfaction 三类清单项；15% 双标注子集达到 89.4% 完全一致率与 0.83 二次加权 kappa；并提出 LongBanana-Gen 智能体框架，对比直接生成与 ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) 工作流。

Result: 最小化智能体框架即优于直接生成；RPR 工作流通过增强证据接地与修复，带来依赖具体 harness 设计的增益；基准具备高标注一致性。

Conclusion: LongBanana 为度量稠密视觉证据使用提供可审计测试平台，并作为开发更强智能体式图像生成系统的共享目标。

Abstract: We introduce LongBanana, to our knowledge the first expert-designed and expert-verified benchmark for long-context multi-reference image synthesis in the age of agent-driven image generation. The task requires retrieving dispersed visual evidence, rejecting distractors, binding entities and relations, and composing one coherent final image from multiple references. LongBanana contains 90 carefully designed tasks organized by a 3-by-3 taxonomy; each task includes about 32 reference images, 20 constraints, and 50 evaluation items, yielding 4,670 expert-specified atomic evaluation items in total. Its graph-grounded evaluation design instantiates each task with reference images and an entity-relation graph, then converts node, edge, and whole-graph obligations into checklist items for Content Fidelity, Relational Binding, and Global Constraint Satisfaction. Reviewed expert labels include a 15% double-labeled subset with 89.4% exact agreement and 0.83 quadratic weighted kappa. We further introduce LongBanana-Gen, an agent-harness framework for long-horizon image generation; experiments show that minimal agent harnesses improve over direct generation, while a ReAct-Plan-Exec-Reflect (RPR) workflow yields harness-dependent gains by strengthening evidence grounding and repair. Together, LongBanana provides an auditable testbed for measuring dense visual evidence use and a shared target for developing stronger agent-driven image-generation systems. Code and data are available at the project page: https://long-banana.com/.

</details>


### [125] [CounterFactual Retrieval for Evidence-Aware Individual Animal Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06864)
*Upadhyay, Ankit K.,Algasov, Aleksandr,Nepovinnykh, Ekaterina,Zolotarev, Fedor,Eerola, Tuomas,Kälviäinen, Heikki,Stewart, Charles V.,Picek, Lukas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出反事实前景检索(CFR)评测协议，用全图/仅前景/仅背景(捷径)三种视图对比检索表现，把单一准确率变成可解释的"证据画像"，并发布34个数据集约0.5M掩码与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体动物重识别正走向共享benchmark与基础模型，但高检索准确率无法说明模型究竟依赖动物本体特征还是身份相关的背景/轮廓等捷径信号，现有评测缺乏证据归因。

Method: CFR在同一query-gallery任务上分别评测三种视图：全图（标准检索性能）、仅前景（前景支撑的检索）、仅捷径（去除动物后从背景/轮廓中可恢复的身份信号），从而得到证据分解式的检索画像；配套自动掩码生成流程。

Result: 在34个数据集上生成约0.5M掩码，给出各数据集/模型的证据画像，可量化模型对背景捷径的依赖程度及前景真实判别能力。

Conclusion: 检索精度不应作为唯一指标，需辅以证据感知评测才能判断模型是否学到个体本体特征；成果（掩码、代码）可作为该领域标准化评测工具。

Abstract: Automated identification of individual animals is moving from small curated studies toward shared benchmarks and reusable foundation models. This progress is useful, but current evaluation leaves the evidence behind a match unresolved, i.e., high retrieval accuracy does not show whether a model used animal features or identity-correlated shortcuts. We address this with Counterfactual Foreground Retrieval (CFR), an evidence-aware evaluation protocol. CFR evaluates the same query-gallery task under different views: full image, foreground-only, and shortcut-only. The full-image measures standard retrieval performance; the foreground-only measures foreground-supported retrieval; and shortcut-only view measure how much identity signal is recoverable from background, or silhouette. This turns a retrieval score into an evidence profile: how well the model performs on the original images, how much of that performance remains when only the animal is visible, and how much identity signal remains recoverable when the animal is removed. We release around 0.5M masks for 34 datasets and code for mask generation and CFR evaluation.

</details>


### [126] [PixelSpline: Continuous Space-Time Video Super-Resolution via Endpoint-Anchored Shared Pixel Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06870)
*Yuan, Wanqi,Li, Nianyi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出连续时空视频超分框架：长上下文编码 + 连续HR特征提升 + 端点锚定PixelSpline，将双向独立运动统一为单条连续轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时空视频超分仅用两端帧推断运动，并对两个方向独立建模，易出现对应歧义与轨迹不一致。

Method: 长程观测增强端点表征；在目标HR网格上连续上采样构造细节外观特征；PixelSpline把每像素运动建模为两端共享的单一连续轨迹，据此将端点颜色warp为粗中间帧，并对齐HR特征做残差重建。

Result: 在多种空间与时间上采样设置下取得良好的质量—效率折中（摘要未给出具体指标）。

Conclusion: 单一连续轨迹建模优于双向独立运动估计，长上下文+连续HR特征提升是有效范式。

Abstract: Existing continuous space-time video super-resolution methods often infer motion from only two endpoint frames and model the two motion directions independently, making them vulnerable to ambiguous correspondence and inconsistent trajectories. We introduce a continuous STVSR framework that combines long-context encoding, continuous HR feature lifting, and an endpoint-anchored PixelSpline. Long-range observations provide more reliable endpoint representations, while continuous upsampling constructs detailed appearance features directly on the requested HR grid. PixelSpline explicitly models each pixel's motion as a single continuous trajectory shared by both endpoints. Using this trajectory, our method warps endpoint colors into a coarse intermediate frame and aligns the HR features for residual reconstruction. Experiments across spatial and temporal upsampling settings demonstrate that the proposed framework offers a favorable quality--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [127] [Procedural Adversarial Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06890)
*Nugent, Jack,Deng, Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出程序化对抗学习框架PAL，用Simple-PAT算法在可解释程序生成器的低维控制参数空间中做零阶优化，按需搜索困难样本，并构建PAL-Dojo程序生成器集（表面法向、分割、人体姿态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在平均误差很低的情况下仍在看似简单的场景失败，且这些失败样本未必存在于固定数据集中，因此需要在输入空间主动搜索困难实例并针对性训练。

Method: 将程序生成器视为可解释程序（低维控制参数如布局、光照、姿态→渲染图像及标签），构建minimax训练与对抗评估框架；Simple-PAT采用零阶优化在参数空间按需生成对抗训练样本；发布PAL-Dojo，包含表面法向估计、图像分割、人体姿态估计三套高效程序生成器。

Result: 面对5千万次聚合查询的跨模型黑盒对手，Simple-PAT将最坏情况误差相对标准训练降低3.15倍（几何平均），相对最强鲁棒训练基线降低2.63倍。

Conclusion: 在可解释程序生成器的参数空间进行零阶对抗搜索是一种有效且样本高效的鲁棒性提升途径，优于常规训练与现有鲁棒训练基线。

Abstract: For many computer vision tasks, neural networks can be trained to produce a low average error, yet they still fail on seemingly simple scenes. Reducing these failures requires first finding them, but they may not be in a fixed dataset. We study procedural adversarial learning (PAL), a framework of minimax training and adversarial evaluation which searches the input space of a procedural generator for difficult instances. The procedural generator is an interpretable program mapping a low-dimensional vector of control parameters (e.g. layout, lighting, pose) to a rendered image and its label. We introduce the Simple-PAT algorithm, which uses zeroth-order optimization in the procedural generator's parameter space to propose adversarial training samples that are generated on demand. We also create PAL-Dojo, a collection of efficient procedural generators for surface normal estimation, image segmentation, and human pose estimation. Against a cross-model black-box adversary with 50M aggregate queries, Simple-PAT reduces worst-case error by a geometric mean of 3.15x compared to standard training and 2.63x compared to the strongest robust training baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/princeton-vl/procedural-adversarial-learning.

</details>


### [128] [Occlusion for Free: Detecting Occlusion from the Disagreement Between Two Monocular Distance Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06899)
*Onyenweaku, Chibueze Praise,Adele, Toyibat,Abdulkareem, Sikirulahi Opeyemi,Eje, Obed Honour*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用单目检测框的"表观高度"与"单目度量深度"两种估计的排序关系，实现无需遮挡标注、无需训练的遮挡检测评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遮挡信息对机器人有用，但遮挡估计器通常需要部署环境难以获得的遮挡标注；作者还发现在跨域（德国→尼日利亚拉各斯）场景下检测器精度减半，说明依赖本地标注的信号价值有限。

Method: 观察：遮挡几乎不改变表观高度（检测框仍覆盖整个人），但会把单目度量深度拉向遮挡物表面。因此仅比较两种距离估计的相对大小即可得到遮挡分数；只需深度误差近似为纯增益（可无标签验证），无需焦距、身高或深度尺度。作者同时测试了深度融合、遮挡仿真增广、注意力三种架构方案，均未在训练分布外提升检测。

Result: KITTI 重度遮挡检测 AUC 0.909（含漏检行人为 0.888）；固定距离带内 AUC 0.894–0.925，而单独真值距离接近随机；CARLA 合成场景（有真实可见度）达 0.819，对比单用距离的 0.644。

Conclusion: 利用已有的相机几何量即可免标注地读出遮挡程度，且不是距离代理；在基准未覆盖的部署环境（如缺乏本地标注的低资源场景）中，此类无需本地标注的信号更有价值。

Abstract: Knowing that a pedestrian is occluded is useful to a robot even when the detector cannot recover the hidden pixels, but occlusion estimators are normally trained on occlusion annotations that deployment settings rarely have. We show that occlusion can be read off two distance estimates a camera-only pipeline already computes. Apparent height is largely unchanged by occlusion, because the detected box still spans the whole person; monocular metric depth is pulled toward the occluder, because the nearest surface inside the box is what a depth map reports. Only their ordering matters, so no focal length, stature, or depth scale is needed, provided the depth model's error is close to a pure gain, a condition that can be checked without labels. The score detects heavy occlusion on KITTI with AUC 0.909 (0.888 over all annotated pedestrians, including those the detector misses), requires no training and no occlusion labels, and is not a proxy for range: within fixed distance bands it holds AUC 0.894-0.925 while true distance alone sits near chance. On synthetic CARLA scenes with measured visibility it reaches 0.819, against 0.644 for distance alone. We arrived here after measuring three standard architectural responses to occlusion, depth fusion, occlusion-simulating augmentation, and attention, none of which improved detection outside its training distribution in the configurations we tested, and after finding that a detector trained on German roads loses half its accuracy on self-annotated street scenes in Lagos, Nigeria. Signals that need no local annotation are worth more in settings that benchmarks do not represent.

</details>


### [129] [Motion-guided Contrastive Regularization For Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06901)
*Chen, Yiwen,Hou, Qiqi,Jeong, Jisoo,Zhu, Yinhao,Jiang, Huaizu,Said, Amir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现JEPA式世界模型存在"静态坍缩"失败模式，并提出运动引导的对比正则化（motion-guided contrastive regularization）来修复表征，显著提升视觉控制规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式世界模型仅靠分布正则（如SIGReg）无法保证表征保留控制所需信息；作者识别出"静态坍缩"——编码器保留与任务无关的episode级静态内容（如背景）却抑制操控相关状态变化。由于静态内容时间上可预测但跨episode多样，该失败在SIGReg下仍持续存在。

Method: 控制实验：在OGBench-Cube上加入随机背景，验证规划性能显著下降。方法：运动引导对比正则化——将预测的隐空间转移与同episode候选观测的转移对比，降低静态背景身份对区分动作结果的有用性；用光流相似度作为软对比目标，使运动相似的候选获得相似监督。最终目标=隐空间预测+SIGReg+该对比正则。

Result: OGBench-Cube（随机背景）上规划成功率52%→78%；LIBERO-Object上14%→54%；在另外四个视觉控制benchmark上性能相当。

Conclusion: 仅靠分布正则不足以防止世界模型表征坍缩到静态内容；引入运动引导的软对比监督可有效保留幕内状态区分与相关转移的相似性，且改动轻量、可迁移。

Abstract: JEPA-style world models learn action-conditioned latent dynamics without reconstructing observations, but distributional regularization alone does not ensure that their representations preserve information needed for control. We identify static collapse, a failure mode in which the encoder preserves episode-specific static content while suppressing manipulation-relevant state changes. Because static content is temporally predictable yet diverse across episodes, this failure can persist despite regularizers such as SIGReg. A controlled study on OGBench-Cube shows that introducing randomized backgrounds substantially degrades planning performance while leaving the underlying manipulation task unchanged. To address this problem, we propose motion-guided contrastive regularization. Our approach compares predicted latent transitions with transitions to candidate observations from the same episode, reducing the usefulness of static background identity for distinguishing action outcomes. Optical-flow similarities provide soft contrastive targets, allowing candidates with similar motion to receive similar supervision. Combined with latent prediction and SIGReg, our objective encourages representations to preserve within-episode state distinctions and similarities between related transitions. Our method improves planning success over LeWorldModel (LeWM) from 52% to 78% on OGBench-Cube with randomized backgrounds and from 14% to 54% on LIBERO-Object, while maintaining comparable performance on four other visual-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [130] [From Pixels to Actions - Towards Native Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06918)
*Fan, Weichen,Diao, Haiwen,Wu, Penghao,Hu, Siyuan,Huang, Ziqi,Liu, Tianqi,Sun, Mingze,Wang, Ziyi,Cai, Zhongang,Deng, Hanming,Wang, Quan,Yang, Lei,Lu, Lewei,Hong, Fangzhou,Chen, Zhaoxi,Lin, Dahua,Liu, Ziwei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NEO-VLA proposes an encoder-free VLA framework where images are tokenized by lightweight patch-conv layers and evolve jointly with language in the backbone, letting a flow-matching action expert access instruction-conditioned visual representations at multiple depths.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing VLA models assume perception is first compressed by a fixed visual encoder before action reasoning, but manipulation depends on visual cues (local geometry, boundaries, depth, subtle state changes) that are easily lost in that abstraction. The paper asks whether action policies should consume a fixed visual endpoint or directly access evolving visual computation.

Method: Structurally encoder-free architecture: images tokenized via lightweight patch convolutional layers, fused with language tokens and processed jointly through the backbone; a flow-matching action expert conditions on intermediate visual features from different depths. Controlled comparisons against encoder-based counterparts, fine-tuning ablations, scale ablations, and layer-wise probing of action-critical signals.

Result: Native encoder-free architectures consistently outperform encoder-based counterparts in controlled settings; full fine-tuning narrows but does not close the gap, and extra scale alone cannot recover it. Layer-wise probing shows action-critical signals often peak well before the final visual representation. NEO-VLA achieves stronger and more robust control across diverse manipulation settings and distribution shifts.

Conclusion: Embodied control benefits from access to visual representations across the computational hierarchy rather than a single fixed encoder output, supporting encoder-free, depth-aware multi-level feature conditioning for VLA policies.

Abstract: Vision-language-action models are typically built on a simple premise: perception is first compressed by visual encoders, and action reasoning begins only afterwards. Yet manipulation often hinges on precisely the visual signals most vulnerable to this abstraction, e.g., local geometry, boundaries, depth, and subtle state changes. This raises a fundamental question: should action policies inherit a fixed visual endpoint, or directly access evolving visual computation? We introduce NEO-VLA, a structurally encoder-free vision-language-action framework. Images are tokenized by lightweight patch convolutional layers and evolve jointly with language throughout the backbone, allowing a flow-matching action expert to draw from instruction-conditioned visual representations at different depths. This simple change reveals a consistent pattern. Native architectures outperform encoder-based counterparts under controlled comparisons; full fine-tuning reduces but does not erase the gap, and additional scale alone cannot recover it. More strikingly, layer-wise probing shows that the action-critical signals often peak well before the final visual representation. Across diverse manipulation settings and distribution shifts, NEO-VLA consequently achieves stronger and more robust control. Together, these results suggest that embodied control benefits from access to visual representations across the computational hierarchy, rather than a fixed encoder output.

</details>


### [131] [T$^2$-Splat: Adaptive Topology Mesh Splatting with Texture Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06974)
*Zhihao Tang,Youjia Zhang,Mingbo Zhao,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T²-Splat将三角网格拓扑自适应与外观精修解耦：用Safe RDT初始化网格连通性，用DTE通过边折叠/顶点分裂调整三角形分配，再用逐面Mip纹理在固定网格上精修外观；在紧凑网格下取得有竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角面片splatting能把新视角合成接入传统图形管线，但为保留外观细节往往需要过高细分，导致网格冗余、效率低，因此需要更可控的面数与拓扑。

Method: Safe Restricted Delaunay Triangulation (Safe RDT) 提供初始网格连通性；Dynamic Topology Evolution (DTE) 通过边折叠与顶点分裂动态分配三角形；随后在固定网格上用逐面残差Mip-texture结合尺度自适应滤波精修外观。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks & Temples、DTU上以紧凑网格取得竞争性结果；Mip-NeRF 360上PSNR较MeshSplatting提升0.71dB；压缩实验显示面预算可降至原始的10%，网格复杂度可灵活控制。

Conclusion: 解耦拓扑与外观的三角splatting框架可在显著减少面数的同时保持或提升渲染质量，便于对接常规图形管线，但主要面向静态场景逐场景重建。

Abstract: Triangle-based splatting connects novel view synthesis with conventional graphics pipelines, but often requires excessive tessellation to preserve appearance details. We present T$^2$-Splat, which decouples topology adaptation from appearance refinement. Safe Restricted Delaunay Triangulation (Safe RDT) initializes mesh connectivity. Dynamic Topology Evolution (DTE) then adapts triangle allocation through edge collapse and vertex split operations. Residual per-face Mip-textures then refine appearance on the fixed mesh using scale-adaptive filtering. Experiments on Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks & Temples, and DTU demonstrate competitive results with compact meshes. T$^2$-Splat improves PSNR by 0.71dB over MeshSplatting on Mip-NeRF 360. Additional compression experiments demonstrate flexible control of mesh complexity, supporting face budgets as low as 10% of the original face count. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [132] [Batch-Conditioned Semantic Anchors for Robust Transductive Adaptation of Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06993)
*Mohammed Rahman Sherif Khan Mohammad,Ardhendu Behera,Sandip Pradhan,Swagat Kumar,Amr Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JPTA框架，将直推式视觉语言模型（VLM）适配视为批次条件化的语义参考估计：以CLIP文本原型为语义锚点，在批次可靠性校准下向图像侧结构漂移，缓解"激进适配漂移"与"固定原型欠适配"之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直推式适配方法存在核心权衡：激进方法利用批内结构但遇稀疏批次、先验偏移或标签空间覆盖不足时会漂移；保守方法保留CLIP文本语义但过度固定，即使批次提供了强纠正证据也难以适配。

Method: JPTA不把文本原型当作固定先验或可随意替换的表示，而视为可校准的语义参考。通过估计批次先验与软图像侧原型，构造"传输后的语义参考"（最优传输式），其影响强度由批次可靠性缩放，从而在批次证据不足时保持保守、证据充分时适度漂移。

Result: 在标准基准、低有效类场景、全类评估与在线流式设定下均稳定优于TransCLIP与StatA，在稀疏批次与先验偏移批次上增益最大。

Conclusion: 锚点漂移、有效支撑集与缺失类质量等诊断表明JPTA在不产生无约束直推漂移的前提下修复了有效类不匹配问题；稳健的直推式VLM适配应以可靠批次证据重新校准语义参考，而非在无约束直推与固定原型之间二选一。

Abstract: Transductive adaptation improves vision-language models at test time by refining predictions on an unlabeled batch, but current methods face a key trade-off. Aggressive approaches exploit batch structure but can drift when batches are sparse, have prior shift, or cover little of the label space. Conservative methods better preserve CLIP text semantics, but fixed semantic references can under-adapt even when the batch provides strong corrective evidence. We propose JPTA (Joint Prior and Transport Adaptation), a prior-aware framework that casts transductive VLM adaptation as batch-conditioned semantic reference estimation. Instead of treating CLIP text prototypes as fixed priors or freely replaceable, JPTA views them as semantic references that shift toward image-side batch structure only under calibrated evidence. It estimates batch priors and soft image-side prototypes to form transported semantic references whose impact is scaled by batch reliability. Across standard benchmarks, low effective-class regimes, all-class evaluation, and online streams, JPTA consistently outperforms TransCLIP and StatA, with the largest gains on sparse and prior-shifted batches. Anchor-drift, active-support, and absent-class-mass diagnostics show that JPTA fixes effective-class mismatch without unconstrained transductive drift, indicating that robust transductive VLM adaptation should recalibrate semantic references using reliable batch evidence rather than choose between unrestricted transduction and fixed prototypes. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [133] [TraCE: Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07009)
*Wooil Lee,Junpyo Hong,Jongseo Lee,Tuomas Oikarinen,Tsui-Wei Weng,Jinwoo Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TraCE：基于光流轨迹聚类发现"来源灵活"的运动概念，结合对象与场景概念，构建类型化轨迹/对象/场景接口，用于可解释动作识别与VQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频XAI存在"运动来源欠指定"问题：区域中心解释存在来源歧义，单人物姿态序列的运动词表来源不完整，难以说明是何种运动证据支持预测。

Method: 在训练视频上聚类光流轨迹，得到可覆盖人、物体、交互的源灵活运动概念；辅以对象概念与场景概念建模静态上下文；形成typed trajectory/object/scene接口，支撑动作分类预测及VQA的问题条件化概念匹配。5个随机种子、6个基准（NTU RGB+D Mutual、Chiral SSv2、UCF-101、KTH、TOMATO、STAR）评估。

Result: 相对不可解释基线，动作识别平均精度下降约1.0点，VQA名义提升0.4点；运动扰动与概念移除实验显示方向性语义对齐与决策忠实性，样本级干预可支持针对性调试。

Conclusion: 概念式、轨迹驱动的解释框架可在几乎不损性能的前提下提供更完整、来源明确的运动证据解释，兼具分类与VQA通用性。

Abstract: Video understanding models often rely on motion, yet their explanations can obscure which motion sources support a prediction. We identify motion-source under-specification as a limitation of existing video XAI: region-centric explanations can be source-ambiguous, while motion vocabularies restricted to single-person pose sequences are source-incomplete. We propose TraCE (Trajectory-Aware Concepts for Explainable Video Understanding), a concept-based framework that discovers source-flexible motion concepts by clustering optical-flow trajectories from training videos. These concepts represent dynamic evidence from people, objects, and interactions, while object and scene concepts account for static context. The resulting typed trajectory/object/scene interface supports class prediction for action recognition and question-conditioned concept matching for video question answering (VQA). We evaluate TraCE across five random seeds on six benchmarks: NTU RGB+D Mutual, Chiral SSv2, UCF-101, KTH, TOMATO, and STAR. Relative to non-interpretable baselines, TraCE maintains competitive task performance, with an average accuracy drop of 1.0 points for action recognition and a nominal gain of 0.4 points for VQA. Motion perturbation and concept-removal tests provide complementary evidence of directional semantic alignment and decision faithfulness, while sample-level interventions illustrate targeted model debugging. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [134] [Gaussians Can Subtract: Neural Dissolve Fields for Hybrid Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07049)
*Yiyan Wang,Haohan Liu,Tetsuo Shibuya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gaussolve 把网格-高斯混合渲染中的冲突重新表述为"多视角不一致"问题，提出"先溶解、再回填"框架：用轻量神经溶解场学习在何处、从哪些视角溶解不一致的网格内容，再用连续透射率融合规则让残余高斯回填裸露区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网格-高斯混合表示多采用加法形式，令高斯残差去补偿网格误差；但并非所有冲突都源于内容缺失——残差几何误差、遮挡不匹配、纹理投影伪影、视角相关外观都会导致同一网格内容在某视角一致、在另一视角不一致。因此需要区分"错误"与"缺失"。

Method: 将混合渲染建模为多视角不一致问题；引入由标量球谐高斯参数化的 Neural Dissolve Field，学习溶解位置与溶解视角方向；设计连续透射率融合规则，渐进暴露被溶解网格后方的高斯，保留其重建梯度并避免硬性可见性跳变；形成 subtrand-and-refill（先减后补）的联合优化。

Result: 在结构化与半结构化场景上，相比基线获得更高的视觉保真度，同时使用更少的高斯基元并实现更快渲染。

Conclusion: "溶解-回填"式选择性减除比纯加性残差更适合处理网格-高斯混合表示中的视角相关冲突，在效率与质量上取得双赢；属于增量式混合表示优化，缺乏大规模泛化与真实机器人部署验证。

Abstract: Hybrid Mesh-Gaussian rendering combines compact textured surfaces with flexible 3D Gaussian primitives, offering an efficient alternative to purely Gaussian scene representations for structured environments. Existing scene-reconstruction hybrids commonly follow an additive formulation, where Gaussian residuals compensate for errors left by the mesh. However, not every Mesh-Gaussian conflict is caused by missing content: residual geometry errors, occlusion mismatch, texture-projection artifacts, and view-dependent appearance can make the same mesh content consistent in one view but inconsistent in another. We therefore formulate hybrid rendering as a multi-view inconsistency problem and present Gaussolve, a subtract-and-refill framework that selectively dissolves inconsistent mesh content before residual Gaussians refill the revealed regions. We introduce a lightweight Neural Dissolve Field, parameterized by scalar spherical-harmonic Gaussians, to learn where and from which viewing directions mesh content should be dissolved. A continuous transmittance fusion rule progressively exposes residual Gaussians behind the dissolved mesh, preserving their reconstruction gradients and avoiding hard visibility discontinuities. Experiments on structured and semi-structured scenes demonstrate higher visual fidelity with fewer Gaussian primitives and faster rendering. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [135] [Expanding the Quality-Coverage Frontier with Competitive Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07055)
*Wenxuan Peng,Richard Zhang,Haitian Zheng,Jaskirat Singh,Tsai-Shien Chen,Eli Shechtman,Bharath Hariharan,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flow matching生成器存在质量-覆盖权衡：单一速度预测只能对多模态条件取平均。提出训练时赢家通吃（winner-take-all）竞争：加入随机低维条件码z，采样K个候选速度，仅对最匹配目标的候选计算损失，使不同模态各自成形；并由此衍生辅助条件缩放（auxiliary conditioning scaling）引导。ImageNet-256上以不到3%的SiT预训练算力后训练，67% Recall处FDr,6由19.22降至5.91；RAEv2上70% Recall处由16.83降至3.18。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FM生成器在质量与覆盖度间存在固有权衡；无分类器引导（CFG）提升质量却牺牲多样性。作者假设根源在于目标函数性质：一个中间噪声图像可对应多个干净图像，而模型只能输出单一速度，最优也只能对多模态取平均，导致模式坍缩式折中。

Method: 在速度预测器中引入随机采样的低维条件码z；每个训练样本采样K个z生成K个候选速度，只有与真实目标最匹配的候选获得损失（winner-take-all），从而让不同模式分派给不同预测而非被平均。该竞争项同时自然支持一种新的引导方式——辅助条件缩放（auxiliary conditioning scaling），可与传统CFG互补叠加。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上以SiT与RAEv2两个测试平台验证。后训练仅用不到原始SiT预训练算力3%：67% Recall点FDr,6从19.22降至5.91；RAEv2在70% Recall点从16.83降至3.18。质量-覆盖前沿显著外推。

Conclusion: 通过训练期预测竞争将多模态不确定性显式分配给多个候选预测，可缓解FM的质量-覆盖权衡，并提供与CFG正交的新引导维度；计算开销低（<3%预训练算力后训练），适用于不同骨干。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) generators often face a quality-coverage tradeoff. Inference-time techniques such as classifier-free guidance raise quality, but at the cost of coverage. We hypothesize this limitation stems from a basic property of the objective: an intermediate noisy image could correspond to many clean images, yet the model can only predict a single velocity, at best averaging over the multiple possibilities. To mitigate this, we introduce competition among predictions during training. We achieve this by augmenting the predictor with a randomly drawn low-dimensional conditioning code $z$. For each training example, we sample $K$ values of $z$ to produce $K$ candidate velocities that compete to explain the target, and apply the loss only to the best-matching candidate. This winner-take-all objective allows different modes to shape different predictions, rather than being averaged into a single compromise. Furthermore, this term naturally enables a new form of guidance, auxiliary conditioning scaling, which complements traditional guidance methods. Together, these two guidance mechanisms show that our method substantially improves the quality-coverage frontier. We validate on ImageNet-256, using two different testbeds, SiT and RAEv2. Given less than 3% of the original SiT pretraining compute for post-training, at the 67% Recall point, our method reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{r,6}$ from 19.22 to 5.91. On RAEv2, at the 70% Recall point, it reduces $\mathrm{FD}_{r,6}$ from 16.83 to 3.18.

</details>
