Experiments on three image domains show that editor-aware training improves user recovery with both reference-vector matching and binary watermark decoding. Separately trained models reach 87.5% mean identification accuracy among 500 users. When tested with a different editor without retraining, the watermark modules still recover user information. These results demonstrate that learning watermarks through the editing process enables user attribution without modifying the editor.

</details>


### [170] [Neural Image Compression with Internal-External Prior Fusion and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07644)
*Tiancheng Si,Wenlong Xu,Dahua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IE-PFA上下文模型，用可学习字典作为外部先验，结合RWKV空间-通道先验融合与训练期自蒸馏对齐，实现单网络64档变率学习图像压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式图像压缩的上下文模型只依赖当前图像内部先验，忽略了自然图像间共享的常见模式与典型纹理，限制了熵模型的预测精度。

Method: 基于四叉树空间-通道联合建模构建Internal-External Prior Fusion-Alignment框架：编解码器共享的可学习字典充当外部先验；RWKV-based Spatial-Channel Prior Fusion (RSCPF) 以线性复杂度沿空间与通道维度融合内外先验；训练专用的Self-Distilled Prior Alignment (SDPA) 完成先验对齐且不增加推理开销；单模型支持64档质量等级，并配自定义CUDA算子加速。

Result: 在7个基准上达到SOTA率失真性能，相对VTM-22.0-Intra平均BD-Rate节省21.65%；仅29.9M参数；编码61 ms、解码52 ms，为先进神经编解码器中速度最快。

Conclusion: 将外部先验引入上下文建模并结合高效线性复杂度融合与无推理开销的对齐机制，可在小参数量、高速度下显著提升学习式图像压缩的率失真性能，并支持单模型可变码率。

Abstract: Existing context models for learned image com- pression rely primarily on internal priors from the current image, overlooking common patterns and typical textures shared across natural images. To address this limitation, we propose an Internal-External Prior Fusion-Alignment (IE-PFA) context model built upon quadtree spatial-channel joint modeling, where a learnable dictionary shared by the encoder and decoder serves as an external prior. Specifically, an RWKV-based Spatial- Channel Prior Fusion (RSCPF) module efficiently fuses inter- nal and external priors along spatial and channel dimensions with linear complexity, while a training-only Self-Distilled Prior Alignment (SDPA) mechanism aligns them without inference overhead. Moreover, our model supports variable-rate coding over 64 quality levels within a single network and is accelerated by custom CUDA operators. Experiments on seven benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art rate-distortion performance, achiev- ing an average BD-Rate saving of 21.65% over VTM-22.0-Intra, with only 29.9M parameters and the fastest coding speed among advanced neural codecs (61 ms encoding and 52 ms decoding). Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [171] [UniGADD: Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07647)
*Bernard Tomczyk,Agil Aghasanli,Plamen Angelov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniGADD：两阶段监督学习的通用深度伪造检测器，第一阶段用对比损失学习真假判别嵌入，第二阶段仅对真实样本做簇间分离/簇内紧凑精修，提升对未见生成器的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在已知生成器上精度高，但对未见过的生成器泛化差，限制实际部署的对抗内容检测能力。

Method: 两阶段优化：阶段一在真实+已知伪造图像上用监督对比损失学习判别性嵌入；阶段二仅对真实样本施加簇间分离与簇内紧凑约束，精修嵌入空间边界，使真实分布更紧凑、对未知伪造域更易判别。

Result: 在已知生成器上的准确率与SOTA持平，在未见生成器上显著优于对比方法，展示可扩展性与实用性（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 通过在真实数据侧进行表示空间正则化，可不依赖伪造生成器的多样性即获得跨生成器泛化；为开放世界的合成内容检测提供了轻量可行的思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Universal Generator-Agnostic Deepfake Detector (UniGADD), a novel method that employs supervised learning to achieve high accuracy on known deepfake generators, while maintaining robust performance on previously unseen ones. The proposed approach follows a two-stage optimisation process. In the first stage, a contrastive loss encourages the model to learn discriminative feature embeddings from real and known fake images, resulting in strong performance within the training domain. In the second stage, the embedding space is refined by promoting inter-cluster separation and intra-cluster compactness, applied exclusively to real samples. This refinement enhances generalisability, enabling the method to exhibit improved robustness against unseen deepfake generation techniques. UniGADD achieves accuracy on par with state-of-the-art methods for known generators, while significantly outperforming them on unseen cases, demonstrating its scalability and practical applicability for adversarial content detection.

</details>


### [172] [Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling with Target-Aware Bounded Optimization for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07648)
*Ziqiao Weng,Yuanzhi Liang,Haibing Huang,Weidong Cai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GARM+TABO后训练框架：用生成器支撑匹配与内容匹配真实对比构建对齐生成器分布的属性奖励，并将奖励映射为有界函数校准于真实目标样本；配套TA-GRPO（阶段优势缩放+课程+时间步平衡信用分配）优化流式视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成模型后训练需增强目标属性而不损害通用生成能力。奖励引导后训练面临三难：真实数据与生成器输出分布不匹配；属性监督被无关内容混杂；无界奖励最大化导致过优化与画质退化。

Method: 1) GARM生成器对齐奖励建模：生成器支撑匹配缓解分布错配，内容匹配真实对比降低内容混杂，得到生成器相关且聚焦属性的奖励；2) TABO目标感知有界优化：将奖励映射为光滑上界函数，由真实目标正例校准，与优化算法无关，兼容直接奖励梯度与策略式优化；3) TA-GRPO：修改Flow-GRPO，加入固定分阶段优势缩放、生成器到目标课程、时间步平衡信用分配。

Result: GARM提升目标属性表达且生成质量保持竞争力；TABO在属性增强与视觉质量间优于无约束奖励最大化；TA-GRPO相比原始Flow-GRPO提升目标增强，并减少中性提示上的非预期属性泄漏。以定量实验与对比消融为主，未见大规模人工评测细节。

Conclusion: 奖励监督需与生成器分布对齐，优化需数据校准且有界，才能实现可控的定向视觉生成；该框架可迁移到流式视频等策略式生成后训练中。

Abstract: Post-training aims to enhance desired attributes in visual generative models while preserving their general generation capabilities. Reward-guided post-training faces two challenges: real training data may mismatch the generator's output distribution, and attribute supervision may be confounded by unrelated content. Unbounded reward maximization can also cause overoptimization and degrade generation quality. We propose a framework combining Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling (GARM) and Target-Aware Bounded Optimization (TABO). GARM mitigates distribution mismatch through generator-support matching and reduces content confounding using content-matched real contrasts, yielding generator-relevant, attribute-focused reward supervision. TABO maps the learned reward to a smooth, upper-bounded function calibrated from real target-positive examples. This transformation is independent of the optimization algorithm and supports both direct reward-gradient and policy-based optimization. We further introduce Target-Aware GRPO (TA-GRPO), an adaptation of Flow-GRPO that combines fixed stage-wise advantage scaling, a generator-to-target curriculum, and timestep-balanced credit assignment for flow-based video optimization. Experiments show that GARM improves target-attribute expression while maintaining competitive generation quality. TABO better balances attribute enhancement and visual quality than unconstrained reward maximization, while TA-GRPO improves target enhancement over vanilla Flow-GRPO and reduces unintended attribute leakage on neutral prompts. These results highlight the importance of generator-aligned reward supervision and controlled, data-calibrated optimization for targeted visual generation.

</details>


### [173] [ArtCrafter: Feed-Forward Generation of Articulated 3D Object with Analytic Joint Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07658)
*Minh Canh Tu,Quang-Binh Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtCrafter 是一个前馈生成模型，用一对 rest/open 图像一次性生成带部件分割的铰接 3D 资产（网格），并从刚性变换解析出 URDF 关节参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真与训练需要铰接 3D 资产，但从随意拍摄的图像生成时，部件几何与运动学（kinematics）难以保证互相一致；现有方法常在几何与关节参数上解耦或分步处理，导致不一致。

Method: 基于 DiT 的生成模型，在单次前向中同时生成两种状态下的部件级网格；采用受 Slot Attention 启发的 1+2N 槽位布局（1 个共享 base 槽 + N 对 rest/open 槽），使同一对槽绑定同一运动部件；引入 Triplet Attention 通过共享 base 槽锚定跨状态几何一致性，并在解码部件点云上加斥力损失 L_rep 防止部件穿插；最后由每个 rest/open 对的刚性变换解析计算关节类型、轴、原点与范围。

Result: 在分布内与分布外数据集上的实验表明，在相同双状态输入设定下，ArtCrafter 在几何质量与铰接精度上优于现有方法，并与不同输入设定/范式的方 法保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 以槽位配对 + 跨状态一致性约束 + 解析关节求解的方式统一生成几何与运动学，得到可解释、可用的 URDF 输出，为机器人仿真资产构建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Articulated 3D assets are essential for robotic simulation and training, yet their generation from casual images poses a key challenge: recovering part geometry and kinematics that remain mutually consistent. We present ArtCrafter, a novel feed-forward generative model for controllable generation of articulated 3D objects from a pair of images, one depicting the object in the rest state and the other in the open state. At the core of ArtCrafter is a DiT-based generative model that jointly produces part-level meshes for both articulation states in a single forward pass. Inspired by Slot Attention, its denoising process operates on a $1+2N$ slot layout comprising one shared base slot and $N$ paired rest/open slots, encouraging each pair to bind to the same kinematic part. To ensure geometrically consistent and disjoint part generation, we introduce Triplet Attention to enforce cross-state geometric consistency between paired rest/open slots anchored through the shared base slot, and a repulsion loss $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{rep}}$ on decoded part point clouds to prevent inter-part penetration throughout denoising. Given the resulting paired meshes, URDF joint parameters---type, axis, origin, and range---are derived analytically from the rigid transformation between each rest/open pair, providing an interpretable alternative to learned joint-parameter regression. Extensive experiments on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution datasets demonstrate that ArtCrafter outperforms existing method under the same dual-state setting in geometry quality and articulation precision, while remaining competitive with methods operating under different input settings and paradigms.

</details>


### [174] [Which Objects Stay? Learning Hierarchical Persistence Beliefs for Lifelong Open-Vocabulary Object Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07662)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Haolan Zhang,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPer用检索到的视觉/几何相似历史实例估计物体存活率先验（无需类别标签），以共轭Beta信念初始化，并纳入Rosen持久性滤波框架做闭式更新，再随重识别在物体间迁移以实现物体级可移动性的在线学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身物体地图需要判断物体离开视野后是否仍在原位，但现有持久性滤波依赖基于类别的存活先验，在开放词汇场景下不可靠。

Method: 分层持久性滤波：对每个新物体检索视觉与几何相似的训练实例，由其观测历史估计初始存活率（无类别标签）；该估计初始化存活概率上的共轭Beta信念，在Rosen持久性滤波框架下用检测与漏检做闭式更新；物体在新位置被重识别时迁移其持久性信念，从而在时间上学习物体特定的可移动性。

Result: 120条留出仿真序列上一致优于对比方法；检索先验优于CLIP标签先验，物体级在线学习带来额外提升；47个留出3RScan环境上检索先验亦优于SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的检索先验可替代语义类别先验，配合信念迁移实现开放词汇下的终身物体地图持久性估计。

Abstract: Lifelong object maps must estimate whether an object remains at its previously observed location after it leaves view. Existing persistence filters rely on class-based survival priors, making them difficult to apply reliably in open-vocabulary settings. We introduce HiPer, a hierarchical persistence filter that derives persistence beliefs directly from data rather than semantic categories. For a new object, HiPer retrieves visually and geometrically similar training instances and estimates an initial survival rate from their observed histories, without requiring class labels. This estimate initializes a conjugate Beta belief over the object's survival probability, which is updated in closed form using detections and missed observations under Rosen's persistence-filter framework. When an object is re-identified at a new location, its persistence belief is transferred with the object, enabling the map to learn object-specific mobility over time. Experiments on 120 held-out simulated sequences show that HiPer consistently achieves the best performance among competing methods. Retrieval-based priors outperform CLIP-derived label priors, and object-specific online learning provides further gains. Results on 47 held-out 3RScan environments demonstrate that the proposed retrieval-based prior also outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in real-world settings.

</details>


### [175] [CoGenRec: A Unified Generative Framework for Complementary Vision Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07665)
*Yapeng Meng,Yuguo Chen,Xiangru Chen,Shaojun Xu,Xuezhen Wang,Lijian Wang,Zheyu Yang,Yihan Lin,Luping Shi,Rong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出互补视觉传感器(CVS)与生成式重建框架CoGenRec，统一视频去模糊、高时间分辨率插帧与HDR融合，性能显著优于通用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧式相机受带宽、功耗、量子效率限制，在时间分辨率、空间细节、信噪比与动态范围之间存在根本权衡，导致时间欠采样、运动模糊与饱和。CVS以RGB长曝光稀疏路径+时空差分短曝光稠密路径的互补分解缓解该矛盾，但缺少充分利用互补测量的重建方法。

Method: 将两路互补测量编码为多尺度时空特征：曝光感知外观建模+双向时间传播；所得特征约束视频扩散模型（作为生成先验），并额外注入VAE解码器以恢复潜空间压缩中丢失的细节，融合物理测量的保真度与学习先验的外观恢复能力。

Result: SportsSloMo-CVS上PSNR相对最强通用基线分别提升4.40 dB（去模糊）、3.89 dB（插帧）、5.04 dB（HDR融合）；在129个真实CVS采集的未见场景上获得良好感知质量。

Conclusion: 以互补传感+扩散先验的统一框架在极端运动与大动态范围下实现高质量重建，验证传感器-算法协同设计的可行性。

Abstract: Capturing scenes with extreme motion and high dynamic range is fundamentally constrained by trade-offs among temporal resolution, spatial detail, signal-to-noise ratio, and dynamic range under practical bandwidth, power, and quantum-efficiency limits. Conventional frame-based cameras therefore suffer from temporal undersampling, exposure-induced motion blur, and sensor saturation. The Complementary Vision Sensor (CVS) addresses these limitations by decomposing visual information across two complementary pathways, a spatially dense, temporally sparse, long-exposure RGB pathway and a temporally dense, spatially sparse, short-exposure patiotemporal difference pathway. Their complementary measurements provide appearance, structural, and motion cues across a wide dynamic range that enable reconstruction beyond conventional imaging. We present CoGenRec, a complementarity-driven generative reconstruction framework that unifies video deblurring, high-temporal-resolution frame interpolation, and HDR fusion. CoGenRec encodes complementary measurements into multi-scale spatiotemporal features using exposure-aware appearance modeling and bidirectional temporal propagation. These features constrain a video diffusion model and are injected into the VAE decoder to recover fine structures lost during latent-space compression, combining the scene fidelity of physical measurements with the appearance recovery capabilities of learned video priors. On SportsSloMo-CVS, CoGenRec improves PSNR over the strongest general-purpose baseline by 4.40 dB for deblurring, 3.89 dB for frame interpolation, and 5.04 dB for HDR fusion, respectively. It also achieves strong perceptual quality on 129 unseen scenes captured with real CVS. These results establish a unified approach to advance high-quality reconstruction under challenging motion and illumination conditions. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [176] [Egocentric Action Recognition with Objects and Vision-Language Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07673)
*Raúl Boix-Aldeguer,Manuel Benavent-Lledo,David Ortiz-Perez,Jose Garcia-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 结合冻结的SlowFast视频专家与注意力机制的对象专家（以视觉语言模型零样本生成可供性表示，并引入交互可供性判断手是否操作物体）进行后期融合，提升以自我为中心的动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心视频中动作很大程度上由所操作物体决定，但现有方法主要依赖RGB/深度等视觉模态，很少显式建模物体；此外，训练用标注框、测试用检测框带来的性能落差问题未被充分处理。

Method: 冻结SlowFast视频专家 + 基于注意力的对象专家后期融合；物体用VLM零样本生成的可供性表示；训练用标注框，测试分别用官方主动物体检测器与标注框（上界）；再用同一VLM生成“交互可供性”以剔除未被手操作的误检物体。

Result: 无供能时，标注框融合68.4% Top-1，检测框49.0%；该下降约一半源于非主动物体检测。在MECCANO上，引入物体使视频专家从44.9%提升至49.0%，加入两种可供性后达49.9%，优于SlowFast与物体感知RGB基线。

Conclusion: 物体及可供性表征、以及抑制非交互检测，是提升自我中心动作识别的有效且轻量途径；但绝对精度与检测-标注差距仍较大。

Abstract: Action recognition, and more specifically egocentric action recognition, is a core task in computer vision, with applications such as monitoring the daily life activities of elderly people and task assistance. Recent methods rely on visual modalities such as RGB video or depth. However, in egocentric video, an action is largely defined by its objects, which these methods rarely model. We propose a method that combines a frozen SlowFast video expert with an attention-based object expert by late fusion. Each object is represented with its affordances, that is, what can be done with it, generated zero-shot by a vision-language model. Since datasets such as MECCANO annotate only the objects involved in each action, evaluating with these boxes tells the model which objects are active. We therefore train on annotated boxes and evaluate the method with detections from the official active-object detector, and with annotated boxes as an upper bound. Without affordances, our fusion reaches 68.4% Top-1 with annotated boxes but 49.0% with detections. About half of this drop comes from non-active detections. To remove them, the same vision-language model generates an interaction affordance that decides whether a hand is manipulating each detected object. On MECCANO, objects raise the Top-1 of the video expert from 44.9% to 49.0%, and the two affordances raise it to 49.9%, above SlowFast and object-aware RGB baselines. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [177] [GEKey: Learning Geodesic Eccentric 3D Keypoints via Self-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07677)
*Pierre Onghena,Santiago Velasco-Forero,Beatriz Marcotegui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEKey: self-supervised 3D keypoint detection using the geodesic eccentricity transform as an SE(3)-invariant pseudo-label source, achieving SOTA on ShapeNetCoreV2, KeypointNet, CAPE, ITOP and DeformingThings4D.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Supervised keypoint detection needs costly manual annotation; unsupervised methods using local geometric cues tend to be sensitive to noise, deformation and sampling, and often collapse intra-class variation. The authors want intrinsic, annotation-free salient points that remain instance-specific while preserving category structure.

Method: Compute a geodesic eccentricity transform on the point cloud (geodesic distances aggregated per point), which is provably SE(3)-invariant; treat local extrema / high-eccentricity points as instance-specific salient points and use them as pseudo-labels. Train a point-cloud network to regress these points, with a training scheme that balances instance-specific and category-level structure to keep intra-class variation.

Result: Reported state-of-the-art keypoint detection and keypoint-alignment results on ShapeNetCoreV2, KeypointNet, CAPE, ITOP and DeformingThings4D, with robustness to noise, downsampling, non-rigid deformation and real-world scans; code and pretrained models released.

Conclusion: An SE(3)-invariant intrinsic geometric measure is a simple and effective pseudo-label source for self-supervised 3D keypoint detection, generalizing across synthetic, real and deforming data.

Abstract: Point clouds represent 3D object geometry as sets of spatial coordinates. A fundamental task in their analysis is keypoint detection, i.e., identifying a sparse subset of points that captures the most salient features. While supervised methods rely on manual annotations, unsupervised approaches leverage intrinsic geometric properties to localize keypoints without human input. We introduce GEKey, a self-supervised framework that uses the geodesic eccentricity transform, an SE(3)-invariant geometric measure, to identify instance-specific salient points. These points serve as pseudo-labels to train a model that aligns its predictions with the object's intrinsic structure. GEKey is simple, efficient, and robust, preserving intra-class variation by balancing instance-specific and category-level structure. Extensive experiments on ShapeNetCoreV2, KeypointNet, CAPE, ITOP, and DeformingThings4D show state-of-the-art performance in both keypoint detection and alignment, with strong resilience to noise, downsampling, deformations, and real-world conditions. The code and pretrained models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [178] [DEQ-Det: Dual Evidence Queries for Few-Shot Analog IC Device Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07682)
*Zidong Zhao,Rong Huang,Chengxing Fan,Jie Hu,Malik Haris,Ghazal A. Fahmy,Morteza Mousazadeh,Guoqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEQ-Det 提出"双证据查询"，从同一支持图像中构造前景与背景两类互补参考，在少样本条件下检测版图中的模拟集成电路器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 版图图像中的模拟 IC 器件检测可辅助电路分析与检查，但仅有少量标注样本；器件几何变化大，且与 dummy 或走线结构视觉相似，仅靠前景相似度易产生大量误检。

Method: DEQ-Det：在无需额外标注的前提下，从支持图像同时构建前景参考（刻画目标形态）与背景参考（刻画周边版图模式），二者共同引导查询选择与区域验证，将目标外观与版图上下文联合建模；并加入 RoI 背景证据用于抑制干扰。

Result: 在 5 个留出器件类别、3 个随机种子上，10-shot 与 30-shot 的 macro AP50 分别为 39.41 与 53.03，较最强基线分别提升 3.97 与 9.71 点；匹配的 30-shot 消融显示 RoI 背景证据在召回相当的情况下降低汇总误检。

Conclusion: 显式引入背景/干扰模式参考可有效缓解少样本版图器件检测中的假阳性问题，前景-背景双证据联合验证是该设定下的有效设计。

Abstract: Detecting analog integrated circuit (IC) devices in layout images supports circuit analysis and inspection. With only a few annotated examples, variation in device geometry and visual similarity to dummy or routing structures make reliable detection difficult. Foreground similarity alone can highlight plausible targets without distinguishing them from competing layout patterns. We propose Dual Evidence Queries for Detection (DEQ-Det), which constructs complementary foreground and background references from the same support images without additional annotations. Foreground references capture target morphology, while selected background references characterize surrounding layout patterns. Both sources guide query selection and region verification, combining target appearance with layout context. Across five held-out device categories and three random seeds, DEQ-Det achieves macro AP50 of 39.41 and 53.03 at 10 and 30 shots, exceeding the strongest listed baselines by 3.97 and 9.71 points, respectively. A matched 30-shot ablation further shows that RoI background evidence reduces pooled false positives at comparable recall, supporting its role in interference suppression.

</details>


### [179] [ReferClaw: Disambiguating User Intent for Egocentric Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07683)
*Hangyu Qin,Junbin Xiao,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReferClaw 将自我中心流式视频问答重构为交互式澄清过程：三个协作智能体分别负责候选意图识别、澄清提问与证据 grounded 的回答，并以成本感知的有限轮次策略和过程奖励策略优化学习交互技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心 AI 助手面对的用户问题通常简短歧义（如"我把东西放哪了"），视频与对话历史无法唯一确定指代；现有 VideoQA 强行给出单一解释，容易产生看似合理但偏离用户真实意图的答案。

Method: 提出 agentic 框架 ReferClaw：候选解释识别 agent、澄清提问 agent、回答生成 agent 三体协作；采用有界、成本敏感的策略，在期望信息增益与用户负担之间权衡，并限制澄清轮数与问题复杂度；通过意图专属技能更新和带智能体级信用分配的过程奖励引导策略优化进行训练。在增强的自我中心 QA 基准上评测。

Result: 在增强的自我中心 QA 基准上，答案准确率以及"答案+grounding"联合指标均优于现有基线，并展现出跨数据集的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 交互式澄清是解决自我中心流式辅助中在线用户意图歧义的一种切实可行途径。属多智能体 VideoQA 框架，与具身/3D 重建主线相关度有限。

Abstract: Users of egocentric AI assistants often ask short, ambiguous questions whose intended referents cannot be uniquely resolved from video and dialogue history. For example, "Where did I leave it?" may refer to several previously handled objects. Existing VideoQA systems typically commit to a single interpretation, risking plausible but intention-misaligned answers. We introduce ReferClaw, an agentic framework that formulates egocentric streaming QA as an interactive process: the assistant asks clarifying questions to resolve user intent before answering. Three cooperating agents identify candidate interpretations, request clarification, and generate answers grounded in the resolved intent and visual evidence. A bounded, cost-aware policy balances expected information gain against user effort while limiting clarification turns and question complexity. Referclaw learns from interaction through intent-specific skill updates and process-reward-guided policy optimization with agent-level credit assignment. Evaluations on augmented egocentric QA benchmarks demonstrate improved answer accuracy and joint answer-and-grounding performance over existing baselines, alongside zero-shot transfer across datasets. These findings support interactive clarification as a practical approach to resolving online user intent in egocentric streaming assistance.

</details>


### [180] [InfMasking: A Unified Variational Framework for Synergistic Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07702)
*Liangjian Wen,Qun Dai,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Dongkai Wang,Jun Wang,Fuyuan Cao,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfMasking：融合前对各模态特征随机施加无穷多掩码，要求所有掩码下的融合表示与参考目标（无监督时为未掩码视图，有监督时为类代理）保持一致，以迫使融合模块学习跨模态交互而非依赖主导模态或共享信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型的融合模块常靠单一主导模态或模态间共享信息即可拟合训练目标，真正只在多模态同时观测时才出现的协同信息（synergy）往往未被利用。

Method: 在融合之前对每个模态特征独立随机掩码，每个采样掩码对应一种部分跨模态证据组合及其融合表示；约束这些融合表示与参考目标一致（无监督：未掩码融合视图；有监督：任务标签的类代理）。由于目标是无穷多掩码上的期望，作者将掩码融合表示建模为高斯分布，优化闭式变分下界，其总体矩差距由掩码诱导的协方差控制。

Result: 在所评测基准上，InfMasking 提升预测性能；在学到的模态特定表示上取得更高的任务标签协同（PID）平均估计，训练后的预测器也表现出更高的 InterSHAP 交互归因。

Conclusion: 跨多种部分观测组合施加一致性约束，是一种通用且有效的正则化手段，可抑制主导模态依赖并推动融合模块学习在部分观测下仍有意义的跨模态交互。

Abstract: Multimodal models are expected to exploit synergistic information, that is, information available only when multiple modalities are observed together. In practice, however, a fusion module can fit the training objective by relying on a dominant modality or on information that modalities share, and the synergy between modalities is often left unused. We propose InfMasking, a unified multimodal learning framework based on Infinite Masking. Before fusion, InfMasking randomly masks the features of each modality; each sampled mask produces a different partial combination of cross-modal evidence and a fused representation of the corresponding modality interactions. All masked fused representations are required to remain consistent with a reference target: the unmasked fused view when training is unsupervised, and the class proxy of the task label when labels are available. Maintaining this consistency across diverse partial combinations discourages reliance on a dominant modality or on shared information alone, and drives the fusion module to learn cross-modal interaction patterns that remain meaningful under partial observations. Since the objective is an expectation over infinitely many masks, we model the masked fused representations as Gaussian and optimize closed-form variational bounds, whose population-moment gaps are controlled by the masking-induced covariance under this model. On the evaluated benchmarks, InfMasking improves predictive performance and yields higher mean PID estimates of task-label synergy in learned modality-specific representations, alongside higher InterSHAP interaction attribution in the trained predictors. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [181] [FlyingDrones: A Dataset and Benchmark for Optical Flow Estimation from UAV motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07705)
*Laurenz Ruzicka,Roman Pflugfelder,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlyingDrones合成基准（基于物理渲染的稠密光流与分割真值）及分辨率归一化指标EPE Norm，在999对、三种分辨率上评测16类SOTA光流模型族，并用真实UAV视频做无真值运动对齐验证与微调实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中监视、空域监控与远距跟踪中运动线索在表观信息弱时仍具信息量，但小型UAV目标像素少、对比度低、背景动态复杂；现有光流基准以大型物体中心运动为主，无法刻画远距小目标，导致模型在该场景的评估与改进缺乏可靠标尺。

Method: 构建FlyingDrones生成式基准：基于物理渲染（PBR）生成无人机运动序列并提供稠密光流与分割掩码真值；定义按图像尺寸归一化的EPE Norm指标；在按运动幅度与目标尺寸分层的999对光流上，于三种分辨率评测16个SOTA模型族；另外开展两项补充研究——在21个真实UAV相机视频上做无真值运动对齐评估（Spearman排序相关），并用800对FlyingDrones数据对4种架构做微调。

Result: 所有模型族均优于零光流基线，但族间差距随分辨率扩大：1920x1080时全局匹配、循环、Transformer与短时窗时序模型明显领先金字塔、经典、自监督、轻量与扩散类模型；族内checkpoint波动与族间差异相当甚至更大。合成排名对真实视频表现具中等显著预测性（Spearman ρ=0.63，41个checkpoint），但具体头部排序不同；800对微调使留出无人机区域误差最高降低37%，并改善全部四种架构的真实视频定位效果。

Conclusion: 远距小目标光流需要专门基准：分辨率归一化评测可稳定区分模型族，且训练课程（curriculum）与架构同等重要；合成基准排名可作为真实场景的中等强度代理信号，少量真实域相关数据微调即可显著迁移，说明基准具备实用诊断与数据价值。

Abstract: Optical flow is an important cue for drone analysis in aerial surveillance, airspace monitoring, and long-range target tracking, where motion remains informative when appearance is weak. Estimating the motion of small Unmanned Aerial Vehicles, however, is difficult because distant targets occupy few pixels, have low contrast, and move against complex dynamic backgrounds. Standard optical-flow benchmarks emphasize large, object-centric motions and therefore do not characterize distant aerial targets well. We introduce FlyingDrones, a generated benchmark with dense ground-truth optical flow and segmentation masks derived from physically based rendering, and define resolution-normalized metrics (EPE Norm) that normalize flow by image dimensions. We evaluate 16 state-of-the-art model families on a motion- and size-stratified subset of 999 flow pairs at three resolutions. Every family recovers drone motion better than the zero-flow reference, but the gap between families widens with resolution: at 1920x1080, global-matching, recurrent, transformer, and short-window temporal models separate clearly from pyramid, classical, self-supervised, lightweight, and diffusion models. Within-family checkpoint spread is comparable to or larger than between-family spread, so the training curriculum is as important as the architecture. Two complementary studies test the benchmark beyond synthetic accuracy: a ground-truth-free motion-alignment evaluation on 21 cameras of real UAV footage shows that the synthetic ranking carries a moderate, significant signal about real-world performance (Spearman \r{ho} = 0.63 over 41 checkpoints) while the exact top ranks differ, and a fine-tuning study shows that 800 FlyingDrones pairs reduce held-out drone-region error by up to 37% and improve drone localization on the real footage for all four architectures tested.

</details>


### [182] [AffineGS: Unlocking Accurate Geometry in 3DGS via Spatially Varying Affine Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07709)
*Tze Ho Elden Tse,Jizong Peng,Richard Chen,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出单目深度网络存在区域相关的分段线性畸变，故全局尺度-平移对齐失效；提出空间变化仿射校正模型（区域平滑、边缘感知场）将单目视差校正为度量深度，并推导法向感知正则项耦合校正视差与3DGS深度/法向，实现无传感器条件下几何精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS视觉质量高但几何精度不足；现有深度引导方法假设单目深度仅需单一全局尺度-平移对齐，而该假设与真实畸变分布不符。

Method: 诊断单目深度网络的分段线性、区域相关畸变；构建空间变化仿射校正（SVAR）场，用区域平滑且边缘感知的变换将视差转为一致度量深度；设计法向感知正则器，利用空间梯度将校正后视差与3DGS深度及法向耦合。

Result: 在多种设置下保持3DGS光度质量，同时显著提升几何精度，优于现有几何导向SOTA方法，且无需额外传感器。

Conclusion: 纠正了全局对齐的固有假设，为3DGS深度先验正则提供了更合理的空间变化建模与法向耦合机制。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the leading approach for photorealistic novel view synthesis, yet its geometric accuracy lags behind its visual quality. Existing depth-guided methods attempt to resolve this by regularizing the optimization with monocular depth priors, typically using a single global scale-and-shift alignment. Crucially, we discover a fundamental flaw in this assumption: modern monocular depth networks systematically exhibit region-dependent, piecewise-linear distortions. This discovery invalidates standard global alignment strategies, which warp scene geometry by enforcing a single transform. To address this, we introduce a spatially varying affine rectification model that corrects monocular disparity into consistent metric depth using regionally smooth, edge-aware fields. We further derive a normal-aware regularizer that uses spatial gradients to couple the rectified disparity with 3DGS depth and normals. Across diverse experimental settings, our sensor-free method shows strong photometric quality of Gaussian Splatting while substantially improving geometric accuracy and outperforming state-of-the art geometry-focused methods.

</details>


### [183] [Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation in Pixels, Interaction and World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07711)
*Jinkun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAH：基于 SAM 3D Body 手部分支微调与特征蒸馏的快速单目手部网格估计器，同时预测 2D/3D 关节与表面点并拟合 MANO/MHR-Hand，可选估计解剖部位与接触，ViT-L 达 65 fps。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目手部网格估计器通常先预测 MANO 等骨架参数再投影得到 2D 关键点，这种"参数→投影"的路径存在误差累积，且 3D 关节与 2D 预测之间不一致；同时精度与推理速度难以兼得，且接触/部位等结构信息通常依赖目标物体几何。

Method: 核心观察：直接预测的 3D 关节与 2D 预测更一致，且将网格拟合到预测点可降低网格误差，据此构造"预测点 + 拟合"范式。模型以 SAM 3D Body 手部分支为骨干，经微调与特征蒸馏得到 FAH 模型族；输出对应的 2D/3D 关节与表面点，再拟合 MANO 或 MHR-Hand 参数；可选出解剖部位与手-物接触（推理时无需物体几何）；采用因果细化（causal refinement）降低加速度误差，并做相机估计以将手放置到世界坐标。

Result: 在图像空间对齐、手部几何精度、接触估计与世界坐标跟踪上均优于所评估基线；默认 ViT-L 在 RTX 4070 Ti SUPER 上含网格拟合达 65 fps，开启部位与接触为 49 fps；最小 ViT-S 在无网格拟合/部位/接触时达 125 fps。

Conclusion: "直接预测点再拟合网格"优于传统"参数回归再投影"路径，兼顾精度与实时性；蒸馏使小模型可部署，接触与部位估计无需物体几何，适合作为手-物交互与 VR/遥操作中的高效手部前端。

Abstract: Monocular hand mesh estimators commonly predict rig parameters and project the mesh to obtain 2D keypoints. We find that directly predicted 3D joints agree better with 2D predictions, and fitting a mesh to predicted points reduces mesh error. We present FAH (Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation), a family of models built on SAM 3D Body's hand branch through fine-tuning and feature distillation. The models predict corresponding 2D and 3D joints and surface points, fit MANO or MHR-Hand, and optionally estimate anatomical parts and contact without object geometry at inference. Causal refinement reduces acceleration error, and camera estimation places the hands in world space. Experiments show improvements in image-space alignment, hand geometry, contact estimation and world-space tracking over the evaluated baselines. The default ViT-L model runs at 65 fps with mesh fitting on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, or 49 fps with parts and contact enabled. The smallest ViT-S model reaches 125 fps without mesh fitting, parts or contact. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [184] [Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors for Event-Based Point Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07713)
*Xiaokai Song,Zhang Li,Leqi Liu,Yibin Ye,Kun Wang,Xichao Teng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEPT：仅用事件相机的长序列任意点跟踪框架，通过任务自适应事件证据调制（TEEM）与持久轨迹先验（PTP）提升事件TAP与特征跟踪稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在高速运动与极端光照下有优势，但事件活动稀疏且随场景/运动变化，长序列跟踪易漂移，需要选择性使用信息性事件证据并在时间窗间保持轨迹约束。

Method: 基于ETAP架构提出两个模块：(1) TEEM：仅用跟踪监督学习有界可靠性场，在特征提取前对事件张量在空间、时间与通道维度加权；(2) PTP：用Schur补对固定局部二次轨迹模型边缘化，跨重叠窗口传递几何信息，含可见性感知校正与裁剪终端速度初始化。

Result: 仅在合成EventKubric上训练，在5个数据集上评测：TAP任务平均Jaccard优于重训ETAP基线；特征跟踪在feature age与expected feature age上更优；在EC与EDS上超过若干事件+帧融合方法。

Conclusion: 选择性事件证据加权与跨窗轨迹先验可有效缓解事件稀疏性与长时序漂移，纯事件输入可在长序列TAP与特征跟踪上超越基线及部分融合方法；代码开源。

Abstract: Event cameras enable tracking under fast motion and challenging illumination. However, stable long-term point tracking remains challenging: event activity is sparse and varies with scene content and motion, complicating correspondence, while trajectory estimates can drift over long sequences. Together, these challenges motivate a tracker that selectively uses informative event evidence and retains trajectory constraints across temporal windows. We propose Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors (SEPT), an event-only framework for tracking any point (TAP) over long sequences. SEPT introduces two complementary modules: Task-Adaptive Event Evidence Modulation (TEEM) and Persistent Trajectory Prior (PTP). Using only tracking supervision, TEEM learns a bounded reliability field that weights the event tensor across space, time, and channels before feature extraction. PTP carries geometric information across overlapping windows by marginalizing a fixed local quadratic trajectory model with a Schur complement. Its visibility-aware correction refines overlap coordinates, while a clipped terminal velocity initializes coordinates at newly exposed timestamps. SEPT builds on the ETAP architecture. SEPT is trained exclusively on synthetic EventKubric data and evaluated on five datasets across TAP and feature tracking. Results show that SEPT outperforms the retrained ETAP baseline in average Jaccard for TAP and in feature age and expected feature age for feature tracking; it also surpasses several methods combining event and frame inputs on EC and EDS. The source code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [185] [Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck for Multimodal Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07718)
*Liangjian Wen,Jiangtao Zheng,Jiang Duan,Qun Dai,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Jun Wang,Shu-Dong Huang,Xinwang Liu,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIIB：基于部分信息分解（PID）的多模态自监督框架，显式编码冗余/独有/协同信息，用可处理熵目标（变分界+score-based熵估计）实现最小充分表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态自监督面临异质性、信息量不等与复杂交互；对比学习依赖强多视角冗余假设，忽略模态独有与协同信息，导致表征次优。

Method: 双路径架构：模态专有编码 + 多模态融合；以PID分解冗余/独有/协同信息；用替代的可处理熵式目标（变分上界 + score-based 熵估计）强制最小充分表征并抑制多余信息，无需显式互信息估计。

Result: 在多个多模态基准（图像/文本/音频/表格）上一致优于SOTA多模态自监督方法。

Conclusion: 以信息瓶颈视角统一刻画多模态交互，提供了可扩展的自监督训练目标；但摘要未涉具身/3D任务，落地价值需阅读方法细节。

Abstract: Multimodal self-supervised learning is challenged by heterogeneity, unequal informativeness, and complex information interactions across multimodal data sources such as images, text, audio, and tabular data. While contrastive methods effectively align shared semantics, they typically rely on strong multi-view redundancy assumptions, overlooking modality-specific and synergistic information, thus leading to suboptimal representations. We propose the Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck (MIIB), a principled framework grounded in Partial Information Decomposition that explicitly encodes redundant, unique, and synergistic information within multimodal interactions. MIIB employs a dual-path model architecture for modality-specific encoding and multimodal fusion, optimized using surrogate tractable entropy-based objectives that enforce minimal sufficient representations without requiring explicit mutual information estimation. By leveraging variational bounds and score-based entropy estimation, MIIB efficiently captures complex cross-modal interactions while suppressing superfluous information. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art multimodal self-supervised methods. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [186] [BiGrounder: Bidirectional Training Unlocks the Potential of GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07765)
*Dengyao Shi,Tianyu Fu,Fei Hu,Anyang Su,Hanning Wang,Shuo Wang,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双向奖励范式（SFT阶段学习"坐标→指令"的反向生成能力，RL阶段on-policy采样语义一致的指令变体并回投影至目标，用循环一致性门控耦合语义质量与定位成功），实现GUI grounding模型自强化，4B模型在5个基准上取得有竞争力的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI grounding以"指令→坐标"端到端监督，训练集每个目标坐标仅少量描述，难以覆盖真实场景的语言多样性；而引入RL的方法因模型初始探索能力受限，RL阶段增益有限。

Method: 双向训练范式：1) SFT阶段同时训练正向（指令→坐标）与反向（坐标→动作描述）映射；2) RL阶段利用反向能力on-policy采样同一目标的语义一致指令变体，再执行grounding回原目标；3) 循环一致性门控以grounding成功与否筛选语义质量，将模型自身的指令策略转化为持续刷新的强化轨迹来源。

Result: 4B参数模型在5个GUI grounding基准评测中取得有竞争力的准确率，验证该训练范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 自生成指令变体+循环一致性筛选可有效扩充监督信号并突破RL探索瓶颈，为GUI grounding及一般agent定位任务提供数据高效的自强化训练框架。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding aims to map natural language instructions to precise operational coordinates, serving as a critical task for autonomous agent interaction. Current approaches formalize GUI grounding as predicting action-coordinate pairs from instructions, with end-to-end training supervised solely by final coordinates. Training datasets typically provide only a small number of descriptions per target coordinate, whereas real-world scenarios involve a much larger set of varied descriptions for the same target, which limits robustness to linguistic variation. While recent GUI grounding paradigms have begun employing reinforcement learning to enhance localization accuracy, models with limited exploratory capability before reinforcement yield constrained improvements even with identical training data during the reinforcement phase. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bidirectional reward paradigm that enables model self-reinforcement. During SFT, bidirectional training equips the model to generate action descriptions from target coordinates. During RL, the model samples semantically consistent instruction variations on-policy and grounds each variation back to its target. A cycle-consistency gate couples semantic quality to grounding success, turning the model's own instruction policy into a source of continually refreshed reinforcement trajectories. Our 4B-parameter model achieves competitive accuracy across five grounding benchmarks in our evaluation, indicating the effectiveness of the proposed training paradigm. this https URL

</details>


### [187] [Rain Beyond Streaks: Cascaded Realistic Rain and Wet Surface Synthesis via Scene Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07793)
*Melissa Tijink,Kirill Tolstobrov,Shunxin Wang,Estefania Talavera,Duc V. Le,Luuk Spreeuwers,Shaodi You,Nicola Strisciuglio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeRS：利用深度与分割先验的零样本可控生成管线，合成雨纹、反射与积水等场景一致雨效，并构建 Cityscapes-CascadeRS 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雨合成方法只关注雨纹，忽略反射、积水等表面效应，削弱感知真实性与下游视觉任务鲁棒性评测的有效性。

Method: 基于深度与分割先验的物理启发式零样本生成流程，级联生成语义对齐的雨纹、反射与积水，支持可控参数调节。

Result: 生成高感知真实感的城市雨天场景，用户研究与定量指标验证优越性；发布 Cityscapes-CascadeRS 数据集用于下游任务鲁棒性评测。

Conclusion: CascadeRS 提供了可扩展、真实的雨天数据合成框架，可用于评估与提升恶劣天气下视觉系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: Realistic rain synthesis is a persistent challenge for vision systems operating in adverse weather. Existing methods primarily focus on generating rain streaks, often neglecting consequent surface effects such as reflections and puddles, which are critical for perceptual realism and robustness of downstream vision tasks. We introduce CascadeRS, a rain synthesis framework that leverages depth and segmentation priors to render scene-consistent rain and surface effects. In addition to rain streaks, CascadeRS produces reflections and puddles aligned with scene semantics through a physics-inspired zero-shot, controllable generative pipeline. It produces photorealistic rainy urban scenes, with high perceptual realism, which we validate qualitatively with an extensive user study and quantitatively. We produce the Cityscapes-CascadeRS dataset, to study downstream task performance under real rainy conditions. Our results establish CascadeRS as a scalable and realistic rain-generation framework for evaluating and enhancing the robustness of vision in adverse rainy weather. Code and dataset are available via this https URL

</details>


### [188] [Self-Supervised Diffusion Models for Handwritten Signature Verification via Ruin Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07810)
*Peirong Zhang,Zhenhua Yang,Jiaxin Zhang,Moises Diaz,Miguel A. Ferrer,Umapada Pal,Wei Zhou,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SigDiffuser：基于扩散模型的自监督手写签名表征框架，提出"废墟重建"(RFR)前置任务，通过严重揉皱破坏签名结构后由视觉编码器引导去噪器重建，并结合金字塔特征聚合模块(PFAM)，在多语种签名数据集上超越SOTA自监督与签名验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写签名验证(HSV)依赖表征判别力，现有方法几乎全部采用监督学习，自监督学习在该任务上尚未被充分探索；作者希望以破坏-重建式前置任务挖掘签名结构信息。

Method: 扩散模型式自监督框架：设计 Reconstruction from Ruins (RFR) 前置任务，对签名施加严重揉皱退化以破坏结构完整性与书写风格；视觉编码器从完好区域提取笔迹模式，引导去噪器重建被破坏区域；引入金字塔特征聚合模块(PFAM)在编码器与去噪器间做层级聚合与调制。

Result: 在多语种签名数据集上显著优于SOTA自监督方法与HSV模型；消融显示增大揉皱强度反而提升表征质量，极端破坏优于传统轻微形变。

Conclusion: 极端破坏结合扩散式自监督可得到优于温和形变的签名表征，为HSV及其他生物特征领域提供新思路；代码将公开。

Abstract: Handwritten signature verification (HSV) is a critical biometric technique in legal and forensic authentication, whose efficacy hinges on the discriminability of signature representations. Current HSV methods predominantly adopt supervised learning to enhance representation quality, leaving self-supervised learning largely unexplored. We propose \emph{SigDiffuser}, a diffusion-based self-supervised framework for signature representation and verification. At its core, \emph{SigDiffuser} introduces a novel pretext task termed \emph{Reconstruction from Ruins (RFR)}, which deliberately ``ruins'' signatures through severe crumpling, obliterating their structural integrity and writing styles. A visual encoder is employed to capture crucial handwriting patterns from intact signature regions, guiding the denoiser to reconstruct the ``ruined'' portions and learning meaningful representations. Additionally, we introduce a \emph{pyramidal feature aggregation module (PFAM)} to bridge the visual encoder and denoiser, enhancing representation quality via hierarchical aggregation and modulation. Extensive experiments on multilingual signature datasets demonstrate that \emph{SigDiffuser} significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art SSL methods and HSV models. We further show that increasing the crumpling severity on signatures effectively improves representation quality. Notably, our work demonstrates that extreme destruction, coupled with self-supervised diffusion models, can yield superior signature representation compared to traditional mild deformations, paving new avenues for HSV and other biometric domains. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [189] [MV-Sign: Multi-View 3D Motion Integration and Enhanced Text-to-Motion Conditioning for Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07811)
*Singyu Muk,Kaustubh Kundu,Kunyuan Xie,Weijie Wang,Kalin Stefanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多视角3D手势动作数据与文本条件联合优化的手语生成(SLP)框架：构建How2Sign-MV3D高质量数据集，设计含sign-aware ST-VQVAE、伪gloss适配器和关键帧感知检索的SLP模型，DTW-PA-JPE显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D手语生成依赖单视角正面视频拟合的伪3D动作数据，存在严重自遮挡与手部细节丢失，形成噪声监督；同时文本条件建模偏弱，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 1) 跨视角数据策展管线，融合多视角估计以消除遮挡并过滤非手语片段，构建How2Sign-MV3D；2) sign-aware时空VQ-VAE对body-hand联合tokenize；3) pseudo-gloss适配器；4) 关键帧感知检索增强文本-动作对齐。另发布ASLCitizen-3D、MM-WAuslan-MV3D数据集与SGNify-MV多视角视频基准。

Result: 在How2Sign基准上，body与hand的DTW-PA-JPE分别较SOTA降低32.8%和20.9%。

Conclusion: 多视角高保真动作数据与更强的文本条件通路可有效缓解伪3D噪声监督问题，显著提升3D手语生成精度，并附带多套3D/多视角数据资源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate appropriate sign language motion from spoken language text. However, current 3D SLP methods suffer from noisy supervision and weak text conditioning. Specifically, these methods typically rely on pseudo-3D motion data fitted from single-view frontal videos, which introduces severe self-occlusion and loses fine-grained hand details, ultimately degrading downstream production. To overcome these limitations, we propose a comprehensive framework that jointly integrates multi-view 3D motion data and optimizes the text-conditioning pathway. First, we introduce How2Sign-MV3D, a high-fidelity dataset constructed via a novel cross-view curation pipeline that fuses multi-view estimates to resolve occlusions and filter non-signing segments. Second, we design a robust SLP model incorporating a sign-aware spatio-temporal VQ-VAE (ST-VQVAE) for joint body-hand tokenization, a pseudo-gloss adapter, and keyframe-aware retrieval to enhance text-to-motion alignment. Evaluated on the How2Sign benchmark, our framework reduces body and hand DTW-PA-JPE by 32.8% and 20.9% respectively compared to the state-of-the-art. Finally, we release two new high-quality 3D datasets, ASLCitizen-3D and MM-WAuslan-MV3D, alongside SGNify-MV as a multi-view video benchmark to support broader community research.

</details>


### [190] [CMDiff: Left Atrial Scar Segmentation from 3D LGE MRI Using Conditional Mask Diffusion Model: A Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07814)
*Jing Zhang,Bastien Bergere,Jonas Leite,Emilie Bollache,Mikaël Laredo,Alban Redheuil,Nadjia Kachenoura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出条件掩码扩散方法 CMDiff（动态编码器条件的 3D 正弦 UNet），用于稀疏标注的 3D 左心房瘢痕分割；五折交叉验证中性能未超越确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE 心脏 MRI 可无创评估左心房瘢痕，但其低对比度、心房壁薄、标注稀缺且类别极端不平衡，使自动瘢痕分割困难；条件扩散虽能迭代生成掩码，但其在稀疏 3D 瘢痕分割中的适用性尚不明确。

Method: CMDiff 采用直接预测干净掩码（clean-mask prediction）的范式，以动态编码器作为条件，主干为 3D 正弦（sinusoidal）UNet 的迭代生成结构；在 60 例 LASCARQS 公开数据上以五折交叉验证（每折 48 训练 / 12 留出）与确定性基线做同实现对比，并比较了干净掩码预测与噪声预测两种配置。

Result: CMDiff 的瘢痕 DSC 为 0.47±0.08、HD95 为 12.89±6.39 mm、ASD 为 1.91±0.96 mm；确定性基线分别为 0.51±0.08、10.86±5.39 mm、1.58±1.03 mm。同实现下干净掩码预测明显优于噪声预测配置。

Conclusion: CMDiff 在技术上可行但未提升分割性能（DSC 更低、HD95 与 ASD 更高）；直接掩码扩散为该任务提供了可分析的新路径，但尚不构成有竞争力的性能突破。代码已开源。

Abstract: Late gadolinium enhancement (LGE) cardiac MRI enables non-invasive assessment of left atrial (LA) scar, an imaging marker associated with atrial fibrillation ablation outcome. However, automatic scar segmentation is hindered by low contrast, the thin atrial wall, scarce labels, and extreme class imbalance. Conditional diffusion offers iterative mask generation, but its suitability for sparse 3D LA scar segmentation remains unclear. To address this gap, we propose Conditional Mask Diffusion (CMDiff), which predicts clean masks using a dynamic-encoder-conditioned 3D sinusoidal UNet. We compared CMDiff with a deterministic baseline on identical cases in five-fold cross-validation of 60 scar-labelled public LASCARQS subjects, with 48 training and 12 held-out cases per fold. CMDiff achieved a scar DSC of 0.47 $\pm$ 0.08, HD95 of 12.89 $\pm$ 6.39 mm, and ASD of 1.91 $\pm$ 0.96 mm, compared with deterministic baseline values of 0.51 $\pm$ 0.08, 10.86 $\pm$ 5.39 mm, and 1.58 $\pm$ 1.03 mm, respectively. Under the same implementation, clean-mask prediction performed substantially better than the noise-prediction configuration evaluated in this study. Thus, CMDiff was technically feasible but did not improve segmentation performance: DSC was lower, and both HD95 and ASD were higher. Direct mask diffusion therefore provides an analytical alternative, but not a competitive performance advance for this task. The source code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [191] [STAG-Net: Structure-Aware Attention over Landmark Groups for Lightweight Head Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07828)
*Asiri Gawesha Lindamulage,Sanka Mohottala,Dharshana Kasthurirathna,Nuwan Kodagoda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAG-Net：基于人脸关键点分组的轻量头部姿态估计网络，参数量0.012–0.043M，FLOPs比FSA-Net低约三个数量级，在300W-LP/AFLW2000/BIWI上取得基于关键点方法中最低MAE，FaceMesh流程GPU端达322.9 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头部姿态估计对人机交互与驾驶员监控重要，但现有基于图像的方法对资源受限部署而言计算代价过高；希望用稀疏关键点作为输入，在保持精度的同时大幅降低训练与推理成本。

Method: 从人脸关键点回归头部姿态：先做运动感知的关键点选择与空间聚类，再用紧凑的DeepSet编码器处理每组关键点，随后施加自注意力聚合，并引入全局几何描述子作为补充特征。分别使用FaceMesh与FAN两种关键点来源构建变体，在300W-LP上训练，在AFLW2000、BIWI上以跨数据集与subject-independent协议评测。

Result: 在所有评测设置中，STAG-Net在基于关键点的方法里MAE最低；BIWI有效检测帧上FAN变体整体误差也最低，跨数据集3.51°、subject-independent 2.39°。回归器仅0.012–0.043M参数、24.1–83.6K FLOPs（约为FSA-Net的千分之一）。含关键点提取的FaceMesh流程在Tesla T4上322.9 FPS（最快图像baseline为164.5 FPS），CPU上111.0 FPS。按数据规模归一化后训练速度比FSA-Net快7–8倍、比HopeNet/TokenHPE快百倍以上，完整训练28分钟内完成。

Conclusion: 稀疏关键点+分组结构感知注意力是实现极高能效头部姿态估计的有效路线，在精度、推理速度与训练成本上同时优于图像级方法；但其精度上限依赖上游关键点检测质量（以"有效检测帧"为前提统计），属于面向嵌入式/驾驶员监控的高效感知模块，而非具身智能核心方法。

Abstract: Head pose estimation is important for human computer interaction and driver monitoring, but many image based methods remain too costly for resource constrained deployment. We propose STAG-Net, a structure-aware attention over landmark groups for lightweight head Pose estimation that estimates head pose from facial landmarks using movement aware selection, spatial clustering, compact DeepSet encoders, self attention, and a global geometry descriptor. We develop variants using FaceMesh and Face Alignment Network landmarks and evaluate them under cross dataset and subject independent protocols on 300W-LP, AFLW2000, and BIWI. STAG-Net achieves the lowest mean absolute error among all compared landmark based methods in every evaluated setting. On BIWI frames with valid detections, the FAN variant also records the lowest overall error among all compared methods, reaching $3.51^\circ$ under cross dataset evaluation and $2.39^\circ$ under subject independent evaluation. The regressors use only 0.012 to 0.043 million parameters and 24.1 to 83.6 thousand FLOPs, about three orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs than FSA-Net. Despite including landmark extraction and regression, the FaceMesh pipeline reaches 322.9 frames per second on a Tesla T4 GPU, nearly twice the 164.5 frames per second of the fastest measured image based baseline, and 111.0 frames per second on CPU. After normalization by dataset size, STAG-Net processes training samples seven to eight times faster than FSA-Net and more than one hundred times faster than HopeNet and TokenHPE, while complete training finishes within 28 minutes. These results show that STAG-Net combines leading landmark based accuracy with substantially lower training and inference cost.

</details>


### [192] [THG-Omni: Traceable Human-Centric Graph Supervision for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07836)
*Jisheng Dang,Kai Wei,Ronghao Lin,Bimei Wang,Hong Peng,Wei-Shi Zheng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: THG-Omni：用SAM3人物轨迹构建时空图监督，训练全模态模型对视频中人物属性/动作/情感/交互及时间进行可溯源推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态视频问答模型只做答案级监督，不显式输出支撑答案的人物、事件或时间区间，难以学会解读结构化视频证据。

Method: 将SAM3参与者跟踪轨迹与时间定位的Qwen3-VL标注关联，构建以人为中心的时空图；候选语义声明先经源视频与音频校验，再由轨迹几何提供显式时空监督生成训练问题；推理时从评测视频预测图作为输入。

Result: IntentBench上在社交推理、情绪理解、欺骗感知均稳定优于未适配骨干；NExT-QA 89.54%（有匹配图）vs 85.10%（无图）；紧凑图输入78.22%，全图78.02%，无图77.58%；诊断显示预测图在无源视频音频时仍保留可回答问题所需证据。

Conclusion: 结构图监督将证据落地到可识别的参与者与事件上，提升以人为中心的视频推理并对齐可溯源证据；方法偏基准评测，未涉及机器人或物理交互。

Abstract: Omni-modal models can answer questions about video, but their predictions rarely show which participant, event, or interval supports an answer. Moreover, answer-level supervision alone does not teach a model to interpret structured video evidence. To systematically mitigate this issue, we present THG-Omni, a human-centric graph-supervision framework that links Segment Anything Model~3 participant tracks with temporally grounded Qwen3-VL annotations. Candidate semantic claims are checked against the source video and available audio before they are used to construct training questions, while track geometry provides explicit spatial and temporal supervision. The resulting questions teach an omni-modal reasoning model to interpret participant attributes, actions, affect, interactions, and their timing from a structured graph. At inference, THG-Omni uses a predicted graph constructed from the evaluation video. Experiments on IntentBench show consistent improvements over the unadapted backbone across social reasoning, emotion understanding, and deception perception. On NExT-QA, THG-Omni achieves 89.54\% accuracy with the matched graph, compared with 85.10\% without graph input. Further validation experiments show that compact graph input achieves 78.22\% accuracy, compared with 78.02\% using the full graph and 77.58\% without graph input. A complementary diagnostic demonstrates that the predicted graph preserves evidence useful for answering questions even without the source video and audio. Together, these findings show that THG-Omni improves human-centric video reasoning while grounding its evidence in identifiable participants and events. Our codes are available at this https URL

</details>


### [193] [Enhanced MRI brain tumor detection using deep learning in conjunction with explainable AI SHAP based diverse and multi feature analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07843)
*Asif Rahman,Maqsood Hayat,Nadeem Iqbal,Fawaz Khaled Alarfaj,Salem Alkhalaf,Fahad Alturise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 比较LBP、Gabor、DWT、FFT、GLRLM等手工特征与CNN表征在MRI脑肿瘤分类中的效果，结合kNN、RF、SVC、PNN、CNN五种学习器，并用卡方/p值与SHAP分析LBP特征的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像诊断中传统手段分辨率低、有辐射、对比度差，MRI可无创高分辨成像，但需要可靠的特征表征与分类器组合以保证脑肿瘤识别的准确性与特异性。

Method: 多表征方案（LBP、Gabor、DWT、FFT、CNN、GLRLM）与五类学习算法（kNN、RF、SVC、PNN、CNN）的组合对比实验；使用卡方检验与p值验证LBP特征空间的显著性，并用SHAP进行特征重要性归因；在小数据集与大型基准数据集上分别评估性能以考察泛化能力。

Result: 小数据集上CNN达97.8%、SVC达98.06%准确率；LBP结合SVC与CNN具有高特异性与准确率且统计显著；大基准数据集上CNN达98.9%、SVC达96.7%。

Conclusion: LBP特征空间对脑肿瘤鉴别具有显著贡献，CNN在自动化高精度诊断中表现最佳并具备较强泛化能力，该组合可作为MRI肿瘤诊断的有前景方案。

Abstract: Recent innovations in medical imaging have markedly improved brain tumor identification, surpassing conventional diagnostic approaches that suffer from low resolution, radiation exposure, and limited contrast. Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is pivotal in precise and accurate tumor characterization owing to its high-resolution, non-invasive nature. This study investigates the synergy among multiple feature representation schemes such as local Binary Patterns (LBP), Gabor filters, Discrete Wavelet Transform, Fast Fourier Transform, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), and Gray-Level Run Length Matrix alongside five learning algorithms namely: k-nearest Neighbor, Random Forest, Support Vector Classifier (SVC), and probabilistic neural network (PNN), and CNN. Empirical findings indicate that LBP in conjunction with SVC and CNN obtained high specificity and accuracy, rendering it a promising method for MRI-based tumor diagnosis. Further to investigate the contribution of LBP, Statistical analysis chi-square and p-value tests are used to confirm the significant impact of LBP feature space for identification of brain Tumor. In addition, The SHAP analysis was used to identify the most important features in classification. In a small dataset, CNN obtained 97.8% accuracy while SVC yielded 98.06% accuracy. In subsequent analysis, a large benchmark dataset is also utilized to evaluate the performance of learning algorithms in order to investigate the generalization power of the proposed model. CNN achieves the highest accuracy of 98.9%, followed by SVC at 96.7%. These results highlight CNN's effectiveness in automated, high-precision tumor diagnosis. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [194] [OcTreeMamba: Topology-Aware Mamba for 3D Medical Segmentation via Octree Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07865)
*Kaixiang Mai,Jiaqi Yu,Yuzhu Ji,Yiqun Zhang,Zhijie Wang,Haijun Zhang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OcTreeMamba 将八叉树结构与状态空间模型（SSM）结合，通过学习器实现解剖自适应稀疏化，并在八叉树拓扑上双向传播上下文特征，提升3D医学分割的拓扑连续性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Mamba 分割方法采用启发式选择性扫描，破坏三维空间层次结构、引入计算冗余，且忽略三维解剖结构固有的稀疏性。

Method: Structure Learner 通过分层门控与 Gumbel-Softmax 采样实现可微的解剖自适应结构化稀疏化；Dynamic Octree Builder 将选中区域组织为保持层次连通性的多级拓扑；在八叉树上以双向 Tree-SSM 沿叶–父–根、根–父–叶轨迹传播并融合上下文特征。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，方法在拓扑连续性与计算效率上具备竞争力，尤其在血管数据集复杂结构恢复方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 以八叉树稀疏拓扑替代启发式扫描是提升3D SSM 分割上下文建模效率与结构完整性的有效途径。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of 3D anatomical structures is crucial to medical image analysis. Recently, Mamba-based approaches have achieved promising segmentation performance in capturing long-range dependencies with linear-time computational complexity. However, heuristic selective scanning strategies inherently disrupt the intrinsic spatial hierarchy and increase computational redundancy, ignoring the inherent sparsity of 3D anatomical structures. To this end, we propose OcTreeMamba, a hierarchical framework that incorporates Octree structures into SSM, achieving 3D topology-aware long-range context feature propagation and integration. Specifically, a Structure Learner performs anatomy-adaptive structured sparsification through hierarchical gating and Gumbel-Softmax sampling, enabling differentiable selection of anatomical regions. The selected regions are subsequently organized by a Dynamic Octree Builder into a multi-level topology that preserves their hierarchical connectivity. Based on the constructed Octree, a bidirectional Tree-SSM propagates and integrates contextual features along connectivity-aware leaf--parent--root and root--parent--leaf trajectories. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets show that our proposed OcTreeMamba achieves competitive performance \wrt topological continuity and computational efficiency, particularly for recovering the complex structure on vascular datasets. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [195] [PlayGen2: Learning Real-Time Playable Worlds with Explicit 3D State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07867)
*Jihai Zhang,Yuhan Zhou,Junyou Li,Deheng Ye,Qi Dou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlayGen2 将学习式三维世界状态演化与神经渲染解耦，用持久体素占据栅格+实体身份索引的3D状态构成世界记忆，在固定局部区域学习动作条件实体动力学、几何补全与实体发现，再由独立神经渲染器从深度/语义投影生成第一人称观测，实现实时可交互世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成式世界模型把生成帧作为唯一循环表示，导致控制响应性、持久空间结构与动态实体交互能力都被绑定到视角相关的外观合成上，长期空间/时间一致性和交互一致性难以维持。

Method: 1) 持久世界记忆：静态几何用体积占据栅格表示，玩家与非玩家实体用身份索引的3D状态表示；2) 世界状态模型在固定尺寸局部窗口内联合学习动作条件实体动力学、几何补全（未见区域补全）与实体发现，并将更新提交至记忆；3) 状态预测不回灌生成图像，渲染由独立神经渲染器依据世界状态的深度与语义投影完成；4) 以 Doom 为测试床，支持规则化状态干预的可编程玩法与多智能体组合。

Result: 成功学习到几何感知的玩家运动与玩家—NPC交互等世界动力学；完整仿真+渲染管线在 M4 Pro MacBook Pro 上后端延迟约160ms，达实时级；并展示未见 Doom 地图零样本泛化、单玩家模型组合实现多人交互、基于规则状态干预的可编程玩法，代码与预训练权重开源。

Conclusion: 将显式持久3D状态与神经渲染分离，可同时获得时间一致性、可控性与低推理延迟，具备跨地图迁移与可扩展交互能力，为可玩世界模型提供了一条不同于纯视频生成的路线。

Abstract: Building a playable world requires more than generating plausible video: it demands responsive control, persistent spatial structure, and coherent interactions with dynamic entities. Using generated frames as the sole recurrent world representation ties these requirements to view-dependent appearance synthesis, making it difficult to maintain long-term spatial and temporal consistency in generation and interaction. We present PlayGen2, a real-time interactive world model that separates learned 3D state evolution from neural rendering. PlayGen2 maintains a persistent world memory comprising static geometry represented by a volumetric occupancy grid and identity-indexed 3D states for the player and other entities. Within a fixed-size local region, its world-state model jointly learns action-conditioned entity dynamics, geometry completion, and entity discovery, committing updates to a persistent world memory. A separate neural renderer synthesizes first-person observations from depth and semantic projections of the world state, without feeding generated images back into state prediction. Using Doom as a testbed, we demonstrate that the model successfully learns world dynamics including geometry-aware player motion and player--NPC interactions. The framework is computationally efficient, enabling the complete simulation and rendering pipeline to achieve approximately 160ms backend latency on a MacBook Pro with an M4 Pro chip. Beyond single-player interaction, we demonstrate zero-shot deployment on unseen Doom maps, multiplayer interaction through the composition of single-player models, and programmable gameplay through rule-based state interventions. These capabilities demonstrate the transferability and extensibility of the learned game-world model. Code and pretrained model weights are publicly available.

</details>


### [196] [PVDepth: Panoramic Video Depth Estimation via Geometry-Aware Spatiotemporal Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07873)
*Chuanxin Song,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出大规模全景视频RGB-D合成数据集PanoCARLA，并在透视视频深度模型基础上构建PVDepth端到端框架，通过渐进式球面感知噪声初始化与立方体贴图矫正时序建模两个机制解决ERP畸变与时序非线性问题，实现几何准确且时序一致的全景视频深度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景视频深度估计对VR与世界模型应用关键，但受限于大规模训练数据稀缺，以及等距柱状投影(ERP)特有的时空挑战，导致透视深度模型难以直接迁移。

Method: 1) 构建PanoCARLA：大规模合成RGB-D全景视频数据集，含自然运动轨迹与无人机式漫游视角；2) 提出PVDepth端到端框架，由透视视频深度模型适配而来；3) 渐进式球面感知噪声初始化：将噪声分布从平面退火至球面，引导模型适应非均匀信息密度；4) 立方体贴图时序建模模块：引入辅助cubemap时序分支，矫正ERP域的非线性时序动态。

Result: 大量实验表明PVDepth性能优越，可生成几何精确且时序一致的全景深度序列；代码与数据将开源。

Conclusion: 通过数据与针对性几何/时序机制设计，可有效将透视视频深度模型迁移至全景ERP域，为全景视频深度估计提供可行方案与数据基础。

Abstract: Panoramic video depth estimation is pivotal for applications such as Virtual Reality and World Models. However, advancements in this field are impeded by two primary obstacles: the scarcity of large-scale training data and the unique spatiotemporal challenges of Equirectangular Projection (ERP), which hinder the direct transfer of perspective models. In this paper, we first present PanoCARLA, a large-scale synthetic RGB-D panoramic video dataset, featuring natural motion trajectories and drone-like roaming perspectives. Building on this foundation, we propose PVDepth, an end-to-end framework adapted from perspective video depth models. To tackle ERP-specific geometric distortions and consequent non-linear temporal dynamics, we introduce two core mechanisms: (1) A Progressive Sphere-aware Noise Initialization strategy that anneals the noise distribution from planar to spherical, guiding the model to adapt to non-uniform information density; and (2) A Cube-rectified Temporal Modeling module that incorporates an auxiliary cubemap temporal branch to rectify non-linear temporal dynamics in the ERP domain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PVDepth achieves superior performance, generating geometrically accurate and temporally consistent depth sequences. Code and data will be released at https:// this http URL .

</details>


### [197] [InnerLogic-Surg: An Open Surgical Foundation Model via Continual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07883)
*Kun Yuan,Hao Ding,Yiqing Shen,Gasper Podobnik,Benjamin Killeen,Tito Porras,Axel Krieger,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 发布 InnerLogic-Surg 手术视觉编码器家族（21M–300M），在来自 11,965 个网络手术视频、约 2,900 小时、15+ 术式的大规模手术图像上延续 DINOv3 预训练，并加入可学习的特征上采样模块支持低 token 成本的高分辨率密集预测；20 项手术任务线性探针全部最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视觉系统多依赖自然图像预训练编码器或小数据从头训练的专用模型，缺乏大规模手术领域预训练主干，因此希望通过手术持续预训练通用提升下游方法性能。

Method: 沿用 DINOv3 自监督预训练流程，对 Transformer 图像编码器在约 2,900 小时、覆盖 15+ 术式的手术视频数据上做持续预训练以保留表征能力；额外引入可学习特征上采样模块，在低 token 成本下实现高分辨率密集预测；提供 21M–300M 多规模模型。

Result: 在覆盖工作流、器械与解剖识别、分割、检测、单目深度的 20 项手术任务上，相同架构下最大模型取得全部 20 项任务的最佳线性探针成绩，并显示表征可跨术式、解剖区域和任务类型迁移。

Conclusion: 手术持续预训练可获得可迁移的通用表征，是替代传统术式专用表征学习的一种可扩展方案。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are the backbone of modern surgical image analysis, yet most surgical systems still rely on encoders pretrained on natural images or on specialist models trained from scratch on comparatively small datasets. Pretraining encoders on large amounts of surgery-specific data has the potential to generally enhance the performance of surgical image analysis methods simply by providing stronger backbones. Here we present InnerLogic-Surg, an open family of surgical vision encoders (21M--300M parameters) obtained by continually pretraining a transformer-based image encoder on millions of surgical images curated from 11,965 internet-sourced surgical videos spanning ~2,900 hours and more than fifteen procedure types. Our training recipe inherits DINOv3's pretraining stage to preserve representation ability after surgical adaptation, and additionally applies a learned feature-upsampling module for high-resolution dense prediction at low token cost. Across a benchmark covering workflow, instrument and anatomy recognition, segmentation, detection, and monocular depth, under the same architecture, the largest InnerLogic-Surg model achieves the best linear-probe performance in all 20 surgical tasks. We demonstrate that surgical continual pretraining yields transferable representations that generalize across procedures, anatomical regions, and task types, showing our surgical image encoders as a promising and scalable alternative to the procedure-specific representation learning approaches that have traditionally dominated surgical computer vision.

</details>


### [198] [Structure from Collision via Hypothesis-Space Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07900)
*Takuhiro Kaneko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将 Structure from Collision (SfC) 重构为假设空间推理问题，用显式结构假设表示隐藏空腔，全局阶段用前向-only MPM 做无梯度搜索，局部阶段用可微 MPM 做基于梯度的粒子系统精修，从而从碰撞引起的多视角形变观测中恢复物体内部空腔结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏空腔在静态可见表面上没有直接痕迹，只能通过碰撞诱发的表面形变间接推断，这对预测性仿真、机器人操作与无损检测至关重要。已有基于密度表示的 SfC 基线存在两难：表面匹配梯度不直接指向空腔位置，密度梯度在空洞内部或附近又很弱甚至无信息，因此直接优化密度表示难以奏效。

Method: 把 SfC 改写为假设空间推理：用显式结构假设表示隐藏空腔，其变量编码可用的结构先验假设；全局阶段在该假设空间上做无梯度优化，仅依赖前向 MPM 物理仿真评估候选；局部阶段将选中假设细化为拉格朗日粒子系统，并用可微 MPM 仿真做基于梯度的优化。

Result: 在 SfC 基准上，相比基于密度的 SfC 基线，隐藏结构重建与未来形变预测均有提升；并通过内部拓扑压力测试与鲁棒性分析刻画了方法的适用范围与局限。

Conclusion: 以显式假设加混合优化（无梯度全局搜索 + 可微局部精修）替代隐式密度优化，是推断碰撞可观测空腔结构的有效途径；但方法仍受结构假设空间覆盖度与拓扑复杂度限制。整体属于物理仿真驱动的非视域/内部结构重建工作，实验限于仿真基准，尚缺真实机器人或实物验证。

Abstract: Knowledge of the hidden interior structure of an object, including the presence and location of cavities, is essential for predictive simulation, robotic manipulation, and nondestructive inspection. However, such cavities leave no direct signature on the static visible surface, making collision-induced deformation one of the few cues used to infer their presence. Structure from Collision (SfC) addresses this problem by recovering the hidden interior structure of an object from multiview observations of a collision event. SfC is challenging for two reasons: hidden cavities are only indirectly observable through surface motion, and voids correspond to the absence of material, making direct optimization using standard density representations difficult. The density-based SfC baseline inherits both difficulties. Surface-matching gradients do not directly indicate the cavity location, while density gradients can be weak or uninformative inside or near void regions. Therefore, we reformulate SfC as hypothesis-space reasoning, representing hidden cavities as explicit structural hypotheses whose variables encode available structural assumptions rather than relying on implicit density updates. A global stage performs gradient-free optimization over this hypothesis space using forward-only material point method (MPM) simulation. A local stage then refines the selected hypothesis as a Lagrangian particle system through gradient-based optimization using differentiable MPM simulation. On the SfC benchmark, our method improves hidden-structure reconstruction and future prediction over the density-based SfC baseline. Interior-topology stress tests and robustness analyses characterize its scope and limitations. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [199] [XM4D: 4D Reconstruction through Certifiable Geometric Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07902)
*Haoyu Han,Jack Benarroch Jedlicki,Jiahui Lei,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XM4D将4D重建解耦为相机运动估计与非刚性形变两阶段，各自建模为仅含旋转的QCQP问题，通过SDP松弛与GPU加速的Burer-Monteiro黎曼优化求解并获得可验证的全局最优性证书，最后以动态高斯泼溅做光度细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合估计相机运动、场景几何与非刚性形变的4D重建高度非凸，常规优化难以保证全局最优；作者希望借助可认证全局最优的几何优化提升精度与效率。

Method: 阶段一：用基础深度模型将静态点轨迹提升为3D，构建含位姿、深度尺度与静态路标的scaled bundle adjustment，解析消去平移与路标后化为旋转上的QCQP。阶段二：用嵌入形变图(EDG)表示动态运动，将跟踪、刚性与时间正则目标同样化为仅旋转的QCQP。两问题均做SDP松弛，并用基于黎曼优化的GPU Burer-Monteiro求解器高效求解；所得几何初始化动态高斯泼溅进行光度精修。

Result: 在七个基准上验证：静态与动态场景相机运动估计准确，优化效率可扩展至数千帧，新视角渲染质量达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 将非凸4D重建拆解为两个可认证全局最优的旋转-only QCQP是可扩展且有效的方案，结合SDP松弛/BM求解与高斯泼溅细化可获得高精度几何与渲染效果。

Abstract: Accurate 4D reconstruction remains challenging: jointly estimating camera motion, scene geometry, and non-rigid deformation entails highly nonconvex optimization, making global optimality difficult to guarantee. We present XM4D, a framework for accurate and efficient 4D reconstruction built upon certifiably globally optimal geometric optimization. By separating the estimation of camera motion from non-rigid deformation, we formulate both stages as structured quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQP) that admit semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations and verifiable global optimality certificates. For camera motion estimation, we lift static point tracks into 3D using foundational depth prediction models and formulate a scaled bundle adjustment problem that jointly estimates camera poses, depth scales, and static landmarks. By analytically eliminating translations and landmarks, we reduce the problem to a QCQP over scaled rotations. For non-rigid reconstruction, we represent dynamic motion using an embedded deformation graph (EDG) and show that its tracking, rigidity, and temporal regularization objectives similarly reduce to a rotation-only QCQP. Both problems admit SDP relaxations that we solve efficiently with a GPU-accelerated Burer-Monteiro solver based on Riemannian optimization. The recovered geometry further initializes dynamic Gaussian splatting for photometric refinement. Extensive experiments across seven benchmarks demonstrate accurate camera motion estimation on both static and dynamic scenes, efficient optimization that scales to thousands of frames, and state-of-the-art novel-view rendering quality.

</details>


### [200] [StitchEdit: Stitching Depth Priors into Image Editors via Per-Layer Gradient Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07922)
*Qin Guo,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StitchEdit：通过逐层梯度探测发现扩散骨干中编辑目标与深度目标在浅层兼容、深层冲突，据此先用轻量深度去噪适配器激活几何先验，再用探测引导的逐层学习率调度微调适配器以保留有用的深度知识、剪除冲突部分，从而改进依赖3D结构的空间编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导扩散编辑在涉及遮挡、视角变化、物体空间操纵等依赖3D场景结构的空间编辑上表现不佳；已有工作（Marigold）证明预训练扩散骨干蕴含强几何先验，但如何把这些先验重定向用于编辑尚未解决。

Method: 逐层梯度探测分析编辑损失与深度损失在不同Transformer层的梯度兼容性；设计轻量深度去噪适配器激活骨干几何能力；对适配器采用探测引导的逐层学习率调度进行选择性微调（兼容层保留深度知识，冲突层抑制）；并构建StitchEditBench基准（150个依赖几何的编辑任务，覆盖空间变换、遮挡操纵、视角变化）。

Result: 在StitchEditBench上，StitchEdit在感知质量和几何保真度两方面稳定优于基于指令的编辑基线与深度条件编辑基线。

Conclusion: 深度与编辑目标在扩散骨干中是可分层兼容/冲突的，逐层探测引导的适配器微调可有效复用几何先验实现空间一致编辑；局限在于作者自建基准、尚无真实场景部署或大规模用户评估验证。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with diffusion models struggles with spatial edits (e.g. manipulating objects, changing viewpoints, manipulating occlusions) that depend on the 3D structure understanding of a scene. Marigold and follow-up work demonstrate that a pretrained diffusion backbone already harbors strong geometric priors that can be activated for depth estimation, yet how to redirect these priors toward editing remains unresolved. Through per-layer gradient probing, we discover that the editing and depth objectives are compatible in early transformer layers but actively conflict in late layers, explaining why naive depth-conditioned editing either ignores geometry or degrades visual quality. We present StitchEdit, which first activates the backbone's geometric capability via a lightweight depth-denoising adapter, then selectively fine-tunes this adapter for editing using a probe-guided per-layer learning rate schedule that preserves depth knowledge where it helps and prunes it where it conflicts. On the proposed StitchEditBench, a benchmark of 150 geometry-dependent edits spanning spatial transformation, occlusion manipulation, and viewpoint change, StitchEdit consistently surpasses both instruction-based and depth-conditioned baselines on perceptual quality and geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [201] [Towards Scalable Diagnostic-Driven Whole-Slide Image Retrieval for Pathology Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07935)
*Jingsong Liu,Youssef Wally Han Li,Weiwei Zhou,Jing Dai,Elisabeth Wetzer,Peter J. Schüffler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR-WSI：基于固定长度slide嵌入的数字病理全切片图像内容检索框架，用预测或真值诊断标签精化候选并支持在线扩充记忆库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理WSI档案激增但元数据不一致，基于内容的检索可绕开标注；现有检索系统依赖手工索引pipeline和预定义采样策略，可扩展性与泛化性受限。

Method: 提出CLEAR-WSI框架：对WSI生成固定长度嵌入，检索后依据设定用预测诊断标签或真值标签精化候选；新病例增量写入持续增长的记忆库；在2个公开+1个私有临床数据集、4个检索任务上评测，并做消融与延迟分析。

Result: Precision@5较最强基线提升：CAMELYON16 +3.10、BRACS-1 +2.75、BRACS-2 +1.95、X-HER2 +3.75；消融与延迟实验验证各组件贡献及可扩展部署潜力。

Conclusion: 诊断驱动的固定长度嵌入检索可替代手工索引，支持随时间增长的可扩展WSI检索档案，具备临床部署潜力。

Abstract: The growing adoption of digital pathology has rapidly expanded collections of hematoxylin-and-eosin whole-slide images, yet efficient navigation through these archives remains challenging, as metadata is often inconsistent or unavailable. Content-based image retrieval addresses this challenge by identifying similar cases directly from visual representations instead of manual annotations or metadata. Existing retrieval systems have demonstrated promising performance but largely rely on handcrafted indexing pipelines and predefined sampling strategies, constraining scalability and generalization. Here, we present CLEAR-WSI (Constant-Length Embedding for Automatic Retrieval of Whole-Slide Images) as a scalable, diagnostic-driven slide-retrieval framework, validated on both public and clinical data. Using a fixed-length slide embedding, CLEAR-WSI refines retrieved candidates with either a predicted diagnostic label or the ground-truth label, depending on the retrieval setting. Newly processed cases are subsequently added to the expanding memory bank, allowing the retrieval archive to grow over time. On a comprehensive benchmark consisting of two public and one private clinical datasets representing four retrieval tasks, CLEAR-WSI improves Precision@5 over the strongest evaluated baseline, with gains on CAMELYON16 (3.10), BRACS-1 (2.75), BRACS-2 (1.95), and X-HER2 (3.75). Additional ablation and latency studies further examine the contribution of each component and highlight its potential for scalable deployment.

</details>


### [202] [WAM-Sim0.1: World Anchored Memory Meets Online RL for Consistent and Realistic Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07939)
*Jiahao Cui,Jiaye Li,Wenhao Zhang,Yaoyi Li,Shan Luan,Quanhui Tang,Jianhua Han,Lin Cheng,Hang Xu,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAM-Sim：以三级持久层次记忆（位姿对齐结构token、只读场景token、一次性写入实例token）锚定自回归驾驶世界模型，并用在线flow-matching RL（层级对齐视觉奖励+VLM物理裁判）优化自生成rollout，抑制长时漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归驾驶世界模型因条件信号是瞬态的、缺乏持久状态，在长时rollout中出现结构漂移、身份漂移与质量衰减；多尺度条件化还导致视觉清晰度与物理合理性退化。

Method: 提出WAM-Sim：三个时间/语义尺度的持久记忆——结构token编码持久道路拓扑，只读场景token提供带地理定位的背景外观，一次性写入的实例token在遮挡与chunk边界间保持交通参与者身份；并引入在线后训练框架，通过flow-matching RL在自生成rollout上优化，奖励由层级对齐的视觉奖励与VLM物理judge构成。

Result: nuScenes上FID=3.47、FVD=25.72，下游感知24.16 mAP、道路mIoU 71.23；自采数据分钟级rollout中显著缓解结构与身份漂移，长时域保持场景相似度与主体身份一致性。

Conclusion: 不变量记忆在经验上把策略优化约束在锚定上下文内，抑制语义崩塌与奖励hacking；在线RL系统性强化物理常识与细粒度视觉保真度，长时rollout稳定性显著提升。

Abstract: Autoregressive driving world models systematically suffer from structural drift, identity drift, and quality decay over long rollout horizons due to transient conditioning signals that lack persistent state. We present WAM-Sim, a driving world model anchored by a persistent hierarchical memory and optimized via online flow-matching RL. WAM-Sim structures persistent context across three temporal and semantic scales: pose-aligned structural tokens encoding persistent road topology, read-only scene tokens supplying geolocated background appearance, and write-once instance tokens preserving individual traffic participant identities across occlusions and chunk boundaries. To restore visual sharpness and physical plausibility degraded by multi-scale conditioning, we introduce an online post-training framework that optimizes self-generated rollouts via flow-matching RL guided by hierarchy-aligned visual rewards and a vision-language model physical judge. The invariant memory empirically confines policy optimization within the anchored context, helping suppress semantic collapse and reward hacking, while online RL systematically enforces physical commonsense and fine-grained visual fidelity. On the nuScenes benchmark, WAM-Sim achieves a superior generation fidelity (FID=3.47, FVD=25.72) and strong downstream perception performance (24.16 mAP, 71.23 road mIoU). On minute-scale rollouts of self-collected data, WAM-Sim substantially mitigates structural and identity drift, preserving high scene similarity and subject identity coherence across extended horizons. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [203] [IDPrior: Decoupling Identity Generation from Facial Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07941)
*Fadi Boutros,Meriç Demirörs,Darian Tomašević,Vitomir Štruc,Naser Damer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IDPrior：两阶段框架，先在预训练人脸识别模型的嵌入空间学习扩散先验以建模身份分布并采样新身份嵌入，再用其条件化预训练的ID条件扩散模型合成同一身份的多张图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模带身份标注的人脸数据采集成本高且存在隐私伦理问题；现有合成人脸方法多依赖预定义类标签、无条件生成或预设潜采样，未显式建模人脸身份的真实分布。

Method: 解耦身份生成与图像合成：阶段一在FR模型嵌入空间中训练扩散先验，拟合身份嵌入分布并生成新身份嵌入；阶段二将生成嵌入作为条件输入预训练的ID条件扩散模型，实现同一身份多张图像合成；模块化设计兼容现有及未来ID条件DM。

Result: 生成身份更多样、区分度更好，且更接近真实身份分布；用其生成数据训练的人脸识别模型在多个验证基准上与现有合成数据方法持平或更优。

Conclusion: 显式建模身份分布并解耦身份生成与图像合成，可提升合成人脸数据的身份覆盖与质量，为隐私友好的FR训练数据提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: Large-scale identity-labeled face datasets are essential for training modern face recognition models, yet collecting and annotating such data is costly and raises privacy and ethical concerns. Existing synthetic face generation methods typically derive identities from predefined class labels, unconditional image generation, or predefined latent sampling strategies, without explicitly modeling the underlying distribution of facial identities. In this paper, we propose IDPrior, a two-stage framework that decouples identity generation from image synthesis. We first learn a diffusion prior in the embedding space of a pretrained face recognition model to model the distribution of facial identities and generate novel identity embeddings. These embeddings are then used to condition a pretrained identity-conditioned diffusion model (DM), enabling the synthesis of multiple images of the same identity.
