This modular design provides a scalable approach to identity generation while remaining compatible with existing and future identity-conditioned DMs. Experiments indicate that IDPrior generates diverse and well-separated identities while maintaining a closer correspondence to the distribution of authentic identities. Face recognition models trained on datasets generated using IDPrior-generated identities achieve competitive or improved performance compared with existing synthetic-data-based methods across multiple face verification benchmarks. Code and generated datasets are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [204] [Rotation Is Token-Blind: A Structural Criterion for Post-Training Quantization of Feed-Forward Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07955)
*Huanfei He,Bohao Zhang,Jiaxin Zhang,Wei Sui,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知的PTQ量化框架QMapAnything，将旋转/平滑视为"token盲"重参数化群并证明其只产生共模增益、不改变token间相对SNR，并引入统计量Γ预测是否需要差异化偏置校正；W4A8接近全精度，W8A8显存降2.2–3.1倍、实测加速最高3.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D重建模型（几何Transformer）序列长、注意力深，计算与显存开销大，需要PTQ以便边缘部署；但现有PTQ依赖平滑或正交旋转抑制通道级离群值，是token盲的，无法处理几何Transformer中异构token类型共享同一量化网格、少数特殊token对全局输出杠杆过高的问题。

Method: 将保浮点旋转与平滑形式化为token盲重参数化群，理论证明其对所有token施加同一变换，只带来共模增益而相对token SNR不变；提出统计量Γ，结合特殊token的量化劣势与其输出杠杆，判别是否需要超出旋转的差异化偏置校正，从而实现结构感知量化。

Result: 几何基准实验显示W4A8与全精度性能接近；W8A8下峰值显存相对FP32降至1/2.2–1/3.1，真实硬件上最高3.1倍加速。

Conclusion: 为前馈3D重建模型在资源受限边缘端的部署提供了有理论依据且高效的量化路径；但主要验证限于量化效率与几何基准精度，缺少野外泛化与大场景实时推理的进一步证据。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models use Transformers to predict camera poses, depth, and structure in one pass, avoiding iterative multi-view optimization. Long sequences and deep attention incur high compute and memory costs, motivating Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) for deployment. Existing PTQ methods mainly suppress channel-wise outliers by smoothing or orthogonal rotation. Geometry Transformers introduce a different challenge: heterogeneous token types share one activation quantization grid. A few special tokens may exert high leverage over global outputs and be updated by only a subset of blocks. Token-blind transforms, which apply the same transformation to every token, reduce overall quantization error but cannot remove differentiated degradation across token types. We propose QMapAnything, a structure-aware PTQ framework. We cast floating-point-preserving rotation and smoothing as a token-blind reparameterization group, and prove that they yield common-mode gains while leaving relative token SNR unchanged. We introduce a statistic $\Gamma$ that combines a special token's quantization disadvantage and output leverage to predict whether differentiated bias correction is needed beyond rotation. Experiments on geometric benchmarks show that W4A8 closely matches full-precision performance. Under W8A8, peak memory falls by $2.2$--$3.1\times$ versus FP32, with up to $3.1\times$ speedup on real hardware. These results provide a principled and efficient approach to deploying feed-forward 3D reconstruction models in resource-constrained edges.

</details>


### [205] [Bri3D: Bridging the Object-Scene Gap in Single-Image 3D Generation via Scene-Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07961)
*Hongye Hou,Shuai Zhang,Hongjie Li,Zhuoxiao Li,Haiping Wang,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Bri3D 通过"实例感知体素场景锚"把场景级结构推理与冻结的对象级生成先验解耦：先预测彩色编码的场景脚手架（粗几何+布局），再为每个实例提取锚并精炼为保持位姿、兼容先验的体素支撑，驱动冻结对象生成器合成细节几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图生成组合式3D场景需同时恢复细节几何与一致空间布局；SAM3D、MIDI 等对象到场景方法在场景级监督稀缺下难以协调多实例，直接把对象中心生成器扩展到整场景会退化几何并破坏实例尺度、朝向与位姿。

Method: 粗到细框架：预测 color-coded 场景脚手架编码粗物体几何与空间排布；每个实例抽取为 scene anchor，细化为 pose-preserving、prior-compatible 的体素支撑；冻结的对象生成器在锚定义的参考系内合成带纹理细节几何；场景级训练只聚焦空间组织与锚精炼，复用预训练对象先验。

Result: 实验显示在场景布局、物体几何、视觉质量与泛化性上均有提升；布局预测高效，完整场景生成速度快于 SceneGen 与 SAM3D。

Conclusion: 将场景组织与物体尺度细节合成分离的粗到细设计，是在有限场景监督下复用对象生成先验、保持实例位姿与尺度一致性的有效路径，兼顾质量与推理效率。

Abstract: Generating compositional 3D scenes from a single image requires recovering detailed object geometry and coherent spatial arrangements. Existing object-to-scene methods, including SAM3D and MIDI, extend object generation through image-context reasoning or inter-instance modeling. Coordinating objects remains challenging with limited scene-level supervision. Directly extending object-centric generators to entire scenes can degrade geometry and fail to preserve instance scale, orientation, and pose. We propose Bri3D, a framework bridging scene-level structural reasoning and object-level generative priors through instance-aware voxel scene anchors. Bri3D predicts a color-coded scene scaffold encoding coarse object geometry and spatial arrangements. Each instance is extracted as a scene anchor and refined into a pose-preserving, prior-compatible voxel support, enabling a frozen object generator to synthesize detailed textured geometry in the anchor-defined spatial reference. This coarse-to-fine design separates scene organization from object-scale detail synthesis, focusing scene-level training on spatial organization and anchor refinement while reusing pretrained object priors. Experiments demonstrate improvements in scene layout, object geometry, visual quality, and generalization. Bri3D predicts scene layouts efficiently; full scene generation is faster than SceneGen and SAM3D.

</details>


### [206] [Reviving the Discarded High-Resolution Feature for Transformer-Based Tiny Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07994)
*Zehao Wang,Si Wu,Guiping Cao,Xin Li,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReDHF通过跨尺度可变形融合、椭圆引导浅层监督和全尺度查询交互，把DETR类检测器丢弃的高分辨率特征重新用于微小目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微小目标仅占少量像素，细粒度细节在下采样中丢失；最高分辨率特征虽保留局部细节，但因计算代价高被DETR类检测器舍弃，而直接引入又带来密集背景噪声。

Method: 提出三组件框架：(1) Cross-Scale Deformable Fusion (CSDF) 以适中代价构建语义增强的最高分辨率特征，并将细粒度细节注入浅层特征；(2) Ellipse-Guided Shallow Supervision (EGSS) 采用几何自适应辅助监督，引导增强特征学习更可靠的分类与定位先验；(3) Full-Scale Query Interaction (FSQI) 使解码器查询同时获取低层细节与高层语义，聚合更丰富的微小目标线索。

Result: 在多种DETR变体上一致提升基线，AI-TOD-v2 +1.4 AP、VisDrone +1.6 AP、SODA-D +1.5 AP，微小与小目标增益尤为显著，达到所评估基准的SOTA。

Conclusion: 有效复活被丢弃的高分辨率特征可在可控计算成本下显著改善DETR类检测器的微小目标性能，且具备跨基线可迁移性。

Abstract: Tiny object detection remains challenging because tiny instances occupy only a few pixels, and their fine-grained details are easily lost during feature downsampling. Although the highest-resolution feature preserves rich local details for tiny objects, DETR-like detectors often discard it due to its large computational cost, while directly reintroducing this feature also brings dense background noise. To address this problem, we propose ReDHF, a novel framework that Revives the Discarded High-resolution Feature for tiny object detection. ReDHF has three cooperative components. Cross-Scale Deformable Fusion (CSDF) first constructs a semantically enhanced highest-resolution feature and injects its fine-grained details into the shallow feature under a moderate cost. Ellipse-Guided Shallow Supervision (EGSS) then guides the enhanced feature to learn more reliable classification and localization priors by applying geometry-adaptive auxiliary supervision. Full-Scale Query Interaction (FSQI) further enables decoder queries to access both low-level details and high-level semantic information, allowing them to aggregate richer cues for tiny objects. Extensive experiments show that ReDHF consistently improves multiple baselines across different DETR variants and achieves state-of-the-art performance on the evaluated benchmarks. The best ReDHF variants surpass their baselines by +1.4 AP on AI-TOD-v2, +1.6 AP on VisDrone, and +1.5 AP on SODA-D, with especially notable gains on tiny and small objects.

</details>


### [207] [When GeoFMs Meet VHR: Benchmarking Foundation Models on Very High Resolution Earth Observation Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08003)
*Kshitij Raj Sharma,Pierre Adorni,Minh-Tan Pham,Dirk Tiede,Sébastien Lefèvre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VHR-Bench（10个≤1.5m GSD数据集、5类任务、8个领域），系统评测12个编码器在三档预训练体系下的迁移性；结论支持"先泛化后特化"，将DINOv3 ViT-L在102万VHR瓦片续训自监督，冻结骨干即可在少标签下明显提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流地理空间基础模型多在Sentinel-1/2、Landsat等10m或更粗GSD上预训练，而大量实际应用依赖能分辨单体建筑、道路的高分辨率（VHR）影像，二者尺度差异大，尚不清楚哪种预训练体系能有效迁移到VHR尺度。

Method: 构建VHR-Bench：1.5m GSD及更细的10个数据集，覆盖5类任务族、8个应用域、卫星与航空两类平台。评测12个编码器，分三档预训练体系（自然图像、VHR专用、Sentinel规模），全部冻结骨干，并在两种训练数据比例下测试。随后以这些互补结果为指导，在留出的102万VHR瓦片上对DINOv3 ViT-L做自监督续训，下游协议保持不变。

Result: 大规模自然图像预训练与VHR专用预训练均能有效迁移且优势互补：标签稀缺时SkySense最佳，全标签下Web预训练的DINOv3 ViT-L略胜且两者接近；仅用Sentinel尺度影像预训练的编码器在RGB输入下迁移最差。续训后的DINOv3 ViT-L在少标签时相对发布版骨干提升明显，全标签下提升温和，推理参数量与计算量不增加。

Conclusion: 支持"先泛化后特化"策略：强而广的自然图像预训练可经短程VHR续训进一步增强，同时保留其通用视觉特征；VHR尺度的下游任务不必从Sentinel尺度骨干出发。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models are a widely adopted starting point for remote sensing tasks. Most are pretrained on Sentinel-1/2 and Landsat imagery at a ground sampling distance (GSD) of 10m or coarser. Many applications instead rely on very high resolution (VHR) imagery, where individual buildings, roads, and amenities are resolved. We study which pretraining regimes transfer well to this scale. We introduce VHR-Bench, a benchmark suite of ten datasets at 1.5m GSD or finer spanning five task families, eight application domains, and both satellite and aerial platforms. We evaluate twelve encoders in three pretraining tiers with frozen backbones at two training-data fractions. We find that both pretraining on large natural-image collections and VHR-specific pretraining transfer effectively to VHR tasks, with complementary strengths. SkySense leads when labels are scarce; at the full label fraction the web-pretrained DINOv3 ViT-L edges ahead, with the two close together there. Encoders pretrained only on Sentinel-scale imagery transfer least well under RGB input. Guided by these complementary results, we continue the self-supervised pretraining of DINOv3 ViT-L on 1.02 million held-out VHR tiles and evaluate the resulting encoder without changing the downstream protocol. The continued model gives a clear improvement over the released backbone when labels are scarce and a modest one at the full fraction, at no increase in inference parameters or compute. These findings support a generalize-then-specialize strategy: already strong, broad natural-image pretraining can be further improved by a short VHR continuation stage while preserving its general visual features.

</details>


### [208] [Deformable Contact-Aware 3D Object Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08005)
*Songhe Wang,David Miller,Charles A. Kamhoua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将语言引导的3D物体摆放置重构为“掉落至平衡”过程，预测语义释放状态并借助刚柔耦合仿真器实现接触准确的放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接预测刚性6-DoF位姿，忽视室内摆放由接触决定这一物理事实，导致悬浮、穿模、无法使软表面变形。

Method: 提出DCAP：不预测静态位姿，而是构建语义释放状态（落点、物体尺度、可变形部件），交由刚柔耦合物理仿真器在重力与摩擦下演化至平衡，显式分离语义意图与物理实现。

Result: 在2,063组prompt-asset-scene基准上，相比最强刚性基线，VLM物理与自然度提升21.2分、人类偏好提升26.5分，平均穿透深度降低72.3%；相比simulated rollout基线，残余速度降低72.1%。

Conclusion: 物理准确的3D放置需将语义意图与物理实现严格分离建模；释放状态+仿真平衡的范式优于静态位姿回归。

Abstract: Current language-guided 3D object placement typically predicts a rigid 6-DoF pose, ignoring the physical reality that indoor placements are dictated by contact. Consequently, predicted poses often float, interpenetrate supports, or fail to deform soft surfaces. We propose Deformable Contact-Aware Placement (DCAP), a framework that reframes placement as a drop-to-equilibrium process. Rather than predicting a static pose, DCAP constructs a semantic release state that specifies the drop location, object scale, and deformable components, allowing a coupled rigid-deformable simulator to settle the object under gravity and friction. This explicitly separates semantic intent from physical realization. Evaluated on a benchmark of 2,063 prompt-asset-scene cases, DCAP significantly outperforms static baselines. On standard placements, it improves VLM Physics & Naturalness by 21.2 points and human preference by 26.5 points over the strongest raw rigid baseline, while reducing mean penetration depth by 72.3%. Compared to simulated rollout baselines, DCAP reduces residual speed by 72.1%. Ultimately, our results prove that physically accurate 3D placement requires modeling semantic intent and physical realization as strictly separate processes.

</details>


### [209] [NutriVisRAG: Multimodal Exemplar Grounding for Nutrient Estimation with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08054)
*Lubnaa Abdur Rahman,Ioannis Papathanail,Maria Panagiotou,Nefeli Dimitropoulou,Margret Harðardóttir,Elisabeth J. den Brok,Bastiaan E. de Galan,Stavroula Georgia Mougiakakou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NutriVisRAG：用视觉编码器检索带营养标注的相似餐食图像，作为多模态上下文示例供VLM在推理时估计营养（碳水等），无需微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由生活场景下基于图像的膳食营养评估困难；现有检索式VLM方法只用文本食物表征，未研究直接以视觉相似且带标注的餐食作为grounding。

Method: 提出检索增强生成框架NutriVisRAG：冻结VLM，通过图像编码器检索相似标注餐食并作为multimodal in-context exemplars注入提示；在4数据集、4个VLM、3种检索编码器、2种提示策略（整餐/逐项）共96种配置上评测，含胰岛素治疗糖尿病成人自由饮食数据，与两名营养师标注比对。

Result: 96种配置中56种降低主误差；整餐提示提升更频繁（35/48）优于逐项（21/48）；16个VLM–数据集对中13对的最佳NutriVisRAG误差低于最佳Vanilla；GPT-5.4-mini整餐提示下，各数据集最佳检索编码器均降低误差1.7%–35.7%。

Conclusion: 图像+标注构成的多模态示例可免微调地提升推理期营养估计，但收益依赖配置，尚不稳定。

Abstract: Diet is a major determinant of health, yet accurate dietary assessment from free-living meal images remains challenging. Existing retrieval-based Vision-Language Model (VLM) approaches to nutrient estimation typically rely on textual food representations, while, to our knowledge, direct grounding on visually similar annotated meals has not been investigated. We present NutriVisRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation framework that uses a vision encoder to retrieve similar meals with known nutritional annotations and provide them as multimodal in-context exemplars for VLM nutrient estimation at inference time, without task-specific fine-tuning. We evaluate NutriVisRAG across four datasets, four VLMs, three retrieval encoders, and two prompting strategies, including free-living meals from adults with insulin-treated diabetes, where carbohydrate estimates are compared against two dietitians' annotations. Across all 96 configurations (4 datasets x 4 VLMs x 3 retrieval encoders x 2 prompting strategies), NutriVisRAG reduced the primary estimation error in 56, with improvements more frequent under whole-meal (35/48) than per-item prompting (21/48). Across the evaluated configurations, the lowest NutriVisRAG error was below the best Vanilla error in 13 of 16 VLM--dataset pairs. For GPT-5.4-mini, under whole-meal prompting, the best-performing retrieval encoder for each dataset reduced the primary error on all four datasets relative to Vanilla, with reductions of 1.7--35.7%. Overall, our results show that grounding in multimodal exemplars comprising images and respective annotations can improve nutrient estimation at inference time without task-specific fine-tuning, although the benefits remain configuration-dependent.

</details>


### [210] [Spatiotemporal Dynamics-Aware Industrial Anomaly Detection and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08080)
*S-Wei Chen,Siangyu Yang,Yi-Chen Jan,Chun-Yi Lee,Shao-Yuan Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MUSTARD 是首个基于多模态大模型（MLLM）的、面向“动态型”工业缺陷的时空异常检测与推理框架，通过光流引导的关键帧选取与模态增强实现细粒度时空动态感知，并采用仅需少量正常样本的免训练推理流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测（IAD）研究主要依赖静态视觉模式，而真实产线中许多缺陷（如不规则运动）是动态的、无法从静态线索感知；即便是近期的 MLLM 方法也因时序建模能力不足而难以识别此类动态异常。

Method: 提出 MUSTARD 框架：(1) 光流引导的关键帧选取，捕捉局部运动模式与全局动态演化；(2) 引入增强模态以增强运动不规则性表达；(3) 基于正常模式的推理流水线，仅需少量正常参考样本作为检测基准，无需任何模型更新。

Result: 实验表明该方法在时空异常检测上性能优于现有方案，并能给出合理的解释性说明；同时具备跨产品快速适配能力与较高的决策可解释性。

Conclusion: MUSTARD 是首个面向动态型工业异常检测的 MLLM 框架，验证了将细粒度时空动态感知注入 MLLM 的必要性与有效性，代码将公开。

Abstract: Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) is a long-established problem that aims to detect defects in industrial products during manufacturing for quality control. Most existing studies focus on static visual patterns as the basis for detection. However, in real manufacturing environments, many anomalies are dynamics-based, such as irregular movements that cannot be perceived from static cues alone. We observe that even recent Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based approaches fall short in identifying such dynamic anomalies due to limited temporal modeling capability. To address this, we introduce MUSTARD: MUltimodal SpatioTemporal Anomaly Reasoning and Detection, a framework that equips MLLMs with fine-grained spatiotemporal dynamics awareness for detecting and reasoning about dynamics-based defects. MUSTARD introduces optical-flow-guided keyframe selection and augmented modalities to capture critical motion irregularities through local motion patterns and global dynamic evolution. Its normal pattern-based reasoning pipeline requires only a few normal reference samples as the detection basis, avoiding any model updates. This enables efficient adaptation across various products and provides high decision interpretability. MUSTARD is the first MLLM-based framework for dynamics-based IAD. Experiments demonstrate its superior performance in detecting spatiotemporal anomalies and providing plausible explanations. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [211] [TerraMesh-Masks: Open-Vocabulary Segmentation for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08081)
*Benedikt Blumenstiel,Hugues Devimeux,Johannes Jakubik,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TerraMesh-Masks，一个面向地球观测(EO)的大规模开放词汇分割数据集（1600万二值掩码、对齐900万多模态TerraMesh样本，基于OpenStreetMap与Overture标注），并提供523样本/200+类专家审核评测集；据此构建开放词汇版TerraMind，IoU 35.18%，显著优于通用SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇分割(OVS)方法在自然图像上表现良好，但由于语义、尺度、成像模态与地理上下文差异，迁移到地球观测数据效果差；且缺乏大规模EO专用OVS数据集与基准，阻碍该方向研究。

Method: 构建 TerraMesh-Masks 数据集：1600万二值掩码与900万多模态 TerraMesh 样本对齐，语义来自 OpenStreetMap 与 Overture 众包标注；另提供523样本、200+类的专家复核评测集；在 TerraMind 基础上改造出开放词汇版本进行训练与评测。

Result: 开放词汇版 TerraMind 达到 35.18% IoU，对比通用SOTA（约16.29% IoU）与 SAM-3（11.33% IoU），提升显著；数据集以宽松许可公开。

Conclusion: 大规模EO专用OVS数据与基准能显著改善开放词汇遥感分割的迁移性能，为遥感领域开放词汇感知奠定数据与基线基础；但方法层面创新有限，主要贡献为数据与基准。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation (OVS) enables models to segment a variety of concepts specified in natural language, removing the need for fixed label sets and fine-tuning. While recent OVS approaches have shown strong performance on natural images, they transfer poorly to Earth observation (EO) data due to differing semantics, scales, sensing modality, and geographic context. To date, large-scale EO-specific datasets and benchmarks for OVS remain scarce. We, therefore, introduce TerraMesh-Masks, a large-scale open-vocabulary segmentation dataset for EO, comprising 16 million binary masks aligned with nine million multimodal TerraMesh samples and annotated using OpenStreetMap and Overture-derived semantics. In addition, we provide an expert-reviewed evaluation set of 523 samples covering over 200 classes to enable robust evaluation. To demonstrate the impact of TerraMesh-Masks, we build an open-vocabulary version of TerraMind. Our results demonstrate significantly improved performance over general-purpose state-of-the-art models reaching 35.18% IoU versus 16.29% for this http URL and 11.33% for SAM-3. We release the dataset publicly under a permissive license at this https URL .

</details>


### [212] [ATHAAR: A Medieval Scientific Arabic Manuscript Dataset for Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08091)
*Ismail El Jamiy,Khadija Msalek,Guillaume Loizelet,Farah Benamara,Nabil Hathout*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向科学类阿拉伯语手稿转写的标注指南，并构建人工标注数据集ATHAAR，覆盖同一文本的不同抄本、书写风格与内容（含数字），随后用主流VLM开展跨字体、跨章节、跨论著的迁移实验，揭示现有系统在古科学文本上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪阿拉伯语科学手稿的识别受连写连字、变音符号不稳定及训练数据匮乏制约，缺乏统一的转写规范与可用资源，导致当前视觉语言模型难以胜任。

Method: 借鉴文本校勘学（textual criticism）制定科学阿拉伯语手稿转写标注指南；据此人工标注构建ATHAAR数据集，在异文版本、书法风格与内容维度上刻意变化，并纳入数字文本；基于先进VLM设计跨书写体系、跨章节、跨论著的迁移实验。

Result: 实验表明，当前VLM在手稿异文、书体变化与科学文本特有难点下转写性能显著下降，跨章节与跨论著迁移亦表现不佳，暴露出现有系统对古科学文献特性的适应不足。

Conclusion: 该工作为科学阿拉伯语手稿转录提供规范与基准资源，可作为该领域VLM评测与改进的基础；但方法层面以数据与评测为主，未提出新的模型架构或训练策略。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in handwritten text recognition, medieval Arabic scientific manuscripts remain an open challenge for current vision-language models (VLMs) due to cursive ligatures, variable diacritization, and more importantly the lack of training data. This paper aims to address this gap, by proposing the first annotation guidelines dedicated to the transcription of scientific Arabic manuscripts inspired by textual criticism, a well-established approach to historical text editing. We then propose, ATHAAR, a new dataset manually annotated following this guideline. Unlike previous resources in the field, ATHAAR varies both witnesses of the same text, calligraphic styles, and content to deal with texts but also numerals. Relying on state-of-the-art VLMs, we finally design a set of experiments to study models transfer across scripts, chapters, and treatises. Our findings highlight the limitations of current systems when faced with the specificities of ancient scientific texts.

</details>


### [213] [National-scale mapping of tree species composition in Switzerland from high-resolution aerial imagery and multimodal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08095)
*Yixin Zhou,Luca Ferrari,Verena C. Griess,Nataliia Rehush,Konrad Schindler,Mirela Beloiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文用深度学习融合0.1 m航空RGB、机载激光扫描(ALS)、AlphaEarth年度卫星嵌入与气候变量，在瑞士全境30 m分辨率上制图10个主要树种的断面积占比及"其他"类；空间交叉验证macro-R²=0.485，独立森林保护区数据macro-R²=0.552（30 m像元）/0.733（样地），优势类识别率73.2%/81.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有国家尺度森林图多为单优势树种像元分类，无法表达伴生种的混交比例，而树种组成决定生产力与干旱、风倒、虫害等扰动响应；因此需要在像元尺度上刻画冠层混交结构。

Method: 多模态深度回归：(1) 输入为0.1 m航空RGB影像、ALS点云衍生结构特征、AlphaEarth年度卫星嵌入与气候变量；(2) 监督信号为3,992个瑞士国家森林调查(NFI)样地的断面积实测比例，做11类（10树种+Other）比例回归；(3) 采用空间交叉验证评估，并在独立森林保护区数据集上做外部验证，以macro-R²与优势类命中率衡量。

Result: 空间CV下macro-R²=0.485；独立保护区数据上像元macro-R²=0.552、样地0.733，优势类判别率73.2%（像元）/81.1%（样地）；制图揭示枫与白蜡为广泛分布的次要成分（单优势类图遗漏），并重现了从低地阔叶林经云杉到树线附近落叶松与瑞士石松的垂直带序列。

Conclusion: 该工作将遥感多模态与地面调查结合，实现全国尺度亚像元树种组成连续制图，可补充样地统计支撑森林监测、混交林经营与气候适应规划；但对单像元比例回归精度中等（R²≈0.5），受NFI样地代表性与影像时相限制，属于遥感生态制图贡献，与具身智能/三维重建主线关系较弱。

Abstract: Tree-species composition shapes forest productivity and how forests respond to climate disturbances such as drought, windthrow and insect outbreaks. Yet most national forest maps assign each pixel a single dominant species and cannot show the share of accompanying species. We mapped the basal-area shares of ten main tree species and a residual Other class across Switzerland at 30 m resolution. A deep-learning model combining 0.1 m aerial RGB imagery, airborne laser scanning (ALS), annual AlphaEarth satellite embeddings, and climate variables was trained on basal-area observations from 3,992 Swiss National Forest Inventory (NFI) sites. In spatial cross-validation, the model reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.485 across the 11 classes. On an independent forest-reserve dataset, the map reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.552 for individual 30 m pixels and 0.733 for plots, and identified the dominant class in 73.2% of pixels and 81.1% of plots. The map shows maple and ash as widespread minor components that dominant-class maps omit. It also reproduces the elevational sequence from broadleaved lowland stands through spruce to larch and Arolla pine near the treeline. By resolving the species mixture within each 30 m pixel, the map complements field inventory statistics with spatially continuous information that can support forest monitoring, the management of mixed stands, and climate-adaptation planning across Switzerland.

</details>


### [214] [Towards Practical 20 kbps Generative Video Compression via Adaptive Tokenization and Entropy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08099)
*Jiancheng Zhao,Jixiang Luo,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将MiniMax-H3预训练因果视频VAE改造为离散tokenizer，配合小型自回归熵模型与可微码率代理联合优化，实现超低码率视频压缩的高感知质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低码率下细节丢失严重；现有生成式视频压缩多为"先压缩后生成"范式，生成先验仅在重建阶段发挥作用，且生成/重建表征未针对熵编码优化。

Method: (1) 将预训练因果视频VAE适配为离散视频tokenizer，保留重建能力；(2) 设计小型自回归熵模型建模局部与全局token依赖，采用分组预测实现并行解码；(3) 使用可微码率代理联合优化tokenizer与熵模型，使码率梯度引导token分配趋向低成本。

Result: 在UVG、MCL-JCV、HEVC Class B上取得更优感知率失真性能，相对GLC-Video BD-LPIPS节省82.2%–87.5%，BD-DISTS节省78.3%–85.0%。

Conclusion: 将预训练视频生成表征与熵编码联合、端到端可微优化，是提升超低码率生成式视频压缩效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Video compression at ultra-low bitrates remains challenging due to the loss of perceptually important details under limited bit budgets. Recent generative video compression methods leverage pretrained video priors to improve perceptual quality and temporal coherence, but many follow a compress-then-generate paradigm, where generative priors are primarily exploited during reconstruction. Moreover, representations learned for generation and reconstruction are not inherently optimized for entropy coding. In this work, we propose \method, which adapts pretrained video representations into compression-oriented representations while preserving their reconstruction capability. Specifically, we adapt the pretrained causal video VAE of MiniMax-H3 into a discrete video tokenizer and develop a small autoregressive entropy model that captures local and global token dependencies, with grouped prediction for parallel decoding. To further improve coding efficiency, we jointly optimize the tokenizer and entropy model using a differentiable rate surrogate, allowing rate gradients to adapt token assignments toward lower coding costs while preserving reconstruction quality. Experiments on UVG, MCL-JCV, and HEVC Class B demonstrate superior perceptual rate--distortion performance, achieving BD-LPIPS savings of 82.2\%--87.5\% and BD-DISTS savings of 78.3\%--85.0\% relative to GLC-Video.

</details>


### [215] [ProtoGestalt: Interpretable Prototypes for Facial Phenotyping and Diagnosis of Rare Genetic Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08125)
*Ranga Baminiwatte,Alexander Hustinx,Fabio Hellmann,Aaron J Masino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoGestalt：基于原型学习（prototype learning）的可解释面部表型框架，将学习特征约束为局部面部区域选择，实现症候群 vs 对照的筛查与综合征分型，预测由可定位的加性证据解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见遗传病多样且临床特征重叠，诊断困难；面部形态可提供线索，但现有诊断模型多为"黑箱"、可解释性弱，且并非面向未确诊人群的广谱筛查设计。

Method: 扩展原型学习：在特征空间加入约束，使每个原型绑定到特定面部区域，从而形成局部化、可加性的证据链；在多个对照数据集与不同类别先验（prevalence）设置下评估症候群/非受累判别，并测试训练期已观察综合征的识别能力。

Result: 原型表征在症候群与病例匹配对照间产生显著分离，且在对照比例升高时仍保持可用性能；框架可区分不同综合征。

Conclusion: 在已有较准确的专用面部分类模型之外，ProtoGestalt提供可解释替代方案，将预测与解剖学意义明确的面部区域关联，有望支持临床决策与表型-基因关联发现。

Abstract: Rare genetic disorders are difficult to diagnose due to their diversity and overlapping clinical features. Facial morphology can provide diagnostic clues, yet most diagnostic models offer limited interpretability and are not designed to support broad screening for undiagnosed disease. We present ProtoGestalt, an interpretable prototype-based framework for facial phenotyping, rare-disease screening, and syndrome identification. The method extends prototype learning by constraining learned visual features to select facial regions, allowing predictions to be explained through localized and additive evidence. We assess ProtoGestalt's ability to distinguish syndromic from unaffected faces on multiple control datasets and class prevalence values. We further evaluate it's diagnostic performance on syndromes observed during training. The learned prototype-based representations provide significant separation between syndromic and case-matched control images and retain useful performance as the proportion of controls increases. The framework also demonstrates the ability to differentiate among syndromes. Although reasonably accurate specialized models for facial classification of rare disorders already exist, ProtoGestalt offers an interpretable alternative that links predictions to anatomically meaningful facial regions, potentially supporting clinical decision-making and the discovery of phenotype-gene associations.

</details>


### [216] [FreeOcc: Decoupling Ego-Motion for Efficient 4D Occupancy Forecasting via Continuous Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08128)
*Zeping Zhang,Zhuoya Zhao,Samy Metari,Robert Laganière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeOcc：利用下游规划器提供的自车未来轨迹，在条件流匹配框架中显式做$SE(2)$几何解耦，剥离自车运动得到纯动态特征空间，实现无注意力的时序融合与仅作用于残差agent运动学的物理约束ODE；并用不确定性感知混合机制把高置信体素交给单遍解码器、仅对高不确定区域调用ODE求解，28.3M参数、27FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D占据世界模型依赖重交叉注意力或深层递归来分离自车运动与场景动态，算力开销大且冗余——因为自车未来运动本已由下游运动规划器给出，模型无需重新推断。

Method: 1) 将自车运动以$SE(2)$变换显式数学分解出条件流匹配框架，得到与自车无关的动态特征空间；2) 在该空间内做无注意力的时序融合，ODE仅作用于残差agent运动学并施加物理约束；3) 不确定性感知混合：确定性体素走单遍解码，模糊/高不确定区域才走ODE求解器。

Result: 仅28.3M参数（约为同类模型40%），最优集成模式FreeOcc-E达27FPS；nuScenes上NFCD 0.47m、mIoU 32.1%，预测性能SOTA；零样本迁移至SemanticKITTI；在明显的数据包丢失与时间戳抖动下仍保持理想mIoU的92.8%。

Conclusion: 显式几何解耦可替代昂贵的注意力/递归建模，在4D占据世界模型中同时获得轻量、实时与更强泛化与鲁棒性，并可直接利用规划器先验。

Abstract: Current 4D occupancy world models use heavy cross-attention or deep recurrence to disentangle ego-motion from scene dynamics. This requires significant computational capacity. However, this effort is largely redundant since the ego-vehicle's intended future motion is provided by the downstream motion planner. We present FreeOcc, a lightweight continuous-time world model that introduces explicit $SE(2)$ geometric decoupling into a conditional flow-matching framework. By mathematically factoring out self-motion, we create a purely dynamic feature space. This space supports attention-free temporal fusion and allows physics-constrained ODEs to operate strictly on residual agent kinematics. To maximize deployment efficiency, FreeOcc introduces an uncertainty-aware blending mechanism. This routes well-observed voxels to a single-pass decoder, reserving the ODE solver exclusively for ambiguous, high-uncertainty regions. Requiring only 28.3M parameters-roughly 40% of comparable models-our optimal ensemble mode (FreeOcc-E) runs at 27 FPS. On nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance with a 0.47 m Near Field Chamfer Distance (NFCD) and 32.1% mIoU. Furthermore, the model transfers zero-shot to SemanticKITTI. It also proves highly robust, retaining 92.8% of its ideal mIoU under significant sensor packet loss and timestamp jitter.

</details>


### [217] [Describe Your Facial Expressions by Linking Image Encoders and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08138)
*Yujian Yuan,Jiabei Zeng,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"面部表情描述"新任务，用GPT-3.5合成图文对，经三阶段BLIP-2式训练实现自然语言描述面部动作与情绪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表情研究局限于离散情绪类别或AU标签，无法刻画面部动作细节及动作—情绪间的推理关系，需要自然语言级别的细粒度描述。

Method: 三阶段训练框架：以BLIP-2预训练技术构建视觉—语言模型；利用GPT-3.5结合已有情绪类别/AU标注合成并组合caption，弥补缺少图文配对数据的问题。

Result: 能生成描述面部动作细节、情绪及其推理关系的caption，且对训练中未出现的情绪仍具泛化；该视觉—语言任务还提升了中间视觉特征在AU检测与情绪分类上的表现。

Conclusion: 验证了表情描述任务的可行性与合成数据策略的有效性，并显示多任务视觉—语言预训练对细粒度面部理解有正向迁移。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel task of describing human facial expressions of a facial image in natural language, which captures the nuances of facial actions and emotional states beyond traditional emotion categories or facial action units (AUs). To achieve the facial expression captioning model, we propose a three-stage training framework that trains a vision-to-language model using synthesized image-text pairs and the BLIP-2 pre-training techniques. To overcome the challenge of missing training image-text pairs for facial expression captioning, we propose a strategy that involves synthesizing and combining captions using GPT-3.5 and existing annotations on either emotion categories or AUs. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in generating captions that describe details of facial actions and emotions, as well as the inferential relationship between them, even when those emotions are not present in the training data. It is also demonstrated that the vision-to-language task enhances the performance of the intermediate visual features on both AU detection and emotion classification tasks.

</details>


### [218] [Fault-Guided Cross-Layer Prediction for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08140)
*Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将冻结ViT的深度视为离散状态转移序列，用有向预测器学习block间隐状态迁移，利用相邻层余弦相似度发现的"断层"确定源/目标角色，重建误差用于单类工业异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单类工业异常检测中特征重建方法普遍采用，但缺乏对"一个block能向另一个block传递什么信息"的机理分析；深层保留语义却丢失局部细节，需要显式刻画这种信息不可传递性作为异常判据。

Method: 1) 把DINOv2 ViT-L深度当作离散转移序列，学习逐token的block-to-block有向隐状态预测器；2) 用相邻层余弦相似度定位四条显著"断层"，划分稳定深度段，固定源层与目标层角色（深→浅或浅→深指向敏感层）；3) 以有界前景先验（仅用训练图像标定）衰减背景证据，同时保留前景外缺陷；4) 同一预测器架构与仅训练集校准协议可迁移至ViT-B和RADIO-B。

Result: MVTec-AD图像AUROC 99.75、VisA 99.42、Real-IAD 94.27（Real-IAD物体级AUROC 96.26）；256×256像素级MVTec-AD P-AUROC/P-AUPRO 98.46/95.97，VisA 99.21/96.12，Real-IAD 99.26/96.59。

Conclusion: 深度断层结构为源/目标层选择提供了可解释、可跨骨干迁移的准则，配合保守前景先验，单一预测器架构在三个工业数据集上取得竞争性单类异常检测性能。

Abstract: Feature reconstruction is widely used for unsupervised industrial anomaly detection in the single-class setting, where only normal images from one product category are available for training. We propose a single-class detector that asks what information one block can pass to another. We treat the depth of a frozen vision transformer as a sequence of discrete transitions. A directed predictor learns a block-to-block hidden-state transition pointwise over tokens. Adjacent-layer cosine similarity exposes four sharp fault lines in DINOv2 ViT-L, separating stable depth segments. The deepest blocks retain semantic context but lose local detail; a prediction from deep or shallow sources to sensitive targets therefore exposes the information that cannot be transported. The fault structure fixes the source and target roles, and the same rule transfers to ViT-B and RADIO-B. We use a bounded foreground prior calibrated solely on training images: it attenuates background evidence without also erasing defects outside the estimated foreground. Using one predictor architecture and a common training-only calibration protocol, image AUROC is 99.75 on MVTec-AD, 99.42 on VisA and 94.27 on Real-IAD. On Real-IAD the object-level AUROC is 96.26. At 256x256 pixel resolution, P-AUROC/P-AUPRO are 98.46/95.97 on MVTec-AD, 99.21/96.12 on VisA and 99.26/96.59 on Real-IAD.

</details>


### [219] [MCUFlowNet: Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search for Optical Flow on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08147)
*Enmin Lin,Jona Beysens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE-NAS: 面向带神经加速器的MCU的宏-微两级NAS框架，将峰值SRAM与延迟解耦，用于无人机稠密光流，产出MCUFlowNet系列Pareto最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密光流网络可用于微型无人机避障，但专为带神经加速器的微控制器（MCU）设计的稠密光流网络很少，且片上SRAM严重限制输入分辨率；现有网络（如EdgeFlowNet）未针对此硬件约束优化。

Method: 提出macro-to-micro NAS：(1) 宏设计固定骨干与峰值SRAM，通过profile定位峰值SRAM来自全分辨率输出头，用双线性上采样+卷积替换转置卷积（降峰值SRAM 18%），并加两个轻量上下文模块在不增峰值的前提下降低EPE；(2) 微设计在共享同一峰值的子网空间中搜索算子与深度，以编译器估计延迟与精度做权衡，无需显式显存约束。在Grove Vision AI V2（Ethos-U55）上以EdgeFlowNet为基座实施，并迁移到PIDNet-S语义分割。

Result: 最快的MCUFlowNet-S比重新训练的EdgeFlowNet快1.76x，EPE仅高1.6%；2 m/s模拟森林飞行中无碰撞到达目标率51.5% vs 41.8%。PIDNet-S变体在相近精度下快1.17x。

Conclusion: 通过显存-延迟解耦的两级NAS，可在严格SRAM预算的MCU上获得Pareto最优稠密光流模型，兼顾实时性与避障成功率，且框架具跨任务（分割）通用性。

Abstract: Dense optical flow allows small drones to avoid obstacles. However, few dense optical flow networks are designed for microcontrollers (MCUs) with neural accelerators, whose on-chip SRAM limits the input resolution. We present PACE-NAS, a macro-to-micro neural architecture search framework that decouples memory from latency: a macro design fixes the backbone and its peak SRAM, and a micro design searches operators and depths to trade accuracy against compiler-estimated latency. We apply it to EdgeFlowNet on a Grove Vision AI V2 board (Ethos-U55), where profiling locates the peak SRAM at the full-resolution output head. Replacing transposed convolutions with bilinear upsampling and convolutions lowers the peak SRAM by 18%, and two lightweight context modules lower the endpoint error (EPE) without raising the peak. All searched subnets share this peak, so the search needs no memory constraint. We name the Pareto-optimal models MCUFlowNet; the fastest variant, MCUFlowNet-S, runs 1.76x faster than a retrained EdgeFlowNet at a 1.6% higher EPE and, in simulated forest flights at 2 m/s, reaches the goal without collision in 51.5% of trials, against 41.8%. PACE-NAS also applies to semantic segmentation, where a searched PIDNet-S variant runs 1.17x faster at similar accuracy. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [220] [Improving Context-Shift Robustness of Convolutional Networks via Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08157)
*Jinen Setpal,Chaoyue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ContrastiveCAMs 空间特征归因方法与 Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy (CR-CE) 损失，抑制模型对上下文区域的依赖，提升 CNN 在 context shift 下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像分类模型在测试时遇到与训练分布不同的背景/上下文（context shift）时性能显著下降，根源是模型依赖非因果的上下文线索进行预测；现有特征归因方法存在理论局限，难以可靠定位上下文区域。

Method: 先用空间特征归因引导预测聚焦物体相关特征；指出现有归因方法的理论缺陷并提出 ContrastiveCAMs，生成更忠实的归因图；再基于此设计 CR-CE：在标准交叉熵上加入注意力正则项，压制上下文区域对预测的影响。

Result: 在 Waterbirds、Spawrious、ImageNet/ImageNet-BG、Hard-ImageNet 等中等至大规模数据集上取得一致的 context-shift 鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 通过更忠实的归因图 + 损失层正则化，可有效降低 CNN 对上下文捷径的依赖；方法无需改变网络结构，属通用训练框架。

Abstract: Image classification models often encounter objects in diverse and unpredictable contexts at test time, which may differ substantially from those seen during training. This phenomenon, termed context shift, exposes a key fragility in neural networks: when models rely on non-causal contextual cues for prediction, shifts in context can lead to significant performance degradation. In this paper, we propose a novel training framework to improve the robustness of convolutional neural networks under context shift. Our approach leverages spatial feature attribution to guide models toward making predictions that rely less on contextual regions and more on object-relevant features. As a first step, recognizing a theoretical limitation of existing feature attribution methods we introduce a new variant, ContrastiveCAMs, which produces more faithful attribution maps of model predictions. Building on ContrastiveCAMs, we further propose Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy (CR-CE), a modification of the standard cross-entropy loss that regularizes the model's attention by suppressing the influence of contextual regions, thereby improving robustness to context shift. We evaluate the effectiveness of our approach on several medium-to-large scale datasets (Waterbirds, Spawrious, ImageNet/ImageNet-BG, Hard-ImageNet), and report consistent improvements in context-shift robustness.

</details>


### [221] [Multimodal Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts for 3D Medical Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08185)
*Gizem Mert,Szymon Płotka,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Jarosław B. Ćwikła,Arkadiusz Sitek,Ewa Szczurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedHiVE：将层级化视觉-语言Soft Mixture-of-Experts（两级、按解剖尺度组织的SMoE）与解剖级/全局对比学习结合，用于3D医学CT的细粒度解剖建模，在分割、分类、检测三任务、6数据集上超越11个SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学3D扫描与报告天然具有层级结构，诊断线索集中在特定解剖区域并跨多个组织层级分布；但现有医学视觉-语言模型忽视该结构，无法聚焦临床相关解剖。既有工作要么对静态池化特征做直接的区域-文本对齐，要么仅在视觉侧做层级路由，缺乏动态、跨模态、跨尺度的信息流。

Method: 提出MedHiVE框架：采用两级可学习token路由机制，局部图像与文本特征先经local SMoE处理，再由global SMoE融合为统一多模态表示，视觉与文本token均在按解剖尺度组织的SMoE层级中路由（基于slot的动态跨模态信息流）；结合由报告派生的解剖级与全局级对比学习，在同一空间中同时对齐解剖级和全局视觉-语言表示；在25,000+例带解剖监督的CT扫描上预训练。

Result: 在6个数据集、3项任务（分割、分类、检测）上一致性超越11个SOTA方法，验证层级化建模的重要性；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 按解剖尺度组织的跨模态层级SMoE路由 + 多粒度对比对齐，能有效替代静态池化的区域-文本对齐与纯视觉层级路由，提升3D医学影像的解剖感知表征质量；属医学视觉-语言领域工作，方法思路（层级SMoE路由、尺度对齐对比）对多模态表征学习有借鉴意义，但与Physical AI/具身/3D重建主线关联较弱。

Abstract: Medical 3D scans and reports are inherently hierarchical, with diagnostic cues concentrated in specific anatomical regions across multiple levels of organization. Yet, most medical vision-language models ignore this structure and fail to focus on clinically relevant anatomy. We introduce MedHiVE, which combines hierarchical vision-language Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) with contrastive learning to jointly model fine-grained anatomy and global scan context. To our knowledge, MedHiVE is the first framework to route both vision and text tokens through a two-level SMoE hierarchy organized by anatomical scale, coupling this with report-derived anatomy-level contrastive alignment for 3D medical imaging. This replaces the direct region-text alignment of statically pooled features and vision-only hierarchical routing of prior work with dynamic, slot-based cross-modal information flow across anatomical scales. MedHiVE operates via a two-level, learnable token-routing mechanism, where local image and text features are first routed through a local SMoE and then fused by a global SMoE into unified multimodal representations. Contrastive learning aligns both anatomy-level and global vision-language representations in a unified space. Pre-trained on 25,000+ CT scans with anatomical supervision, MedHiVE consistently surpasses 11 state-of-the-art methods across 6 datasets on 3 tasks: segmentation, classification, and detection, demonstrating the importance of hierarchical modeling. The code for MedHiVE is at this http URL .

</details>


### [222] [ProQuant: Progressive Quantization-aware Training for Edge MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08216)
*Yufei Xue,Yushi Huang,Jiawei Shao,Pingcheng Dong,Yonghao Tan,Shiyao Li,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProQuant：面向低比特（W4A8）多模态大模型的权重-激活量化感知训练框架，含自标注重采样与渐进块激活机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 计算与显存开销大，难以部署到边缘设备；PTQ 在 W8A8 尚可，但在 W4A8 低比特下（尤其小模型）性能急剧下降，需专门的 QAT 方案。

Method: 提出 ProQuant 权重-激活 QAT 框架：1) 自标注重采样（SRL），用模型重新生成开源多模态数据的回复，使有限微调数据分布对齐预训练分布；2) 将多模态理解能力与语言建模能力解耦，提出渐进块激活（PBA）机制优先恢复多模态理解；3) 引入视觉敏感因子 τ 定位多模态关键层。

Result: 在 2B–8B 多个模型与基准上显著优于现有方法；W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct 平均精度提升 6.6%，仅约 1% 精度损失即接近全精度性能。

Conclusion: 低比特 MLLM 量化训练需同时处理数据分布对齐与能力解耦，ProQuant 为边缘端 W4A8 部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) with impressive perception and understanding capabilities will enable a range of downstream applications. However, their substantial computational and memory requirements hinder the real-world implementation, particularly for resource-constrained edge devices. Quantization has emerged as an effective solution for deploying large models. While post-training quantization (PTQ) successfully retains the performance at moderate bit-width (e.g., W8A8), it suffers from severe performance degradation under low-bit quantization (e.g., W4A8), especially for small-scale models that are more suitable for edge deployment. In this paper, we propose ProQuant, a novel weight-activation quantization-aware training (QAT) framework tailored for low-bit MLLMs. First, to align the limited finetuning dataset distribution with the pretrained dataset distribution, we propose a self-relabeling (SRL) scheme to resample the responses of the open-source multimodal dataset. Second, we are the first to take a decoupled view of multi-modal (MM) understanding and language modeling abilities. We propose a progressive block activation (PBA) mechanism to prioritize the MM understanding recovery. We also introduce a vision-sensitive factor $\vec{\tau}$ that effectively identifies the MM-critical layers. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks, covering models with 2B-8B parameters, show that ProQuant significantly outperforms existing methods. For example, our W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct improves average accuracy by impressively 6.6% and achieves performance comparable to full precision counterparts with only approximately 1% performance drop. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [223] [SplatAnything: Gaussian Spacing Normalization for Feed-forward 3DGS across Arbitrary Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08264)
*Hao Yu,Zhenwei Wang,Yongzhen Hu,Yuning Gong,Junta Wu,Xiaowei Zhou,Hujun Bao,Tengfei Wang,Chunchao Guo,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatAnything提出Gaussian Spacing Normalization (GSNorm)，将高斯中心间距的鲁棒场景级统计量归一化到统一空间，修正现有前馈3DGS中"径向距离归一化"与高斯间距尺度不匹配导致的监督歧义，并建立跨数据集、不同输入稀疏度与外推视角的系统基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3DGS方法仅在径向距离归一化空间中预测高斯属性，而相邻高斯中心的间距在不同场景尺度和相机配置下仍差异巨大；这种尺度目标不一致带来系统性偏移和模糊监督。

Method: 提出SplatAnything，核心是GSNorm：以高斯间距的稳健场景级统计量作为归一化基准，为高斯预测提供一致的空间参考；同时构建覆盖多数据集、多稀疏度输入和困难外推视角的基准评测体系。

Result: 大量实验显示达到SOTA，在场景尺度与视角大幅变化条件下增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 尺度归一化对象的错配是前馈3DGS的关键瓶颈，改为归一化高斯间距可显著提升泛化性，并需以外推视角基准严格评估。

Abstract: We identify a key limitation of existing feed-forward 3DGS methods: Gaussian attributes are typically predicted in a radial-distance-normalized space that canonicalizes a global radial statistic of the reconstructed geometry, while the spacing between neighboring Gaussian centers can still vary substantially across scene scales and camera configurations. This mismatch induces systematic shifts in Gaussian-scale targets, leading to ambiguous supervision. To address this, we propose SplatAnything with Gaussian Spacing Normalization (GSNorm), which canonicalizes a robust scene-level statistic of Gaussian spacing to provide a more consistent spatial reference for Gaussian prediction. To rigorously assess our method, we further establish a systematic benchmark spanning diverse datasets, input sparsity levels, and challenging extrapolated viewpoints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SplatAnything achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong gains under substantial variations in scene scale and viewpoint.

</details>


### [224] [DART: Domain-Agnostic Residual Transfer for Generalist Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08271)
*Muhammad Aqeel,Maham Nazir,Marco Cristani,Francesco Setti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART: a plug-in framework for normal-abnormal guided generalist anomaly detection that disentangles residuals into anomaly-relevant and domain-specific components via a Residual Disentanglement Module plus Cross-Domain Meta-Training.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing normal-abnormal guided GAD methods rely on residual signals that entangle transferable anomaly cues with domain-specific appearance factors (texture, geometry, acquisition conditions), limiting cross-domain transfer and causing spurious activations on unseen domains.

Method: Residual Disentanglement Module preserving anomaly-discriminative information while suppressing domain information; Cross-Domain Meta-Training that constructs episodes across source domains; architecture-agnostic, inserted between residual computation and the detection head of existing pipelines.

Result: Consistent improvements over multiple base methods on MVTec AD, VisA and BraTS, with the largest gains under stronger domain shifts. (Only abstract-level quantitative claims; no numbers given.)

Conclusion: Decoupling domain-specific and anomaly-relevant residual components is an effective, modular route to cross-domain generalist anomaly detection.

Abstract: Generalist Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to detect anomalies in unseen domains using only a few reference samples. Recent normal-abnormal guided methods improve this setting by exploiting abnormal references, but their residuals often mix transferable anomaly cues with domain-specific appearance factors such as texture, geometry, and acquisition conditions. This residual domain entanglement limits cross-domain transfer and may produce spurious activations on novel domains. We propose DART, a plug-in framework for normal-abnormal guided GAD that learns to separate residuals into anomaly-relevant and domain-specific components. DART combines a Residual Disentanglement Module, which preserves anomaly-discriminative information while suppressing domain information, with Cross-Domain Meta-Training, which forms episodes across source domains to make the separation effective. The method is architecture-agnostic and can be inserted between residual computation and the detection head of existing GAD pipelines. Experiments on MVTec AD, VisA, and BraTS show consistent improvements over multiple base methods, with the largest gains under stronger domain shifts. this https URL

</details>


### [225] [Interactive Visual Gym: Evaluating Visual Interaction Models with Simulated Users in Interactive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08286)
*Chenhao Zheng,Zixian Ma,Kai Li,Kanchana Ranasinghe,Jieyu Zhang,Artemis Panagopoulou,Weikai Huang,Jianxu Shangguan,Yuanyuan Tan,Silvio Savarese,Juan Carlos Niebles,Ranjay Krishna,Ran Xu,Honglu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Interactive Visual Gym (IVG) 框架：通过可执行任务环境与 persona 条件化用户模拟的联合仿真，对视觉交互模型进行闭环评测与训练；构建 IVG-Bench（315 个交互场景，覆盖烹饪、家务操作、移动界面）。最佳模型 Gemini 4 Argon 在时限内仅完成 53.4% 任务；用人类研究验证交互质量指标与参与者偏好一致率 78.5%；GRPO 概念验证显示闭环训练可提升 Qwen3.5-4B 的任务成功率与交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉交互模型在持续进行的物理任务中为用户提供辅助，其响应会改变用户后续行为与任务结局，因此必须闭环评估；但现有 benchmark 多为离线回放，无法捕捉这种相互影响，需要能联合仿真任务环境与人类用户的闭环基准/训练框架。

Method: 三个可执行任务模拟器（烹饪、家务操作、移动界面）重建日常任务与人对齐的动作空间；persona 条件化用户模拟器在配合助手的同时注入受控执行错误与偏离计划的行为；以 315 个交互场景实例化为 IVG-Bench，并用人类研究校验评测协议；进一步用 GRPO 做闭环训练的概念验证。

Result: SOTA VLM 作为视觉交互模型表现明显不足：最优模型 Gemini 4 Argon 时限内成功率仅 53.4%，在事实依据与可操作指导上存在显著短板，考虑响应时延时常过慢以致无法阻止错误；人工研究中交互质量指标与参与者偏好一致率 78.5%；GRPO 闭环训练使 Qwen3.5-4B 在烹饪环境中任务成功率与交互质量同时提升。

Conclusion: 闭环人机联合仿真评测是必要且可行的新范式，现有 VLM 在真实任务时序约束下远未可用；该环境亦可提供有效学习信号用于闭环训练，但训练结果仅为小规模概念验证，缺乏真实物理/机器人部署与开源证据。

Abstract: Visual interaction models that support users during ongoing physical tasks require evaluation in closed loop, where assistant responses can change subsequent user behavior and task outcomes. Existing benchmarks largely rely on offline replay and therefore cannot capture these effects. We introduce Interactive Visual Gym, a framework for closed-loop evaluation and training of visual interaction models through joint simulation of executable task environments and human users. We contribute three simulators that recreate everyday tasks and human-aligned action spaces across cooking, household manipulation, and mobile-interface domains, together with a persona-conditioned user simulation harness that collaborates with the assistant while incorporating controlled execution errors and other out-of-plan behaviors. We instantiate the framework as IVG-Bench, a benchmark with 315 interaction scenarios. Evaluating state-of-the-art VLMs as visual interaction models reveals substantial limitations: the best-performing model, Gemini 4 Argon, completes only 53.4% of tasks within the time limit, exhibits significant weaknesses in factual grounding and actionable guidance, and is often too slow to prevent errors when response latency is considered. We verify our evaluation protocol with a human study which finds 78.5% agreement between our interaction-quality metric and participant preferences. We further show that the environments provide useful learning signals: in a proof-of-concept GRPO experiment, closed-loop training improves both task success and interaction quality for Qwen3.5-4B model in our cooking environment.

</details>


### [226] [Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting for Online Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08288)
*Linus Franke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIBS 将 Beta 核引入在线增量式 3D 高斯溅射，用形状参数在"软高斯"—"近平顶实体面元"之间插值作为几何置信度调节器，配合置信度感知初始化、误差驱动重定位和闭式保形合并 LOD，加速优化并提升渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 3DGS 重建中，关键帧只对基元做一次性生成和数十次迭代微调，初始化差或缺细节难以纠正；且高斯核本身"软"，需大量重叠才能表现实体表面。

Method: 1) 用 Beta 核替代高斯核，形状参数作为"几何置信度旋钮"；2) 置信度感知生成：依据几何可观性与多视角深度一致性同时初始化不透明度与形状——可验证区域生成实体基元，不可验证区域保持软且可修正；3) 在线稠密化时做误差驱动重定位，把陈旧基元回收至光度误差热点；4) 有限支撑域支持精确 tile 剔除与更快光栅化；5) 闭式保形合并构建 LOD 层级，加速优化并支持实时渲染。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360 的 on-the-fly 协议下，PSNR 最高提升 0.8 dB，优化时间最多减少 40%。

Conclusion: 以 Beta 核形状参数表达几何置信度，是在线增量式高斯溅射中一种有效且开销可控的改进，兼顾质量、速度与实时渲染。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs a scene during capture: each keyframe spawns primitives once and refines them for only a few dozen iterations, leaving little chance to fix poor initialization or missing detail. Gaussians are also inherently soft, needing many overlaps for solid surfaces. We present Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting (HIBS), which brings beta kernels to incremental reconstruction. Their shape parameter interpolates between a soft, Gaussian-like profile and a flat-topped, near-opaque surfel, acting as a confidence dial for geometry. Our confidence-aware spawning uses geometric observability and multi-view depth agreement to initialize both opacity and shape, producing solid primitives where geometry is verifiable and soft, correctable primitives where it is not. As online densification, error-driven relocation recycles stale primitives into photometric-error hotspots. Bounded beta kernel support enables exact tile culling and faster rasterization, and a closed-form, shape-preserving merging formulation provides a level-of-detail (LOD) hierarchy for fast optimization and real-time rendering. On Mip-NeRF 360 in the on-the-fly protocol, HIBS improves PSNR by up to 0.8 dB while reducing optimization time by up to 40%. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [227] [ManimCode: Learning Visual Self-Correction for Educational Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08294)
*Zhengyang Liang,Lizi Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ManimData数据集与ManimCode训练框架：以渲染引导的迭代生成-执行-评审-修订流水线构建24,034条渲染验证轨迹，用角色条件SFT训练单个4B模型，再用RL在自生成状态上强化Reviser，显著提升渲染成功率与视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Manim可编程生成教育动画，但渲染成功不等于视觉与内容正确；渲染结果无法直接指出需修改的代码。现有智能体框架依赖大型（常为闭源）模型，现有Manim数据集多为最终程序，缺乏质量校验与反馈-修订轨迹，难以学习迭代精修过程。

Method: 构建渲染引导流水线（生成→执行→评审→修订，结合检索到的API文档），产出24,034条经渲染验证与质量过滤的记录，保留中间程序、执行错误、视觉批评与修正；对轨迹做角色条件监督微调，训练单一4B的ManimCode以完成编写/评审/修订；随后在自生成状态上用RL训练Reviser，奖励来自渲染结果与无参考质量反馈，以泛化到轨迹外错误。

Result: ManimData-test与ManimBench渲染成功率96.6%/97.0%，同设置未训练4B模型为52.6%/82.0%；视频质量分从2.48→2.94、3.00→3.44。接入两个长视频教育生成系统后，完整成片由19/117升至107/117、53/239升至239/239，教育效果与视觉质量亦提升。

Conclusion: 以小型开源4B模型配合"轨迹数据+角色条件SFT+自生成状态RL"可实现可用的反馈驱动代码精修，逼近大模型智能体效果并可直接嵌入长视频生成系统。

Abstract: Manim supports precise, programmatic video generation, but a successfully rendered educational animation can still have visual defects, missing explanations, or incorrect content. Rendering does not directly identify the code changes needed to correct these visual failures, so feedback-driven revision is needed. Existing agentic frameworks use iterative rendering and revision but typically rely on large models, often proprietary. Existing Manim datasets primarily contain final programs and often lack quality verification and the trajectories of feedback and revisions needed to learn this refinement process. We introduce ManimData and ManimCode, a data and training framework for feedback-driven educational animation generation. We construct ManimData through a render-guided pipeline that iteratively generates, executes, reviews, and revises Manim programs with retrieved API documentation. Its 24,034 render-verified, quality-filtered records preserve intermediate programs, execution errors, visual critiques, and corrective revisions. We use role-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on these trajectories to train ManimCode, a single 4B model, to write, review, and revise animation programs. To improve correction of errors that differ from those in the collected trajectories, we then train the Reviser with reinforcement learning on self-generated states, using rendering outcomes and reference-free quality feedback. ManimCode achieves 96.6% and 97.0% render success on ManimData-test and ManimBench, compared with 52.6% and 82.0% for the untrained 4B model under the same inference setup, while also improving video quality scores from 2.48 to 2.94 and from 3.00 to 3.44. Integrating ManimCode into two long-form educational video generation systems increases completed videos from 19/117 to 107/117 and from 53/239 to 239/239, alongside improvements in educational effectiveness and visual quality.

</details>


### [228] [InstantHand: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for Animatable Hand Avatar Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08330)
*Chanho Kim,Muhammad Salman Ali,Kyeonghwan Gwak,Muhammad Shaheryar,Incheol Park,Jeongwan On,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstantHand：单张图像前馈生成可动画3D高斯手部化身，无需测试时优化，2.84秒重建、161 FPS渲染；提出Soft Alpha Band正则与Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部化身重建依赖多视角采集或逐主体优化（per-subject optimization），可扩展性差；单图前馈方案中，图像平均损失对alpha泄漏监督不足导致细手指轮廓变粗，且图像特征回归的颜色容易漂移到学习先验、丢失观测区域的外观。

Method: 基于现成MANO、相机与掩码估计的单图前馈框架构建可动画3D高斯化身，无测试时优化。Soft Alpha Band正则将监督集中于轮廓边界以抑制alpha泄漏；Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer按每个高斯渲染贡献迁移源图像颜色，保留观测外观，并用全局源身份条件化的学习先验补全未观测区域。

Result: 2.84秒构建化身，161 FPS渲染；在InterHand2.6M上与逐主体优化基线持平，在HanCo零样本评估中具竞争力。

Conclusion: 前馈式单图高斯手部化身可在无优化前提下达到优化基线水平，实时性显著，具备可扩展性；边界聚焦正则与渲染贡献门控的外观迁移是有效的监督设计。

Abstract: Hand avatar reconstruction typically relies on multi-view capture or per-subject optimization, limiting its scalability. We introduce InstantHand, a feed-forward framework that constructs an animatable 3D Gaussian hand avatar from a single image using off-the-shelf MANO, camera, and mask estimates, without test-time optimization. Image-averaged losses provide insufficient supervision for alpha leakage, which thickens thin finger contours. To address this issue, we propose a Soft Alpha Band regularizer that concentrates supervision on silhouette boundaries. Furthermore, colors regressed from image features can drift toward a learned prior even in observed regions. Our Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer preserves observed appearance by transferring source colors according to each Gaussian's rendering contribution, while completing unobserved regions using the learned prior conditioned on global source identity. InstantHand constructs an avatar in 2.84 seconds and renders at 161 FPS, matching per-subject-optimized baselines on InterHand2.6M and remaining competitive in zero-shot evaluation on HanCo.

</details>


### [229] [4D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Micro-Doppler Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08342)
*Adnan Armouti,Guanyue Qian,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4DPS: a physics-based differentiable renderer that fits an explicit moving scene (oriented points with material/normal/velocity) to real camera-radar captures, splatting points into range-Doppler bins to output raw complex radar returns, achieving SOTA mmWave radar reconstruction and downstream sim2real transfer.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Millimeter-wave radar datasets are orders of magnitude smaller than vision/language data, and existing synthesis (forward simulators, learned generators, static inverse renderers) cannot fit real captures of moving bodies, leaving a sim2real gap. Need scalable synthetic radar data generation.

Method: Represent scene as oriented points with material, normal and velocity, initialized from synchronized camera; splat each point into range and Doppler bins to render raw complex returns; apply standard radar signal processing downstream; custom CUDA kernels for efficiency.

Result: 235 sequences on HuPR, 100 on RT-Pose. Median correlations 0.940/0.830 (range-azimuth), 0.829/0.695 (range-Doppler), 0.988/0.966 (micro-Doppler). Pose model trained on renders reaches 21.9 cm MPJPE on real data vs 16.7 cm real training and 127.6 cm RF-Genesis baseline. Fitting takes 5.7 min (HuPR) / 12.9 min (RT-Pose) on one H100.

Conclusion: Enables dataset-scale inverse rendering of mmWave radar for the first time, supporting synthetic data generation for human-centered downstream tasks, scene editing and augmentation.

Abstract: Millimeter-wave radar resolves the micrometer displacements of a moving body as micro-Doppler, enabling gait, gesture, activity, pose and vital-sign sensing. Each task relies on a downstream model trained on real data, yet radar datasets lag orders of magnitude behind vision and language. Brute-force collection will not close that gap, and existing synthesis methods (forward-only simulators, learned generators and static-scene inverse renderers) cannot fit a real capture of a moving body, leaving a sim2real gap. We present 4D Point Splatting (4DPS), a physics-based differentiable renderer that fits an explicit moving scene to a real capture. Using the synchronized camera to initialize the room and body, we represent the scene as oriented points carrying material, normal and velocity, and splat each point into range and Doppler bins. Our output is the raw complex return a real radar records, so we apply the same signal processing on the rendered data to obtain any representation for training any SOTA downstream model. We fit 235 sequences on HuPR and 100 on RT-Pose, two datasets with different radars. Our reconstructions set the state of the art, with median correlations of 0.940 and 0.830 on range-azimuth, 0.829 and 0.695 on range-Doppler, and 0.988 and 0.966 on micro-Doppler. Our renders also set the state of the art in downstream sim2real gap. On RT-Pose, a pose model trained on them reaches 21.9 cm MPJPE on real data, against 16.7 cm for real training and 127.6 cm for the RF-Genesis baseline, where both the rendered- and real-trained models learn nearly the same features. Our custom CUDA kernels fit a sequence in 5.7 minutes on HuPR and 12.9 on RT-Pose on one H100, making dataset-scale inverse rendering of mmWave radar possible for the first time. This work can enable synthetic data generation for any human-centered downstream task and model, and scene editing for data augmentation.

</details>


### [230] [USB Gym: A Gymnasium Testbed for Closed-Loop Ultrasound View Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08352)
*Thanapong Intharah,Zhichao Gao,Yiliang Liu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建USB Gym超声引导仿真测试台，将20种已发表方法接入分割/控制器/奖励三个可替换模块，在同一批60个episode上评估，最佳组合在20张新CT上仅推进到距目标13.7mm（约为pose oracle进度的16%），说明超声引导探针至目标切面任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导（移动探头使图像匹配目标切面）的各子模块方法通常在不同仿真器中分别评测，缺乏统一平台下的端到端整合验证；作者希望回答"把这些已发表方法拼成一个完整引导系统，效果是否足够好"。

Method: 搭建USB Gym测试台：标准引擎基于公开患者CT仿真超声，并由网络从CT标签渲染器官纹理；引导系统分为三个可替换槽位（器官分割器、控制器、奖励函数）。将20种已发表方法在同一60个episode（6位留出患者）上逐一运行，仅当在留出患者上检测到显著更优时才替换原模块；组合结果与原始引导在20张新CT上比较，取较优者为benchmark baseline；另设读取真实探头位姿的pose oracle作为参考上限。结果为探索性分析。

Result: 探头初始平均距目标切面16.0mm；pose oracle可达1.5mm。在组合确定时已知的结果中，仅图像相似度奖励（SSIM）略显著更优，因此组合为"原引导+SSIM奖励"。该组合作为benchmark baseline，最终停留在13.7mm，仅为oracle进度的16%。

Conclusion: 将未经调优的已发表方法与作者自有模块组合，无法将探头引导至目标切面附近，该任务仍属欠探索；测试台可继续接入新方法，且所有引导均未在真实硬件上验证。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Our main goal is ultrasound guidance: moving a probe until its image matches a target view. Published methods for each part of a guide are evaluated in separate simulators. We asked whether these methods, combined into one guide, are good enough. Methods: We built USB Gym, a testbed whose standard engine simulates ultrasound from public patient CT, with a network drawing organ textures from CT labels. Its guide has three replaceable parts (slots), an organ segmenter, a controller and a reward, which received 20 published methods, each run on the same 60 episodes of six held-out patients. In each slot, a published method replaced ours only if detectably better on these patients. The better of this combination and our original guide on 20 new CTs is the benchmark baseline. A pose oracle, reading the exact probe pose available only in simulation, set the reference. The results below are exploratory. Results: On these CTs, the probe started on average 16.0 mm from the target view; the pose oracle brought it to 1.5 mm. Of the results known when the combination was fixed, only an image-similarity reward (SSIM) was slightly but detectably better, so the combination was our guide with that reward. This combination was the benchmark baseline, ending at 13.7 mm (16% of the oracle's progress). Conclusions: Combining untuned published methods with ours did not guide the probe near the target view; the task remains under-explored. The testbed accepts new methods; no guide was tested on hardware.

</details>


### [231] [OraRefer: Segmentation-Guided Multi-Photograph Fusion for Oral Cancer Screening with Guaranteed Patient-Level Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08360)
*Rupam Mukherjee,Mehakdeep Singh,Shirin Dasgupta,Subhamoy Mandal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OraRefer：患者级口腔癌/OPMD筛查模型，分割引导基础模型逐图判定病灶，融合患者多图与风险因素，阈值带有限样本敏感度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习口腔癌筛查模型以单张照片为分类单元，而临床筛查的决策对象是"患者"（是否转诊），且转诊规则需保证漏诊比例上限。

Method: 用分割引导的基础模型对每张照片以预测病灶为条件给出类别判定；自动定位口腔区域，无需推理时标注；将同一患者的多张照片与风险因素做患者级融合（优于注意力多示例学习）；采用带有限样本敏感度保证的校准阈值。

Result: 714名患者共3000张照片上，患者级融合AUROC达0.919；90%敏感度目标下实际检出95%的OPMD/癌症患者，61例癌症中仅漏3例，特异度0.610；阈值无需重训练即可在本地校准集上设定。

Conclusion: 患者级建模与可保证的阈值校准使手机照片口腔筛查具备可部署的临床转诊决策能力，且各诊所可自设操作点。

Abstract: Oral cancer and the oral potentially malignant disorders (OPMD) that can precede it are visible on the oral mucosa, which makes smartphone photographs a promising tool for screening. Deep learning models for this task classify individual photographs. However, screening refers patients, not photographs: a patient is usually photographed several times, and the referral decision must miss no more than a set share of patients with disease. We propose OraRefer, a patient-level screening model that reads each photograph with a segmentation-guided foundation model that conditions its class decision on the predicted lesion, combines all photographs of a patient with risk factors, and refers through a threshold with a finite-sample sensitivity guarantee. The model localizes the mouth itself, so inference needs no annotation. On 3000 photographs of 714 patients, patient-level fusion reaches an AUROC of 0.919 and outperforms attention-based multiple-instance learning. At a 90% sensitivity target, OraRefer detects 95% of patients with OPMD or cancer and misses 3 of 61 patients with cancer, at a specificity of 0.610. Because the threshold is calibrated without retraining, each clinic can set its own guaranteed operating point on a local calibration sample.

</details>


### [232] [Adaptive Low-Light Enhancement for Road Anomaly Detection: A Multi-Architecture Benchmark and Edge Deployment Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08393)
*Obed Honour Eje,Oluwatosin Kolade,Glory Bagai,Taliha Abiodun Folorunso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在模拟光照与天气退化下评测四种目标检测架构，发现统一应用SCI低光增强在黄昏级别反而降点（mAP@50 -16.1%~-30.7%），提出基于逐帧Rec.601亮度的无训练自适应路由器，在Jetson Nano上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶道路异常检测需在夜间、雨雾等退化视觉条件下可靠工作，低光增强并非总是有益，需要按条件自适应选择增强策略。

Method: 基准测试YOLOv8n、YOLOv12n、RT-DETR、Faster R-CNN在物理可信的光照/天气退化下；提出轻量、免训练的亮度自适应路由，按逐帧Rec.601亮度在无增强、中等SCI、去噪+强SCI三档间切换；并在NVIDIA Jetson Nano边缘硬件上验证整条流水线。

Result: 统一应用SCI在黄昏级光照下造成显著负收益（-16.1%至-30.7% mAP@50）；所提路由器挽回大部分黄昏损失，同时保留低光增益；边缘设备可实时运行。

Conclusion: 低光增强应当条件化应用；亮度驱动的免训练路由是轻量实用的方案。但贡献限于检测鲁棒性工程，与Physical AI核心议题关联较弱，方法洞见有限。

Abstract: Road anomaly detection systems on autonomous vehicles must function reliably under degraded visual conditions including darkness, rain, and Harmattan dust haze. This paper benchmarks four object detection architectures, YOLOv8n, YOLOv12n, RT-DETR, and Faster R-CNN, under physically grounded lighting and weather degradation, and shows that uniform application of the SCI low-light enhancement network is actively harmful at dusk-level lighting (-16.1% to -30.7% mAP@50). We propose a lightweight, training-free luminance-adaptive router that selects between no enhancement, moderate SCI, and denoising plus strong SCI based on per-frame Rec. 601 luminance, recovering most of the dusk penalty while preserving low-light gains, and we validate the full pipeline on NVIDIA Jetson Nano edge hardware.

</details>


### [233] [SM4RT: Cascaded Feed-forward Model for 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08436)
*Pang-Chi Lo,Jhao-Syun Lai,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SM4RT：先用前馈模型重建3D几何，再以预测结构为引导估计运动，并将每像素3D运动表达在对应相机坐标系中以降低相机运动歧义，同时用弱监督损失弥补真实运动标签稀疏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法将几何重建与运动估计分开处理，且常在单一坐标系下预测运动，须显式补偿相机移动，导致动态场景中运动估计歧义大；同时真实世界逐点运动标签稀缺。

Method: 多任务级联前馈网络：第一阶段从RGB视频重建三维几何；第二阶段由预测结构引导（bootstrap）运动估计。将每像素3D运动在各自相机坐标系中表达。引入弱监督损失，用另一时刻的已标注点位置约束未标注点的运动。

Result: 在Kubric、Waymo、Point Odyssey三个动态场景数据集上运动估计多数指标最优；在五个动态场景数据集上几何重建与SOTA相当，未出现任务间性能此消彼长。

Conclusion: 几何重建与运动估计的级联统一框架不仅避免性能权衡，还能通过更强的几何推理反哺运动估计，验证了两任务顺序耦合的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce SM4RT, a multi-task cascade feed-forward model for dynamic scene reconstruction from RGB videos. While existing feed-forward approaches typically address geometry reconstruction and motion estimation separately--and often predict motions in a single coordinate frame that must account for camera movement--SM4RT solves these tasks sequentially by first reconstructing 3D geometry and then bootstrapping motion estimation from the predicted structure. By expressing each pixel's 3D motion in its corresponding camera frame, SM4RT reduces ambiguity arising from camera motion. To address the sparsity of real-world motion labels, SM4RT further incorporates a weakly supervised loss that regularizes unlabeled point motions using labeled point positions at the other time step. We evaluate motion estimation on three dynamic-scene datasets--Kubric, Waymo, and Point Odyssey--and SM4RT achieves the best performance on most metrics compared with prior baselines. We additionally benchmark geometry reconstruction on five dynamic scene datasets and show that SM4RT achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods. Overall, our unified framework demonstrates that combining geometry reconstruction and motion estimation in a cascade not only avoids performance trade-offs but also enhances motion estimation through improved geometric reasoning.

</details>


### [234] [Edge-Assisted Crowdsensing for Explainable Cyclist Safety Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08441)
*Hongsen Cao,Chia-Yen Chiang,Mona Jaber,Meihui Wang,Yasmin Fathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Edge-assisted crowdsensing system: handlebar cameras + VLM attribute extraction + OpenStreetMap infrastructure + bike kinematics fed into an explainable boosting machine (EBM) to predict crowdsourced 1-9 cyclist perceived-safety scores, with exact per-attribute attribution in rating points.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cyclist fatality rates remain high and AVs complicate mixed traffic; crash statistics and heavy multi-model detection pipelines cannot scale to continuous, route-level monitoring of perceived safety.

Method: Handlebar cameras capture street-level frames; a single-prompt VLM converts each frame into structured traffic attributes; EBM regressor fuses these with OSM road infrastructure and on-bike kinematics to predict a 1-9 safety score. Inference is placed at the network edge (fastest self-hosted VLM 2.49 s/frame, CPU scoring 0.4 ms). Evaluated on 1,362 annotated frames from two London routes with 5-fold CV, plus inter-annotator agreement, attribute audit, and sensitivity analysis.

Result: EBM achieves 72.2% binary accuracy and MAE 1.011, within one point of black-box ensembles. Shape functions give exact attribution: physical lane separation +0.97, painted lane +0.70, construction site -0.65. Annotator alpha = 0.4695. With re-encoding, a 20 s sampling interval serves 1,000 riders at 54 Mbit/s at a cost of 1.4 accuracy points.

Conclusion: Interpretable, edge-deployable VLM+EBM crowdsensing makes continuous perceived-safety monitoring practical and lets planners price infrastructure changes directly in rating points for cost-benefit analysis.

Abstract: Cyclist fatality rates remain high despite policy support for active travel, and autonomous vehicles add complexity to mixed traffic. Neither crash statistics nor multi-model detection pipelines scale to continuous monitoring of perceived safety along a route. We present an edge-assisted crowdsensing system: handlebar cameras supply street-level frames, a vision-language model (VLM) turns each frame into structured traffic attributes through a single prompt, and an explainable boosting machine (EBM) combines them with OpenStreetMap road infrastructure and on-bike kinematics to predict a crowdsourced 1-9 safety score. Extraction belongs at the network edge: the fastest self-hosted model needs 2.49 s per frame and scoring takes 0.4 ms on a CPU. On 1,362 annotated frames from two London routes, the EBM regressor reaches 72.2 percent binary accuracy and a mean absolute error of 1.011 under five-fold cross-validation. It is within one accuracy point of black-box ensembles. Its per-feature shape functions give exact attribution: physical lane separation is worth +0.97 rating points, a painted lane +0.70 and a construction site -0.65. Planners can therefore price an infrastructure change in rating points, and the results enter cost-benefit analysis directly. We also report inter-annotator agreement (alpha = 0.4695), an attribute audit, and a sensitivity analysis linking attribute error to accuracy. With re-encoding, a 20 s sampling interval serves 1,000 riders at 54 Mbit/s at a cost of 1.4 accuracy points, making continuous monitoring practical.

</details>


### [235] [EgoGeneralist: Unifying Egocentric Action-Video Tasks through Video-Native Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08477)
*Yanlin Jin,Bowen Yang,Cheng Zhang,Yifan Yang,Zhe Li,Jianfei Cai,Kai Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoGeneralist adapts pretrained video generators into a generalist model that unifies action-conditioned egocentric video generation, 3D human pose estimation, and joint action-video generation via a role-adaptive action renderer and cross-stream action-video coupling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pretrained video models have strong visual priors but do not natively represent human action in metric 3D space; the authors aim to bridge visual knowledge and metric 3D human motion within a single generative framework.

Method: Role-adaptive action renderer producing video-native representations of multidirectional action-video pairs, so metric motion serves both as spatial control signal and as recoverable generation target; cross-stream action-video coupling with lightweight adaptation to align metric motion with visual dynamics while preserving the pretrained visual prior; a single generalist framework covering five action-video tasks.

Result: Although adapted only on controlled laboratory recordings, the model generalizes to diverse unseen scenes and interactions, achieving effective action control and visual quality.

Conclusion: Pretrained video generators can be turned into unified, metric-motion-aware egocentric action-video models with light adaptation, bridging visual priors and metric 3D human motion; absolute quantitative comparisons are not stated in the abstract.

Abstract: Modern video models possess strong visual priors, yet do not natively represent human action in metric 3D space. We present EgoGeneralist, which adapts pretrained video generators to unify action-conditioned egocentric video generation, pose estimation, and joint action-video generation. Our role-adaptive action renderer provides video-native representations for multidirectional action-video learning, making metric motion usable as both a spatial control signal and a recoverable generation target. Cross-stream action-video coupling establishes close correspondence between metric motion and visual dynamics through lightweight adaptation while preserving the pretrained visual prior. Together, these designs establish a generalist framework spanning five action-video tasks. Despite adaptation only on controlled laboratory recordings, EgoGeneralist generalizes to diverse unseen scenes and interactions with effective action control and visual quality, bridging pretrained visual knowledge and metric 3D human motion.

</details>


### [236] [Where and Why is Grounded Reasoning Useful?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08485)
*David Yan,Le Xue,Forrest Huang,Qiuyi Zhang,Dustin Tran,Andrew M. Dai,Yinfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过链式思维分析方法衡量模型对 grounding 输出的实际使用程度，并对 grounded reasoning 训练配方做消融，发现其仅在"不可描述指代"任务上收益显著，据此提出 BrickVision 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grounded reasoning（在思维链中输出相关位置的文本坐标）被广泛用于复杂视觉推理，但其收益与局限缺乏系统研究，需要明确"何处、为何"有用。

Method: 提出链式思维分析方法，量化模型在推理轨迹中对所输出 grounding 的使用程度；对 grounded reasoning 训练配方进行细致消融；并构造多个合成任务以隔离任务属性（可描述 vs 不可描述指代）。

Result: Grounded reasoning 提升计数任务，但在广泛空间推理与感知基准上相对无坐标 grounding 的基线无显著增益；多个现有基准仅靠可描述文本指代即可求解；为此发布需不可描述视觉指代的 BrickVision 基准。

Conclusion: Grounded reasoning 的价值高度依赖于任务是否需要不可描述的视觉指代，现有基准存在评定偏差，BrickVision 用以填补该空白。

Abstract: Grounded reasoning, where a model outputs text coordinates of relevant locations in chain-of-thought reasoning, is a common strategy for solving complex visual reasoning problems. However, the benefits and limitations of grounded reasoning are understudied. We study where and why grounded reasoning is most useful. To do so, we introduce a chain-of-thought analysis method to measure how much a model uses its outputted grounding in its thinking trace and perform a detailed ablation study of a grounded reasoning training recipe. We find that grounded reasoning improves counting but shows no significant gain over baselines without coordinate grounding across a wide range of spatial reasoning and perception benchmarks. Using several synthetic tasks, we show that grounded reasoning is most useful when a task requires undescribable references, which are visual concepts that are difficult to describe in language. However, we observe that many existing visual reasoning benchmarks are solvable with only describable text references. To close this gap, we introduce BrickVision, a new benchmark that requires undescribable visual references to solve.

</details>


### [237] [Do You Miss the Nights We Spent Crafting Figures? Rethinking Teaser and Framework Figures in the Age of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08486)
*Xiaoqi Zhao,Youwei Pang,Kailai Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 对200篇论文中371幅动机图与框架图做视觉回溯分析，用多模态评审模型按六个传播/设计维度评分，并用源约束的生成式AI修订原图，发现修订提升清晰度（+0.428）但降低美学一致性（-0.111），主张 AI 辅助、作者主导的设计范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图示通过抽象、构图与强调把研究想法视觉化，生成式AI让这些选择更易实现，但也带来"解释清晰度"与"目的性视觉表达"的问题；作者希望用实证方式考察AI介入科学图示的收益与代价。

Method: 构建371幅图（160篇配额主样本×4个时段 + 40篇立意式风格对照样本）的语料；单一多模态评审模型对六维度进行评分，每图重复3次判定；对全部371幅图做源约束修订，其中369幅通过保真度检查；结合原图评分的时间序列比较与个案分析。

Result: 原图评分未见跨时段的一致下滑；修订使清晰度平均提升0.428分、美学一致性下降0.111分（5分制）；六维平均增益在动机图上大于框架图；个案显示新增解释可强化对比说明，但版式层级与视觉经济性问题仍未解决。

Conclusion: 模型得到的证据支持"研究者主导设计、AI仅辅助探索"的协作模式：作者仍需对解释目的、构图与科学关系的准确性负责；图集与评估记录已公开。

Abstract: Scientific figures give research ideas a visual form through choices of abstraction, composition, and emphasis. Generative AI offers new ways to realize these choices, raising questions about explanatory clarity and purposeful visual expression. We examine these questions through a visual retrospective of 371 motivation and framework figures from 200 papers. The corpus comprises a quota-based main sample of 160 papers spanning four temporal periods and a separate purposive style-contrast sample of 40 papers. A single multimodal judge evaluates six dimensions of communication and design, with three repeated judgments per figure. We attempt source-constrained revisions of all 371 figures, of which 369 pass the recorded fidelity checks. Original-figure ratings show no uniform decline across periods. Revisions increase mean clarity ratings by 0.428 points and decrease aesthetic coherence ratings by 0.111 points on a five-point scale. Gains in the six-dimension average are larger for main-sample motivation figures than for frameworks. Case analyses show that added explanation can clarify comparisons while leaving typographic hierarchy and visual economy unresolved.
