These model-based findings support researcher-led design, with AI assisting exploration and authors retaining responsibility for explanatory purpose, composition, and scientific relationships. The accompanying atlas and evaluation records are available at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Rewrite, Then Veto: Rewriter-Agnostic Edit Gating for LLM-Based Post-OCR Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05539)
*Cacho, Jorge Ramón Fonseca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EditVeto：在LLM全上下文OCR纠错后加一个0.6B参数的"否决门"，将改写与OCR输入按字符对齐并切分为差异块，逐块决定保留或回退，从而在改善错误的同时抑制"改对为错"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能修复历史文献的噪声OCR转录，但也会把原本正确的文本改坏；oracle分析显示：给定真实错误位置，按片段生成反而不如不纠错，而对整篇改写结果做编辑否决却有效，因此需要一个事后门控机制。

Method: 字符级对齐改写与OCR输入→差异切块→0.6B小模型门控逐块保留/回退；门控由两个Qwen3模型的改写数据训练后冻结，在八个HIPE-OCRepair-2026测试集上跨重写器评估（含两个未见模型），另做竞赛输出、重训门控、零样本Qwen3.8-27B三组补充实验。

Result: 冻结门控使全部五个重写器改善：加权cMER下降0.0026–0.0044，偏好分提升0.15–0.25（配对bootstrap，p≈0.0002）；在竞赛输出上四个队中三个cMER下降，冠军队反而从0.0050升至0.0065；重训门控对LoRA重写器cMER降0.0011–0.0021，集成达cMER 0.0069/偏好0.928；零样本Qwen3.8-27B cMER 0.0114→0.0108。

Conclusion: 中小规模编辑否决门可稳健、可迁移地抑制LLM纠错中的过度修改，但纯门控仍弱于有领域预训练的冠军方案（0.0050），仅与第二名持平（0.0071）；主要收益来自微调与集成，门控为次要组件。

Abstract: LLMs can repair noisy OCR transcripts of historical documents, but they also change text that was already correct. We propose EditVeto, a veto gate placed after a full-context LLM rewrite. The rewrite is character-aligned to the OCR input, its differences are split into blocks, and a 0.6B-parameter gate keeps or reverts each block. An oracle analysis motivates the design: given the true error locations, per-span generation is worse than no correction, whereas vetoing a full rewrite's edits helps. Trained on rewrites by two Qwen3 models and evaluated, frozen, on the eight HIPE-OCRepair-2026 test sets, the gate improves all five rewriters tested, two of them unseen in training: weighted cMER falls by 0.0026-0.0044 and the preference score rises by 0.15-0.25 (paired bootstrap, p ~ 0.0002 each). On published competition outputs it lowers cMER for three of four teams but raises it for the winning run (0.0050 to 0.0065). In a second, disclosed test look, a retrained gate still improves LoRA-tuned rewriters (cMER -0.0011 to -0.0021), and an ensemble of their gated edits reaches a cMER of 0.0069 and a preference score of 0.928, mostly through fine-tuning and pooling rather than gating. Built only on public models and data, it is behind the winner (0.0050) and tied with the second-ranked run (0.0071), both from the only team using domain pre-training, and ahead of all other published runs. A third, secondary look shows the unchanged gate improving zero-shot Qwen3.8-27B (cMER 0.0114 to 0.0108) but only the preference score of its LoRA-tuned version.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [When can multi-fragment 2D/3D registration be trusted? Calibrated certification and uncertainty-driven view planning for pelvic fracture fluoroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05618)
*Xiang, Bowen,Miga, Michael I.*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出闭环“配准-校准误差界-判定/规划下一视角”框架：对每个骨折碎片做全碎片配准，用校准的sandwich协方差给出误差界，标记不可信碎片并据此停止或规划新视角；仿真60例CT上验证覆盖率、误停率与规划效率，并在5例真实骨盆透视上检验无错误2mm认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将分割CT配准到透视可定位所有骨折碎片，但只有“可信”时才能指导手术。现有方法只给出点估计或局部误差，缺少跨视角的证据积累与可认证的停止判据，需要在术中决定是否继续拍摄或规划下一视角。

Method: 闭环流程：每次拍摄后对全部碎片配准，用校准的sandwich协方差界定误差界，标记不合理/歧义碎片，然后停止或规划下一视角。评估：60例留出骨折CT的仿真透视（含真实图像不确定性）；提出新判据“新增视角不应使任一片段移动超出其误差界”；另设最小最大化（minimax）主动视角规划；并考察所有视角共享的分割误差对判据的影响。

Result: 95%误差界仅覆盖83%的未标记碎片（5mm内为91%）；从第二视角起14%超5mm失效，其中三分之一误差界≤2mm。片段位移（加视角时）区分紧界失效最好（探索性AUC 0.94，界0.81）。“新视角移动不超界”判据将2mm误停率从23%降至6%（345 vs 362 正确停止），但更多轨迹未被认证；延迟停止无效，其他信号同等接受率下误停10–15%。共享分割误差（中位Dice 0.89）时误停升至21%（15例，探索性）。Minimax规划比无检验的临床协议更快认证（4.20 vs 4.72 次，Holm p<0.001），但无判据显著更早达到预设的2mm真实误差终点。真实5例完整骨盆透视中2mm认证零错误。

Conclusion: 校准的局部误差界不足以支撑手术决策；可信度必须依赖跨多视角的一致性证据（尤其是新视角引起的片段位移）。这一结论对部分扰动鲁棒性差，共享分割误差是主要风险来源。

Abstract: Registering a segmented CT to fluoroscopy can locate every fracture fragment but can guide surgery only when trusted. After each shot, our closed loop registers all fragments, bounds their errors with a calibrated sandwich covariance, flags implausible or ambiguous ones, and stops or plans the next view. On simulated fluoroscopy of 60 held-out fracture CTs with realistic image uncertainty, 95% bounds covered 83% of unflagged fragments and 91% of those within 5mm, but from the second view on 14% failed by over 5mm, a third with bounds of at most 2mm. A fragment's shift when a view was added separated tightly bounded failures best (exploratory; AUC 0.94, bound 0.81). Requiring that the newest view moved no fragment beyond its bound, preferred post hoc, lowered false stops at 2mm from 23% to 6% overall, with 345 instead of 362 correct ones but more trajectories uncertified; delaying the stop did not, and other signals at equal acceptance left 10-15% false. With a segmentation error shared by all views (median Dice 0.89), 21% of this rule's stops were false against 10% without it (15 cases; exploratory). Minimax planning certified sooner than the clinical protocol without the check (4.20 versus 4.72 shots, Holm p <0.001), but no criterion reached the pre-specified endpoint, a true 2mm error, significantly sooner, even in the true basin. On real fluoroscopy of five intact pelves no 2mm certificate was false. Trust takes evidence across views, not only a calibrated local bound.

</details>


### [240] [Dual Flow: Joint Image-Measurement Flow for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05843)
*Yesilyurt, Sevket Ege,Oktem, Figen S.*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: Dual Flow: 在图像域与测量域上建立耦合流模型,联合估计干净图像与去噪测量,使结果严格满足前向模型,无需调参且重建速度快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型求解线性逆问题多在图像域用含噪测量做条件/引导,最终解与观测的物理一致性仅被软性约束,且常需繁琐的引导尺度等超参数调节。

Method: 将图像重建与测量去噪表述为耦合估计问题,在图像域和测量域构建联合流;仅用 y0 初始化测量状态,之后两域状态基于当前值联合更新。推导采样更新,证明其由给定联合状态下的干净图像条件均值决定;推导联合状态的充分统计量闭式形式,将条件均值转化为含算子相关相关噪声的图像域高斯去噪问题,并训练速度预测网络从充分统计量估计条件均值。推理仅需设定步数。

Result: 由构造保证解满足前向模型;重建时间显著短于对比方法;在多种线性逆问题与图像数据集上失真指标与感知质量均达 SOTA;并分析了对模型失配的鲁棒性、多样化解生成与不确定性刻画能力。

Conclusion: 耦合双域流为线性逆问题提供了一致性有保证、免调参、快速的统一求解框架,兼具后验采样能力。

Abstract: Existing generative model-based approaches to linear inverse problems typically use the acquired noisy measurement to condition or guide sampling in the image domain. In this work, we introduce Dual Flow, a novel joint flow model for solving linear inverse problems by jointly estimating the clean image and its corresponding noise-free measurement from the observation. Specifically, Dual Flow formulates image reconstruction and measurement denoising as a coupled estimation problem, using coupled flows in the image and measurement domains. The acquired noisy measurement $\mathbf{y}_0$ is used only to initialize the measurement state; thereafter, the image and measurement states are jointly updated based on their current values. By construction, the final image and measurement estimates are guaranteed to satisfy the underlying forward model. We derive the sampling updates for the joint flow and show that they are governed by the conditional mean of the clean image given the current joint state. We further derive a closed-form expression for a sufficient statistic of the joint state and show that the required conditional mean can be obtained by solving an image-domain Gaussian denoising problem involving operator-dependent, correlated noise. We train a velocity-prediction network using this formulation to estimate the conditional mean from the derived sufficient statistic. Dual Flow requires no hyperparameter tuning beyond selecting the number of inference steps and achieves substantially shorter reconstruction times than competing methods. Extensive experiments across various linear inverse problems and image datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both distortion metrics and perceptual quality. Additional analyses assess robustness to model mismatch, as well as the ability to generate diverse plausible reconstructions and characterize reconstruction uncertainty.

</details>


### [241] [TOP: Transmission-Oriented Remote Sensing Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06430)
*Cao, Zi-Han,Liang, Jing-Ze,Wu, Shao-Jie,Di, Jin-Cheng,Deng, Liang-Jian,Vivone, Gemine*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出面向星地下行链路的全色锐化框架TOP，将紧凑二值隐码编码、星上融合与高效下行传输统一于单一管线，在50倍压缩比下GaoFen-2融合PSNR达31.4 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全色锐化方法默认高分辨率观测已完整下传至地面，忽略了有损压缩带来的扰动，导致模型在下行传输场景下鲁棒性差。

Method: 将遥感观测编码为紧凑二值隐空间，星上轻量融合模块直接预测融合后的二值隐码，仅下行传输该融合码，从而降低载荷与星上计算开销。

Result: 在多光谱与高光谱设置下，相同或更高压缩比时融合质量优于代表性全色锐化方法搭配标准有损编解码器的组合；GaoFen-2上50×下行压缩比下融合PSNR为31.4 dB。

Conclusion: 该管线在严格带宽约束下兼顾低载荷下行与轻量星上处理并保持高融合质量，可推广至其他面向传输的遥感任务。

Abstract: Pansharpening has been extensively studied as a fundamental image fusion task in remote sensing. However, most existing methods focus on improving fusion quality under the implicit assumption that high-resolution observations have already been fully downlinked to the ground. As a result, existing models are highly vulnerable to perturbations introduced by lossy compression used for downlink transmission. To address this limitation, we present, to the best of our knowledge, the first transmission oriented pansharpening (TOP) framework, which unifies compact latent coding, onboard pansharpening, and efficient downlink transmission within a single satellite-toground pipeline. TOP encodes remote-sensing observations into a compact binary latent space, where a lightweight onboard fusion module can efficiently predict the fused binary latent code. Since only the compact fused code is downlinked, the transmission payload is substantially reduced. Overall, TOP enables low-payload downlink and lightweight onboard processing while maintaining high fusion quality under severe bandwidth constraints. Experiments across multispectral and hyperspectral settings show that TOP consistently achieves superior fusion quality at higher compression ratios than representative pansharpening methods paired with standard lossy codecs. For example, on GaoFen-2, TOP attains a fusion PSNR of 31.4 dB at a downlink compression ratio of 50x. This design also points toward transmissionoriented processing for other remote-sensing tasks. The code will be made available at https://github.com/JingZe-Liang/TOP.git.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.IM'></div>

# astro-ph.IM [[Back]](#toc)

### [242] [Object-independent evaluation of small-data astronomical image classifiers: architecture, representation, and acquisition shortcuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06484)
*Marinho, Eraldo Pereira*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 该文构建天文学四分类数据集（星系、球状星团、星云、疏散星团，370个物理天体，DSS三波段FITS），在自适应视场与统一角视场等条件下比较自训练CNN-Transformer与ImageNet预训练ResNet-50、ViT-B/16，并设置基于采集/星表元数据的对照实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型天文图像数据集上报告的精度可能混杂目标复用、视场差异、采集参数与星表衍生属性，而非真正反映天体形态特征的学习。作者希望量化这些混淆因素对分类性能的贡献。

Method: 构建370个星表支持天体的四类数据集，按物理目标分组划分（222/75/73）以避免目标泄漏；三种视图（自适应全视场、统一角视场、两种中心区域掩膜对照）下，以三个固定种子比较自训练CNN-Transformer、ImageNet预训练ResNet-50与ViT-B/16；另用采集与星表元数据做基线，统计上用Holm校正与聚类bootstrap区间。

Result: 自适应视图一次性、目标独立测试的平均平衡精度：自训练模型0.773±0.014，ResNet-50 0.874±0.054，ViT-B/16 0.866±0.023；Holm校正后无主架构对比拒绝零假设，ResNet-50与ViT-B/16仅差0.007（95%聚类bootstrap区间[-0.048,0.062]）。仅用采集与星表元数据对照即达0.721–0.735，中心区域掩膜自适应视图达0.731–0.800。

Conclusion: 方法确实泛化到未见物理目标，但图像上下文、采集设置与星表属性携带大量类别信息；因此高精度不能作为学习到目标形态的证据，且该比较无法将架构效应与预训练效应分离。

Abstract: Reported performance in small astronomical image datasets can reflect object reuse, field-of-view differences, acquisition settings, and catalogue-derived properties as well as astronomical morphology. We designed \dataset{} to measure these effects in a four-class task comprising galaxies, globular clusters, nebulae, and open clusters. The dataset contains 370 catalogue-supported physical objects, each represented by three Digitised Sky Survey FITS bands, and uses a group-constrained split of 222 training, 75 validation, and 73 test objects. We compared a custom CNN--Transformer trained from scratch with ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50 and ViT-B/16 under an adaptive full-field representation, a common angular field, and two central-region-masked controls, using three fixed seeds. In the one-pass object-independent test, mean balanced accuracy on the adaptive view was $0.773\pm0.014$, $0.874\pm0.054$, and $0.866\pm0.023$ for the custom model, ResNet-50, and ViT-B/16, respectively. No primary architecture contrast rejected its null after Holm correction; ResNet-50 and ViT-B/16 differed by 0.007 balanced-accuracy points (95\% cluster-bootstrap interval $[-0.048,0.062]$). Controls based on acquisition and catalogue metadata reached 0.721--0.735, and central-region-masked adaptive views reached 0.731--0.800. These results demonstrate generalisation to unseen physical objects, but also show that image context, acquisition settings, and catalogue-derived object properties carry substantial class information. In this regime, high accuracy cannot be interpreted as evidence that a classifier learned target morphology, and the comparison does not isolate architecture from pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [CoSafe: Cooperative Runtime Safety Assurance for Mixed-Traffic Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06204)
*Wang, Dingji,Lu, You,Jiang, Haowen,Chen, Yanggang,Bi, Yuyang,Chen, Bihuan,Bao, Shenglin,Peng, Xin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CoSafe：模块化、与ADS无关的混合交通协同运行时安全保证框架，借助V2X让路侧单元进行场景级危险监测与多车协同干预，替代仅靠自车接管的方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时安全保证多为自车中心式接管，但混合交通中的违规常由多车各自局部合理却联合不相容的决策引起，单车独立接管无法可靠化解。

Method: 利用V2X通信使路侧单元(RSU)监测场景级危险，并对涉及多车的危险进行联合缓解；框架模块化、与具体ADS解耦，可与7种SOTA ADS集成。

Result: 在InterDrive基准上，集成CoSafe使AV驾驶得分平均提升70.03%，安全违规减少87.80%，识别不安全AV的F3达0.930，并满足实时性约束。

Conclusion: 将运行时安全保证从自车接管扩展到场景级协同干预，可显著提升混合交通下自动驾驶的安全表现，具备实时可用性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of autonomous driving systems (ADSes), real-world roads are evolving into mixed-traffic environments where autonomous vehicles (AVs) and other traffic participants (e.g., human-driven vehicles (HDVs), cyclists, and pedestrians) coexist and interact with each other. However, current ADSes still incur various safety violations in mixed traffic scenarios, raising a strong demand for runtime safety assurance frameworks that can continuously monitor hazards and prevent potential violations. Existing ego-centric assurance approaches are insufficient for mixed traffic, where violations often arise from locally reasonable yet jointly incompatible decisions among multiple vehicles and cannot be reliably resolved through independent takeover. To bridge this gap, we propose CoSafe, a modular and ADS-agnostic cooperative runtime safety assurance framework for mixed-traffic autonomous driving to extend runtime safety assurance from ego-centric takeover to scene-level cooperative intervention. CoSafe leverages vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication to enable roadside units (RSUs) to monitor scene-level hazards and jointly mitigate hazards involving multiple vehicles. We integrate CoSafe with seven state-of-the-art ADSes, and our evaluation on the InterDrive benchmark demonstrates that CoSafe improves the driving score of AVs by 70.03% on average, reduces safety violations by 87.80%, and accurately identifies unsafe AVs with an F3 score of 0.930, while satisfying real-time constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Relevance Propagation as Entropic Optimal Transport on Filippov Neural Networks: Sinkhorn Propagation and Region Relevance in Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05594)
*Gibaru, Olivier,Nyiri, Eric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将层级相关性传播（LRP）重构为一个单一的变分问题求解：LRP是前向激活流在活跃计算图上的无散度流，且与后向转移律的相对熵为零（非负参考下即后向路径上Markov测度间的KL散度）；由此统一导出LRP_0、LRP_{z+}、LRP_{α,β}及新规则Sinkhorn Relevance Propagation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRP长期被视为启发式归因方法，缺乏理论根基，各规则（LRP_0、LRP_{z+}、LRP_{α,β}）关系不清，也难以推广到非光滑/不连续网络结构。作者希望给出统一的变分/测度论解释，并把解释性归因与可靠性度量（Schrödinger bridge）纳入同一框架。

Method: 用Filippov算子有向无环图形式化前馈网络，配合Clarke-Filippov广义梯度微积分，覆盖分段光滑与不连续结构（如硬路由MoE）；在该"活跃计算图"上把LRP定义为相对熵为零的无散度流，参考量的选择决定具体规则，并引入逐节点可变权重得到LRPN_{α,β}（Hebbian式权重）；逐边取熵得到Sinkhorn Relevance Propagation；同时把反向传播刻画为Lagrangian松弛的伴随方程，并把条件流解释为Schrödinger桥。

Result: 证明ReLU与max-pooling网络上LRP_0等价于Gradient×Input，且在折点处存在显式跳跃，而LRP_{z+}保持连续；VGG-16上守恒恒等式在舍入误差内成立，各规则通过映射符号、偏置节点所占输出份额、faithfulness、定位性与类别依赖性区分；ConvNeXt-Tiny上LRPN_{α,β}守恒输出，其Hebbian权重取得测试规则中最高的ABPC。

Conclusion: LRP被证明是单一变分原理的解而非启发式，规则差异可归约为参考测度的选择，框架可扩展到非光滑网络，并提供从归因流到条件证据流（Schrödinger桥）的精确记账视角。

Abstract: Relevance propagation, which redistributes a network's prediction backward through the layers, is usually treated as a heuristic. We show that it is the solution of a single variational problem: Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) is the divergence-free flow on the active computational graph of zero relative entropy to the backward transition law of the forward activation flow; for a non-negative reference, this is a relative entropy between Markov measures on backward paths. The rules LRP$_0$, LRP$_{z^+}$ and LRP$_{\alpha,\beta}$ follow from this principle by the choice of a reference, and are instances of one rule, LRPN$_{\alpha,\beta}$, whose weights may vary from node to node, for instance with the node's activity, in the spirit of Hebb's principle. The principle rests on a measure-theoretic formalization of feed-forward networks as directed acyclic graphs of Filippov operators, which covers piecewise-smooth and discontinuous architectures (e.g. hard-routed Mixture-of-Experts) with a Clarke-Filippov generalized-gradient calculus; it also characterizes backpropagation as the adjoint equation of a Lagrangian relaxation. On networks of ReLU and max-pooling nodes, LRP$_0$ is Gradient$\times$Input, with an explicit jump across a kink where LRP$_{z^+}$ stays continuous. Taking the entropy edge by edge yields a new rule, Sinkhorn Relevance Propagation. Aggregated over an input region, the flow of a non-negative reference tells, with exact accounting, which hidden units carry its evidence, the conditioned flow being a Schr\"odinger bridge. On VGG-16, the conservation identities hold to round-off, and the rules are told apart by the sign of their maps, the share of the output left to the bias nodes, faithfulness, localization and class dependence; on ConvNeXt-Tiny, LRPN$_{\alpha,\beta}$ conserves the output and its Hebbian weights give the highest ABPC of the rules tested.

</details>


### [245] [Beyond Accuracy: Information-Theoretic Decoder Evaluation Under Latent Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05658)
*Kim, Tasha,Ballyk, Benjamin,Lei, Anson,Posner, Ingmar,Jones, Oiwi Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将脑-机器人辅助控制建模为POMDP，证明解码器可区分性决定后悔是"有界"还是"线性增长"的相变，并给出仅用混淆矩阵即可O(K²)计算的闭式后悔预测器U_id，在EEG等两类数据上验证其优于准确率指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑-机器人解码器以单试次分类准确率评估，但辅助控制本质是序贯的：规划器需在时间上积分间歇、含噪的解码输出，单步准确率只是下游任务性能的代理指标，可能严重误导传感器与解码器选择。

Method: 把脑-机器人控制形式化为POMDP；证明当且仅当任意目标对均为解码器可区分时，全时域后悔一致有界，否则线性增长；指出最差目标对Chernoff信息Γ_min为边界量；推导可由任意混淆矩阵在O(K²)时间内算出的闭式后悔预测器U_id(C)。

Result: 在两类正交信息通道域上，U_id能解释准确率之外的累积规划后悔；在真实EEG数据（BNCI2014001 被试5）上出现"准确率陷阱"：两个解码器验证准确率同为35.1%，留出后悔却相差9.6倍，且U_id能预测该差异；同信号控制实验进一步以跨独立特征通道的解码器确认U_id优势。

Conclusion: U_id可作为即插即用诊断工具，用于序贯辅助控制中的传感器与解码器评估与选择，弥补单试次准确率评估的缺陷。工作属脑机接口/机器人辅助控制交叉的理论-实证研究，非具身智能核心方法（VLA、世界模型、3D重建等），亦无真实机器人演示或部署证据，故为相关背景/工具类优先级。

Abstract: A central challenge in assistive brain-to-robot control is decoding a user's latent intent from complex and noisy brain signals. Prior approaches evaluate decoders as single-trial classifiers with accuracy-based metrics, but this neglects the inherently sequential nature of assistive control. Since a planner must integrate intermittent, noisy decoder outputs over time, single-step accuracy can only be a proxy when predicting downstream task performance. We formalize brain-to-robot control as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), and prove that there is a sharp phase transition: regret is uniformly bounded across the horizon as long as pairs of goals are all decoder-distinguishable, but grows linearly otherwise. We show that a single channel statistic, the worst-pair Chernoff information $\Gamma_{\min}$ sets this boundary. Then, we derive a closed-form regret predictor $U_{\mathrm{id}}(C)$, that can be computed from any confusion matrix in $O(K^2)$ time. Across two domains spanning orthogonal information channels, we show that $U_{\mathrm{id}}$ predicts cumulative planning regret beyond what accuracy can explain. We then highlight a pathological accuracy trap, in which two decoders have identical validation accuracy 35.1% on real EEG data (BNCI2014001 subject 5) but result in a difference of $9.6\times$ in held-out regret, which $U_{\mathrm{id}}$ predicts. Same-signal control confirms $U_{\mathrm{id}}$'s advantage with decoders that span independent feature channels. Ultimately, our work provides a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate and select sensors and decoders in sequential assistive control.

</details>


### [246] [Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255)
*Hongeun Kim,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeNO将奖励引导的初始噪声优化摊销为学习到的残差传输，冻结预训练生成器，用Local GRPO与标量奖励学习锚点条件下的噪声修正分布，实现推理时单次噪声修正即可获得目标奖励提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流模型的奖励对齐通常基于随机轨迹建模，不适合少步确定性生成器；而**梯度无关的初始噪声优化**虽无轨迹依赖，却需在推理时逐样本昂贵搜索。

Method: 提出LeNO：学习锚点条件下的噪声修正分布，以Local GRPO + 标量奖励训练（无需可微奖励/轨迹梯度），保持生成器冻结；针对高斯初始噪声与保范投影，推导修正噪声分布与原始先验间KL散度的可解上界，作为有原则的正则项；推理时仅需一次噪声修正。

Result: 在少步文本到图像与蛋白质结构生成（含昂贵黑盒可设计性奖励）上评估，LeNO以单次噪声修正提升目标奖励，同时基本保持生成质量与多样性。

Conclusion: 奖励可通过学习式初始噪声摊销对齐少步确定性生成器，避免测试时迭代优化；KL上界正则为偏离先验提供理论约束，方法具跨域通用性。

Abstract: Reward alignment for diffusion- and flow-based generative models is often formulated over stochastic trajectories, making it less suitable for few-step deterministic generators. Initial noise optimization provides a trajectory-free alternative, but existing gradient-free methods require costly per-sample search at inference. We introduce LeNO (Learning Noise Optimization), which amortizes reward-guided initial noise optimization into a learned residual transport while keeping the pretrained generator frozen. LeNO learns an anchor-conditioned distribution over noise corrections using Local GRPO and scalar rewards, eliminating iterative test-time optimization. For Gaussian initial noise with norm-preserving projection, we derive a tractable upper bound on the KL divergence between the corrected noise distribution and the original prior, providing a principled regularization objective. We evaluate LeNO on few-step text-to-image and protein structure generation, including expensive black-box designability rewards. Across both domains, LeNO improves target rewards with a single noise correction at inference while largely preserving generation quality and diversity.

</details>


### [247] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Scaling Generalist Robot Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06905)
*Korniak, Michal,Bagatella, Marco,Römer, Ralf,Nauman, Michal,As, Yarden,Vignesh, Pranav,Agarwal, Manan,Diaz-Bone, Leander,Zalewski, Piotr,Czarnecki, Mikołaj,Treven, Lenart,Saxena, Shashwat,Venkataraman, Sreyas,Patil, Sarvesh,Zhou, Zhiyuan,Marta, Daniel,Mees, Oier,Shafiullah, Nur Muhammad Mahi,Sferrazza, Carmelo,Sukhija, Bhavya,Schoellig, Angela P.,Krause, Andreas,Simchowitz, Max,Wagenmaker, Andrew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作研究通用机器人策略能否在部署阶段实现"多任务、通用化"的在线自我改进，并给出算法配方：系统评估价值估计、策略提取与资源分配等设计选择，发现基于价值的RL与训练任务分布是关键，且可泛化到未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人策略虽有强零样本能力，但部署时仍需适配；已有的迭代自我改进（RL）几乎只在单任务设定下探索，与基座策略的多任务能力不匹配，因此需回答"通用策略能否多任务泛化式改进以及靠什么算法配方实现"。

Method: 构建一套结构化评测以检验通用改进能力；系统性消融价值估计、策略提取与资源分配等算法与工程选择；在部署中进行多任务在线RL自我改进并考察对未参与在线训练任务的泛化。

Result: 通用策略可跨多样任务在线提升；正确配方还能提升未在在线训练中出现的任务的表现，并明确了泛化发生的条件——训练任务分布与基于价值的RL是决定性因素。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是机器人基础模型在线通用化改进的首批证据之一；强调了任务分布设计与value-based RL的重要性，为部署期持续学习提供配方。

Abstract: The introduction of generalist robot policies has enabled a single, large model to learn to perform a variety of tasks from a vast pre-training corpus. While these models often exhibit powerful zero-shot generalization capabilities, they typically require an adaptation phase to achieve sufficient performance in deployment. Iterated self-improvement via reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated promise in enabling this adaptation, yet such approaches have been largely explored in single-task settings - where the goal is to simply improve on a narrow deployment task - which lies in stark contrast to the multi-task capabilities of the base policy. In this work we ask whether generalist robot policies are capable of multi-task, generalist improvement in deployment - targeting many diverse tasks simultaneously - and what algorithmic recipe enables this. To answer this question, we first develop a set of structured evaluations to test generalist improvement capabilities, and systematically evaluate algorithmic and practical design choices regarding value estimation, policy extraction and resource allocation. We show that not only can generalist policies be improved online across diverse tasks, but that the right recipe can enable generalization and increase performance on tasks not considered during online training. We characterize when this generalization occurs and show that both the training task distribution and the use of value-based RL are critical. To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first evidence for online generalist improvement for robot foundation models.

</details>


### [248] [IMUDINO: Self-distilled Foundation Model for Inertial Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07437)
*Zhoutong Ye,Chengwen Zhang,Zixuan Wang,Xufeng Jian,Zhe He,Qingyang Wan,Xutong Wang,Jiaqi Liu,Mingze Sun,Yu Mei,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IMUDINO是首个面向惯性传感的通用基础模型，将DINO自监督范式迁移到IMU数据，通过语义保持的运动变换模拟运动模式与传感器配置变化，并用带频率感知位置编码的纯Transformer原生支持变长输入与变采样率，在HAR、健康、动作捕捉与里程计等任务上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有惯性基础模型多局限于惯性传感的单一子领域，在HAR等任务上甚至不及有监督SOTA，且普遍要求固定输入长度与采样率、对未见传感器位置泛化差，通用性远不及视觉与语言FM。

Method: 将DINO自监督学习范式改造用于IMU数据；设计一组语义保持的运动增强/变换以模拟运动模式与传感器安装配置的多样性；模型采用朴素Transformer编码器加频率感知位置编码，天然适配可变输入长度与采样率；预训练所得表示可同时提供全局与稠密特征。

Result: 在人体活动识别、健康监测、动作捕捉与里程计等多类任务上达到与各领域SOTA有监督方法相当或更优的水平；所学特征在跨用户、跨传感器配置、跨任务以及机器人里程计中的跨本体迁移上具备良好泛化性。

Conclusion: IMUDINO提供了一个简单而有效的通用惯性感知骨干，可作为惯性传感的基础模型底座，推动通用惯性FM的发展。

Abstract: We present IMUDINO, the first foundation model (FM) for inertial sensing to achieve competitive performance across diverse sensing tasks including human activity recognition (HAR), health, motion capture, and odometry. Prior inertial FMs often focus on one subfield of inertial sensing and sometimes underperform state-of-the-art supervised methods (e.g., in HAR), making their scope considerably narrower than FMs for vision and natural language. Moreover, they often require fixed input length and sampling rate, and generalize poorly to unseen sensor locations. To build a general-purpose foundation model for inertial sensing, we adapt the DINO self-supervised learning (SSL) paradigm to inertial data through a set of semantics-preserving motion transformations that simulate variations in motion pattern and sensor setup. For model architecture, we use a plain transformer encoder with frequency-aware positional encoding that natively supports varying input length and sampling rate. This simple setup proved remarkably effective for learning global and dense features generalizable across users, sensor setups, tasks, and, in the case of robot odometry, embodiments. We hope IMUDINO can serve as a backbone for inertial sensing and kickstart the development of general-purpose inertial FMs.

</details>


### [249] [RLGPA: A Reinforcement Learning-Guided Granularity Planning Attack for Decision-Based Sparse Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07512)
*Chao Zhou,Shenghua He,Jiaxin Mo,Qiangguo Yu,Fengchuang Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLGPA：用强化学习在线调度稀疏对抗攻击的支撑集缩减“执行器”及其粒度，在硬查询预算下用掩码Dueling Double DQN选择7种“策略-尺度”动作，CIFAR-10 稀疏度0.01下达到61%定向攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策式黑盒稀疏攻击只能获得top-1标签，且最优的扰动恢复/缩减策略随支撑集收缩而变化：粗粒度删除在早期有效，尾部阶段需要更细粒度、依赖历史的测试；因此需要自适应调度而非固定策略。

Method: 提出RLGPA在线强化学习框架：掩码Dueling Double DQN在7个稳定策略-尺度动作中决策；每个动作生成相对于当前支撑集的恢复分组，并在每次宏决策后重建分组；保持可行性的执行器只提交对抗性恢复，并把支撑集增益与查询代价反馈给规划器，从而在硬查询预算下调度缩减执行器与粒度。

Result: CIFAR-10稀疏度0.01下定向ASR 61%（SparseEvo 25%，Pointwise 0%）；ResNet50/ViT在稀疏度0.005下为75.5%/73.0%（对比29.0%/15.5%）；固定探测测试中40张图像后中位L0由165降至23且无需更新；状态盲与状态打乱对照显著退化，证明学到的策略利用了攻击状态。

Conclusion: 主要增益来自状态条件化的执行器调度与动态重划分；该方法通过强化学习自适应选择支撑集缩减策略与粒度，显著提升查询受限条件下的稀疏决策式对抗攻击效果。

Abstract: Decision-based sparse attacks must reduce perturbation support using only the victim's predicted label. The best restoration strategy varies as support contracts: coarse removal works early, while tail states need finer, history-aware tests. We propose RLGPA, an online reinforcement learning method that schedules support-reduction executors and their granularity under a hard query budget. A masked Dueling Double DQN selects among seven stable policy--scale actions; each action produces support-relative restoration groups that are rebuilt after every macro decision. A feasibility-preserving executor commits only adversarial restorations and feeds support gain and query cost back to the planner. Under the matched top-1-label oracle, RLGPA reaches 61\% targeted ASR at sparsity 0.01 on CIFAR-10 (vs. 25\% SparseEvo, 0\% Pointwise), and 75.5\%/73.0\% on ResNet50/ViT at sparsity 0.005 (vs. 29.0\%/15.5\%). A fixed-probe test reduces median \(L_0\) from 165 to 23 after 40 images without any update. State-blind and state-shuffled controls degrade significantly, confirming that the learned policy uses attack state; the main gains come from state-conditioned executor scheduling and dynamic repartition. The source code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [250] [Comparing Classification and Feature Learning by Analyzing Their Loss Functions in Normalized Spaces with Different Scale Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07741)
*Qiufu Li,Huibin Xiao,Linlin Shen,Weijia Fan,Xi Jia,Yongdong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出分类精度只反映样本级局部性质，而特征学习追求数据集级的类内紧凑与类间可分性；据此从特征学习第一性原理重新设计BCE损失，并在归一化特征空间分析BCE与CE的极小值（均可导致Neural Collapse）及随尺度因子γ的收敛行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督特征学习通常通过训练分类模型实现，隐含假设"分类精度高即特征质量好"。作者指出该假设不成立：分类精度刻画的是模型在数据集上的样本级局部性质，而特征学习目标是获得数据集级全局性质（类内紧凑性、类间可分性）。因此需要一个与特征学习目标对齐的损失函数。

Method: 从特征学习第一性原理出发，重新设计用于特征学习的二值交叉熵（BCE）损失，作为分类中CE损失的对应物；在归一化特征空间中解析分析BCE与CE损失的极小值结构（均导向Neural Collapse），并研究尺度因子γ变化时两种损失的收敛行为，结合大量实验验证。

Result: BCE与CE的极小值均可导致Neural Collapse（不同类样本特征最大紧凑且最大分离）。固定训练策略下，γ极大时可获得完美分类精度但收敛到理想特征几何更慢；γ极小时分类对微小扰动高度敏感；中等γ可同时实现可靠分类与良好结构特征。实验证明BCE在长尾识别与开集识别上具有优势。

Conclusion: 分类精度与特征质量随特征空间尺度γ呈现不同的变化规律，"精度高即特征好"的假设不成立；中等尺度因子是兼顾分类可靠性与特征几何结构的必要选择；BCE损失因更契合特征学习的全局要求，在长尾与开集识别场景下优于CE。

Abstract: Classification and feature learning are two closely interrelated fundamental tasks. Feature learning in supervised settings is typically achieved by training classification models, under the common assumption that higher classification accuracy implies better feature quality. In this paper, we first point out that classification accuracy reflects the sample-wise local properties of a model on a dataset, whereas feature learning aims to learn features with good dataset-level global properties, such as intra-class compactness and inter-class separability. From the first principle of feature learning, we redesign the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss, a counterpart of the cross-entropy (CE) loss used in classification. Then, in normalized feature space, we analyze the minima of the BCE and CE losses and show that both can lead to neural collapse, where sample features from different classes become maximally compact and maximally separated. More importantly, by investigating the convergence behaviors of the two losses as the scale factor $\gamma$ varies in the normalized space, we reveal how classification accuracy and feature quality change differently across spaces of different sizes. Under a fixed training strategy, a very large $\gamma$ achieves perfect classification accuracy but slows convergence toward the ideal feature geometry, whereas a very small $\gamma$ makes classification highly sensitive to small perturbations. A moderate $\gamma$ is therefore necessary for simultaneously achieving reliable classification and well-structured features. Extensive experiments validate these findings and demonstrate that BCE loss exhibits advantages in long-tail and open-set recognitions, as it better aligns with the global requirements of feature learning.

</details>


### [251] [Adapters as Data: The Latent Space of LoRAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07775)
*Moritz Grävinghoff,Konstantin Wehmeyer,Aron Asefaw,Boris Knyazev,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LoRA身份适配器视为一种数据模态，用Transformer自编码器学习非线性潜空间，并把采样、编辑、逆变换等操作与直接在原始权重空间做同样操作进行对比；进一步把序贯自编码器扩展到混合秩适配器的共享潜空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作把LoRA适配器当作成品而非数据模态；虽已发现适配器群体位于低维流形且线性PCA基足以支撑采样/编辑/逆变换，但作者想知道学习到的非线性潜表示能带来什么额外收益，以及如何统一处理不同秩的适配器。

Method: 在同一LoRA身份适配器种群上训练Transformer自编码器，将每个适配器映射到潜空间；对每个操作与直接在原始适配器权重上的相同操作进行对照；并扩展序贯自编码器，使混合秩适配器共享同一潜空间。

Result: 潜空间保留了权重空间中已有的能力（采样多样身份、编辑单个属性）；潜空间对位移更鲁棒——失效时退化为扭曲但仍有效的人脸而非纹理；扩展后的序贯自编码器可在共享潜空间中容纳混合秩适配器。

Conclusion: 将不同架构（秩）的适配器权重视为统一的通用模态具有可行性，学习到的非线性潜表示是进一步推广到异构适配器群体的机制。

Abstract: LoRA adapters are usually treated as finished artifacts rather than as a data modality. Prior work has shown that LoRA adapters populate a low-dimensional manifold that a linear PCA basis already organizes well enough to support different operations such as sampling, editing, and inversion. We ask what a learned, nonlinear latent adds beyond this. We train a transformer autoencoder on the same population of LoRA identity adapters, mapping each into a latent space and compare every operation against the identical operation applied directly to the raw adapter weights. The latent preserves the capabilities already available in weight space, sampling diverse identities and editing individual attributes. Beyond preservation, we show that the latent is far more robust to displacement than weight space, degrading into distorted but still valid faces rather than texture. Fixed-dimensional weight-space representations cannot natively host adapters of more than one rank, so we extend our sequential autoencoder to hold mixed-rank adapters within one shared latent. This gives a path toward treating adapter weights of varying architecture as a single, general modality rather than one tied to a fixed rank, and points to a learned, nonlinear latent as the mechanism that could carry this generalization further, toward heterogeneous populations of adapters.

</details>


### [252] [Flux as a Codec: Zero-Shot Lossy Compression with Flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07929)
*Fuma Kimishima,Jinjia Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FluxC 以预训练扩散模型 Flux 为先验构建零样本（zero-shot）编解码器，系统分析编码路径、重建方法与低码率失真的影响，并证明基于 ODE 的重建优于祖先采样，在自然图像上达到与近期神经图像压缩方法相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiffC 已表明预训练扩散模型可作零样本编解码器，但编码路径选择与重建方法如何影响率–失真–感知（rate–distortion–perception）行为尚不清楚，作者希望厘清这些因素并验证 Flux 作为零样本编解码先验的可行性。

Method: 沿三条轴线分析：一是编码代价与比特分配；二是在匹配 SNR 条件下比较不同扩散路径对理想信息率与跨时间步比特分配的影响；三是比较祖先采样、单步 ODE 重建与多步 ODE 重建三种重建方式，并在高斯源与自然图像上做实验验证。

Result: 匹配 SNR 时路径选择不改变理想信息率，但改变比特在各时间步上的分配；ODE 重建（尤其多步 ODE）比祖先采样取得更低失真，低码率区间优势更明显；FluxC 在高斯源与自然图像实验中验证了上述预测，且与近期神经图像压缩方法有竞争力，部分情形更优。

Conclusion: 编码路径主要影响比特的时间分配而非理论码率上限，重建方式（ODE 优于祖先采样）才是降失真的关键；这表明固定预训练扩散模型可作为一种有理论依据的零样本编解码器，无需任务特定训练。

Abstract: We propose FluxC, a zero-shot codec that uses Flux as a compression prior. Although DiffC has shown that pretrained diffusion models can serve as zero-shot codecs, we ask how the choice of coding path and reconstruction method affects rate--distortion--perception behavior. We analyze FluxC along three axes: coding cost and bit allocation, reconstruction method, and low rate distortion. At matched signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), we show that the path choice does not affect the ideal information rate, but changes how bits are allocated across time steps. For reconstruction, we compare ancestral sampling, one-step ODE reconstruction, and many-step ODE reconstruction. Our analysis shows that ODE-based reconstruction can achieve lower distortion than ancestral sampling, especially in the low rate regime. Experiments on Gaussian sources and natural images support this prediction. Moreover, FluxC performs competitively with, and in some cases outperforms, recent neural image compression methods, suggesting that Flux can serve as a principled zero-shot codec.

</details>


### [253] [Leakage- and Label-Aware Benchmarking of Class-Imbalance Strategies for Multimodal Chest X-Ray and EHR Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07950)
*Dang-Khoa Bui,Quang-Huy Tran,Minh-Khoi Nguyen-Bui,Duc-Tuan Ngo,Thanh-Trong Tran,Tuan-Khoi Nguyen,Hoang-Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对 MIMIC-CXR+EHR 多模态诊断基准做审计，发现记录级划分存在泄漏、标签源自单次住院编码的关键词匹配，患者分离评估下原有结论（类别不平衡策略、树模型优势、EHR 价值）均不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态胸部X光与EHR的类别不平衡基准多在"记录级"划分上评估，同一患者的多张影像会跨训练/测试集；作者怀疑此类评估高估性能、掩盖标签构造问题，因此系统审计该范式的有效性。

Method: 在 29,083 张 MIMIC-CXR 影像（10,479 名患者、8 个诊断目标）上做三阶段审计：(1) 用"EHR 向量查表复制标签"的查找表基线对照最优模型；(2) 将标签回溯至 MIMIC-IV 诊断编码，统计标签与影像所属住院编码的一致性；(3) 采用患者分离划分、住院对齐与规则精炼标签重新评估不平衡策略、树集成 vs 逻辑回归、EHR 变量与图像编码器（DenseNet、CheXpert 编码器、RAD-DINO），并做显著性检验与预训练数据重叠检查。

Result: 查找表基线几乎追平最佳模型；未见患者上所有模型退化至"全预测阳性"水平。约三分之一影像标签与其自身住院编码不符。患者分离评估下：无任何不平衡策略使宏 F2 提升超过 0.01；调优树集成相对逻辑回归无一致优势；发布的 EHR 变量停留在全阳性基线水平，而影像时刻可得的诊断史与图像嵌入超出该基线（规则精炼标签下，未见这些影像的 CheXpert 编码器亦如此）；RAD-DINO 在自监督预训练中已见 96.6% 影像。加入编码器后 F2 增益不显著；探索性 AUROC 增益仅 RAD-DINO 显著。

Conclusion: 记录级划分与单住院标签复制会造成严重泄漏与标签错配，导致基准结论不可靠；作者提出可复用的诊断工具、防泄漏评估协议与检查清单，主张以患者分离+住院对齐标签作为该类基准的最小要求。

Abstract: Benchmarks of class-imbalance strategies for multimodal chest X-ray (CXR) and electronic health record (EHR) prediction are often evaluated on record-level splits of cohorts in which one patient contributes several radiographs. We audit such a benchmark on 29,083 MIMIC-CXR radiographs of 10,479 patients with eight diagnosis targets. First, the record-split benchmark was misleading: a lookup table that copies the label of a development-set radiograph with the same EHR vector nearly matched the best model, and on unseen patients every model fell to the level of predicting every case positive. Second, tracing the labels back to MIMIC-IV explained why: each patient's labels are keyword matches on the diagnosis codes of a single admission, copied, with one EHR vector per patient, to all of the patient's radiographs, so about a third of the radiographs carry labels that differ from the codes of their own admission. Third, under patient-separated evaluation with admission-aligned and rule-refined labels, the initial conclusions do not hold: in the evaluated configurations no imbalance strategy raised the observed mean macro F2 over no augmentation by more than 0.01, tuned tree ensembles showed no consistent advantage over tuned logistic regression, and the released EHR variables stayed at the level of the all-positive baseline, whereas the diagnostic history available at imaging time and image embeddings exceeded it, including, with rule-refined labels, a CheXpert-trained encoder that never saw these radiographs; RAD-DINO had seen 96.6% of them during self-supervised pretraining. Adding either encoder to the diagnostic history gave no statistically significant F2 gain; exploratory AUROC gains were significant with RAD-DINO but not with DenseNet. We provide reusable diagnostics, a leakage-aware protocol and a checklist.

</details>


### [254] [How Far Can Untrained Neural Networks Go in Anomaly Detection? From Tables to Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08136)
*Hyojun Yi,Geunyoung Kim,Hogeon Seo,Seunghyoung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM用"固定随机非线性特征 + 正常参考马氏距离打分"实现异常检测，无需神经网络权重优化或预训练表示，在ADBench 47个表格数据集与27个视觉类别上达到竞争性水平，并提出理论刻画非线性变换对马氏分数差距的均值/协方差贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前异常检测多被视为表示学习问题，默认需要学习特征或AD专用优化。作者质疑这一视角，认为异常的"内禀可分性"可能被忽视，因而探究"未训练网络"在异常检测中能做到多好，并为评估学习表示的真实增益提供一个先于学习的基线。

Method: 提出PRISM框架：用固定随机初始化（不训练）的神经网络提取非线性特征，再以正常样本估计均值与协方差，进行马氏距离打分。分为表格版PRISM-T与视觉版PRISM-V（密集像素定位）。两者均不进行神经权重优化，也不使用预训练表示。理论上分析非线性变换如何分别通过均值项与协方差项影响马氏分数差距，并辅以经验性的分数可分性分析。

Result: ADBench 47个表格数据集上，PRISM-T取得最高平均AUROC 86.4%、第二高平均AUPRC 65.9%；MVTec AD与VisA共27个视觉类别上，PRISM-V图像级与像素级平均AUROC分别为90.4%与96.4%。

Conclusion: 强异常检测性能并不必然依赖学习表示；随机非线性特征配合健全的统计打分即为强基线。PRISM可作为"先于学习"的参照基线，用于衡量学习表示在异常检测中的边际价值。

Abstract: Anomaly detection (AD) is often framed as a representation-learning problem, where strong performance is expected to require learned features or AD-specific optimization. However, this view can overlook the intrinsic separability of anomalies from normal references, even in the absence of learned representations. We therefore investigate how far untrained neural networks can go in anomaly detection. To this end, we propose PRISM, a framework combining fixed random nonlinear features with normal-reference Mahalanobis scoring for tabular detection and dense visual localization. Across 47 tabular datasets in ADBench, PRISM-T achieves the highest mean AUROC (86.4%) and the second-highest mean AUPRC (65.9%) among the compared methods. On MVTec AD and VisA, covering 27 visual categories, PRISM-V achieves mean image-level and pixel-level AUROCs of 90.4% and 96.4%, respectively. Notably, both models achieve these results without neural weight optimization or pretrained representations. A theoretical analysis further characterizes how nonlinear transformations affect the mean and covariance contributions to the Mahalanobis score gap, while empirical analyses examine the resulting score separability. These findings establish PRISM as both a competitive anomaly detector and a prior-to-learning baseline for assessing the added value of learned representations in anomaly detection.

</details>


### [255] [Detecting Weather Fronts from Satellite Observations: What Does Reanalysis Add?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08148)
*Ioannis Dravilas,Miruna Stoicescu,Roope Tervo,Carlos Horn,Oriol Hinojo Comellas,Sina Montazeri,Andrea Meraner,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用同一ML模型做像素级锋面分类，比较仅用静止红外卫星观测与加入28个ERA5再分析场两种配置；卫星+ERA5将CSI@150从0.402提升到0.575，并发现低层风最不可由卫星表征推断且对性能影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天气锋面是性质对比气团间的过渡带，与中纬度降水和强天气相关；作者希望检验ML能否检测锋面，并量化再分析资料对检测性能的贡献。

Method: 两种配置的同一ML模型进行像素级锋面分类，覆盖北大西洋与欧洲：(1)仅用静止红外卫星观测；(2)额外加入28个ERA5再分析场；用150 km容差的邻域CSI评价，并分析各物理量可预测性（R²）与移除风场/湿度的影响。

Result: 仅卫星模型CSI@150为0.402，处于不同气象机构分析间一致性区间内；卫星+ERA5为0.575，提升0.173。低层风最难由卫星表征推断（R²=0.23，温度0.88），移除风场或湿度导致检测性能下降最大。

Conclusion: 再分析场显著改善ML锋面检测，卫星观测本身已具备可用的锋面信息；风场与湿度是关键补充信息，说明卫星表征在低层动力场方面存在系统性不足。

Abstract: Weather fronts are transition regions between air masses with contrasting thermodynamic properties and are associated with mid-latitude precipitation and severe weather. We investigate machine learning (ML) methods for detecting weather fronts and quantify the contribution of reanalysis data to performance. We compare two configurations of the same ML model for pixel-wise frontal classification over the North Atlantic and Europe: (1) using only geostationary infrared satellite observations, and (2) additionally using 28 ERA5 reanalysis fields. The satellite-only model achieves a neighbourhood critical success index at a 150 km tolerance (CSI@150) of $0.402$, which lies within the range of agreement between analyses from different meteorological services. The satellite$+$ERA5 model achieves $0.575$, an improvement of $0.173$. Our analysis shows that low-level winds are the least predictable from the satellite representation ($R^2 = 0.23$, compared with $0.88$ for temperature), while removing winds or moisture causes the largest decreases in detection performance.

</details>


### [256] [Interpretable Multimodal MRI-Clinical Fusion for Functional Outcome Prediction After Acute Ischemic Stroke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08154)
*Sina Raeisadigh,Henning Müller,Abderrahmane Hedjoudje*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估急性缺血性卒中第1天多模态MRI（ADC/DWI/FLAIR）在临床变量之外对三个月功能结局的增量预后价值，发现FLAIR-临床融合与ADC-临床融合表现最佳，而全模态融合未带来一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望明确第1天多模态MRI影像是否能在结构化临床变量（如年龄、NIHSS）之外提供额外的预后信息，并探索在样本量有限（66例）条件下数据高效、可解释的建模方式。

Method: 66例AIS患者；从ADC、DWI、FLAIR各模态提取三维预训练ResNet嵌入，用折内隔离的PCA降维，与结构化临床变量及ADC衍生病灶生物标志物拼接；采用线性SVM，在八折分层分组交叉验证下穷举评估各模态组合。

Result: 临床单模态AUC=0.899±0.151；纯影像模型AUC约0.67–0.73；FLAIR-临床融合最高（AUC=0.948±0.061），ADC-临床融合次之（0.941±0.094）；完整ADC-DWI-FLAIR融合未稳定优于单模态临床融合。特征重要性显示年龄、NIHSS子评分、ADC病灶负荷与学习到的FLAIR表征为主要贡献，FLAIR激活图定位于类病灶区域。

Conclusion: 紧凑的第1天FLAIR与ADC表征可在临床变量之外提供互补预后信息，支持在AIS中实现可解释、数据高效的结局预测；但受限于小样本，需外部验证。

Abstract: This study evaluates the added prognostic value of day-1 multimodal MRI for predicting three-month functional outcome after acute ischemic stroke (AIS). Sixty-six AIS patients with ADC, DWI, FLAIR, and clinical data were analyzed. Three-dimensional pretrained ResNet embeddings were extracted from each MRI modality, reduced using fold-isolated principal component analysis, and combined with structured clinical variables and ADC-derived lesion biomarkers. Linear support vector machines were evaluated using eight-fold stratified group cross-validation across MRI modality combinations. Clinical-only modeling achieved strong performance (AUC = $0.899 \pm 0.151$), while MRI-only models showed limited discrimination (AUC $\approx$ $0.67$-$0.73$). Among multimodal configurations, FLAIR-clinical fusion achieved the highest mean cross-validated performance (AUC = $0.948 \pm 0.061$), followed by ADC-clinical fusion (AUC = $0.941 \pm 0.094$). Full ADC-DWI-FLAIR fusion did not consistently outperform single-modality clinical fusion. Feature-importance analysis identified age, NIHSS subscores, ADC lesion burden, and learned FLAIR representations as key contributors, while qualitative FLAIR activation maps showed responses over lesion-like regions. These findings suggest that compact day-1 FLAIR and ADC representations provide complementary prognostic information beyond clinical variables and support interpretable, data-efficient outcome prediction in AIS.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Quadratic Attention: Separable Gaussian Operators for ODE Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08236)
*Carlos Boned Riera,David Romero Sanchez,Oriol Ramos Terrades*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SSoG：用可分离的轴向分解高斯核混合替代稠密自注意力，将每层代价从O(N²)降到次二次，且固定核时全局Lipschitz连续，从而以Picard–Lindelöf定理保证连续深度变体解的唯一性；在CIFAR-100 224×224上Top-1提升2.2–4.1点（离散/连续）、5.4–8.1点（loop结构），参数更少，高分辨率下吞吐与显存显著占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力的二次复杂度限制了ViT在高分辨率下的扩展；同时点积注意力的Lipschitz常数在紧致域外无界，导致连续深度（Neural ODE式）Transformer缺乏全局解的存在唯一性保证。作者希望在保持表达力的同时获得次二次复杂度与理论上的良定性。

Method: 提出SSoG空间算子：以可学习的、沿轴因子化的高斯核混合替代稠密注意力，实现可分离（separable）计算，使每层开销对N次二次。理论分析表明固定核的SSoG为全局Lipschitz连续，进而由Picard–Lindelöf定理确保连续深度变体在整个积分区间上解唯一。在离散Transformer、权值共享looped-Transformer与连续深度（Neural ODE）三种架构下，控制深度与训练配方不变进行对比。

Result: CIFAR-100@224×224：离散与连续设定下，三种模型宽度上Top-1提升2.2–4.1点，参数量减少8.8–16.6%；looped设定提升5.4–8.1点。推理时刻缩放实验（同batch、最高1024×1024）：连续深度架构下吞吐最高提升154%、峰值显存降低超60%，差距随分辨率增大而扩大；离散与looped设定在该分辨率下吞吐亦提升30%以上。

Conclusion: SSoG在离散、权值共享和连续深度三类Transformer中均提供了参数高效、理论可解释且实用的稠密注意力替代方案，其收益随分辨率提升而放大，验证了次二次复杂度与全局Lipschitz性带来的实际价值。

Abstract: Self-attention carries a quadratic cost in token count, which limits how far Vision Transformers can scale with input resolution. We introduce SSoG, a separable sum-of-Gaussians spatial operator that replaces dense self-attention with a learned mixture of axis-factorized Gaussian kernels, reducing the per-layer cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to subquadratic in $N$. Unlike dot-product self-attention, whose Lipschitz constant is unbounded outside a compact input domain, SSoG with fixed kernels is globally Lipschitz continuous, which, via the Picard--Lindelöf theorem, guarantees that the continuous-depth variant has a unique solution on the whole integration interval, a global guarantee that dense-attention counterparts lack in general. On CIFAR-100 at $224\times224$, in a matched discrete-Transformer setting and in the continuous setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 2.2--4.1 points across three model widths while using 8.8--16.6\% fewer parameters, with depth and training recipe held fixed. In a looped-Transformer setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 5.4--8.1 points. We further probe inference-time scaling by evaluating on inputs up to $1024\times1024$ at matched batch size: in the continuous-depth (Neural ODE) architecture, SSoG reaches up to 154\% higher throughput and over 60\% lower peak memory than dense attention, with the gap widening as resolution grows, consistent with the difference in asymptotic cost. In the discrete and looped settings, SSoG also achieves over 30\% higher throughput than dense attention at this resolution. Together, these results establish SSoG as a practical, parameter-efficient, and theoretically grounded alternative to dense attention across discrete, weight-shared, and continuous-depth Transformer variants.

</details>


### [258] [FewStepRL: Fine-tuning Few-step Diffusion Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08240)
*Haitao Lin,Minsi Ren,Yang Li,Xingtong Ge,Zheyuan Hu,Peiyan Hu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统化地将强化学习目标从多步采样瞬时速度适配到少步扩散模型的区间平均速度，通过x空间变换、梯度分解与锚点适配三阶段，在统一代码库中给出FlowGRPO、AWM、DiffusionNFT、DiffusionOPSD、ReFL的少步版本，并实验比较各类RL目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型RL后训练中每轮rollout开销主导训练成本；少步模型可降本，但其建模区间平均速度，而现有RL目标均针对多步采样的瞬时速度，两者不匹配。

Method: 三阶段(变换-分解-适配)：1) 变换到x空间，用统一策略预测表达各目标(默认在干净端，过渡/流映射目标在显式层)；2) 将各目标梯度分解为向参考的自回归项与奖励增强项，方法差异归结为锚点与权重的选择；3) 锚点由少步模型在传输终点或沿切向计算，瞬时速度从网络自身或辅助网络恢复。

Result: 奖励可微且梯度可负担时，基于奖励梯度的方法最有效；否则DiffusionNFT类方法虽单步成本较高，但迭代较少即可达到高奖励；增加推理步数无系统性总体收益，但部分输出在2–4倍采样步数下可见改善。

Conclusion: 提出统一的少步扩散RL目标框架与模块化实现，厘清了目标间的等价关系与方法选择依据，为少步生成模型的RL后训练提供实践指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training method for diffusion models, but most RL objectives roll out the current policy at every iteration, and with a many-step sampler these rollouts typically dominate the cost of training. Few-step models cut this cost, yet they model an average velocity over an interval, whereas existing RL objectives are built for an instantaneous velocity with a many-step sampler. We adapt RL objectives to few-step models systematically in three phases, namely a transformation, a decomposition and an adaptation. The transformation to $x$-space expresses each objective we consider through a common policy prediction, at the clean end by default and at an explicitly marked level for transition or flow-map objectives, so that objectives stated over densities, velocities or rewards become comparable. The decomposition writes the gradient of each of these objectives as the sum of a self-regression toward a reference and a reward augmentation, so that a method reduces, at the gradient level, to a choice of anchors and weights. The adaptation computes each anchor from a few-step model at the end of its transport or along its tangent, with the instantaneous velocity recovered from the network itself or from an auxiliary network. This yields few-step versions of FlowGRPO, AWM, DiffusionNFT, DiffusionOPSD and ReFL in one modular codebase. In our experiments, reward-gradient-based methods are the most effective when the reward is differentiable and its gradient is affordable; otherwise DiffusionNFT-style methods generally reach high rewards in relatively few iterations despite a sometimes higher per-iteration cost. Extra inference steps show no systematic aggregate benefit in our experiments, although selected outputs visibly improve with two to four times as many sampling steps as in training.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [259] [FFMT: Frequency-Factorized Multi-Rate Tokenization for Music-to-Dance Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07384)
*Qirong Guo,Ruijie Li,Zeyan Li,Sicheng Shu,Guanzhi Ding,Weihang Ding*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: FFMT对舞蹈动作做频率分解式离散tokenization：低频编码根相对骨架"编排"、高频编码局部动作残差，并采用非对称的token率与码本规模，配合"先规划后细化"的自回归生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 舞蹈动作同时包含缓慢变化的整体身体构型与快速局部动作，单一分辨率、单一码本的离散运动表示难以兼顾两者，需研究显式时频分解如何指导时间分辨率与码本容量的分配。

Method: 提出frequency-factorized motion tokenization (FFMT)：将根相对运动分解为低频舞蹈编排骨架与高频残差；低频分量使用较低token率与较小码本，高频残差使用较高token率与较大码本；该表示进一步导出"先规划后细化(plan-then-articulate)"的自回归分解——低频token先建立粗粒度编排，高频token在已生成计划与音乐条件下细化局部动作。

Result: 在AIST++与FineDance上取得较强的分布保真度，同时保持多样性与音乐-动作同步性；消融实验一致支持频率分解、非对称时间分配以及低频到高频条件化的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式时频分解是设计离散运动tokenizer的有效归纳偏置，可同时改善生成质量与计算分配效率，并为分层自回归动作生成提供自然结构。

Abstract: Dance motion combines slowly varying body configurations with rapid local movements. We investigate how explicit temporal-frequency decomposition can guide the temporal resolution and codebook capacity of discrete motion representations. We introduce frequency-factorized motion tokenization (FFMT), which separates root-relative motion into a low-frequency choreographic scaffold and a complementary high-frequency residual. FFMT uses a lower token rate and a smaller codebook for the low-frequency component, and a higher token rate and a larger codebook for the high-frequency residual. This representation further induces a plan-then-articulate autoregressive factorization: low-frequency tokens establish coarse choreography, while high-frequency tokens realize local motion details conditioned on the generated plan and music. Experiments on AIST++ and FineDance show strong distributional fidelity while preserving realistic diversity and music--motion synchronization. Ablations consistently support frequency factorization, asymmetric temporal allocation, and low-to-high conditioning.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Cross-Modal Semantic Successive Refinement for Generative Image Communication over Underwater Acoustic Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06205)
*Pei, Xu,Wang, Bin,Zhu, Guangxu,Zhang, Yi,Fan, Haiyan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出跨模态语义逐次精化的水下声学图像生成通信框架：发射端传文本、紧凑瓶颈特征与RVQ压缩的视觉边信息，接收端先由文本+瓶颈融合预测基估计，再用RVQ索引做残差校正，并用单步Pixel Mean Flow模型重建；训练加CLIP语义一致性约束，传输按分支/量化阶段重要性做分层不等差错保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下声学通信带宽受限、信道复杂，传统图像传输编码效率与重建可靠性难以兼顾；需在极低带宽下利用语义先验与少量辅助信息实现高质量重建。

Method: 发射端发送文本描述、瓶颈特征和残差向量量化(RVQ)压缩的视觉边信息；接收端融合文本与瓶颈特征预测中间视觉表示的基估计，再用RVQ索引恢复补充信息进行残差校正，最后用一步Pixel Mean Flow生成模型重建图像。训练引入基于CLIP的文本-图像语义一致性约束；传输阶段按信息分支与RVQ量化阶段重要性分配保护资源（分层不等差错保护）。在COCO 2017图像与Watermark实测水声信道记录上仿真，并做码率分配实验。

Result: 仿真表明文本与瓶颈特征提供互补的语义与结构条件，RVQ视觉边信息进一步提升重建质量，分层不等差错保护改善低SNR下的恢复；码率分配实验揭示在有限视觉特征编码预算下基础条件可靠性与视觉细节重建之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 在受限带宽下，语义-结构条件+轻量视觉边信息+单步生成重建+分层保护可有效实现水声图像传输；码率分配权衡为实际资源调度提供指导。

Abstract: Underwater acoustic communication faces challenges such as limited available bandwidth and complex channel environments, which impose demanding requirements on efficient image transmission and reliable reconstruction. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a generative image communication framework based on cross-modal semantic successive refinement. At the transmitter, text descriptions, compact bottleneck features, and visual side information compressed by residual vector quantization are transmitted. At the receiver, text and bottleneck features are first fused to predict a base estimate of the intermediate visual representation. The received RVQ indices are then used to recover supplementary visual information and perform residual correction, followed by image reconstruction using a one-step Pixel Mean Flow model. During training, a CLIP-based text-image semantic consistency constraint is introduced to preserve the semantic correspondence between generated results and the original text descriptions. During transmission, protection resources are allocated according to the importance of different information branches and RVQ quantization stages. Simulations using COCO 2017 images and measured underwater acoustic channel records from Watermark show that text and bottleneck features provide complementary semantic and structural conditions, RVQ visual side information further improves reconstruction quality, and layered unequal error protection helps improve recovery at low SNRs. Code-rate allocation experiments reveal a tradeoff between the reliability of fundamental conditions and visual-detail reconstruction under a limited visual-feature coding budget, providing guidance for allocating protection resources in underwater acoustic image transmission.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Robots in the City: Persona-Based Perceptions of Urban Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05954)
*Jang, Keonhee,Lee, Kanghoon,Yun, Seokwoo,Kim, Namwoo,Yoon, Yoonjin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 用18张首尔街景图与成对比较任务，检验机器人（无/配送机器人/人形）在昼夜与道路等级下对街道安全感的影响，比较两个persona-conditioned MLLM与25名真人判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市公共空间中行人常只"看得到"而不与机器人交互，机器人在场是否改变街道的安全感感知尚不清楚；同时希望评估persona-conditioned MLLM能否作为人类感知判断的代理。

Method: 生成18张受控变化的首尔街景图（时段×道路等级×机器人条件），两个MLLM（Qwen3.8-27B、Gemma4-26B）各自以500个合成persona为条件做成对"更安全"选择，25名真人完成同一任务，并对模型理由文本做定性分析。

Result: 模型与人类都偏好无机器人街道，且偏好配送机器人胜过人形；夜间、年长persona更倾向选择有机器人；道路等级影响较小。模型理由中配送机器人作障碍物、人形作威胁或保护者。与人类一致的是夜间效应，但模型对人形相对配送机器人的偏好在人类中高得多，Gemma对人形的夜间效应约为人类三倍。

Conclusion: 机器人对安全感的影响依时段而变；persona-conditioned MLLM只捕捉到部分人类判断，不能直接替代人类感知研究。

Abstract: Pedestrians increasingly encounter robots in urban public spaces without interacting with them, but how robots change the perceived safety of a street remains unclear. We examine how robot presence and type affect perceived safety across urban contexts. We generate 18 images of Seoul streets that vary in time of day, road class, and robot condition (no robot, a delivery robot, or a humanoid). Two multimodal large language models (MLLMs), Qwen3.8-27B and Gemma4-26B, each conditioned on 500 synthetic personas, choose the safer image in each pair, and 25 human respondents complete the same task. Both models prefer streets without robots and favor the delivery robot over the humanoid. Robots are chosen more often at night and less often by older personas, whereas road class has smaller effects. In the model rationales, the delivery robot appears as a physical obstacle, whereas the humanoid appears as either a threat or a source of protection. Like humans, both models chose robots more often at night, but chose the humanoid over the delivery robot far less often, and Gemma showed a night effect for the humanoid about three times that of humans. These results show that the effect of a robot on perceived safety depends on when it appears, and that persona-conditioned MLLMs capture only part of human judgments.

</details>


### [262] [Fluidic Body: Swarm Robots as a Fluid Extension of the Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06068)
*Eguchi, Michikuni,Yoshida, Shigeo,Nishimura, Mai,Hiraki, Takefumi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出Fluidic Body系统：在人与群体机器人之间引入"虚拟流体"，用户通过开合手掌改变流体属性（软/硬），从而在避障与物体搬运任务间无缝切换集群行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人可重塑形态作为人机交互的物理接口，但现有方法要求用户指定集群"去哪里"，缺乏随任务直观改变"集体行为"的手段。

Method: 以虚拟流体作为手与集群之间的中介层，手的开合手势映射为流体黏度/刚度等属性，进而调制集群控制器的软硬与内聚性：张手→柔软可变形、绕障流动；握拳→刚性内聚体，可推物。在两组仿真实验与一次实体机器人演示中评估，含17人VR用户研究与88人现场演示。

Result: 控制器在避障与搬运综合任务上优于任务无关的固定行为基线；VR用户完成任务更快更可靠，主观负荷更低、代理感更强；效果迁移至实体机器人演示。

Conclusion: 把集群视为人们本就熟悉的"流体"材料，可使机器人群体成为人体的可变形的延伸，实现直观、任务自适应的集群行为操控。

Abstract: Swarm robots have attracted growing interest as a physical interface for human--computer interaction, since a swarm can reshape its formation to fit the objects and surroundings it works with. For a human operator, however, adapting the swarm's behavior remains difficult. Most existing methods require the user to specify where the swarm should go, and they offer no intuitive way to change the collective's behavior as each task demands. We present Fluidic Body, a system that lets a person operate a swarm of fluid-imitating robots as an extension of the hand. The system places a virtual fluid between the hand and the swarm, and the user adapts the swarm's behavior to each task by changing the fluid's properties through simple hand movements. When the user opens the hand, the swarm becomes soft enough to deform and flow around obstacles. Closing the hand turns it into a hard, cohesive body that can push objects. We evaluated the controller's performance and its operability in two simulation experiments and one demonstration with physical robots. First, our controller handled both obstacle avoidance and object transportation better than an existing method that behaves the same way in every task. Second, in a VR user study, 17 participants completed the combined avoidance-and-transportation task faster and more reliably than with that method. They also reported a lower workload and a stronger sense of agency. Finally, this experience carried over to physical robots in a real-world demonstration (88 participants). These results suggest that treating a swarm as a fluid, a material people already understand, allows a robot swarm to work as a deformable extension of the human body.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Distributed Geometric Optimization and Control of Rigid Differential-Drive Formations from Relative-Position Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07163)
*Thanacha Choopojcharoen,Erman Selim,Ramisha Anjum,Baris Fidan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对仅有一个绝对位置锚点、绝对航向与相对位置测量的差速驱动机器人队形控制，提出由优化推导的两层“估计—控制”框架：分布式图近端估计器重建质心、锚点向量与中心化坐标，控制器将质心/朝向近端步骤与距离梯度队形调节结合，消除朝向漂移，并给出局部指数收敛性与ROS 2 Gazebo及5台实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于距离的队形控制只固定队形形状，位置与朝向不受约束；而已有方法多依赖GPS/全绝对定位或复杂通信。作者希望在仅有一个绝对位置锚点、绝对航向、机体坐标系相对位置测量与邻域通信的极简感知条件下，同时调节形状、位置与朝向三个自由度。

Method: 从优化问题出发设计两层结构。估计层：图近端估计器（graph-proximal estimators）重建质心、锚点向量与中心化坐标；精确近端更新可在有限步内达到一致，有限次原始-对偶迭代保证有界重建误差。控制层：将近端质心步与朝向步同距离梯度队形调节组合，并用一次有限旋转消除形状步引起的朝向漂移；通过离散Lyapunov分析证明估计器收敛后的局部指数收敛。

Result: 估计器在精确近端更新下有限步收敛，原始-对偶迭代下误差有界；闭环系统在估计收敛后局部指数收敛；方法在ROS 2 Gazebo仿真与5台实物机器人实验中验证有效。

Conclusion: 该工作表明距离队形控制中的位置与朝向自由度可由优化导出的一致性估计与近端控制统一调节，在极简传感（单锚点+绝对航向+相对位置）下实现队形、位置与朝向的闭环镇定，并具备仿真与实机可复现性。

Abstract: Distance based formation control fixes the shape of a robot team but leaves its position and orientation unconstrained. We regulate all three for differential drive robots that have only one absolute position anchor, absolute headings, body frame relative position measurements, and communication with their neighbors. We derive a two layer estimation control scheme directly from optimization. The first half of the result is a set of graph-proximal estimators that reconstruct the centroid, the anchor vector, and the centered coordinates. Exact proximal updates reach consensus in finitely many steps, and a finite number of primal dual iterations leaves a bounded reconstruction error. The controller combines proximal centroid and orientation steps with distance gradient shape regulation, and a finite rotation removes the orientation drift caused by the shape step. A discrete Lyapunov analysis gives local exponential convergence once the estimators have converged. We validate the method in ROS 2 Gazebo and in experiments with five physical robots.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970)
*Jun Liu,Dajie Sun,Zhenglun Kong,Zhenjiang Fan,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Wenbin Zhang,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对Cosmos Policy与RoboCasa上的world-action模型test-time scaling做"前提链"审计：候选信号、实际加速、评测完整性三项均未达标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: world-action模型日益依赖test-time scaling（候选选择、自适应算力分配）提升控制性能，但这些收益可能建立在未经验证的前提上；作者希望在声明前系统性核查收益是否真实可信。

Method: 采用premise-first、预注册的实验方法，在Cosmos Policy与RoboCasa上分别审计三条前提：(1)候选是否携带可被利用的决策信号（学习value selector vs 随机、hindsight oracle上界、状态级候选结果一致性）；(2)名义计算节省是否转化为端到端加速（token pruning的FLOPs削减与实测wall-clock加速对比）；(3)评测完整性（发现并修复计算密集规划路径上悄然出现的渲染损坏）。

Result: 干净数据上学习型value selector比随机选择差1.66pp；hindsight oracle仅提升1.40pp且episode级置信区间跨零；82%状态候选结果完全相同，选择空间极小。Token pruning降低单次前向FLOPs最多47%，但部署路径端到端加速最多1.15×（排除decode路径1.25×），未达1.5×目标；自适应分配实验在统计功效门槛下无定论。另定位并修复一处静默渲染损坏（沿计算密集规划路径发生率显著上升，但不影响官方direct-policy结果），并发布检测器、修复与脚本。

Conclusion: test-time scaling应以"前提链"（决策信号→实际效率→评测完整性）逐环审计，而非仅看成功率；相关结论限定于所测配置。

Abstract: World-action models increasingly apply test-time scaling, spending extra inference computation on candidate selection and adaptive allocation to improve control. Such gains are credible only when candidates carry exploitable signal, nominal compute savings become real speedup, and evaluation stays reliable. We audit all three on Cosmos Policy and RoboCasa with a premise-first, pre-registered methodology. On clean data, a learned value selector is 1.66 pp worse than random; a hindsight oracle gains only 1.40 pp, with an episode-level interval spanning zero; and 82% of states show identical candidate outcomes, leaving little selection headroom. Token pruning cuts per-forward FLOPs by up to 47% but yields at most a measured 1.15$\times$ end-to-end speedup on the deployed path (1.25$\times$ on a decode-excluded path), short of a 1.5$\times$ target, and our adaptive-allocation experiment is inconclusive under a power gate. We further identify and fix a silent rendering corruption whose rate rises sharply along compute-heavy planning paths while leaving the official direct-policy result unaffected, and release a detector, fix, and scripts. These findings show why test-time scaling should be audited as a chain of premises (decision signal, realized efficiency, evaluationintegrity) rather than by success rate alone. Conclusions are scoped to the tested configuration.

</details>


### [265] [BrainBind: Bridging Synchronized EEG and fMRI through Long-Form Naturalistic Video Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05581)
*Wang, Shixiao,Wu, Rining,Liu, Jian K.*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrainBind 将同步采集的 EEG 与 fMRI 映射到冻结的长视频嵌入空间，作为共享的视觉-语义参照进行跨模态检索，并在 CineBrain 数据集上给出五种子 Recall@K 矩阵分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经解码系统通常单独对齐单一模态，且多针对静态图像或短视频；真实世界视觉是动态且场景丰富的，EEG 与 fMRI 在时间/空间尺度上互补。作者希望在同步长时自然观看条件下研究双模态联合解码，并处理重复叙事上下文与大量语义相似候选带来的检索歧义。

Method: 构建 BrainBind 框架：将同步 EEG 与 fMRI 信号映射到冻结的长视频嵌入空间（作为公共视觉-语义参照而非字面神经编码）；在两个视频锚点（如 Omni 等）与四种系统配置（含 Joint-Dual）下，于自建 CineBrain 数据集上进行五随机种子 Recall@K 检索评估，并增加 episode-disjoint 划分以量化重复刺激身份与叙事线索的贡献。

Result: Omni 锚点下 fMRI 对完整候选列表的组织能力最强（MRR=23.82，nDCG@10=25.31）；Joint-Dual 配置取得最高平均标签感知 R@1（13.52%）且排序剖面不同。episode-disjoint 评估显示随机划分性能部分源于重复刺激身份与叙事上下文。不同代理空间与系统配置产生明显不同的 top-rank 与 rank-wide 检索特性。

Conclusion: 在同步长视频神经数据上，代理嵌入空间的选择与系统配置共同决定检索剖面；双模态联合对齐可行但绝对检索精度仍低，提示需更严谨的划分协议与更强的跨模态表征。

Abstract: Real-world vision is dynamic and scene-rich, while EEG and fMRI resolve it at complementary temporal and spatial scales. Most neural decoding systems, however, align one modality in isolation and mostly against static images or short clips. We study the synchronized case: EEG and fMRI are recorded during the same long-form naturalistic viewing, giving a shared stimulus but also recurring narrative context and many semantically similar candidates. We present BrainBind, a framework that maps synchronized EEG and fMRI into frozen long-form video embedding spaces and treats those spaces as common visual-semantic references, not literal neural codes. On CineBrain, a five-seed Recall@K 2 x 4 matrix across two video anchors and four system configurations shows distinct retrieval profiles. With Omni, fMRI organizes the full candidate list most strongly (MRR = 23.82, nDCG@10 = 25.31), while Joint-Dual gives the highest mean label-aware R@1 (13.52%) with a distinct rank-wide profile. The complementary episode-disjoint evaluation quantifies the contribution of repeated stimulus identity and narrative context to the random split. Across these experiments, proxy choice and system configuration produce distinct top-rank and rank-wide retrieval profiles within the present synchronized dataset. The code is available at https://github.com/Forainest789/Brainbind.

</details>


### [266] [Risk Control Certifies Rates, Not Magnitudes: An Audit of Selective Fetal Biometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05851)
*Yang, Zhengshuai,Jiang, Linna,Li, Changyun,Zhao, Guihu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文审计一个基于工具调用的产前头围(HC)生物测量流水线，指出选择性预测的有限样本风险保证只约束"粗差发生率"而非"误差幅度"，并用Clopper-Pearson校准的分割完整度阈值仍会产生271.3 mm的极端误差；最终发现通过收紧测量量的解剖可容许区间（下限50→55 mm）可直接消除该失效族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前超声生物测量必须输出头围，无法支撑时必须拒答。选择性预测（selective prediction）可在覆盖率与错误率间权衡，但其有限样本保证只界定"错误率"，不界定"错误幅度"。作者希望检验：一个满足风险证书的流水线是否仍可能输出几何上完整、却在数值上严重错误的测量，并给出比"提高评分阈值"更可靠的防护手段。

Method: 在HC18数据集999张标注图像的两个独立预注册四路划分上进行审计，每个划分重新训练分割器3次。用精确Clopper-Pearson风险控制校准轮廓完整度阈值（α=0.10），并与人工设定下限对比；分析可接受样本的完整度评分与真实数值误差的关系；随后约束"可容许区域"（测量量在解剖学上有定义的取值范围），比较收紧下界与通过完整度阈值抑制两种策略在覆盖率与最坏误差上的代价。

Result: 校准阈值产生的风险证书与人工设定下限几乎无法区分，但流水线仍输出271.3 mm误差（真实HC为324.6 mm，被接受的小环呈几何完整、完整度评分0.94）；约150个答案中仅1个离群点，使α=0.10的认证风险界依然成立。第二个未触碰的划分在不同图像上复现同一机制（233.3 mm）。将可容许下界从50 mm提高到55 mm仅损失0.4个覆盖率点，却把两个划分的最坏误差分别压到11.4 mm与11.1 mm；而用完整度阈值抑制同样问题则损失19.0与12.1个覆盖点。在解剖学定义的70 mm处，冻结测试的MAE为1.91 mm与1.81 mm。

Conclusion: 有限样本风险证书只控制错误比例，不能防止单个极端量级的错误；基于图像质量的评分对"几何完整但尺度错误"的失效本质不可见。以解剖学可定义的测量量取值范围作为硬约束，是消除该类失效族、且覆盖率代价极小的高效手段，应作为选择性预测之外的独立防线。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound biometry must return a head circumference (HC) and refuse when the image cannot support one. Selective prediction trades coverage against error, but its finite-sample guarantees bound the rate of gross errors, not their magnitude. We audit a tool-grounded biometry pipeline on two separately pre-registered four-way splits of the 999 labelled HC18 images, retraining the segmenter three times on each. Calibrating the contour-completeness threshold by exact Clopper-Pearson risk control returns a certificate indistinguishable from a hand-set floor, yet the pipeline still emits a 271.3 mm error: a small, geometrically complete ring fitted to an image whose true HC is 324.6 mm. The accepted candidate scores 0.94 completeness, so the score cannot see the failure, and one outlier among $\approx 150$ answers leaves the certified risk bound ($\alpha = 0.10$) intact. A second, untouched split reproduces the mechanism on a different image (233.3 mm). Constraining the admissibility region---the anatomical range in which the measurand is defined---removes the family instead: raising the lower bound from 50 to 55 mm costs 0.4 coverage points and cuts the worst-case error on both splits to 11.4 and 11.1 mm, whereas suppressing it through the completeness threshold costs 19.0 and 12.1 points. At an anatomically defined 70 mm the frozen tests give 1.91 and 1.81 mm MAE.

</details>


### [267] [A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Personalized Reasoning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06259)
*Kim, Dohyun,Lhim, Junyong,Park, Kwanyong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MPRBench 基准，含 12 个子任务，评测多模态大模型在多模态个性化推理上的能力，并支持代表性错误案例的可解释分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型（MLLM）在异构个人信息上的整体化个性化推理能力尚未被充分探索，缺乏系统评测手段。

Method: 构建 MPRBench 基准，覆盖 12 个个性化推理子任务与真实场景挑战，并设计可解释的错误案例分析机制；对现有模型做广泛实验。

Result: 现有模型个性化推理表现不佳，主要瓶颈为个性化概念识别不准确、对无关个人信息敏感。

Conclusion: MPRBench 定位为基准/评测工作，用于推动 MLLM 个性化推理研究，未提出新的推理方法或物理世界建模方法。

Abstract: People often make decisions by holistically reasoning over heterogeneous forms of personal information. In this paper, we study the relatively underexplored capability of multimodal personalized reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To this end, we introduce \texttt{MPRBench}, a novel and comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to evaluate personalized reasoning across a wide range of tasks and real-world challenges. \texttt{MPRBench} consists of 12 sub-tasks and additionally supports interpretable analysis of representative error cases. Through extensive experiments, we find that current personalized MLLMs still struggle with personalized reasoning due to several key challenges, including inaccurate recognition of personalized concepts and sensitivity to irrelevant personal information. We hope that MPRBench will stimulate further research on personalized reasoning in MLLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kkdh15/MPRBench

</details>


### [268] [Interactive World Simulation with Cosmos for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06627)
*NVIDIA,:,Ahmad, Anis,Allen, Jon,Anwar, Malik Aqeel,Archer, Jesse,Bala, Maciej,Barejko, Piotr,Basant, Aarti,Blick, Dan,Bolzhelarskyi, Taras,Che, Henry,Chen, Jin,Chen, Yukang,Choe, Tae Eun,Dong, Yuzhu,Duca, Nat,Feng, Liang,Ferroni, Francesco,Fu, Xiao,Gambhir, Mohit,Goel, Vivek,Goldenthal, Rony,Gupta, Aryaman,Hassani, Ali,Hayes-Roth, Nathan,He, Yong,Honke, Michael,Huang, Shengyu,Huang, Wei,Hutchins, DeLesley,Indupuru, Suneel,Jannaty, Pooya,Jin, Jingyi,Kalra, Janmesh,Khetan, Ashna,Kong, Xin,Korovko, Aleksandr,Kuang, Hui,Kuang, Zhengfei,Kurian, George,Lafleche, Jean-Francois,Lee, Jayjun,Leung, Justin,Lew, William,Li, Xiang,Li, Zhaoshuo,Lin, Chen-Hsuan,Liu, Ming-Yu,Lu, Xiangyu,Lu, Yuhang,Ma, Martin Ding,Ma, Qianli,Mahajan, Aditya,Martin, Miguel,Matai, Janarbek,McCaffrey, Jonathan,Miao, Qing,Mirzaei, Ashkan,Munir, Mustafa,Oh, Sangmin,Pankratz, David,Pham, Trung,Reeb, Marilyn,State, Gavriel,Tang, Jiaxiang,Tang, Shitao,Tang, Yue,Tavakkoli, Tolou,Temirov, Alexander,Tsou, Hweimi,Vasilev, Sergei,Volk, Thomas,Wang, Andrew Z.,Wang, Hao,Wang, Shijie,Wang, Zhonghao,Wehr, David,Xin, Shumian,Xu, Mengyao,Xu, Yao,Yang, Shuai,Zalewski, Sophia,Zeren, Pengcuo,Zha, Cindy,Zhang, Jing,Zhautouskaya, Yuliya,Zheng, Kaiwen,Zhu, Andrew,Zhuang, Rick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NVIDIA推出统一框架，将Cosmos世界基础模型适配为Physical AI的实时交互式世界仿真器，支持动作条件的视觉后果预测与外接物理状态驱动的观测生成，并演示相机导航、机器人操作、可控视频迁移和多视角自动驾驶仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI训练与评测需要可交互、可演化的虚拟环境，但现有世界模型多为离线视频生成，难以对动作与场景控制做出实时响应，也缺乏与外部物理仿真器状态对接的统一接口。

Method: 以共享框架形式改造Cosmos世界基础模型：将视觉观测以流式方式对动作/场景控制做闭环响应，同时提供两条通路——直接预测动作的视觉后果，以及由外部仿真器维护的物理状态条件生成观测。在相机导航、机器人操作、可控视频迁移与多视角AV仿真上验证。

Result: 摘要为系统级演示性描述，未给出量化指标；主要成果是多场景统一的可交互仿真能力（导航、操作、视频迁移、多视角AV）以及开源代码与模型权重（GitHub/HuggingFace）。

Conclusion: 该工作为Physical AI提供了可复用的交互式世界仿真基础，兼具动作条件预测与仿真器耦合能力，并完全开源；但摘要未披露实时性数字、保真度评测与真机闭环证据，需查阅正文与项目页确认。

Abstract: We present a shared framework that adapts Cosmos world foundation models for real-time interactive world simulation in Physical AI. By streaming visual observations in response to actions and scene controls, the framework enables users and embodied agents to interact with evolving virtual environments. It supports both predicting the visual consequences of actions and generating observations from physical state maintained by an external simulator. We demonstrate this approach across camera navigation and robot manipulation, controllable video transfer, and multi-view autonomous vehicle (AV) simulation. These examples provide a shared foundation for developing interactive virtual environments for Physical AI training and evaluation. Our code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/nvidia/cosmos and https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/cosmos3-examples. The project website is available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/cosmos-simulation.

</details>


### [269] [Generate-then-Rank: Learning Symbolic Goal Interpretation for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07825)
*Jisen Lei,Huizhi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段"先生成后排序"的目标解释方法：先SFT让LLM适配EAI输出约定，再用CORE从模型自身生成的候选中学习偏好排序，在EAI家庭场景上超越prompting、CoT与知识图谱基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身智能体中，目标解释（自然语言指令→形式化符号目标）的错误会逐级传播到规划与执行，因此既需要生成合适候选，也需要可靠地在候选中选择。

Method: 两阶段流程：(1) 监督微调使LLM对齐Embodied Agent Interface的输出约定；(2) Candidate-Output Ranking from Errors (CORE)，基于模型自身生成的备选输出构建偏好学习以对完整输出排序。在EAI household场景上评测DeepSeek-V3 chat、DeepSeek-Reasoner、Qwen2.5-7B/72B等开源LLM。

Result: 所提方法优于简单prompting、chain-of-thought及知识图谱方法等基线模型。

Conclusion: 生成-排序范式可有效提升LLM在具身目标解释中的符号化转换准确性与选择可靠性。

Abstract: Goal interpretation is to translate a natural language instruction into a formal symbolic goal that a planner can execute. It is critical for embodied agents because any error here propagates to every downstream planning and action step. Doing it well requires both generating suitable candidates and reliably selecting among them. We present a two-stage generate-then-rank approach to Goal Interpretation in the Embodied Agent Interface (EAI). First, supervised fine-tuning adapts a Large Language Model (LLM) to EAI's output convention. Second, Candidate-Output Ranking from Errors (CORE) learns preferences over complete outputs from the model's own generated alternatives. We conducted experiments on the household scenes of the Embodied Agent Interface Benchmark. Multiple open source LLMs including DeepSeek-V3 chat, DeepSeek-Reasoner, and Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen2.5-72B were evaluated. The proposed method outperformed the baseline models including LLMs with simple prompting, chain-of-thought, and knowledge graph based approaches.

</details>


### [270] [GuidedWAM: Training-Free Energy Guidance for World Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07912)
*Xiyan Su,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Hojun Lim,Frank Diermeyer,Johannes Betz,Markus Lienkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GuidedWAM：用能量引导在采样阶段把新驾驶目标注入冻结的世界动作模型（WAM），无需重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 将驾驶策略与视觉世界模型耦合于单一生成网络，训练后目标固定，添加新驾驶目标必须重训，代价高且不灵活。

Method: 提出模型无关框架 GuidedWAM：在采样器中间"干净动作估计"上构造可微能量函数作为目标，仅需对冻结模型的前向访问；辅以采样期引导调度、逐能量归一化与速度有界修正来稳定采样。

Result: NAVSIM v2 hard-split 上，引导使三个公开冻结 WAM 平均提升 1.8–3.6 EPDMS（三次采样种子平均），每步采样时间增加 <10%。

Conclusion: 能量引导可作为 WAM 训练后的实用控制通道，实现目标可插拔，为世界模型的可控生成提供通用范式。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) for autonomous driving couple the driving policy and the visual world model in a single generative network whose objectives are fixed post-training. Conditioning on a new driving objective requires retraining. We study energy guidance as a general mechanism and present GuidedWAM, a model-agnostic framework for injecting driving objectives into frozen WAMs at sampling time. Our framework constructs each objective as differentiable energies on the sampler's intermediate clean-action estimate, which needs only forward access to the frozen model, and is stabilized by sampling-time guidance scheduling, per-energy normalization, and velocity-bounded correction. On the NAVSIM v2 hard-split, guidance improves all three frozen, publicly released WAMs by 1.8 to 3.6 EPDMS, averaged over three sampler seeds, and adds less than 10% to the sampling time per step. These results position energy guidance as a practical post-training control channel for WAMs.

</details>


### [271] [The Geometry of Simulated Perspectives: Dissociating Representation and Behaviour in a Multimodal Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08262)
*Mathis Immertreu,Fitim Abdullahu,Thomas Kinfe,Claus Metzner,Helmut Grabner,Patrick Krauss,Achim Schilling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究 persona（观察者视角）条件化下视觉有趣性判断的表征几何：在 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 上用100张图像×53种提示条件，发现早期层编码图像身份、后期层涌现persona结构；提示框定对内部表征影响强于persona内容，而persona内容对行为评分影响更大，存在表征—行为解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型可以"模拟不同观察者"，但被赋予的视角如何重塑其内部表征、这些表征变化是否支配其判断尚不清楚。

Method: 对 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 做系统性探针实验：100张图像 × 53种提示条件，正交变化 persona 内容与引入该 persona 的语言框定，同步采集行为评分与逐层隐状态，分析表征几何（表征距离）随深度的重组。
