Result: 表征几何随处理深度系统性重组：早期层主要编码图像身份，persona 特异的表征结构出现在较深层。提示框定对内部表征的形塑强于 persona 身份本身，而 persona 内容对行为判断影响更大；极简的身份陈述即可改变表征却不改变回答，说明表征距离未必反映行为相关性。

Conclusion: 模拟视角在人工认知系统的表征空间中表现为可分离结构，但内部表征几何与其功能/行为后果之间存在根本性区分，prompt-induced 表征漂移不能直接等同于行为效应。

Abstract: Multimodal language models can simulate different observers, yet it remains unclear how assigned perspectives reshape their internal representations and whether these changes govern their judgements. Here, we investigate the representational geometry of persona-conditioned visual interestingness in Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct. Across 100 images and 53 prompting conditions, we systematically vary persona content and the linguistic framing used to introduce it, while recording behavioural ratings and layer-wise hidden states. We observe a systematic reorganisation of representational geometry across processing depth: early layers primarily encode image identity, whereas persona-specific structures emerge in later layers. Strikingly, prompt framing reshapes internal representations more strongly than persona identity, whereas persona content has a greater impact on behavioural judgements. This dissociation is further illustrated by minimal identity statements that alter representations even without changing responses. These findings demonstrate that representational distance does not necessarily reflect behavioural relevance. More broadly, they reveal how simulated perspectives emerge as distinct structures within the representational space of artificial cognitive systems, highlighting a fundamental distinction between the geometry of internal representations and their functional consequences.

</details>


### [272] [Learning the Task or Learning the Verifier? Rethinking Criteria Exposure in Embodied Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08318)
*Wenbo Huang,Jianxin Bi,Shunyu Yang,Ruiyang Si,Rui Ni,Qiang Wang,Shulong Jiang,Yintianrun Zhang,Feixiang Ruan,Kewei Zhang,Ye Huang,Shukai Gong,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出机器人仿真基准中普遍存在"标准泄露"(Criteria Exposure, CE)——将基准的成功判定条件直接暴露给学习智能体。控制实验显示，有CE时编码智能体虽能快速提高判定成功率，但标准稍作微调（不违背原始指令）后优势即消失，且习得技能跨任务迁移差；提出"指令忠诚标准"可更真实反映智能体生成程序的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身编码智能体在仿真基准上报告的成绩不断攀升，但许多工作的验证器标准原本用于筛选模仿学习策略，其要求往往严于语言指令本身。若把这类标准暴露给智能体，分数可能不再忠实反映"指令任务是否完成"，存在指标被过拟合的风险，需要受控实验加以检验。

Method: 在自改进具身智能体上做受控对照实验：设置CE与非CE两组条件；测量给定标准下的成功率；再对标准做不偏离原始指令的轻微改动（如度量细节、阈值、判定条件变化）复测；并评估跨任务技能迁移。此外设计"指令忠诚标准"（instruction-faithful criteria），与原始标准对比验证其与真实任务完成的吻合度。

Result: CE条件下智能体迅速产出在给定标准下成功率更高的代码，成功率优势显著；但标准轻微变更后该优势消失，说明提升主要来自对验证标准的过拟合而非对指令的理解。同时CE下发展的技能跨任务迁移表现差。指令忠诚标准能更好地区分真实完成与投机对齐，更忠实反映智能体程序性能。

Conclusion: 基准标准不应直接暴露给编码智能体，否则会诱导指标刷分与技能脆弱化；评测应以指令忠诚的判据为准，并配合标准扰动与迁移性测试来衡量具身智能体的真实能力。

Abstract: Self-improving embodied agents are increasingly being developed and compared on robot simulation benchmarks. Several recent works expose the benchmark's success criteria to the learning agent, a practice we call Criteria Exposure (CE). These criteria, originally written to verify policies trained by imitation learning, often demand more than the corresponding language instructions, so a score from such a verifier may not faithfully reflect whether the instructed task was completed, especially for robot-control programs written by an agent. As embodied coding agents report ever higher benchmark scores, a natural question arises: when a coding agent is exposed to the benchmark's criteria, does it generate code that better performs the instructed task, or code that simply overfits to the verification criteria? We conduct controlled experiments with a self-improving agent with and without CE, and find that with CE the agent quickly produces code with a higher success rate under the given criteria, but this advantage disappears when the criteria are changed slightly without departing from the original instructions. More importantly, the skills the agent develops under CE transfer poorly across tasks. We further propose instruction-faithful criteria and show that they better reflect the performance of agent-generated programs.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [A Theoretical Analysis of a Bilevel Optimization Formulation for Combined Local-Global Optical Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08359)
*El Mostafa Kalmoun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文给出CLG光流正则化参数学习的双层优化形式（下层LK数据项+二次/TV正则，上层最小化与真值的平方距离），在有限维、满秩假设下分析最优参数的存在性、正性与严格局部最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLG光流模型中正则化参数通常靠经验或网格搜索设定，缺乏理论保证；作者希望用双层优化自动学习该参数，并从数学上刻画解的存在性与性质。

Method: 建立有限维双层问题：下层由局部结构张量构建的Lucas–Kanade数据项加二次或TV正则，满秩假设保证下层解唯一；上层最小化与ground-truth光流的平方距离。对二次模型推导最优参数存在性、正性与严格局部最优的充分条件；对TV模型利用大参数时下层解趋于零证明最优参数总存在，并给出考虑两分量经结构张量耦合的正性充分条件。合成实验验证条件及其失效情形。

Result: 二次模型：给出存在性、正性、严格局部最优性的充分条件；TV模型：证明最优参数总是存在（下层解在大参数下消失），并给出涉及两分量耦合的正性条件；合成算例展示条件成立与失效的情况。

Conclusion: 该工作为CLG光流的超参数学习提供了严格的存在性与正性理论刻画，属于纯理论分析，未涉及真实数据或学习算法可扩展性验证，对物理AI/3D重建主线价值有限。

Abstract: This paper analyzes a bilevel formulation for learning the regularization parameter of combined local-global (CLG) optical flow models. The lower-level problem computes the flow from a Lucas--Kanade data term and a quadratic or total variation (TV) regularizer, and the upper-level problem selects the parameter that minimizes the squared distance to a ground-truth flow. The setting is finite-dimensional, and a full-rank assumption makes the lower-level solution unique. For the quadratic model we derive sufficient conditions for the existence of an optimal parameter, for every optimal parameter to be positive, and for strict local optimality. For TV regularization an optimal parameter always exists, because the lower-level solution vanishes for large parameters, and we give a sufficient condition for positivity that accounts for the coupling of the two flow components through the local structure tensors. Synthetic examples illustrate the conditions, including cases in which they fail.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [274] [Category differences in similarity between fixation density maps and psychological potential fields for sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06930)
*Naoyuki Awano,Yuki Hayashi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文比较了注视密度图（FDM）与心理势场（PPF）在动物、非动物与无意义形状三类刺激下的相似性（AUC），并提出基线校正AUC与个体差异度量；结果显示基线校正AUC可显著区分三类且效应量大，旋转呈现未产生类别特异性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有少量基于简笔草图的研究发现FDM与PPF的相似度随物体类别变化，但尚不清楚这种相似度在更广类别（动物/非动物/无意义形状）以及高认知负荷条件（旋转呈现）下的表现，也难以将类别相关变异与刺激本身带来的基线差异分离。

Method: 以草图刺激做自由观看眼动实验，计算注视密度图FDM与心理势场PPF的逐时相似度；采用ROC曲线下面积AUC量化相似度，并提出"基线校正AUC"剥离刺激依赖的基线水平；引入变异度分数刻画个体差异；用线性混合模型检验类别与朝向（旋转）主效应及交互作用。

Result: 基线校正AUC在三类别间均呈显著差异且效应量大；原始AUC因草图表征影响无法区分动物与非动物；类别×朝向交互不显著，说明旋转未带来类别特异效应；无意义形状类在相似度与变异度上持续偏低，与两类有意义物体清晰区分。

Conclusion: FDM–PPF相似度在自由观看条件下呈现稳定的类别依赖性，且必须进行基线校正才能把类别相关变化与刺激依赖的基线水平分开；该框架为视觉认知计算建模提供可量化工具。

Abstract: When an object comes into view, humans can instantaneously recognize it if known to them. However, assessing how this recognition takes place remains challenging. A few studies using simple sketches have reported varying degrees of image similarity between the fixation density map (FDM), which visualizes eye movements and durations, and the psychological potential field (PPF), which visualizes part of the visual cognition process, depending on the object category. This study reports how the similarity behaves across three categories---animal, non-animal, and meaningless shape---and under rotated presentation, which is known to increase cognitive load during object recognition. The similarity was quantified through area under the curve (AUC) measures, including a baseline-adjusted AUC that isolates the early rise in similarity from stimulus-dependent baseline levels. A variability score was introduced to assess individual differences, and linear mixed models were used to evaluate category and orientation effects. Results indicated that the baseline-adjusted AUC yielded significant differences among all three categories with large effect sizes, whereas the raw AUC did not distinguish between the animal and non-animal categories due to the influence of sketch representation. No significant Category $\times$ Orientation interaction was observed, indicating that rotated presentation did not produce a category-specific effect. The meaningless shape stimuli yielded consistently low similarity and variability scores, clearly separating them from the two meaningful object categories. These results characterize the behavior of the FDM--PPF similarity under free viewing and indicate that baseline adjustment is necessary to separate category-related variation from stimulus-dependent baseline levels.

</details>


### [275] [An Evolutionary Hypothesis for White's Illusion: Constant Percentage Correction and Modern Individual Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06935)
*Bekir Tevfik Akgün,Ergun Akleman*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 论文用常数百分比亮度校正的前向模型复现White错觉，并提出群体均值稳定源于进化保守机制、个体差异源于学习适应的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者先前发现：为达到"恒定感知"，不同个体选择略有差异的校正百分比，但群体平均值在多次实验中异常稳定。本文试图为这一人群层面的规律性及个体差异提供计算与进化层面的解释。

Method: 修改此前提出的前向半透明性模型，对背景亮度施加常数百分比校正，检验其能否复现White错觉。

Result: 常数百分比校正可复现White错觉，为观察到的群体层面规律性提供一个简洁的计算解释；个体差异解释为围绕共享计算响应在学习/适应上的变异，尚属可检验假说。

Conclusion: 该模型给出White错觉的紧凑计算表述，并提出关于情境亮度判断个体差异的可检验进化解释，但缺乏直接的实验或神经证据。

Abstract: White's illusion is a striking demonstration of context-dependent brightness and lightness perception. In earlier work, we developed a forward translucency-based formula capable of producing approximately constant perceived appearance across varying backgrounds. While validating that formulation, we observed a notable population-level pattern: individuals selected slightly different correction percentages for "constant perception," while the population average remained remarkably stable across experiments. In this paper, we propose an evolutionary hypothesis for this phenomenon. We suggest that a simple constant-percentage correction function could have emerged as an adaptive mechanism for maintaining useful material and reflectance judgments under variable illumination. Under this interpretation, the stability of the population mean may reflect a conserved perceptual mechanism, whereas individual differences may reflect learning, adaptation, or variation around a shared computational response. Using a modification of our previously published forward model, we show that White's illusion can be reproduced by applying a constant-percentage correction to background luminance. The model therefore provides a compact computational account of the observed population-level regularity and motivates a testable evolutionary interpretation of individual variation in contextual lightness judgments.

</details>


### [276] [Reversible Dynamics, Asymmetric Actions: Parking as a Trajectory-Level Affordance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07965)
*Adam Y. Shavit*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该文通过三层“间隙”指标（静态适配F、无限修正可及A∞、K次换向预算可及A_K）严格刻画倒车入库与正向入库的非对称性，并给出单场景内F=A∞=0.5 m但有限K下差异显著的可证明结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “倒车入位优于车头先入”是常见驾驶建议，但低速车辆动力学可逆，路径可反演，表面上二者应等价。作者指出其悖论根源在于任务终点朝向不同（起点相同、终点朝向相反），真实差异并不在可达路径本身，而在有限修正预算下的可及性。

Method: 形式化定义三个间隙量：F（最优可接受位姿处与障碍的最小间隙）、A∞（无限修正下全程可保持的间隙）、A_K（限K次前进/后退换向的间隙），满足F≥A∞≥A_K。在完全给定的单场景（车、车位、通道、起点）中，用可认证（certified）可达性论证给出上界与显式路径下界；对两种任务分别计算K=1、2、3时的间隙界，并分析起点位置依赖（第4.15节）。另提出行为实验，考察人类是否区分fit、eventual access与budgeted access，并与“车身扫掠”等简单线索及学习到的机动标签对比。

Result: 该场景中两任务均得A∞=F=0.5000 m，故可证明的进退非对称全部来自有限K：单次换向下倒车入库有显式路径≥0.4410 m，而正向入库被认证上界限制在d0<0.1720002 m；两次换向下正向入库可达≥0.2833 m。因此对任意d∈[d0, 0.2833) m，均先前进时倒车需恰好1次换向、正向需恰好2次；均先后退时则为2次与3次。

Conclusion: 进退非对称性源于资源受限下的换向次数差异，而非最终可保持间隙；起点位置会影响该对比。论文进一步提出可检验的人类感知实验假设，将“预算化可及性”作为理解机动规划与人机判断的量化框架。

Abstract: Drivers are often told to back into a tight parking space, not drive in nose first. The advice looks paradoxical, because a slow car is dynamically reversible: reverse its speed and replay the steering backward, and it retraces a feasible path. Played backward, though, reverse-in shows a car driving forward out of the space. Reverse-in and forward-in are different tasks: they share a start position but end facing opposite ways. We separate three clearances between car and obstacles. Static fit $F$ is the clearance the parked car keeps at its best acceptable destination pose. Eventual access $A_\infty$ is the clearance it can keep all the way there with unlimited corrections. Resource-bounded access $A_K$ is the clearance that survives a budget of $K$ reversals, switches between forward and reverse. They satisfy $F\ge A_\infty\ge A_K$. In one fully specified scene (one car, bay, aisle and start), a certified argument proves $A_\infty=F=0.5000$ m for both tasks. In this scene, all certified forward/reverse asymmetry therefore arises at finite $K$: it lies in how many corrections each task needs, not in the clearance either can eventually keep. With one reversal, an explicit path gives reverse-in at least $0.4410$ m clearance. A certified reachability argument caps forward-in's one-reversal clearance at $d_0<0.1720002$ m. With two reversals, forward-in gets at least $0.2833$ m. To keep a clearance strictly above any $d$ with $d_0\le d<0.2833$ m, reverse-in thus needs exactly one reversal and forward-in exactly two when both tasks move forward first. If both move in reverse first, the counts are two and three. The contrast depends on the start position (Section 4.15). Proposed experiments test whether observers distinguish fit, eventual access and budgeted access. They compare these with simpler cues, such as how the turning body sweeps, and learned manoeuvre labels.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [277] [AgentCount: An Agentic Workflow for Traceable Colony Counting and Insight Generation from Agar Plate Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05398)
*Liu, Zhuosheng,Weber, Austin E.*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: AgentCount 是一个开源的 agentic 工作流，用七个 agent 从琼脂平板照片完成菌落计数与结果解读，基于 SAM 3 多文本提示分割加形态学过滤，并保留全部中间产物以供追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统菌落计数耗时、依赖人工操作者，且最终计数缺乏可审计的记录，无法追溯哪些对象被计入。作者希望用可复现、可审查的自动化流程替代人工计数。

Method: 七 agent 流水线：图像质量控制、平板定位、菌落检测、计数、CFU 计算、验证、报告。检测使用 SAM 3 配合多个文本提示与形态学过滤；随后计算重复样本统计量，由 LLM agent 生成结果叙述；质量分数、分割掩膜、检测框、警告等中间输出全部保留。流程还可扩展到对存档微生物种群数据的技能引导分析。

Result: 与两项已发表研究回溯对比，基准测试 241 个端点（含肉类类似物病原体表中全部 216 个 mean±SD 值）；在 168 个具备完整 n=6 重复来源的端点上，164 个（97.6%）在 0.01 log10 以内一致。

Conclusion: AgentCount 表明 agentic 工作流在应用微生物学中具有潜力，尤其是可追溯、可审查的自动化分析范式；但验证主要依赖与历史文献的一致性，缺少独立金标准或前瞻实验。

Abstract: Colony counting is commonly used to evaluate microbial populations in food safety testing and microbiological studies. Despite its wide application, manual counting is time-consuming and operator-dependent, and the final count often lacks a record of the objects included in the measurement. To address these limitations, this study presents AgentCount, an open-source agentic workflow for colony counting and result interpretation from agar plate images. Plate photographs and plating metadata are processed through seven agents for image quality control, plate localisation, colony detection, counting, CFU calculation, validation, and reporting. Colony detection is performed using Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) with multiple text prompts and morphology-based filtering. After that, replicate statistics are calculated and a large language model (LLM)-based agent is used to generate a results narrative. Intermediate outputs, including quality scores, segmentation masks, detections, and warnings, are retained to support review of the reported values. The workflow also extends to skill-guided analysis of archived microbial population data. In a retrospective comparison with two previously published studies, we benchmarked 241 published endpoints, including all 216 mean +- SD values of the meat-analogue pathogen tables; on the 168 endpoints with a complete n=6 replicate source, 164 (97.6%) agreed within 0.01 log10 units. Overall, AgentCount demonstrates the promising potential of agentic workflows for applications in applied microbiology.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [Jev with Foresight: Safe Language-Guided Autonomous Driving via a Language World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05448)
*Qu, Yansong,Chen, Jiancong,Labi, Samuel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Jev-AD 将"类型化概率决策模型 Jev"与"语言世界模型（预测候选动作下周围车辆未来 2 秒运动）"结合，用代码把预测转为可预见后果供 Jev 比较，从而替代在生成文本中隐式推理。仿真汇入 500 车次场景碰撞率 26.0%→2.4%（0.25s/决策），自由指令达成率 93%；NGSIM 真实数据回放 500 场景碰撞率 48.2%→2.8%，世界模型预警 93% 危险动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驾驶智能体依赖生成文本推理，耗时为秒级，且只能从场景隐式推断动作后果，缺少显式的动作-未来交互预测，导致决策慢且不安全。

Method: 提出 Jev-AD：Jev 为类型化决策模型，对预定义问题输出概率而非生成文本；配套语言世界模型对每个候选动作预测邻近车辆未来 2 秒轨迹；代码层将预测转译为"预见的后果"，由 Jev 直接比较而非从坐标反推。在仿真高速汇入与真实 NGSIM 回放中评估。

Result: 仿真 500 车次：碰撞率 26.0%→2.4%，单决策 0.25s，自由指令意图达成 93%；NGSIM 500 回放：碰撞率 48.2%→2.8%，从该交通数据习得的世界模型预见 93% 的危险动作。

Conclusion: 显式前向预测（世界模型+概率化决策）显著优于文本内隐式推理，兼顾实时性与安全性，但验证限于高速汇入场景，缺少实车闭环验证。

Abstract: Large language models understand passengers' driving commands, but as driving agents they reason in generated text for seconds and must infer each action's consequences implicitly from the scene. We present Jev-AD, which pairs Jev, a typed decision model that answers predefined questions with probabilities, not generated text, with a language world model that predicts for every candidate action how nearby vehicles move over the next two seconds. Code turns these predictions into foreseen consequences, which Jev compares instead of inferring them from coordinates. In simulated highway merging (500 car-episodes), this foresight lowers the same decision model's collision rate from 26.0% to 2.4% at 0.25 seconds per decision, and Jev-AD achieves the passenger's intent in 93% of free-form command trials. In 500 replayed real-world NGSIM episodes, foresight lowers the collision rate from 48.2% to 2.8%, and the world model, learned from this traffic, foresees 93% of dangerous actions. Project page: https://ys-qu.github.io/jev-ad-website.

</details>


### [279] [Ctrl-F: Force as an Interface for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05507)
*Zhao, Mengjie,Chen, Yuxin,Shao, Yiyang,Hong, Chuye,Chen, Jiakang,Sreenath, Koushil,Tomizuka, Masayoshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Ctrl-F提出分层“运动-力”框架：以显式三维接触力指令（手/足等指定接触点）扩展人形运动跟踪，统一控制器在跟踪参考运动的同时施力，高层力规划器基于物体状态与本体感知在线生成力指令，实现搬运、拖动、推、踢等强持续力交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有运动跟踪策略只解决自由空间运动（知道身体该在哪），不知如何与环境交互；而把物体纳入目标的工作多把交互抽象为接触或几何约束，缺少对“施多大力”的刻画，导致大而持续交互力的困难场景（重物推/踢、拖动、负重变化）研究不足。

Method: 分层架构：(1) 统一motion-force控制器，以每个指定接触身体部位（手/足）的三维力为显式指令，在跟踪参考运动的同时对世界施加该力；(2) 高层力规划器，根据物体状态与本体感知在线输出力指令。仿真与真机验证，含任务中途加载的在线适应。

Result: 仿真与硬件上完成需要大且持续交互力的loco-manipulation；手部拖动、推动、举升与足部踢击均可执行，推/踢任务中成功操作重达20kg箱体，而无力感知基线失败；任务中额外负载引入后能在线调整力指令。

Conclusion: 力是衔接运动模仿与物体交互的自然指令通道——不仅规定何处接触，还规定多大力；该显式力接口使不具备力感知的跟踪器难以覆盖的重载交互任务变得可解，并具备在线负载适应能力。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking has scaled to general policies that reproduce diverse human motion, but the resulting behavior is in free-space: the tracker knows where the body should be, not how it should interact with the environment. Other work makes the object part of the objective, yet abstracts that interaction to contact or geometry, which leaves the demanding cases under-explored. We argue that force is a natural command channel between motion imitation and object interaction, one that specifies not just where to make contact but how hard. We present Ctrl-F, a hierarchical motion-force framework that augments motion tracking with an explicit force command, a 3-D force at each designated contact body such as a hand or a foot. The hierarchical framework comprises: (1) A unified motion-force controller tracks the reference motion while applying the commanded force to the world. (2) A high-level force planner that issues a force command online based on object state and proprioception observations. In simulation and on hardware, Ctrl-F completes loco-manipulation that demands large, sustained interaction force and adjusts its command online as the load changes, both out of reach of a force-blind tracker. On hardware, Ctrl-F demonstrates hand-based dragging, pushing, and lifting, as well as foot-based kicking, successfully manipulating boxes weighing up to 20kg in pushing and kicking tasks where the force-blind baseline fails, while adapting online to additional loads introduced mid-task.

</details>


### [280] [MESA: An Evaluation Framework for Compositional, Semantic, and Spatial Generalization in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05567)
*Wilcox, Albert,Chang, Frank,Nguyen, Nhi,Chakraborty, Aishani,Collins, Jeremy A.,Saxena, Vaibhav,Joffe, Benjamin,Karamcheti, Siddharth,Garg, Animesh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MESA是一个面向语言条件机器人操作策略的评测框架，含五个评测套件，分别隔离分布内性能与空间、物体实例、物体类别、子任务组合四个泛化维度，并提供基于改进MimicGen的MESA-Gen数据生成流水线，发布单臂MESA与首个双臂大规模基准BiMESA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作基础模型基准存在两极分化：或范围狭窄且已饱和，对SOTA方法缺乏区分信号；或范围宽泛但缺乏受控实验；或依赖大规模脏乱开源数据训练。缺少可精确、可复现地度量语言条件策略泛化能力的手段。

Method: 构建五套互相隔离的评测套件，分别考察分布内性能与四个泛化轴（空间构型、物体实例、物体类别、子任务组合）；提出MESA-Gen（改进MimicGen的扩展任务与示范生成流水线）以规模化填充套件；在两个平台上实例化并开源单臂MESA与双臂BiMESA；评测7种基线，覆盖扩散策略、VLM方法、预训练VLA。

Result: 最强模型在MESA上仅52%、在BiMESA上仅49%；MESA排名与RoboArena评分总体一致，表明其捕捉到超越仿真的方法级差异。

Conclusion: MESA提供了诊断"何处、为何失败"而非仅"是否失败"的细粒度泛化评测，具有区分度且留有充足提升空间；BiMESA填补了双臂泛化基准的空白，为VLA与语言条件策略的可复现比较建立基线。

Abstract: Despite growing interest in foundation models for robotic manipulation, systematic evaluation of their capabilities remains elusive. Existing benchmarks are either narrow and saturated, offering little signal on SOTA methods, broadly scoped, providing diversity at the cost of controlled experimentation, or require training on large, messy open-source datasets. We introduce MESA, an evaluation framework designed for precise, reproducible measurement of language-conditioned policy generalization. MESA provides five evaluation suites that isolate in-distribution performance and four distinct axes of generalization: spatial configuration, object instance, object category, and subtask composition, enabling researchers to diagnose where and why policies fail, not just whether they fail. To populate these suites at scale, we develop MESA-Gen, a pipeline for task and demonstration generation built by improving MimicGen. We instantiate the framework on two platforms and release both: the single-arm MESA suite, and BiMESA, the first large-scale benchmark for bimanual generalization. We evaluate seven baseline methods spanning diffusion policies, VLM-based approaches, and pretrained VLAs, finding that the strongest model achieves only 52% on MESA and 49% on BiMESA, providing meaningful signal while leaving ample room for progress. MESA rankings are broadly consistent with RoboArena scores, suggesting that MESA captures method-level differences relevant beyond simulation. Project website: https://pairlab.github.io/MESA.

</details>


### [281] [ELA: End-to-End Latent Action Policy Learning from Video Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05571)
*Hsu, Cheng-Chun,Stone, Peter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELA: 端到端联合优化潜在动作模型、潜在动作策略与动作解码器，从无动作标签视频中学习，实现少量真机示教即可部署的模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作标注数据稀缺而无动作演示（如视频）丰富；现有潜在动作方法仅从观测转移中学习动作相关表征，未针对下游控制优化潜在动作，导致策略难以建模、且难精确解码为连续机器人动作。

Method: 提出ELA端到端框架，联合训练三个模块：潜在动作模型（从无标签视频中提取潜在动作）、潜在动作策略（在潜在动作空间上训练）、动作解码器（将潜在动作映射为连续机器人动作）。三者共享训练目标、联合优化，使潜在空间同时对策略学习和动作解码友好；解码器仅约4M参数，作为轻量插件接入现有模仿学习方法，无需修改策略架构或训练目标。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界操作任务上，ELA一致优于现有潜在动作基线；配合少量动作标注机器人示教与大量视频数据即可取得更好性能；可与现有模仿学习方法无缝集成。

Conclusion: 将潜在动作的优化目标直接对齐下游策略学习与动作解码，是提升视频驱动模仿学习可用性的关键；轻量解码器设计使该框架具备良好兼容性与可移植性。

Abstract: Robot imitation learning is limited by the scarcity of action-labeled data, while action-free demonstrations such as videos are abundant. Recent latent-action methods learn action-relevant representations from observation transitions, but they do not optimize latent actions for downstream control, leading to latent representations that are difficult for policies to model and difficult to accurately decode into continuous robot actions. This paper introduces ELA, an end-to-end framework for learning from video demonstrations via latent actions jointly optimized for policy learning and action decoding. ELA jointly trains a latent action model, latent action policy, and action decoder, enabling policies to leverage abundant video data with only a handful of action-labeled robot demonstrations. Experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation tasks show that ELA consistently outperforms prior latent action baselines. Moreover, it integrates seamlessly with existing imitation learning methods through lightweight 4M-parameter action decoders, requiring no changes to policy architectures or training objectives. Additional materials can be found on: https://end2end-latent-action.github.io/

</details>


### [282] [Contact-Implicit Whole-Body Trajectory Optimization for Dynamic Humanoid Locomotion on Parameterizable Uneven Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05585)
*Mauceri, Roberto,Dafarra, Stefano,Romualdi, Giulio,Gabiccini, Marco,Pucci, Daniele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于接触隐式全身优化的仿人机器人轨迹规划器，用光滑曲面模型近似可参数化地形，并设计适用于任意光滑曲面的新接触约束，实现复杂非共面地形上的动态运动规划，并在 ErgoCub 真机上初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接触隐式轨迹优化多假设地面平坦水平，难以在台阶、斜坡等可参数化复杂地形上生成仿人机器人动态、无需预定接触序列的可行运动。

Method: 1) 建立光滑曲面数学模型近似地面几何（台阶、斜坡等）；2) 设计新的接触约束，将刚体-地面交互由平面/水平面推广至任意光滑曲面；3) 在接触隐式全身优化（NLP）框架下，将高层需求嵌入代价函数与初始猜测以生成动态轨迹。

Result: 在非共面地形上完成初步可行性测试，仿真与真机（ErgoCub）均验证了在不同地形拓扑上规划运动及动态轨迹生成的有效性。

Conclusion: 接触隐式全身优化配合光滑地形近似与推广的接触约束，可免预设接触序列地在复杂地形上规划仿人机器人动态运动；目前仅为初步可行性验证，尚缺定量指标与鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: This letter presents a trajectory planner for humanoid robots to perform dynamic motions on complex, parameterizable terrains using a contact-implicit whole-body optimization framework. The planner generates feasible movements without predefined contact sequences, considering the robot's full dynamics and kinematics. To plan bipedal locomotion on steps, ramps, and other terrains, we propose a mathematical model to approximate ground geometry with a smooth surface. The rigid interaction between the robot and the ground is modeled via novel contact constraints, extending the domain of applicability from flat and horizontal surfaces to any arbitrary smooth surface. Effectiveness is demonstrated using nonlinear programming to plan motions on various terrain topologies. We also explore dynamic trajectory generation by embedding high-level requirements in the cost function and the initial guess. Preliminary feasibility tests were conducted over non-coplanar terrains, both in simulation and on the real robot ErgoCub.

</details>


### [283] [$\mathcal{C}^\infty$ Smooth Trajectory Planning with Application to Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05615)
*Grassmann, Reinhard M.,Alkayas, Abdulaziz Y.,Chen, Lingyun,Haddadin, Sami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种 C^∞ 平滑轨迹生成器，在满足运动学约束前提下产生无穷可微轨迹，可统一覆盖从协作机器人到软体连续体机器人的各种平滑性需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续体机器人自由度多、含关节与连杆柔性导致系统阶次高，其平滑性要求未知且可能远高于已知的 C^4（协作机器人）、C^5（四旋翼）等标准；因此需要一个能泛化到任意平滑度需求的统一轨迹生成方法。

Method: 构造 C^∞ 平滑轨迹生成器：在定义的运动学极限（速度/加速度等边界）内，生成无穷次可微的路径，使速度、加速度、加加速度、snap、crackle、pop 等所有阶导数连续；特别处理零边界条件情形，即轨迹起止端所有导数均为零。

Result: 在仿真与硬件上对方法进行验证与评估，结果显示生成的轨迹满足约束且具备无穷阶导数连续性，可推广至任意平滑度等级的状态序列过渡。

Conclusion: C^∞ 轨迹生成可统一满足不同复杂度机器人的平滑性要求，为软体连续体机器人提供可证明的平滑性保障，并兼容现有高平滑度需求系统。

Abstract: High motion performance and avoiding structural vibrations rely on smooth trajectory planning. The smoothness requirement depends on the robot's complexity in terms of the number of degrees of freedom and system order due to the flexibility of the joints and links. For industrial robots, the smoothness requirements have been determined and subsequently taken into account. Although unknown for soft continuum robots, the exact smoothness requirement can potentially be much larger than previously considered ones such as $\mathbf{\mathcal{C}^4}$ for collaborative robots and $\mathbf{\mathcal{C}^5}$ for quadcopter drones. To address this gap, we introduce a $\mathbf{\mathcal{C}^\infty}$-smooth trajectory generator that respects defined kinematic limits and generates infinitely differentiable trajectories. The generated path addresses and generalizes to all smoothness requirements, where a transition between sequential states is needed. Therefore, all trajectories have continuous velocity, acceleration, jerk, snap, crackle, pop, etc. Of particular interest is the case of null boundary conditions, i.e., all derivatives at the start and end of the trajectory are equal to zero. We verified and evaluated our proposed approach in simulations and on hardware.

</details>


### [284] [Correct Robots Before They Make Mistakes: Proactive Human-in-the-Loop Intervention via Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05667)
*Jiang, Xinkai,Jiang, Jing,Zhou, Hongyi,Vanjani, Pankhuri,Baki, Ahmad,Suliga, Jakub,Li, Ge,Li, Zhuoyue,Neumann, Gerhard,Lioutikov, Rudolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHIL：在策略预测的未来轨迹被执行前，操作员通过XR界面预览并主动接管，将拒绝的预测与同一物理状态、同一时间跨度上实际执行的纠正动作配对，用偏好学习使策略偏向纠正行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习策略缺乏纠错机制，行为克隆易累积误差；传统HG-DAgger类人在回路方法通常在失败已发生后才接管，纠错数据与策略预测的时间对齐也较差，难以把人的意图有效"教"给策略。

Method: 提出PHIL框架：1）策略预测未来运动并可视化，操作员可在不良动作发生前预览与干预；2）接管期间仍持续查询策略并通过XR界面在物理工作空间中显示其预测轨迹；3）将拒绝的策略预测与从同一物理状态、同一horizon执行的实际纠正动作块配对；4）基于偏好的后训练（preference-based post-training）让策略偏好纠正动作。在扩散策略与预训练VLA（X-VLA）上，于4个真实机器人操作任务验证。

Result: 在5个纠正episode/条件设置下，相较HG-DAgger，平均成功率：扩散策略从44.0%提升至70.3%，X-VLA从57.8%提升至76.9%；且主动纠错训练后的策略在成功episode中表现出更少的多余恢复行为。跨多种演示预算均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 主动（预测前）接管+时间对齐的纠正配对+偏好优化，是一种简洁有效的机器人策略后训练范式，可显著提高成功率并减少冗余恢复行为。

Abstract: This paper presents Proactive Human-in-the-Loop (PHIL), a framework for proactive policy correction and preference-based post-training. PHIL enables operators to preview policy-predicted future motion and intervene before anticipated undesirable actions. During takeover, PHIL continues querying the policy and displaying its predicted trajectories in the physical workspace through an extended reality (XR) interface. PHIL pairs rejected policy predictions with corrective action chunks subsequently executed from the same physical state over the same horizon, and preference-based post-training encourages the policy to favor these corrections. This paper evaluates PHIL on four real-world manipulation tasks using a diffusion policy and a pretrained vision-language-action policy. With five correction episodes per condition, PHIL increases mean success rates across the evaluated tasks and demonstration budgets from 44.0% to 70.3% for the diffusion policy and from 57.8% to 76.9% for X-VLA, compared with HG-DAgger. Separately, policies trained with proactive corrections show fewer unnecessary recovery behaviors in successful episodes. Project page: https://intuitive-robots.github.io/PHIL-page/.

</details>


### [285] [OdorSpaces: Visual-Olfactory Embodied Navigation in 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05738)
*Lee, Changyeon,Yu, Kelin,Wei, Zheng,Ni, Shuna,Gao, Ruohan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OdorSpaces：基于FDS三维气味输运/扩散仿真扩展Habitat-Sim的视觉-嗅觉具身导航平台与基准，含气体泄漏定位与早期火源定位两任务，并给出多模态RL基线策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嗅觉是关键的空中化学线索通道，但具身AI中几乎未被探索；已有机器嗅觉工作多局限于静态语义气味识别，缺少物理一致的气味传输仿真与导航基准。

Method: 在Habitat-Sim上耦合Fire Dynamics Simulator(FDS)进行三维气味传输与扩散仿真，使智能体在光真实感室内场景中同时使用视觉与嗅觉观测；构建两个源搜索场景（不可见气体泄漏定位、早期火源定位）；开发融合时序视觉观测与气味测量的多模态强化学习策略。

Result: 所提多模态RL策略在多种基线上取得更优表现，为后续视觉-嗅觉具身导航研究提供了参考基线。

Conclusion: 该工作将物理仿真嗅觉引入具身导航，开辟视觉-嗅觉多模态导航新方向，但方法层面为初步基线，且缺乏真实机器人验证；作为仿真平台与基准，属相关背景/工具类价值。

Abstract: Olfaction provides critical information about airborne chemical cues, yet it remains largely underexplored in embodied AI, with limited prior machine olfaction work focusing primarily on semantic odor perception. We introduce OdorSpaces, a physics-based simulation platform and benchmark for visual-olfactory embodied navigation. OdorSpaces augments Habitat-Sim with 3D odor transport and dispersion simulated by Fire Dynamics Simulator (FDS), enabling agents to navigate photorealistic indoor environments using both visual and olfactory observations. The benchmark includes two representative source-seeking scenarios: invisible gas-leak localization and early-stage fire localization. We develop an initial multimodal reinforcement learning policy that integrates visual observations and olfactory measurements over time. Experiments show that the policy outperforms a diverse set of baselines, establishing a strong reference for future research on visual-olfactory embodied navigation.

</details>


### [286] [OUCH: Objective, Uniform, Controlled Haptic Stimulation for Automated Neurological Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05741)
*Choi, Sherry,Li, Sitao Sunny,Romero, Joseph,Chin, Lillian,Ramayya, Ashwin G.,Okamura, Allison M.,Chen, Ava*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OUCH: a wrist-worn pneumatic soft actuator (stacked pouch actuators + rigid indenter + buckling spacer) that delivers objective, uniform noxious mechanical stimulation for bedside neurological assessment, outperforming a commercial handheld algometer in contact pressure concentration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Noxious-stimulus motor responses are used to assess neurological function in disorders of consciousness, but manual bedside application yields large variation in force, duration, frequency, and location, undermining objectivity and repeatability.

Method: Stacked pneumatic pouch actuators drive a rigid indenter through a compliant spacer designed to buckle at a set load so contact occurs only during actuation. Characterization via blocked-force tests, clinician evaluation, finite element analysis, and a deformable pressure testbed; benchmarked against a commercial handheld pressure algometer.

Result: At 200 kPa inflation, estimated contact pressure reached 18,500 kPa, >25x the algometer tip, with lower total force; deformation localized near the surface with comparable deep-tissue deformation; preliminary perceptual tests showed discomfort at ~89% lower applied force than the algometer.

Conclusion: Combining pneumatic actuation with designed contact geometry enables controlled, repeatable noxious stimulation at the wrist, providing a foundation for objective neurological assessment with a compact wearable soft actuator.

Abstract: Motor responses evoked by noxious stimulation are used to assess neurological function in hospitalized patients with disorders of consciousness. In standard bedside assessments, these stimuli are applied manually, resulting in substantial variation in force, duration, frequency, and location. Here, we present OUCH (Objective, Uniform, Controlled Haptic stimulation), a wrist-worn device that uses pneumatic actuation to deliver noxious mechanical stimuli. OUCH combines stacked pneumatic pouch actuators, a rigid indenter, and a compliant spacer designed to buckle at a set load, so that the indenter contacts the skin only during actuation. We characterize the device using blocked force tests, clinician evaluation, finite element analysis, and a deformable pressure testbed, and benchmark it against a commercially available handheld pressure algometer. At 200 kPa pouch inflation pressure, our indenter concentrated its load into an estimated contact pressure of 18,500 kPa, over 25 times that of the algometer tip, while requiring less total force. On the deformable testbed, it localized deformation near the surface, while deformation at depth remained comparable to the algometer. As a result, in preliminary perceptual testing, our indenter elicited discomfort at approximately 89% lower applied force than the algometer, demonstrating the feasibility of evoking discomfort with a compact, wearable soft actuator. These results show that combining pneumatic actuation with designed contact geometry enables controlled, repeatable noxious stimulation at the wrist, providing a foundation for objective neurological assessment.

</details>


### [287] [GRASP: Gradient-Guided Sampling for Contact-Rich Physics-Based Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05754)
*Liu, Yang,Wang, Tong,Bao, Yiming,Yang, Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRASP：无需可微模拟器，结合采样探索与梯度引导细化的物理仿真轨迹重定向方法（GRASP-TO 拟合局部二次代价模型以引导 MCMC 复新，GRASP-MPC 用热启动滚动时域并扩展到长演示），在灵巧手与人形机器人上实现高精度重定向并提升下游 PPO 跟踪与真机部署效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体演示到机器人控制的物理级重定向需在接触动力学下复现参考运动，现有方法多依赖可微模拟器或任务特定接触奖励，难以通用且保真。

Method: 提出 GRASP：将采样式探索与梯度引导细化结合，无需可微模拟器。GRASP-TO 扩展 tempered SMC，对归档 rollout 拟合局部二次代价模型，用其梯度引导 MCMC 复新，Metropolis-Hastings 校正保持目标分布；种群分析关联恢复梯度与高斯平滑。GRASP-MPC 通过热启动滚动时域规划扩展到长演示。共享单一跟踪目标，无任务特定接触奖励。

Result: 灵巧手与人形机器人实验实现精准重定向，双手机器人 OakInk2 1352 条演示成功率 92.8%；细化后的参考提升笔旋转与人形-物体交互的 PPO 跟踪效果，并在真机上展示灵巧操作与全身运动。

Conclusion: 以采样+局部梯度代理的方式在缺乏可微模拟器时实现高质量逆动力学式重定向，可作为通用演示预处理提高下游机器人学习与真机部署表现。

Abstract: We study physics-based retargeting of human demonstrations for robot learning: finding robot controls that reproduce reference motions under contact dynamics. We propose GRASP (Gradient Recovery from Archived Samples for Proposals), which combines sampling-based exploration with gradient-guided refinement without requiring a differentiable simulator. GRASP-TO extends tempered sequential Monte Carlo by fitting local quadratic cost models to archived rollouts and using their gradients to guide Markov chain Monte Carlo rejuvenation. Metropolis-Hastings correction preserves the target distribution for each fixed proposal kernel, and a population analysis connects the recovered gradients to Gaussian smoothing. GRASP-MPC scales this optimizer to long demonstrations through warm-started receding-horizon planning. Experiments on dexterous hands and humanoids show accurate retargeting with a shared tracking objective and no task-specific contact rewards, including 92.8% success on 1352 bimanual OakInk2 demonstrations. The refined references improve PPO tracking in pen spinning and humanoid-object interaction. We also demonstrate dexterous manipulation and whole-body humanoid motion on real hardware.

</details>


### [288] [Closed-Loop Adaptive Sampling on a Generative Ocean Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05776)
*Hafezi, Shayesteh,Papalia, Alan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多机器人闭环自适应采样框架，用单一生成式海洋模型同时完成潜空间集合预报、不确定性估计、A-/D-最优采样规划与观测同化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏海洋观测价值有限；学得生成式海洋模型能以低算力给出高分辨率预报与集合，但其不原生提供经典信息规划所需的显式不确定性，且预报在潜空间演化而非物理变量空间，难以直接用于闭环机器人采样。

Method: 在潜空间预报集合，解码为物理海洋状态，用集合统计量构造A-最优/D-最优采样目标；在预测洋流下满足机器人可达性约束选择采样点；测得数据通过观测条件扩散采样回灌潜状态；规划开销小。

Result: 120个测试场景中A-/D-最优采样总体优于静态、随机与系统覆盖基线，附加规划开销很小；同时发现若干场景中模型不确定性与预报误差发生背离。

Conclusion: 生成式模型可端到端服务闭环多机器人自适应采样，但不确定性量化仍需改进；后续工作应解决不确定性与真实误差不一致的问题。

Abstract: Adaptive sampling can substantially improve the value of sparse ocean observations by directing mobile robots toward measurements that are most informative for an evolving environment. Recent learned generative ocean models can produce high-resolution forecasts and ensembles at substantially lower computational cost than traditional numerical models, creating an opportunity to use rich predictive forecasts directly in closed-loop robotic sampling. However, these models do not natively provide the explicit uncertainty representations assumed by classical informative-planning methods, and their forecasts often evolve in learned latent spaces rather than directly in physical ocean variables. We develop a multi-robot closed-loop adaptive sampling framework that uses a single learned generative model for forecasting, uncertainty estimation, planning, and measurement assimilation. The model forecasts an ensemble in latent space, decodes it to physical ocean states, and uses ensemble statistics to construct A- or D-optimal sampling objectives. We select sampling locations subject to robot reachability under predicted currents. The collected measurements are assimilated back into the latent state using observation-conditioned diffusion sampling. Across 120 test scenarios A- and D-optimal sampling generally outperforms static, random, and systematic-coverage baselines while adding little planning overhead. Our experiments uncover several scenarios in which model uncertainty diverges from forecast error, motivating future work in uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [289] [ZipDrift: Generating Multimodal Short-Horizon MPC Costs with One-Step Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05793)
*Zhang, Ke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZipDrift用一步"漂移生成器"(drifting generator)学习MPC代价修正的多模态分布,仅依赖候选修正的闭环代价评估训练,并用学到的critic在少量候选中排序,从而改善短时域MPC在双模态超车场景中的近视问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短时域MPC快但短视;ZipMPC用模仿长时域专家的上下文相关代价修正来弥补,但其最优修正常是多模态的(如可从两侧超车,正负修正均有效,专家依赖微小代价差决胜),确定性回归会平均各模态或继承专家不一致的tie-breaking。

Method: 提出ZipDrift:在MPC代价修正空间上训练单步漂移生成模型,使采样向低闭环代价倾斜;训练只使用候选修正的代价评估,不需对优化器反向传播。生成若干候选后用学到的critic排序,再由基于模型的MPC强制执行动力学与约束。在ZipMPC派生的运动学超车基准(1000个新上下文、5个训练seed、8维分段修正)上与ZipMPC式回归器、同预算的随机候选+critic排序、等选择机制的MDN对比。

Result: 基准中过半上下文为双模态,手工短时域MPC在28%的此类场景中撞车。8候选择优:ZipDrift碰撞率0.74%,理想化ZipMPC式回归器3.4%,随机候选+critic 2.1%,MDN 0.76%。漂移的优势在单样本质量:预注册的未见上下文测试中单漂移样本碰撞率8.7% vs MDN 13.2%,ZipDrift只需4个候选即可低于1%(MDN/回归需8个)。候选数很多时所有方法因critic被选择过程利用而退化。

Conclusion: 将多模态生成式采样与critic排序结合,可在不牺牲MPC安全性(动力学/约束仍由MPC保证)的前提下显著降低短视MPC的失败率;核心增益来自样本分布质量而非仅排序预算。局限是critic误差在大量候选下会被选择过程利用。

Abstract: Short-horizon model predictive control (MPC) is fast but myopic. ZipMPC compensates by learning a context-dependent correction of the short-horizon cost that imitates a long-horizon expert. We observe that the optimal correction is often multimodal: when a slower opponent can be passed on either side, good corrections of opposite sign coexist, and the expert's choice between them hinges on tiny cost differences. Deterministic regression then averages modes or inherits inconsistent expert tie-breaking. We propose ZipDrift, which trains a one-step drifting generator over MPC cost corrections, tilted toward low closed-loop cost and learned only from cost evaluations of candidate corrections, without differentiating through the optimiser. A few generated candidates are ranked by a learned critic, and the model-based MPC then enforces dynamics and constraints. In a kinematic overtaking benchmark derived from ZipMPC, more than half of the contexts are bimodal and the manual short-horizon MPC crashes in 28% of them. With an eight-dimensional stagewise correction, evaluated on 1000 fresh contexts and five training seeds, ZipDrift with eight candidates crashes in 0.74% of episodes, compared with 3.4% for an idealised ZipMPC-style regressor and 2.1% for critic-ranked random candidates with the same budget. A mixture density network (MDN) with the same selection is equally good (0.76%). The benefit of drifting lies in sample quality: in a pre-specified test on further unseen contexts, a single drifting sample crashes in 8.7% of episodes against 13.2% for the MDN, and ZipDrift needs four candidates instead of eight to fall below 1%. With many candidates, all methods degrade as selection exploits errors of the critic.

</details>


### [290] [RACE: Calibrated Cumulative Scoring for Rollout-Free Robot Policy Checkpoint Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05838)
*Kim, Changyeon,Nah, Yekyung,Lee, Kimin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RACE：一种无需真实 rollout 的检查点排序指标，用固定成功轨迹集、状态条件阈值校准动作偏差并用连乘累积，使排名与部署成功率对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练中评估学习到的机器人策略部署表现困难：直接测任务成功率需对每个候选检查点做昂贵的真实试验。平均验证损失虽免 rollout，但其检查点排序常与部署表现不一致。作者指出两大缺陷：(1) 惩罚动作偏差却不考虑策略能否从中恢复；(2) 对后期参考预测等权赋分，忽略前期偏差可能已使执行偏离参考。

Method: RACE 基于一组固定的held-out成功轨迹做免 rollout 评分。将动作偏差与从这些轨迹中相似状态处动作差异导出的状态条件阈值比较校准；再用running product聚合，使显著偏差削减后续参考预测的贡献；最后在时间与轨迹上平均累积得分作为检查点分数。

Result: 仿真中 RACE 在各验证来源上优于基线，而多个基线在验证集变化时性能急剧下降；真机上其排序在多种策略骨干与不同难度任务上与部署成功率一致；扰动分析显示 RACE 能区分对成功率影响差异显著的偏差，即便基线响应相似。

Conclusion: RACE 提供了无需真实试验、对验证数据来源鲁棒且与真机部署表现一致的检查点选择方法，可作为训练期策略评估的实用替代。

Abstract: Assessing the deployment performance of learned robot policies during training is challenging, as directly measuring task success requires costly real-world trials for each candidate checkpoint. Averaged validation loss offers a rollout-free alternative, but its checkpoint rankings can be inconsistent with deployment performance. We analyze two limitations of this criterion: it penalizes action deviations without considering whether the policy can recover from them, and gives later reference predictions equal weight even when earlier deviations may lead execution away from the reference. We propose RACE, a novel rollout-free metric for ranking checkpoints using a fixed set of held-out successful trajectories. RACE calibrates action deviations against state-conditional thresholds derived from action differences at similar states in these trajectories. We aggregate these scores through a running product, so that a substantial deviation reduces the contribution of subsequent reference predictions. Averaging the resulting cumulative scores over time and across trajectories yields the checkpoint score. In simulation, RACE outperforms baselines across validation sources, while several baselines degrade sharply when the validation set changes. On real robots, its rankings align with deployment success across the evaluated policy backbones and tasks of varying difficulty. Perturbation analyses further show that RACE distinguishes deviations with markedly different effects on task success, even when baselines respond similarly.

</details>


### [291] [Time-to-Goal: Learned Temporal Metrics for Long-Horizon Planning with JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05847)
*Yu, Ruilong,Ye, Fei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Time-to-Goal (T2G)轻量度量，用离线轨迹三元组+margin ranking loss显式学习潜空间中的时间排序，替代JEPA的欧氏目标代价，在冻结编码器/预测器的前提下提升平坦世界模型的潜空间规划成功率（32.7%→63.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA类架构通过比较预测潜状态与编码目标来规划，但表征训练目标是预测与分布正则，并未显式约束"时间上更接近目标的状态代价更低"这一排序性质。作者发现平坦LeWM的欧氏目标代价在长时距偏移上趋于饱和，不同时间距离的状态获得相近平均代价，削弱了时间区分度，导致规划缺乏有效进度信号。

Method: 先分析LeWM欧氏goal cost随时间的分布，定位长偏移下代价塌缩的条件；再引入T2G：从离线轨迹采样潜状态三元组，用margin ranking loss训练一个轻量度量，使时间更近的状态对距离更小，无需奖励或策略标签；推理时将T2G度量替换欧氏规划代价，JEPA编码器与预测器保持冻结。

Result: 在AntMaze与FourRoom诊断实验中，T2G在长偏移处保持更强的时间对比度并改善near–far排序；在四个导航与操作基准上，平均规划成功率由32.7%（flat LeWM）提升至63.0%，接近甚至超过分层H-JEPA基线。

Conclusion: 目标代价中的时间排序是可单独学习、且对潜空间规划收益显著的缺失组件；仅通过后置学习度量即可在平坦世界模型上大幅改善规划，说明表征学习与规划代价之间存在可解耦的设计空间。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) support planning by comparing predicted latent states with an encoded goal. For this comparison to guide temporal progress, states closer in time to the goal should receive lower costs. However, training representations for prediction and distribution regularization does not directly enforce this ordering. We examine this mismatch in flat LeWorldModel (LeWM) by analyzing how its Euclidean goal cost changes with temporal separation. Our analysis identifies conditions under which states at different long offsets receive similar average costs, weakening their temporal distinction. To learn the missing ordering explicitly, we introduce Time-to-Goal (T2G), a lightweight metric trained on latent triples from offline trajectories. A margin ranking loss encourages smaller distances for temporally nearer states, requiring no rewards or policy labels. The learned metric then replaces the Euclidean planning cost while the JEPA encoder and predictor remain frozen. Diagnostics on AntMaze and FourRoom show that T2G preserves stronger temporal contrast and improves near--far ranking at long offsets. Across four navigation and manipulation benchmarks, T2G improves the average planning success rate from 32.7\% (flat LeWM) to 63.0\% and approaches or even surpasses the performance of the hierarchical H-JEPA baselines. These results show that learning temporal ordering for the goal cost can substantially improve latent planning even with a flat world model.

</details>


### [292] [Does RRT* Work on Uneven Terrain Meshes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05885)
*Jerome, Otobong,Nascimento, Tiago*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对三角网格曲面地形，指出经典RRT*不可用、朴素"弦边"Mesh RRT*最小化了错误目标（代价低于测地线长度），改为用投影点沿网格边连接的地表路径作为边后，返回路径长度在每个粗糙度下都在精确测地线的0.07–0.16%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RRT*的渐近最优性仅在欧氏空间开子集上被证明；网格曲面体积为零导致均匀采样命中率为零、无法容纳clearance球、弦穿越折叠处会离开曲面，因此需要重新审视RRT*在网格地形上的可迁移性及其代价度量正确性。

Method: 先分析原始RRT*在网格上的失效原因，再提出Mesh RRT*并证明其弦边代价（允许段偏离曲面容差tau）是曲面上不存在的路径长度、严格低于测地线；最后以"曲面上的投影点跨越所跨网格边连接、按自身路径长度计费"替换弦边，其余算法不变。

Result: 数值实验中30,000次迭代下弦代价平均比精确测地线低至多2.7%（路径不可达），返回路线反而长0.1–0.5%；重连保持偏差边，偏差仅随步长线性衰减，缩小tau是以连通性换偏差；改用地表边后返回路径长度平均在测地线0.07–0.16%以内，在粗糙地形上逼近度提升2–3.4倍。

Conclusion: RRT*在网格曲面上可行，但前提是边代价必须是真实曲面可行路径的精确长度；弦近似属于系统性代价偏差，投影地表路径是低改动、高精度的修正方案。

Abstract: The optimal rapidly exploring random tree (RRT*) is proven asymptotically optimal on open subsets of Euclidean space with straight-line local planning. We ask whether it works on a triangle-mesh terrain, a two-dimensional surface in R^3. First, the original RRT* cannot run on a mesh: the surface has zero volume, so uniform sampling never hits it, no clearance ball fits in it, and chords across a fold leave it. Second, the surface reformulation we call Mesh RRT*, with its naive edge, the chord costed by its length, solved every run on heightfields with slopes up to 75 degrees but minimises the wrong objective: its cost, the straight-line length of a polyline whose vertices lie on the surface while its segments may leave it by a tolerance tau, is the length of no route on the surface and lies strictly below the geodesic length on any curved mesh. In numeric simulation and after 30,000 iterations the reported cost is on average up to 2.7% below the exact geodesic, a cost no path attains, and the returned route on average 0.1-0.5% longer than it. Rewiring keeps the biased edges, the bias decays only linearly with the step on polyhedral surfaces, and shrinking tau trades bias for connectivity. Third, replacing each chord by a path on the surface above it, built from projected points joined across the mesh edges they straddle and costed by its own length, makes every edge cost the exact length of a feasible path; with no other change the exact length of the returned routes is on average within 0.07-0.16% of the geodesic at every roughness tested, two to 3.4 times closer than with chords on the rougher terrains. The planner is in MeshNav3D 0.2.0 (release pending).

</details>


### [293] [FWIK: Multi-Waypoint IK Selection via Lookahead Graph Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05897)
*Cao, Jinglan,Zhong, Guohui,Zhang, Chengwei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FWIK：在线冗余机械臂逆运动学解选择方法，利用未来路点小窗口构建分层关节空间图，以运动时间估计排序候选路径，并用学习模型剔除易自碰撞的转换；最终由MoveIt 2规划器验证并执行首个可行目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对末端位姿序列逐点求解IK时，冗余臂在每个路点存在多个有效构型；仅针对下一路点贪心选择构型可能导致后续路点规划时间变长或规划失败。

Method: FWIK生成多个IK候选解，在分层关节空间图中连接这些候选，并用简单的运动时间估计对候选路径排序；训练一个学习模型预测并剔除转换易导致自碰撞的路径；随后按排名顺序用标准MoveIt 2规划器（Pilz、RRT-Connect、CHOMP、STOMP）检查下一路点IK目标，选中首个产生有效轨迹的目标执行。

Result: 在Panda与KUKA LBR iiwa两款7自由度机械臂仿真中，每设置5次重复、每次100条50路点序列：FWIK在八种机器人-规划器组合中完成率达96.8–100%，且轨迹规划时长最短，平均比STAMPEDE低12.8–13.1%，比IKLink低22.5–25.6%，比重试式TRAC-IK低27.5–37.2%。

Conclusion: 引入小规模未来路点窗口可提升序列完成率并缩短规划运动时间；FWIK将碰撞筛选前置、轨迹有效性验证仍交由运动规划器，思路简洁且即插即用。

Abstract: An ordered sequence of end-effector poses can give a redundant robot arm multiple valid joint configurations at each waypoint. Inverse kinematics (IK) computes these configurations. Choosing a configuration only for the next waypoint can leave the arm poorly placed for later waypoints. This may increase planned motion time or cause a later planning failure. This paper presents Future-Window Inverse Kinematics (FWIK), an online method that selects IK solutions by considering a small window of future waypoints. FWIK generates several IK candidates, connects them in a layered joint-space graph, and ranks candidate paths with a simple motion-time estimate. A learned model removes paths whose transitions are likely to cause self-collision. A standard MoveIt 2 planner then checks the next-waypoint IK goals from the remaining paths in ranked order, and the first goal that yields a valid trajectory is selected for execution. We evaluate FWIK in simulation on two 7-DoF manipulators, the Franka Emika Panda and the KUKA LBR iiwa. Each method-planner setting contains five repetitions of 100 sequences with 50 waypoints. Across Pilz, RRT-Connect, CHOMP, and STOMP, FWIK completes 96.8-100% of sequences and gives the shortest planned trajectory duration in all eight robot-planner settings. Its mean duration is 12.8-13.1% lower than STAMPEDE, 22.5-25.6% lower than IKLink, and 27.5-37.2% lower than retry-based TRAC-IK. The results show that a small future window can improve sequence completion and reduce planned motion time while leaving final trajectory validation to the motion planner.

</details>


### [294] [Diversity-Aware Experience Replay via Statistical Leverage Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05929)
*Saxena, Shaifalee,Scheinker, Alexander*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用回放缓冲区中样本的统计杠杆值（基于高峰度特征列）构造采样分布，放弃重要性权重校正，并在轨迹窗口层面处理关系型稀有事件，提升机械臂稀有任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略深度强化学习的回放缓冲区被大量routine、近乎重复的转移主导，而定义任务边界的稀有事件极少被采样，导致物理机器人上稀有任务学习不足。

Method: 1) 用统计杠杆（leverage）为每条转移打分，只用存储数据、无需标签/奖励/价值网络；2) 按分数与均匀采样混合抽取小批量；3) 发现按方差最大方向构建分数在one-hot动作为主时失效（指示列方差占优），改为选择峰度最大（尾最重）的特征列以暴露小规模远距离样本群；4) 放弃优先回放的重要性权重校正（完全校正会退化回均匀采样），改为直接优化重平衡目标；5) 对关系型稀有情形，将同一尾部分数规则应用于以目标为参照的短轨迹窗口描述。

Result: 在三关节机械臂绕障碍推物任务中，稀有任务成功率从0.49提升至0.69，接近有标签oracle上限0.74。

Conclusion: 基于峰度的杠杆式重平衡采样可在无监督、无价值函数的条件下有效挖掘稀有转移，且关系型稀有性需在轨迹窗口层面而非单坐标层面对齐信号。

Abstract: Off-policy deep reinforcement learning trains on random samples from a replay buffer of past experience. In physical robot settings, that buffer is dominated by routine: thousands of near-identical transitions from normal operation, while the rare events that define the edges of the task are rarely sampled. We propose scoring every stored transition by its statistical leverage, which measures how unusual it is relative to the buffer's own data, computed from the stored numbers alone, with no labels, rewards, or value network. We draw each mini-batch from a mixture of this score and uniform sampling. First, the natural way to build the score along the directions where the data varies most turns out to be blind when actions are stored as one-hot indicators, because indicator columns always win the variance ranking; we instead select the feature columns with the heaviest tails (largest kurtosis) where small far-away populations are revealed. Second, the importance weights that prioritized replay uses to keep gradients unbiased cancel the method exactly: with full correction, training matches uniform sampling, so we drop the correction and deliberately optimize the rebalanced objective. Third, when the rare situations of a log are relational, visible only in how a stretch of behavior relates to its goal, not in any single stored coordinate-per-transition scores are blind; applying the same tail rule to short trajectory windows described relative to their goal restores the signal, and on a three-joint arm that must push an object around an obstacle it raises the rare-task success from 0.49 to 0.69 against a labeled-oracle ceiling of 0.74.

</details>


### [295] [LumiTac: Compact, High-Rate Six-Axis Optical Tactile Sensing for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05936)
*Wu, Hao,Guo, Haotian,Feng, Yu,Wang, Yutong,Zhou, Jianshu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LumiTac：一种12 mm×4.75 mm、<2 g、约7美元的低剖面无相机光电触觉传感器，通过5发光+5 RGBW接收的光谱编码与MLP实现六轴力/力矩重建，独立测试R²=0.9657–0.9941，在线303.57 Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六轴触觉反馈在机器人界面中需要紧凑、低成本且可实时在线重建的传感器；现有光学触觉方案多依赖相机，体积与成本高，难以集成到指尖等受限空间。

Method: 将5个光发射器与5个RGBW光接收器分置于相对电路板，经半透明硅胶耦合；压缩、剪切、弯曲、扭转引起的层间位移调制内部光场，形成20路光谱编码通道；用紧凑多层感知机从20通道回归3个力与3个力矩分量。评测含独立测试集、复合加载、轻载与快速敲击实验及机器人操作与人机交互演示。

Result: R² 0.9657–0.9941；力MAE 0.054–0.190 N，力矩MAE 0.304–1.419 mN·m；在线平均更新率303.57 Hz，软件处理延迟中位数0.722 ms；复合加载、小力敏感度与瞬态响应实验表现良好，并完成触觉引导操作与物理人机交互演示。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了低成本、超薄、无相机的六轴光学触觉传感路径，具备高刷新率与毫秒级延迟，适合指尖级机器人操作与人机交互集成；但重建依赖轻量MLP回归，跨传感器泛化与长期漂移未在摘要中充分验证。

Abstract: Integrating six-axis tactile feedback into robotic interfaces requires compact sensors with accurate load measurement and rapid online reconstruction. This paper presents LumiTac, a low-profile, camera-free optoelectronic tactile sensor combining spatially distributed illumination with spectral encoding. Five photoemitters and five RGBW photoreceivers are mounted on opposing circuit boards coupled through translucent silicone. Load-induced interlayer motion modulates the internal light field under compression, shear, bending, and torsion. The sensing head measures 12 mm in diameter and 4.75 mm in thickness, weighs less than 2 g, and has a component cost of approximately USD 7. A compact multilayer perceptron reconstructs three force and three moment components from 20 optical channels. Independent testing yielded coefficients of determination (R-squared) of 0.9657-0.9941, with mean absolute errors of 0.054-0.190 N for forces and 0.304-1.419 mN m for moments. Online prediction achieved an average update rate of 303.57 Hz and a median software processing latency of 0.722 ms. Experiments involving combined loading, light weights, and rapid tapping evaluated load tracking, sensitivity to small forces, and transient response, respectively. Robotic demonstrations further validated its applicability to tactile-guided manipulation and physical human-robot interaction. This study paves the way for compact, high-rate optical tactile sensing in robotic manipulation and human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [296] [Reflection-Robust Stereo Visual-Inertial Odometry via Kalman-Filtered Ground-Height Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05938)
*Hwang, Uihyun,Shin, Sungjae,Kim, Dongjae,Myung, Hyun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量级反射感知前端：实时估计地面高度，仅剔除以地面高度为界判定的地下虚拟特征，并用NIS门控的标量卡尔曼滤波稳定估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内反光地板会生成镜面“虚拟特征”，被三角化为地面下方虚假3D点，导致错误特征关联与不一致视觉残差，破坏基于特征的立体VIO。

Method: 地面区域掩膜限定验证范围；结合立体高度不确定性与位姿不确定性构造帧级地面高度观测；标量卡尔曼滤波+NIS门控获得稳定高度估计；用不确定性感知安全裕度选择性剔除仅位于地面以下的候选特征（不整体抑制地面区域特征）。

Result: 在真实反光地板室内序列上，前端总体提升反射主导场景下的轨迹精度，同时保持地面高度估计准确，并能在地面高度真实变化时恢复。

Conclusion: 以“几何一致性+高度先验”替代粗暴的地面特征剔除，是低成本、可插拔的反射鲁棒VIO前端方案。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry relies on visual features that correspond to 3D points with consistent projections across sequential frames. In reflective indoor-floor environments, however, specular floor patterns can be detected and triangulated as virtual features below the physical ground plane, producing unreliable feature associations and inconsistent visual residuals. This paper proposes a lightweight reflection-aware front-end for feature-based stereo VIO. Rather than suppressing all floor-region features, the proposed method estimates the ground height in real time and rejects only feature candidates whose inferred 3D positions are below the estimated ground height. A floor-region mask restricts the validation process to reflection-prone regions, while stereo height uncertainty and pose uncertainty are used to construct frame-level ground-height measurements. A scalar Kalman filter with Normalized Innovation Squared (NIS) gating then provides a stable ground-height estimate, and an uncertainty-aware safety margin is used for selective below-ground virtual feature rejection. Experiments on real reflective-floor indoor sequences show that the proposed front-end generally improves trajectory accuracy in reflection-dominant environments while maintaining accurate ground-height estimates and recovering from actual ground-height changes.

</details>


### [297] [Learning Object-Centric Motion Priors from Human for Robotic Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05979)
*Hong, Zhengdong,Zhang, Guofeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用人类-物体交互(HOI)数据集预测未来手-物体状态，将预测的物体未来状态作为通用RL奖励项，减少任务特定奖励工程，实现多指灵巧手跨任务、跨物体的泛化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手操作高维、动力学复杂，难以设计任务特定奖励；同时HOI数据集蕴含丰富的任务与具身交互知识，但通常仅被用于模仿学习，尚未被充分用作通用奖励来源。

Method: 从HOI数据中学习整体预测未来手-物体状态（hand-object state prediction），把预测的物体未来状态作为强化学习的通用奖励项（类似目标状态/轨迹匹配奖励），与RL策略联合训练，而非直接模仿人类动作。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界共三个操作任务上验证，成功率与对新物体的泛化性均超过现有SOTA；并成功迁移到不同机器人手，验证跨具身兼容性。

Conclusion: 预测式HOI知识可作为可复用的通用奖励，降低奖励工程成本并提升灵巧操作泛化与跨具身迁移能力。

Abstract: Manipulating diverse objects with multi-fingered dexterous hands is challenging due to the high dimensionality and complex dynamics. Human-Object Interaction (HOI) datasets provide rich knowledge about task information and embodied interactions. Instead of solely imitating the human demonstrations, our method learns to holistically predict future hand-object states by leveraging these datasets. The predicted future states of the object can serve as a general-purpose reward term for reinforcement learning, reducing reliance on task-specific reward engineering and enhancing generalization across tasks. We conduct extensive experiments on three manipulation tasks in simulation and the real world. Our approach outperforms existing SOTA methods in both success rate and generalizability on novel objects. Furthermore, we validate the cross-embodiment compatibility of our methods by successfully deploying the skills on different robot hands.

</details>


### [298] [The Effectiveness of Sliding Mode Control for Robust Vision Stabilization under the Plant-Binding Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06028)
*Yang, Sooyeun,Choi, Jongseong Brad,choi, Seung Bok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究视觉稳定化中边界层滑模控制（SMC）相对LPF/PID等因果基线的激活条件，提出"被控对象绑定"（扰动速度接近或超过执行器速率上限）为SMC生效的操作性前提，并在手持平移稳定与虚拟限速PTZ角向重构图两个域中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界层SMC已用于视觉稳定化，但其相对更简单因果基线（LPF、PID）究竟在何种条件下才有优势一直不明确，缺少可操作的激活判据。

Method: 以"执行器速率/斜率上限绑定"作为假设条件；在12段1080p手持平移视频上用LPF、PID、SMC、L1做留一交叉验证对比；再构造虚拟带斜率限制的PTZ被控对象做角向重构图实验，并通过扫描模拟斜率上限来进入绑定区；另做SMC到达增益k的消融。

Result: 非绑定区：平移域SMC与LPF统计不可区分（打平），角向域PID在99分位指向误差上优于SMC 5.85度（95%CI不含零）。绑定区（仅靠扫描模拟斜率上限可达，两数据集本身均落在绑定区外）：平移仍打平，角向最坏情况下SMC优于PID（Cohen's d最高+0.83）。PTZ默认100°/s恰位于交叉点，可解释此前角向设置中PID小幅领先的结果；到达增益k只在窄窗口内有效，抬高会恶化平均跟踪。

Conclusion: SMC的优势并非普适，而由被控对象速率绑定条件决定；给出可检验的激活判据与增益选择警示，说明已有文献中SMC/PID结论差异可用斜率上限位置解释。

Abstract: Boundary-layer Sliding Mode Control (SMC) has been applied to vision-based stabilization, but identifying its activation condition against simpler causal baselines (LPF, PID) has been elusive. The operational condition is plant binding: the disturbance velocity approaches or exceeds the actuator slew limit, so the bounded per-step actuation budget binds. We validate this in two independent vision domains: handheld translation stabilization (12 1080p clips; LPF, PID, SMC, L1) and angular re-framing against a virtual slew-rate-limited pan-tilt-zoom (PTZ) plant. Outside binding, leave-one-out cross-validation makes SMC and LPF on translation statistically indistinguishable, and PID beats SMC on the angular 99th-percentile pointing offset by 5.85 degrees (95% CI clear of zero); inside binding -- reached only by sweeping the simulated slew limit, as both captured datasets land outside it -- the translation gap stays a tie and SMC beats PID on the angular worst case (Cohen's d up to +0.83). The PTZ plant's 100 degrees/s default sits on the cross-over, which explains a small-margin PID result previously reported in the angular setting. A complementary ablation shows raising the SMC reaching gain k costs mean tracking outside a narrow window.

</details>


### [299] [Online Adaptation to Latent Physical Properties for Learning from Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06118)
*Kajitsuka, Kotaro,Murooka, Masaki,Kanehiro, Fumio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出从示范中联合学习动力学模型与物体物理属性隐表示，并用在线估计的隐表示调制模仿策略，实现对物理属性不同但外观相同物体的操作自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外观相同但物理属性（如质心）不同的物体需依据交互中获得的信息调整抓取与接触位置，现有模仿学习难以在线推断并适应这类隐属性。

Method: 联合学习动力学模型与物体专属隐表示（示范含适应行为），以隐表示条件化模仿策略；执行时冻结动力学模型与策略权重，依据预测-实际观测差异在线更新隐表示。

Result: 真实机器人与仿真实验中，基于隐表示的在线自适应提升了任务成功率；但仿真中未在示范中出现过的质心位置上成功率仅约30%，对未见物理属性的泛化有限。

Conclusion: 在线隐属性推断可有效支持接触与重抓取自适应，但隐表示的分布外泛化仍是主要瓶颈，需更强的物理先验或主动探索。

Abstract: Manipulating visually identical objects with different physical properties requires adapting actions based on information acquired through interaction. We address the problem of learning from demonstrations how to adapt manipulation actions, such as regrasping and changing contact locations, based on physical-property information acquired during interaction. To this end, we propose a learning-from-demonstration method that learns latent representations of physical properties needed to predict object responses and conditions a policy on online estimates of these representations. A dynamics model and object-specific latent representations are jointly learned from demonstrations that include behaviors adapting to the objects' physical properties, and the learned representations are then used to condition an imitation policy. During execution, the dynamics model and policy weights remain fixed, while the latent representation is updated based on the discrepancy between predicted and actual observations, allowing interaction with the object to inform action generation. Real-world and simulation experiments with objects whose centers of mass differ suggest that online adaptation through latent representations of physical properties can improve task success rates. However, in simulation, success rates on center-of-mass locations absent from the demonstrations remained around 30%, indicating limited adaptation to unseen physical properties. Project page: https://isri-aist.github.io/latent-dynamics-project-page/

</details>


### [300] [3D Pose Estimation in Projection Space via Pixel-level Visible Light Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06121)
*Eguchi, Michikuni,Hiraki, Takefumi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将像素级可见光通信(PVLC)的平面空间编码扩展为三维位姿估计：用四个共面光电传感器接收的像素坐标构造PnP问题，实现投影仪-设备间无外部传感基础设施的实时3D位姿估计（约8ms延迟、滤波后19.2mm RMSE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影式AR系统中，投影仪与设备的精确位置对齐通常依赖外部传感基础设施；PVLC可在保持图像质量的前提下通过高速亮度调制嵌入不可见空间编码实现自对齐，但其所传信息本质上是平面的，因此现有方法只能处理平面运动设备，无法估计空中机器人等三维空间设备的位姿。

Method: 把PVLC位姿估计建模为Perspective-n-Point问题：设备上四个共面光电传感器经PVLC接收的2D像素坐标作为像点，其已知的3D布局作为物点，从而求解三维位姿，并结合卡尔曼滤波平滑结果。

Result: 系统3D位姿估计延迟约8ms，卡尔曼滤波后3D位置RMSE为19.2mm，且在位姿估计同时保持高质量投影图像内容。

Conclusion: 首次实现无需外部传感基础设施、与高质量投影内容协同的实时三维位姿估计，把PVLC从平面跟踪推广到三维空间。

Abstract: Projector-based augmented reality enables natural interaction between users and devices by overlaying digital information onto the physical world through projected imagery. A key technical requirement of such systems is accurate positional alignment between the projector and the devices. Pixel-level Visible Light Communication (PVLC) achieves this alignment without external sensing infrastructure by embedding invisible spatial codes into each projected pixel through high-speed brightness modulation that preserves image quality. However, the information that PVLC transmits is inherently planar. PVLC has therefore been limited to devices moving on a flat surface and cannot estimate the 3D pose of devices in three-dimensional space, such as aerial robots. To extend PVLC-based pose estimation to three dimensions, we formulate it as a Perspective-n-Point (PnP) problem. The 2D pixel positions that four coplanar photosensors on the device receive via PVLC serve as the image points, and their known 3D layout provides the object points. Experiments show that the proposed system estimates 3D pose with a latency of approximately 8~ms and a 3D position RMSE of 19.2~mm after Kalman filtering. These results confirm the first realization of real-time 3D pose estimation coordinated with high-quality projected image content without external sensing infrastructure.

</details>


### [301] [A Layered View of LLM Support in Underwater Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06162)
*Celi, Federico,Zacchini, Leonardo,Ferri, Gabriele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出观点性（extended abstract）论文：水下机器人海试中真正瓶颈在于有限通信/部分可观测下的故障诊断、遥测解读、任务调整与多异构平台协调，主张LLM应作为“操作支持层”而非直接控制器，并给出分层架构——传统自主性负责控制/感知/估计/任务执行，LLM模块负责调试、人机交互、受限重规划支持、编排与任务后分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下现场任务的难点往往不是合成新的低层控制律，而是通信受限、部分可观测、船时昂贵条件下的复杂系统运维；海试中大量精力消耗在故障诊断、遥测解释、任务调整与跨操作员协调上，故希望把LLM放在这一更高运营层级发挥作用。

Method: 纯观点/立场性论述，无具体模型实现或实验：提出以结构化载体状态、告警、日志、任务上下文以及既有规划/验证工具作为LLM的grounding来源；提出分层架构设计（传统自主性栈 + LLM支持层），并明确当前在grounding、一致性与因果推理上的局限使其不适合直接控制。

Result: 无论文级实验或量化结果；仅给出“在我们的经验中”这一来自海试实践的非形式化论据，以及对LLM能力边界的定性判断。

Conclusion: LLM在水下机器人中的现实价值定位是“高层运营支持与编排”，而非实时闭环控制；未来可能演进但不保证。该视角为分层系统设计与后续评测（如带工具grounding的诊断/重规划任务）提供方向。

Abstract: This extended abstract discusses a specific and, in our experience, practically relevant role for large language models (LLMs) in underwater robotics. Our main premise is that, in underwater fielding missions, the immediate difficulty is often not the synthesis of new low-level control laws, but the operation of complex robotic systems under limited communications, partial observability, and expensive field time. In particular, a significant fraction of the effort during sea trials is devoted to fault diagnosis, telemetry interpretation, mission adjustment, and coordination across heterogeneous assets and operators. We argue that LLMs may be useful at this higher operational level, especially when grounded in structured vehicle state, alarms, logs, mission context, and existing planning or validation tools. At the same time, current limitations in grounding, consistency, and causal reasoning make these systems lack the predictability required to guarantee direct control, especially in real-world critical applications, although we do not exclude a future evolution in this direction. This viewpoint suggests a layered architecture in which conventional autonomy remains responsible for control, perception, estimation, and validated mission execution, whereas LLM-based modules serve as an additional support layer for debugging, operator interaction, constrained replanning support, orchestration, and post-mission analysis.

</details>


### [302] [Can Astra Solve RoboMME without Breaking the Bank?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06184)
*Chen, Bingao,Fang, Haoquan,Liu, C. Karen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三层系统：GPT-6 Astra（System 2）基于视觉历史选择子任务，π0.5 VLA（System 1）执行，微调Qwen3-VL-4B（System 1.5）判断子任务是否完成；RoboMME上成功率79.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作常需记忆已不可见的信息（遮挡前位置、示范序列顺序）。大型VLM规划器可读历史，但每个动作块前调用代价高、速度慢。需在保持长程记忆能力的同时降低调用频率。

Method: 异步三层架构（System 2/1/1.5）：高层VLM从存储的视觉历史中选一个grounded子任务并保持指令不撤销，直到轻量监控器（微调Qwen3-VL-4B）在每个动作块后判定完成或任务特定事件触发；底层π0.5 VLA持续在新观测上执行，不阻塞于高层。

Result: RoboMME上79.13%成功率，平均3.63次Astra调用、80.84秒/回合；比最佳非oracle基线高34.62点，比提供真值子目标的GroundSG+Oracle低4.95点。

Conclusion: 用轻量完成监控器作为高层调用与底层执行间的门控，可大幅削减大模型调用开销，同时接近oracle子目标性能；架构范式（分层异步+完成判定）比单点模型更重要。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires remembering information that is no longer visible, such as an object's location before occlusion or the order of a demonstrated action sequence. A large vision-language planner can read this history, but calling it before every action chunk is slow and costly. We present a three-tier system for the RoboMME benchmark. GPT-6 Astra (System 2) selects a grounded subtask from stored visual history, a $\pi_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy (System 1) executes it, and a fine-tuned Qwen3-VL-4B monitor (System 1.5) checks after each action chunk whether the subtask is finished. The instruction is kept until the monitor reports completion or a task-specific event occurs, while the policy keeps acting on new observations. On RoboMME, the system succeeds in 79.13% of episodes with an average of 3.63 Astra calls and 80.84 seconds per episode. This is 34.62 points above the best published non-oracle baseline and 4.95 points below GroundSG+Oracle, which gives the low-level policy ground-truth subgoals.

</details>


### [303] [Beyond Terminal Constraints: Reliable Robot Control with Learned Safe Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06196)
*Lunardi, Gianni,Fontanari, Elias,Saveriano, Matteo,Del Prete, Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出比经典控制不变集更宽的一类安全集族，通过在预测时域内约束多个节点（而非仅终端状态）的MPC方案保证递归可行性；安全集为适当控制不变集的内近似时可借助"安全中止"策略保证安全。6关节机械臂仿真与硬件实验验证，约束违反更少且追踪性能与计算效率相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC与强化学习等先进机器人控制的安全保障仍是开放问题；现有方法依赖控制不变集，但非线性系统的精确不变集一般不可解，数值近似又会削弱理论保证。

Method: 不再单纯依赖控制不变性：(1) 定义若干比经典控制不变集更宽的新安全集类别；(2) 设计相应的MPC方案，通过约束预测时域中多个节点（而非仅终端状态）的可行性条件来保证递归可行性；(3) 当安全集是合适控制不变集的内近似时，引入safe-abort策略，在缺乏递归可行性时仍保证安全。

Result: 在6关节机械臂上进行大量仿真，相比SOTA方法约束违反更少，同时保持可比的追踪精度与计算效率；硬件实验证明方案在真实机器人上可行。

Conclusion: 安全性不必须依赖控制不变性；更宽泛的安全集结合多节点约束MPC与safe-abort机制，可在放宽理论前提的同时提供安全保障，且具备真机可部署性。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in advanced robot control methods such as Model Predictive Control (MPC) and Reinforcement Learning remains an open challenge. A common approach relies on control-invariant sets, yet their exact computation for nonlinear systems is generally intractable, and numerical approximations compromise theoretical guarantees. In this work, we show that safety does not need to rely exclusively on control invariance. We introduce novel classes of safe sets, each broader than classical control-invariant sets, and analyze corresponding MPC schemes that guarantee recursive feasibility by constraining different nodes of the prediction horizon rather than only the terminal state. Furthermore, when the safe set is an inner approximation of a suitable control-invariant set, our methods can ensure safety through a safe-abort strategy, even in the absence of recursive feasibility. We validate our approaches in extensive simulations on a 6-joint robot manipulator, showing fewer constraint violations than state-of-the-art methods while maintaining comparable tracking performance and computational efficiency. Hardware experiments further demonstrate the practicality of our schemes for real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [304] [Riemannian Optimization-based Whole-body Obstacle Avoidance for Mobile Continuum Robots in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06214)
*Tang, Duo,Deng, Ping,Armanini, Sophie,Shen, Yajing,Fukuda, Toshio,Della Santina, Cosimo,Lu, Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向移动连续体机器人的统一规划-控制框架：全局层用A*结合最小加加速度规划生成平滑参考轨迹，局部层用几何一致的逆运动学求解器与黎曼优化协调移动基座与连续体变形，实现受限环境下的全身避障运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将高层路径规划与局部运动控制分离处理，导致基座-臂协同不足、运动学一致性差、碰撞避免困难；受蛇、象鼻、章鱼臂的全局运动与局部形状协同适应启发，需要统一规划与控制。

Method: 两层框架：全局层A*搜索+最小加加速度（minimum-jerk）规划生成光滑长时程参考轨迹；局部层在运动学与环境约束下进行实时几何细化，采用几何一致的逆运动学求解器与黎曼流形优化，联合优化移动基座运动与连续体变形以避障。

Result: 在仿真穿管任务中与多个基线规划器对比，并在真实世界实验中验证密集障碍导航与狭小空间操作；结果显示运动一致性、可行性与适应性均有提升。

Conclusion: 统一的分层规划-控制框架可支撑移动连续体机器人在受限环境中的协同全身运动，兼具长时程规划平滑性与实时局部几何可行性。

Abstract: Inspired by the coordination of global motion and local shape adaptation in snakes, elephant trunks, and octopus arms, we propose a unified planning and control framework for mobile continuum robots in cluttered and confined environments. Existing approaches often address high-level path planning and local motion control separately, limiting coordinated base-arm motion, kinematic consistency, and collision avoidance. Our framework integrates long-horizon trajectory planning with real-time geometric refinement under kinematic and environmental constraints. The global layer combines A* search with minimum-jerk planning to generate smooth reference trajectories. The local layer uses a geometry-consistent inverse kinematics solver and Riemannian optimization to coordinate mobile-base motion and continuum deformation while avoiding obstacles. We evaluate the framework through simulated pipe-traversal tasks with multiple baseline planners and real-world experiments involving navigation among dense obstacles and manipulation in confined spaces. Results demonstrate improved motion consistency, feasibility, and adaptability, supporting coordinated whole-body motion of mobile continuum robots in constrained environments.

</details>


### [305] [Corridor-Based Monitoring and Emergency Braking Decision for Automated Vehicle Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06287)
*Pan, Junnan,Nenchev, Vladislav,Mansharamani, Mohak,Sotiriadis, Prodromos,Englberger, Ferdinand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于自动驾驶冗余安全通道的紧急制动决策方法：以预测运动走廊（PMCM，依据规划路径曲率与车辆几何构造包络边界）过滤激光雷达测量，再用GDETA对边界内目标独立评估几何占据与时序紧迫性，硬门限+软OR仲裁并结合连续多帧确认，最后在碰撞仿真、校园实测与蒙特卡洛场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆需要独立于主系统的冗余安全通道来判断是否紧急制动，而嵌入式硬件算力受限，要求方法既保守可靠又轻量实时。

Method: 1) PMCM：根据规划路径与车辆几何构造曲率感知的走廊边界，保证直道与弯道下均覆盖完整扫掠车身；2) 用该走廊过滤LiDAR点云；3) GDETA：将几何占据度与时序紧迫度解耦打分，任一来源结论明确时经硬门限触发制动，两者均不确定时用软OR仲裁融合部分证据；4) 连续扫描确认机制抑制瞬时测量噪声导致的误触发。

Result: 走廊覆盖性在受控碰撞仿真与实车校园行驶数据上评估；制动决策在前车制动与横向穿越两类遭遇的蒙特卡洛仿真中评估（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 该方法为受限算力下的安全冗余通道提供了可解释、可分解的制动决策框架，但评估以仿真为主，实车证据局限于走廊覆盖，缺少真实闭环制动性能与安全指标数据。

Abstract: This paper addresses emergency-braking decisions for a redundant safety channel in automated vehicles. The channel is responsible for identifying hazards from sensor data and determining whether braking is necessary, given the constrained computational resources of embedded hardware. We present an emergency-braking approach that uses a predicted motion corridor to filter \ac{lidar} measurements and then decide whether the ego vehicle must brake. \ac{pmcm} constructs a curvature-aware boundary from the planned path and vehicle geometry so that the monitored region contains the full swept body on both straight and curved paths. \ac{gdeta} then decides whether the ego vehicle must command an emergency brake for objects inside this boundary, scoring geometric occupancy and temporal urgency independently. Hard gates permit braking when either source is conclusive; soft-OR arbitration combines partial evidence when neither gate is active. Consecutive-scan confirmation reduces sensitivity to transient measurement noise. Corridor coverage is evaluated in controlled collision simulations and recorded campus driving. Braking decisions are evaluated in Monte Carlo simulations of lead-vehicle braking and transverse-crossing encounters.

</details>


### [306] [Scale Anchoring via Implicit Semantics and Sequence-Consistent Loop Closure for VGGT-Based SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06308)
*Jin, Ziyi,Yu, Kangjin,Wang, Beichen,Jiang, Can,Xia, Zhongpu,Xu, Jie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VGGT-SLAM 2.0 扩展：固定根节点尺度锚定 + 短序列辅助的直接回环连接，用 SALAD 选图对与联合 VGGT 推理提供尺度锚，回环直接以单个 Sim(3) 因子连接中心帧节点，无需辅助回环子图；TUM 9 序列平均 Sim(3) ATE 由 0.1018 m 降至 0.0451 m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈几何基础模型可无标定做单目稠密重建，但独立重建的子图之间存在尺度漂移，即使有效的重访也可能产生跨子图不一致的测量；VD 需在不破坏原有重叠链的前提下抑制尺度漂移并高效闭合回环。

Method: 在 VGGT-SLAM 2.0 上加入固定根节点尺度锚定与短序列辅助的直接回环。用 SALAD 在初始子图与每个新子图间选图像对，联合 VGGT 推理利用隐式语义-几何线索给出仅尺度锚（保留原重叠链）；回环候选用相邻图像对做一致性检验与平移-尺度对齐，每个通过候选在已有中心帧节点间生成一个鲁棒 Sim(3) 因子，无需辅助回环子图。另做候选审计统计短序列检验拒绝的视觉重叠对。

Result: 17 序列对比中，9 条 TUM 序列平均 Sim(3) ATE 从基线 0.1018 m 降到 0.0451 m；锚定在全部 7 个子模块无回环消融中均有提升；3 个 Clio 场景的固定回环对比显示直接连接流程优于被测辅助子图流程；候选审计揭示短序列检验额外拒绝部分视觉重叠对，暴露验证环节的局限。

Conclusion: 将增益明确分解为长程尺度锚定与回环连接方式两部分，直接 Sim(3) 回环连接在保持精度的同时避免辅助子图开销，但候选验证仍需改进（存在误拒视觉重叠对）。

Abstract: Feed-forward geometric foundation models enable dense monocular reconstruction without requiring camera calibration, but independently reconstructed submaps can accumulate scale drift, and even a valid revisit can yield an inconsistent cross-submap measurement. We extend VGGT-SLAM 2.0 with fixed-root scale anchoring and short-sequence-assisted direct loop connections. SALAD selects an image pair between the initial submap and each eligible new submap; joint VGGT inference then uses learned implicit semantic-geometric cues to supply a scale-only anchor while preserving the original overlap chain. For loop closure, neighboring image pairs support a consistency check and translation-scale alignment.
