Each accepted candidate contributes one robust Sim(3) factor between existing center-frame nodes, without an auxiliary loop submap. A 17-sequence comparison lowers mean Sim(3) ATE on nine TUM sequences from 0.1018 to 0.0451 m relative to the VGGT-SLAM 2.0 baseline. Anchoring improves all seven no-loop module ablations. On three Clio scenes, a fixed-loop comparison favors the direct connection pipeline over the tested auxiliary-submap pipeline. A separate candidate audit exposes additional rejections of visually overlapping pairs by the short-sequence check. These comparisons distinguish the gains from long-range scale anchoring and loop connection choices from the limitations of candidate verification.

</details>


### [307] [Vortex-Induced Torque in Grasping Soft Objects: Influence of Geometry and Pneumatic Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06359)
*Mykhailyshyn, Roman,Motoda, Tomohiro,Virgala, Ivan,Fey, Ann Majewicz,Domae, Yukiyasu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: The work characterizes vortex-induced torque when a vortex gripper grasps compliant objects, quantifying the coupled effects of supply pressure, tangential nozzle diameter, surface geometry, and curvature, and shows that Gaussian Process Regression best predicts torque from combined pneumatic and geometric parameters.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vortex grippers are widely used for packaging, sorting, and assembly, but object–gripper interaction with compliant/soft objects—especially the transmitted vortex-induced torque—remains hard to predict, limiting dexterous manipulation of such objects.

Method: A series of experiments measuring against supply pressure, tangential nozzle diameter, surface geometry (dome vs. cylindrical, concave vs. convex) and curvature, followed by a comparison of multiple machine-learning regression models validated by repeated cross-validation to predict torque from pneumatic and geometric inputs.

Result: Increasing nozzle diameter mainly increases the torque's sensitivity to supply pressure rather than uniformly raising torque; dome-concave surfaces yield substantially larger torque than dome-convex, while cylindrical concave/convex differ little; the strongest, most repeatable response appeared for a dome-concave surface with favorable geometric matching; Gaussian Process Regression showed the best overall predictive performance.

Conclusion: Vortex-induced torque can be estimated from combined pneumatic and geometric parameters, providing a basis for predicting and exploiting torque in the dexterous manipulation of soft objects.

Abstract: Vortex grippers are commonly integrated into automated systems for handling objects with complex geometries in packaging, sorting, and assembling. However, their interaction with compliant objects remains difficult to predict, particularly with respect to the vortex-induced torque transmitted during grasping. This study investigates vortex-induced torque during the grasping of compliant objects and quantifies the coupled effects of supply pressure, tangential nozzle diameter, surface geometry, and curvature. The results show that increasing the tangential nozzle diameter primarily enhances the sensitivity of the generated torque to supply pressure rather than producing a uniformly higher torque. Surface geometry also strongly influences the rotational interaction, with dome-concave surfaces generally producing substantially greater torque than corresponding dome-convex surfaces, whereas the difference between cylindrical concave and convex surfaces is comparatively small. The strongest and most repeatable response was observed for a dome-concave surface exhibiting favorable object-gripper geometric matching. Among the evaluated machine-learning regression methods, Gaussian Process Regression provided the best overall predictive performance under repeated cross-validation, demonstrating that vortex-induced torque can be estimated from combined pneumatic and geometric parameters. These findings provide a basis for predicting and utilizing vortex-induced torque in the dexterous manipulation of soft objects.

</details>


### [308] [UniLoop: Unified Loop Closure Detection from Retrieval to Geometric Verification and Relative Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06378)
*Yu, Jingwen,Wang, Pengyu,Wu, Jin,Chen, Weinan,Tan, Ping,Zhang, Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniLoop统一了回环检测流程：复用预训练几何基础模型的层级特征，同时用于全局描述子检索、跨视角解码器验证和相对位姿约束，减少冗余主干计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回环检测将全局检索、几何验证、相对位姿估计串联为独立模型，导致计算冗余与视觉表示割裂。

Method: 共享图像编码器产出紧凑全局描述子；同一特征送入跨视角解码器做成对验证；原始几何预测提供相对相机约束用于位姿图优化。

Result: MSLS Recall@10 96.6、Pitts30k 99.3 为对比方法最佳；验证分支 95.63 AP、96.29 ROC-AUC，超越现有几何验证基线。

Conclusion: 统一特征复用可提升回环检测鲁棒性与精度并降低主干冗余计算，但文中缺少真实机器人端到端部署与实时性证据。

Abstract: Loop closure detection conventionally cascades separate models for global retrieval, geometric verification, and relative pose estimation, leading to redundant computation and disconnected visual representations. We propose UniLoop, a unified framework that reuses the hierarchical representations of the pretrained geometric foundation model throughout this pipeline. A shared image encoder produces features for compact global descriptors; the same features are passed to the cross-view decoder for pairwise verification; and the original geometric predictions provide relative camera constraints for pose-graph optimization. Experiments on place recognition, loop verification, and relative pose estimation show that UniLoop improves loop-closure robustness and accuracy while reducing redundant backbone computation relative to modular pipelines. UniLoop achieves the best Recall@10 on MSLS (96.6) and Pitts30k (99.3) among the compared methods, while its verification branch reaches 95.63 AP and 96.29 ROC-AUC, surpassing the existing geometric verification baselines.

</details>


### [309] [TwinBridge: Digital Twin-Driven Policy and Reward Transfer for Real-World Reinforcement Learning in Laboratory Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06442)
*Sun, Shu'ang,Qiu, Yibo,Ye, Haoliang,Huang, Zan,Tian, Ao,Ding, Kan,Xu, Ronald,Sun, Mingzhai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TwinBridge 用数字孪生（3D Gaussian Splatting + 物理仿真）为真实实验室操作任务提供策略初始化与稠密视觉奖励，实现实机在线强化学习，5 个任务上平均自主成功率 98%，每任务实机交互不超过 25 分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界演示采集成本高、稠密过程奖励难以获取，导致在线强化学习在精密实验室操作中难以落地；需要一种能同时提供策略初始化与稠密奖励、且不依赖昂贵真机数据的方案。

Method: 1) 基于 3D Gaussian Splatting 与物理仿真联合构建数字孪生；2) 在物理前置条件与操作顺序约束下，由仿真器状态给出任务事件与几何进度，生成仿真演示与仿真强化学习奖励，用于策略预训练；3) 将该监督蒸馏为视觉奖励模型，实机交互时结合执行历史预测结果计算稠密奖励，支持人在环策略精调。

Result: 在 5 个真实实验室任务上，平均自主成功率达 98%，每任务实机交互时间 ≤25 分钟；实验显示稠密奖励相比稀疏奖励显著提升交互效率，基于仿真的策略初始化进一步加速学习。

Conclusion: 数字孪生可作为真机在线的“奖励源+初始化器”，将仿真中的几何与事件级监督迁移到视觉奖励模型，从而以极低真机代价获得高成功率策略，为精密操作任务的 sim-to-real 强化学习提供可复用范式。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning offers an effective approach to precise laboratory manipulation, yet costly real-world demonstrations and limited access to dense process rewards hinder its practical application. We present TwinBridge, a digital twin-driven framework that supplies policy initialization and dense visual rewards for real-world online learning. We construct digital twins by combining 3D Gaussian splatting with physics simulation. Here, simulator states provide task events and geometric progress under physical prerequisites and ordering constraints, yielding rewards for policy pretraining with simulated demonstrations and reinforcement learning. We distill this supervision into a visual reward model. During real-world interaction, its predictions are combined with execution history to compute dense rewards for human-in-the-loop policy refinement. Across five real-world laboratory tasks, TwinBridge achieves a final mean autonomous success rate of 98% with at most 25 minutes of real-world interaction per task. Experiments show that dense rewards improve interaction efficiency over sparse rewards, and simulation-based policy initialization further accelerates learning.

</details>


### [310] [$A^3$-VLA: Automatic Perception-Guided Attention Alignment for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06447)
*Zhang, Shengzhe,Zhang, Qi,Shen, Dazhong,Wang, Chao,Zhai, Shaopeng,Xiong, Hui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A³-VLA derives task-specific perceptual guidance from interaction-induced visual changes (candidate target/robot regions, refined by a segmentation model) and injects it via an attention-allocation loss plus a region-language contrastive loss, improving several VLA backbones by 6.5%–25.2% success.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA models are trained with action-prediction objectives only, lacking fine-grained task-specific perceptual supervision, so they rely on task-irrelevant visual shortcuts and attend poorly to target objects and key interaction regions, hurting performance and generalization.

Method: Unsupervised extraction of heuristic candidate regions from interaction-induced visual change (with robot/target localization), offline refinement via a visual segmentation model, then two auxiliary objectives: (1) an attention-allocation loss enforcing VLM attention on task-critical regions when language or robot-state tokens query visual input; (2) a contrastive loss aligning candidate target region representations with language instructions and pushing apart task-irrelevant regions.

Result: Consistent gains across multiple VLA backbones on simulation and real-robot manipulation benchmarks, with average success-rate improvements of 6.5%–25.2%.

Conclusion: Task-intrinsic perceptual supervision—derived automatically from manipulation dynamics rather than human annotation—is an effective, backbone-agnostic way to align VLA attention with task-critical regions and improve manipulation success and generalization.

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action Models (VLAs) enable robots to perform manipulation tasks by leveraging Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to process visual observations and language instructions. However, the action prediction objective itself lacks fine-grained task-specific perceptual supervision, making it prone to relying on task-irrelevant visual shortcuts, leading attention away from target objects and key interaction areas, thereby limiting its operational performance and generalization ability. To address this, we propose Automatic Perception-Guided Attention Alignment for Robot Manipulation ($A^3$-VLA), a framework that derives task-specific visual guidance from the manipulation process itself. Specifically, $A^3$-VLA first generates heuristic candidate regions for target objects and the robot from interaction-induced visual changes, and refines them with a visual segmentation model offline. These regions serve as explicit perceptual guidance through two complementary objectives: an attention allocation loss that encourages sufficient focus on task-critical regions when language or robot state tokens query the visual input, and a contrastive learning loss that aligns the representations of candidate target regions with language instructions while separating them from task-irrelevant regions. Extensive experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that $A^3$-VLA consistently improves multiple VLA backbones, with an average success rate gain of 6.5% - 25.2% across benchmarks.

</details>


### [311] [Beyond Infraction Scores: Failure-Boundary Profiles for Closed-Loop Evaluation of Driving and Robot Navigation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06453)
*Bulat, Milan,Ardelean, Gino,Kirby, Ellington*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出CARLA闭环基准的驾驶分数(路线完成率×违规惩罚)无法区分"停车避障"与"带碰撞强行通过"两类行为，提出failure-boundary profiles：沿单一测量因子(走廊宽度、对向车间隙)变化的场景族中，用实测认证每个变体应当通行还是应停车，估计commitment(尝试通行概率)与competence(无接触通过概率)两条曲线及其0.5交叉点区间，并给出decision accuracy指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶基准的单一驾驶分数将"安全停车"与"鲁莽通过"混为一谈：在双车封堵走廊中，带碰撞穿过的agent得52.2，而停车agent仅37.8–40.6，即分数奖励了错误行为。需要一种诚实反映"该通行时通行、该停时停"的评估方式。

Method: 按场景族构造单因子扫描(如走廊宽度、对向车时隙)，每个变体由实测认证为可通行/阻塞两类标签；沿该轴为每个agent估计commitment与competence概率曲线，定位其与0.5的交叉点，并给出考虑仿真器run-to-run方差的置信区间；以decision accuracy汇总(可通变体上无接触通过、阻塞变体上停车)，在7个CARLA族上评测7个驾驶agent，并原样迁移到Isaac Sim中的仓储机器人。

Result: 规则专家decision accuracy为0.74，得分最高的学习型agent TFv6仅0.60；驾驶分数相近的agent失效模式各异，能在阻塞走廊可靠停车的agent仍极少在对向车流中等待间隙。机器人侧，所测导航策略仅在3个族中的1个能停于阻塞间隙，两个微调版本通过更多间隙但完全不再停车。

Conclusion: 单一驾驶分数掩盖了"该停/该过"决策能力的差异，failure-boundary profile提供了可认证、可比较且跨域(车辆与机器人)通用的诊断性评估；同时暴露出通行与停车能力之间的此消彼长。

Abstract: Closed-loop driving benchmarks in CARLA rank agents by a driving score that multiplies route completion by an infraction penalty. This score does not distinguish an agent that stops in front of an obstacle from one that pushes past it. In a corridor closed by two parked cars, for example, one agent that drives through with contact averages 52.2 on the blocked widths, while the agents that stop average between 37.8 and 40.6. We propose failure-boundary profiles, an evaluation in which each scenario family varies one measured factor, such as corridor width or the time gap in oncoming traffic, and each variant is certified from measurement as one where the agent should proceed or one where it should stop. Along this axis we estimate two curves per agent, commitment (the probability of attempting the passage) and competence (the probability of passing without contact), and locate where each crosses one half, with intervals that account for the simulator's run-to-run variance. The certified labels also give a summary metric, decision accuracy, which credits clean passes on passable variants and stops on blocked ones. We evaluate seven driving agents on seven CARLA families. The rule-based expert reaches a decision accuracy of 0.74 and TFv6, the highest-scoring learned agent, 0.60. Agents with similar driving scores fail in different ways, and agents that reliably stop at a blocked corridor still rarely wait for a gap in oncoming traffic. We also apply the method, unchanged, to a warehouse robot in Isaac Sim. The navigation policy we test stops at blocked gaps in only one of three families, and two fine-tuned versions of it pass more gaps cleanly but no longer stop at all.

</details>


### [312] [Robocasa-Mem: Benchmarking Environment and Process Memory in Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06475)
*Chen, Sihang,Ye, Chen,Chen, Wei,Lei, Xiaohan,Tang, Zhipeng,Zhang, Sha,Zhang, Yuan,Chen, Zixuan,Deng, Jiajun,Li, Houqiang,Zhang, Yanyong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboCasa-Mem 基准（9 个依赖记忆的移动操作任务，分环境记忆与过程记忆）及 Behavior-Conditioned Evaluation 评测方式，并提供 Hierarchical Memory-Augmented Policy (HMAP) 基线（高层结构化记忆规划 + 低层 VGGT-Omega 几何增强 VLA 控制）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动操作基准对"依赖已不可观测的任务相关信息（交互历史）进行多步决策"的评估不足，缺乏对记忆依赖行为的系统评测手段。

Method: 构建 RoboCasa-Mem：九个移动操作任务，分环境记忆（空间位置、物体属性、物体关系）与过程记忆（单任务进度、多任务调度）；提出 Behavior-Conditioned Evaluation，对多解任务由机器人早期动作推导后续约束以评测行为一致性并抑制固定目标的捷径；提供 1,800 条成功示范用于策略训练；基线 HMAP 采用层级结构：高层规划器用结构化实体/过程记忆生成子任务，低层 VLA 控制器用子目标条件查询注入 VGGT-Omega 几何信息生成动作。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅报告基准、评测协议、1,800 条示范数据与 HMAP 基线的建立。

Conclusion: RoboCasa-Mem 与 HMAP 为记忆依赖的移动操作行为提供系统评测与记忆感知策略开发基础。

Abstract: In the real world, mobile manipulation robots must perform complex object interactions and make multi-step decisions, often relying on task-relevant information that is no longer observable. However, existing benchmarks provide limited evaluation of such memory-dependent decision-making in mobile manipulation. To address this gap, we introduce RoboCasa-Mem, a new benchmark comprising nine mobile manipulation tasks that require agents to use interaction history at key decision points. Specifically, the tasks are grouped into two categories of memory: One is environment memory, which captures spatial locations, object attributes, and object relationships. The other is process memory, which captures single-task progress and multi-task scheduling. Particularly, we introduce Behavior-Conditioned Evaluation. For tasks with multiple valid execution paths, instead of fixing a single target or trajectory, the evaluator derives later task constraints from the robot's earlier actions. This allows us to evaluate whether later behavior is consistent with the robot's own interaction history while reducing shortcuts based on fixed target assignments. We provide 1,800 successful demonstrations for policy training. We also develop a baseline on our benchmark, namely Hierarchical Memory-Augmented Policy(HMAP). Its high-level planner uses structured entity and process memories to generate subtasks. Its low-level VLA controller uses subgoal-conditioned queries to incorporate VGGT-Omega geometry into action generation. Together, RoboCasa-Mem and HMAP baseline support systematic evaluation of memory-dependent behavior and the development of memory-aware mobile manipulation policies.

</details>


### [313] [H^2MARL: Hierarchical Heterogeneous Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning for Humanoid Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06523)
*Hu, Xuemin,Li, Kaixin,Xia, Qingqing,Wang, Yuxin,Xie, Wei,Tang, Bo,Chen, Long*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 H^2MARL 分层异构多智能体强化学习框架：以 JDoF（单关节自由度）级"物理异构感知"策略分解建模人形全身控制，并用闭环策略-执行器协同优化减少高层规划与底层执行的分布失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身控制存在高维耦合动力学与关节自由度间的显著物理异构性。现有多智能体 RL 方法仅做粗粒度智能体划分或直接套用通用异构智能体算法；分层控制器又常将高层规划与底层执行分开优化，导致动作不协调。

Method: 1) 将每个力矩驱动 JDoF 视为一个智能体，并以显式描述符编码其驱动、运动学与动力学属性，实现物理异构感知的策略分解；2) 闭环策略-执行器协同优化机制，利用共享交互轨迹联合调整高层位姿规划器与低层关节执行器，缩小规划-执行分布偏差。

Result: 在 MuJoCo 与 Isaac Gym 上的大量实验表明，相较同构、粗粒度以及独立优化基线，H^2MARL 在运动稳定性、步态协调性与动作质量上均稳定提升。

Conclusion: JDoF 级异构建模与规划-执行闭环联合优化是提升人形全身协调控制的有效途径；但验证仅限仿真，尚缺实机部署证据。

Abstract: Coordinated whole-body control of humanoid robots remains challenging due to high-dimensional coupled dynamics and substantial physical heterogeneity across joint degrees of freedom (JDoFs). Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning methods either employ coarse agent partitions or directly apply generic heterogeneous-agent algorithms, while hierarchical controllers commonly optimize high-level planning and low-level execution independently, leading to uncoordinated motion. In this paper, we propose H^2MARL, a hierarchical heterogeneous multi-agent reinforcement learning framework with two key mechanisms. We first formulate humanoid whole-body control using a JDoF-level physical-heterogeneity-aware policy factorization, where each torque-actuated JDoF is conditioned on an explicit descriptor of its actuation, kinematic, and dynamic properties. Moreover, we develop a closed-loop policy-executor co-optimization mechanism that jointly adapts the high-level pose planner and the low-level joint executor using shared interaction trajectories, thereby reducing the distribution mismatch between planning and execution. Extensive experiments in MuJoCo and Isaac Gym demonstrate that H^2MARL consistently improves locomotion stability, gait coordination, and motion quality over homogeneous, coarse-grained, and independently optimized alternatives.

</details>


### [314] [Playing Soccer Like Humans Do: Learning Versatile and Agile Humanoid Soccer via Latent Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06564)
*Shao, Haoyu,Wang, Yunshen,Zhang, Jingze,Zhu, Shaohang,Li, Yong-Lu,Zhang, Tianzhu,Jia, Baoxiong,Huang, Siyuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: soccerbot：以大规模人类足球动捕数据蒸馏结构化潜空间运动先验，构建分层强化学习框架，在动力学可行的运动流形内训练单一技能条件策略，统一实现移动、带球、停球、踢球，并零样本迁移至Unitree G1真实人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人足球需在连续动态行为谱上融合敏捷运动、控球与冲击性接触动力学。现有方法要么只覆盖有限技能，要么完全舍弃运动先验导致动作不自然，尚未同时解决"统一技能集"与"类人运动质量"的问题。

Method: 分层强化学习：底层为从大规模人类足球动捕数据蒸馏得到的结构化潜运动先验（潜空间运动流形），上层为技能条件策略，将策略学习约束在该动力学合理的流形内；配合面向鲁棒性的仿真设计（域随机化、扰动/接触建模等）实现sim-to-real。

Result: 单一策略在统一公式下学会移动、带球、停球与踢球，技能切换时产生流畅且类人的过渡；零样本迁移至Unitree G1真机，在真实环境中完成全部足球技能集，表现出敏捷、自然与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将动捕运动先验作为策略学习的结构化约束，可同时获得技能统一性与类人运动质量，是人形机器人全身接触型任务（含冲击性球体交互）的可扩展范式；但缺乏定量指标与代码公开。

Abstract: Humanoid soccer requires a robot to seamlessly combine agile locomotion, ball manipulation, and impact-rich contact dynamics across a continuous, dynamic behavioral spectrum. Yet existing approaches either cover only a limited set of skills, or forego motion priors entirely and produce unnatural behaviors, leaving the combination of a unified skill set and human-like motion quality unaddressed. We present soccerbot, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that resolves both limitations through a structured latent motion prior distilled from large-scale human soccer motion capture data. By constraining policy learning within this dynamically plausible motion manifold, a single skill-conditioned policy naturally learns to perform moving, dribbling, trapping, and kicking in a unified formulation, while inherently producing human-like motion and fluid transitions during commanded skill switching. Augmented by robustness-oriented simulation design, soccerbot transfers zero-shot to the Unitree G1 humanoid robot, demonstrating agile, natural, and robust execution of the full soccer skill repertoire under real-world conditions.

</details>


### [315] [FOSCo-Net: Ego-centric Voxel Completion for Forest LiDAR Occlusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06588)
*Esfandiyar, Iman,Belter, Dominik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自监督体素补全框架 FOSCo-Net，用单帧激光雷达扫描重建被树干遮挡的几何，以时序累积观测作自动监督，并比较柱坐标/笛卡尔自车网格与多种解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温带森林中地面机器人受树干造成的系统性 LiDAR 遮挡，形成持续盲区，损害可通行性估计。

Method: 自监督体素几何补全：以单次扫描为输入，时序累积多次观测作为监督标签；系统对比柱面与笛卡尔自车中心网格表示，以及多种解码器（最佳为门控跳跃连接解码器）。

Result: 重建质量与通行安全性稳定提升；门控跳跃连接解码器表现最可靠，显著降低误判障碍物（假障碍预测）数量。

Conclusion: 自监督时序监督可行地缓解森林 LiDAR 遮挡盲区，网格表示与解码器设计对补全质量有实质影响。

Abstract: Ground robots operating in temperate forests suffer from systematic LiDAR occlusions caused by tree trunks, creating persistent blind regions that degrade traversability estimation. In this paper, we propose FOSCo-Net, a self-supervised voxel completion framework that learns to reconstruct occluded geometry from a single scan using temporally accumulated observations as automatic supervision, and we evaluate cylindrical and Cartesian ego-centric grids with multiple decoder designs. FOSCo-Net consistently improves reconstruction quality and traversal safety, with a gated skip decoder achieving the most reliable performance and significantly reducing false obstacle predictions compared to baselines.

</details>


### [316] [Dynamic Shape Control of Soft Trunk Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06625)
*Zheng, Hehui,Stampanoni, Marc,Kaundinya, Roshan S.,Hinchet, Ronan,Haller, George,Katzschmann, Robert K.*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于数据学习的绝热谱子流形（aSSM）全身模型 + MPC，实现修剪螺旋面软躯干机器人硬件上的全身形状控制，闭环形状调节性能超线性基线10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 螺旋面几何的软躯干机器人具有极强形状变化能力，但高度非线性导致基于模型的控制困难；已有 aSSM 控制器仅在相似肌腱驱动躯干上实现末端执行器跟踪，缺乏全身形状控制手段。

Method: 从硬件数据学习修剪螺旋面软躯干机器人全身的 aSSM 降维谱子流形模型，并将其嵌入模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于形状调节与人机交互任务。

Result: 闭环动态形状调节优于线性基线约10倍；分段塑形与网球平衡（>25 s）成功，线性基线失败；训练12小时的免模型强化学习策略在同一平衡任务上失败。

Conclusion: aSSM-based MPC 是软体硬件上实现全身形状控制的实用路径，模型驱动的降维谱方法显著优于线性基线与无模型RL。

Abstract: Controlling the shape profile of soft trunk robots extends their reach into complex regions of the workspace and enhances their use in practical applications. Recent advances in soft robot design have enabled soft trunk robots to achieve greater flexibility in conforming to complex shapes. Specifically, trunk robots whose geometries are designed as helicoids have been shown to enable extreme shape-changing capabilities. Their compliance makes them highly nonlinear, which in turn makes shape control difficult. On similar tendon-driven trunk robots, model-based controllers based on the theory of adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs) have achieved high accuracy in closed-loop end-effector tracking. We extend this approach by learning an aSSM model of the full body of a trimmed-helicoid soft trunk robot from data and use this in a model predictive control (MPC) scheme to demonstrate a range of shape control tasks on hardware. On closed-loop dynamic shape regulation, our method outperforms the available linear baselines by a factor of 10. In human-in-the-loop interactive demos, our method completes section-by-section shaping and balances a tennis ball for over 25 seconds, while the linear baselines fall short. A model-free reinforcement learning policy trained for 12 hours on hardware fails on the same balancing task. These results establish aSSM-based MPC as a practical route to full-body shape control on real soft hardware.

</details>


### [317] [SCRUB: Contact-Based Surface-Frame Estimation for Force-Motion Control with an Armless Quadruped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06649)
*Selvaraj, Sourav,Chessal, Juan Pablo Espinosa,Agheli, Mahdi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCRUB: 基于接触的"表面坐标系"估计器，通过力线索与速度线索的卡尔曼滤波给四足机器人提供墙面法向，从而在未知、近竖直表面上用力控滑动工具完成擦洗/打磨/检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 擦洗、打磨、检测机器人在未知无地图表面上以受控力按压并滑动工具，需要表面法向；现有法向估计器需摩擦模型或假设工具瞬时速度沿表面切向，而行走步态会违反该假设，无法用于无臂四足的机身贴附作业。

Method: 提出SCRUB接触式表面坐标系估计器，用卡尔曼滤波融合两路线索估计法向：(1) 力线索——测量估计偏差在运动方向上的倾斜，若摩擦直接反向于运动，则无论摩擦系数大小，当且仅当偏差为零时该线索为零；但若滑动方向判错，摩擦会表现为虚假倾斜；(2) 速度线索——测得沿运动方向的倾斜，因行走与按压造成工具进出运动，故从工具速度中减去指令按压速率，再对每个步态周期取平均，视该均值为沿表面方向。控制器据此预测性地按压并滑动工具。

Result: 在Unitree Go1硬件上、标称速度与负载下，SCRUB完成全部墙面横移与柱面绕行任务；移除任一线索、去除步态平均、或改用固定法向时，均无一次绕行成功。

Conclusion: 无需已知、轴对齐或视觉预测法向，仅凭接触力与步态平均后的速度即可在近竖直未知表面实现力控滑动；力线索与速度线索互补、步态周期平均缺一不可，构成无臂四足贴壁作业的关键感知-控制组件。

Abstract: Wiping, sanding, and inspection robots press a sliding tool on a surface with controlled force. Controlling that force needs the surface normal, which is unknown on real-world, unmapped surfaces. Surface-normal estimators can help estimate the surface normal, but they either need a friction model or assume that the instantaneous tool velocity lies along the surface; however, walking violates this assumption. We propose SCRUB, a contact-based surface-frame estimator, which lets an armless quadruped slide a body-mounted tool along near-vertical surfaces in the absence of known, axis-aligned, or vision-predicted normals. SCRUB's predictive controller presses and slides the tool using a Kalman-filter estimate of the normal from two cues: 1) A force cue measures the estimate's tilt across the motion. If friction directly opposes the motion, the cue is zero exactly when that tilt is zero, for any friction coefficient. However, if the cue's sliding direction is wrong, friction appears as a false tilt. 2) A velocity cue measures tilt along the motion. Walking and pressing move the tool in and out. SCRUB therefore subtracts the commanded press rate from the tool velocity, averages the result over each gait cycle, and treats this mean as lying along the surface. On Unitree Go1 hardware at nominal speed and load, SCRUB completed every wall traverse and column orbit. No orbit completed with either cue removed, without gait averaging, or with a fixed normal.

</details>


### [318] [Detecting Silent Action-Interface Mismatch with Calibrated Relational World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06653)
*Zhu, Rui,Li, Xurui,Tang, Haixu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将学习到的状态-动作-效应关系作为离线契约，用于检测机器人动作的语义失配（轴置换、符号反转、旋转缩放、夹爪极性反转）并定位可能的出错字段；在LIBERO Spatial十个任务上，以2.5%误报率比较了ridge前向残差（46.1%召回）、四分量联合高斯混合（61.0%）及其精确条件分布（65.7%），联合评分定位率95.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作可能在满足形状与范围约束的情况下语义错误，常规约束检查无法发现；作者希望研究能否用学习到的状态-动作-效应关系作为离线诊断契约，检测此类失配并定位出错字段。

Method: 在干净留出转移上校准告警阈值，采用固定逆词表实现闭集定位；以容量受控的方式对比三种打分器：ridge前向残差、四分量联合高斯混合的联合密度、同一混合模型的精确条件密度，在2.5%经验干净误报率下进行匹配率比较。

Result: LIBERO Spatial十个任务432个源回合上，召回率分别为ridge 46.1%、联合高斯混合61.0%、条件密度65.7%；联合评分较ridge高14.9个百分点，条件化再提升4.7个百分点。定位率联合评分为95.8%，ridge为94.6%。Object/Goal任务可扩展，但DROID、HYDRA三任务、短时故障、小偏移、精确修复与模拟缓解均未通过门槛，xArm仅贡献一个任务。

Conclusion: 证据仅支持一个有边界的离线诊断工具及检测-定位权衡关系，不支持"联合密度优越性"或跨机器人安全性结论；匹配率比较属回顾性诊断，并非部署校准保证。

Abstract: A robot action can satisfy shape and range constraints yet carry the wrong semantics: axes may be permuted, signs inverted, rotations rescaled, or gripper polarity reversed. We study learned state-action-effect relations as offline contracts for detecting these mismatches and ranking their likely fields. Clean held-out transitions calibrate alarms; a fixed inverse vocabulary supports closed-set localization. On ten LIBERO Spatial tasks comprising 432 source episodes, a capacity-controlled comparison at 2.5% empirical clean false-positive rate gives 46.1% recall for a ridge forward residual, 61.0% for a four-component joint Gaussian mixture, and 65.7% for the exact conditional of that same mixture. The joint score retains a 14.9 percentage-point advantage over ridge, while conditioning improves recall by a further 4.7 points. Joint scoring instead has higher localization: 95.8% versus 94.6%. These matched-rate comparisons are retrospective diagnostics, not deployment calibration guarantees. Earlier Object/Goal results broaden tasks within the LIBERO convention; DROID, all three HYDRA tasks, short temporal faults, small offsets, exact repair, and simulated mitigation fail their respective gates, while xArm contributes one task only. The evidence supports a bounded offline diagnostic and a detection-localization tradeoff, rather than joint-density superiority or cross-robot safety.

</details>


### [319] [Compounding Physical RSI: Turning Execution Failure into Inherited Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06654)
*Yang, Yixuan,Chen, Qiang,Shao, Run,Dong, Shuwei,Li, Wenhao,Wang, Yunpeng,Fang, Wenhan,Wu, Haomiao,Wang, Xingyu,Man, Lichen,Zhang, Rong,Cui, Xuezhi,Liu, Yuan,Lu, Bingyan,Ma, Jiawei,Ma, Xiaoyang,Sun, Zhemin,Yao, Zhuorui,Dong, Yutong,Zhao, Yuxuan,Mu, Yichong,Jiang, Jiabao,Deng, Wenxuan,Zhou, Junling,Chen, Yi,Zhang, Deyu,Yan, Yiming,Yuan, Yige,You, Shan,Wang, Fei,Li, Haifeng,Xu, Hongyan,Cao, Yichao,Su, Xiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Compounding Physical RSI（CSU-AI-07）自进化策略框架：通过自动化阶段级能力缺口诊断、行为正则化离线RL的阶段化补救、以及权重空间候选评估的技能保持，将执行失败转化为可累积的技能增益，在 RoboDojo-Sim 42 个任务上以 Overall 26.15、成功率 20.70% 位列榜首。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略通常在静态离线数据上训练，而物理操作是多阶段序列展开的：下游阶段的弱点只有在通过前序阶段后才能暴露，因此每解决一个瓶颈就会浮现下一个隐藏失败模式。这种分阶段结构天然适合递归自我改进（RSI），且整体成功率近似为各阶段通过率之积，递归改进可在多代之间乘性放大。但物理环境中存在三重障碍：终端反馈难以定位失败阶段、失败轨迹不包含纠正动作、共享参数更新会遗忘已掌握技能。

Method: 三阶段框架：① 自动能力缺口评估——将失败 rollout 回溯到进度中断的具体任务阶段，并生成结构化改进目标；② 补救阶段——以该目标为条件筛选经过验证的阶段相关经验，用行为正则化离线强化学习精炼策略，无需每步在线 rollout；③ 保持阶段——在继承的参考策略与精炼策略之间构造权重空间插值候选，在全任务套件上评估并选出最佳候选作为下一代参考状态。

Result: 在 RoboDojo-Sim 全部 42 个任务上评测，三 seed 平均 Overall Score 26.15、成功率 20.70%，排行榜第一，领先次优方案 5.92 分与 5.82 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将 RSI 的“阶段-诊断-修正”循环与权重空间技能保持结合，可在离线数据约束下实现跨代可累积的机器人策略自我提升；但证据限于仿真基准排行榜，绝对成功率仍偏低（约 20%），真实机器人迁移与长期递归稳定性有待验证。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies are trained on static offline datasets, yet physical manipulation unfolds sequentially across stages. Because a weakness in a downstream stage remains unobservable until the policy passes earlier ones, each resolved bottleneck exposes the next hidden failure mode. This staged structure calls for Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI), in which a policy iteratively executes, diagnoses failures, and updates itself. Moreover, because task completion behaves like a product of stage-level pass rates, recursive gains can multiply across generations. In physical environments, three bottlenecks obstruct compounding: terminal execution feedback rarely localizes the stage that caused a failure, failed trajectories do not supply the corrective action, and updating shared parameters can degrade previously mastered skills. We propose Compounding Physical RSI, a self-evolving policy framework that turns execution failures into heritable capability. First, automated capability-gap assessment traces failed rollouts to the task stage where progression breaks down and formulates a structured improvement objective. Second, conditioned on this objective, our remedy stage curates validated, stage-relevant experience and refines the policy via behavior-regularized offline reinforcement learning, without requiring costly online rollouts at each optimization step. Third, to safeguard existing skills, our retention stage constructs weight-space candidates between the inherited reference policy and the refined policy, evaluates them across the task suite, and promotes the best candidate as the reference state for the next generation. Evaluated across all 42 tasks of the RoboDojo-Sim benchmark, CSU-AI-07 ranks first on the leaderboard with a three-seed mean Overall Score of 26.15 and Success Rate of 20.70%, outperforming the next-best entry by 5.92 points and 5.82 percentage points.

</details>


### [320] [Which Physical Tactile Cues Matter for Contact-Rich Imitation Learning? A Controlled Real-Robot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06673)
*Qin, Xuhao,Huang, Xiyan,Dong, Haoyu,Zhang, Xi,Xiao, Chenxi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在真实机器人上用受控实验比较了同一视触觉流分解出的三种物理可解释触觉信号（RGB 外观图、法向/深度图、标记点流场）对策略学习的影响，并比较了触觉编码器与视觉-触觉融合策略，720 次真机试验得出四条结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉策略通常直接把触觉观测当图像输入，难以判断究竟是哪一类物理线索（接触外观、几何形变、切向位移）在贡献策略性能，缺乏对触觉表征的消融式理解。

Method: 基于 LeRobot 平台并增强自研视触觉感知系统，从同一触觉图像流中解耦出 RGB 接触图像、法向/深度图、标记点流场三种表征；在相同示范数据与相同策略骨干下，分别评估单一线索与组合线索的贡献，并对比不同触觉编码器与视觉-触觉融合方式（含 cross-attention）。

Result: 720 次真机试验显示：(1) 触觉感知将总成功率由 57.8% 提升至 78.9%，但贡献随任务阶段变化；(2) 标记点流是单一最有效线索，成功率 74.4%，在已建立接触的调控阶段尤其关键；(3) 按物理线索因子化编码可稳定提升性能，说明不同线索需定制化处理；(4) 触觉融合效果依赖任务，但 cross-attention 以轻量代价达到与预训练触觉编码器相当的性能。

Conclusion: 视触觉信息应被显式分解为物理可解释的子线索并分别编码，而非笼统地作为图像处理；标记点流与 cross-attention 融合是性价比最高的默认选择。该工作提供了真机规模的触觉表征消融证据，对触觉操作策略设计具指导意义。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors capture multiple aspects of physical interaction within a single image stream, including contact appearance, geometry, indentation depth, and tangential deformation. However, existing visuo-tactile policies often directly process tactile observations as images, making it difficult to determine which physical cues contribute to policy learning. This paper presents a controlled real-robot study that compares physically interpretable tactile signals using the LeRobot platform, enhanced with our visuo-tactile perception system. From the same tactile stream, we derive tactile RGB images, normal/depth maps, and marker-flow fields, representing contact appearance, geometry, and tangential deformation, respectively. Using identical task demonstrations and the same policy backbone, we evaluate the individual and combined contributions of these tactile cues. We further compare tactile encoders and visuo-tactile fusion strategies. Evaluation across 720 real-robot trials yields four key findings: (1) tactile perception substantially improves the overall success rate from 57.8% to 78.9%, although its contribution varies across task stages; (2) marker flow is the most effective individual cue, achieving a success rate of 74.4% and playing a particularly important role in regulating established contact; (3) cue-factorized encoding consistently improves performance, highlighting that different physical cues require tailored processing techniques; and (4) although the effectiveness of tactile fusion is task-dependent, cross-attention offers a lightweight solution with performance comparable to that of pretrained tactile encoders.

</details>


### [321] [Cognitive and Behavioural Effects of Haptic Feedback for ROV Teleoperation in Hazardous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06689)
*Brown, Joshua,Farkhatdinov, Ildar,Jenkin, Michael*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过真实水下RROV实验，考察力触觉反馈（软体振动触控板传递环境信息）对操作员在未知横流中作业的认知负荷与驾驶行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂水下环境中遥操作ROV面临三维动态障碍与不可见水流，设备昂贵且失事后难以回收，亟需在不增加认知负担的前提下提升操作员对隐性危险的感知与安全性。

Method: 开展实水（in-water）被试研究：ROV在受控横流条件下运行，通过软体振动触控板以触觉通道呈现环境/流场信息；对有无触觉反馈、有无横流等条件进行主观量表（NASA-TLX类认知负荷）与客观驾驶速度/时间指标的对比分析。

Result: 未知横流显著提高操作员的体力与时间需求；加入触觉反馈可缓解这些负荷并提升自评表现。触觉条件反而带来最高的心理需求与挫败感，但操作员在触觉提示下驾驶速度显著更快，表明信心更高。

Conclusion: 触觉反馈初期需要更多认知资源来解读不可见危险，但能有效增强情境意识并促成更自信的导航，为水下遥操作安全提供了一种人机交互层面的改进路径。

Abstract: Teleoperating a Remotely Operated Vehicle (ROV) in complex underwater environments presents unique challenges. Dynamic, three dimensional obstacles and unseen water currents pose real risks to expensive robotic hardware in environments in which recovery can be next to impossible. This paper explores the cognitive effects of haptic feedback to improve vehicle safety in such environments. An in-water study is analysed with a ROV exposed to controlled cross-currents, with environmental information presented via a soft and vibrating haptic touchpad. Results demonstrate that while encountering an unseen cross-current significantly increases an operator's physical and temporal demand, the introduction of haptic feedback mitigates these factors and improves self-assessed performance. Interestingly, the haptic condition also produced the highest levels of mental demand and operator frustration. Despite this increased mental workload, operators drove the ROV significantly faster when receiving haptic cues compared to the current-only condition, indicating a higher level of user confidence. Ultimately, these findings suggest that while haptic feedback may initially require more cognitive processing to interpret unseen hazards, it successfully enhances operator awareness and promotes more confident navigation in hazardous environments.

</details>


### [322] [When to Ask for Help: Uncertainty-Triggered Aerial Refinement for Off-Road Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06702)
*Samineni, Pranay,Thorpe, Adam,Chubick, John,Balakrishnan, Anand,Ellis, Christian,Chaudhuri, Swarat,Topcu, Ufuk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"规划器条件化"空地协同方法：以 MPPI rollout 在不确定地图下与已解析假设下的预测改善量作为是否请求无人机航测的判据，实现按需空中感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机空中感知可弥补地面车辆视域与量程不足，但何时请求辅助、应采集何种信息难以决策；固定支撑角色、任务结构或单纯不确定性驱动的现有方法无法保证辅助对地面导航真正有益。

Method: 在每个规划周期，评估地图不确定性对当前 MPPI 采样的影响；若导航性能仍退化，则在多个"已解析"地图假设下重评同一组 rollout，选出带来最大有用提升的地图层，请求对应空中量测，融合返回信息后重新规划。

Result: 三条越野路线共180次仿真试验：目标到达率98.9%，平均无人机飞行时间相比持续贪心空中建图减少87.7%；聚合统计每1秒无人机飞行时间可节省2.94秒UGV导航时间；约2 km实车实验验证了不确定性消解与导航恢复。

Conclusion: 以"信息是否提升预测导航质量"作为价值函数，可显著降低空中感知开销并保持高成功率，适用于异构空地协同的按需主动感知。

Abstract: Heterogeneous air-ground teams can use aerial sensing to address the limited range and viewpoint of ground-vehicle sensors, but deciding when the UGV needs assistance and what information the UAV should collect remains a challenge. This paper presents a planner-conditioned air-ground assistance method that evaluates how uncertain map information affects the UGV's current MPPI rollouts. When navigation remains degraded, we score the same rollouts under resolved map hypotheses, select the map layer with the largest useful improvement, request the corresponding aerial measurement, fuse the returned information, and replan. Unlike approaches driven by fixed support roles, task structure, or uncertainty alone, our method conditions assistance on whether resolving uncertainty improves predicted ground navigation. Across 180 simulation trials on three off-road routes, the method achieves 98.9% goal attainment while reducing mean UAV flight time by 87.7% relative to continuous greedy aerial mapping. Aggregated across the routes, each second of estimated UAV flight time saves 2.94 s of UGV navigation time. Field experiments spanning approximately 2 km of UGV operation demonstrate uncertainty resolution and successful navigation recovery.

</details>


### [323] [Where Is VLA Inference Time Spent? Bottlenecks and Optimization Interactions Across GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06710)
*Waseem, Muhammad Danish,Hassan, Ahmed Ali-Eldin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对七类VLA推理优化方法（CUDA Graphs、编译、token剪枝、量化等）在七种SOTA VLA模型、四类GPU（H100到Jetson Thor）上进行系统测量研究，分析CPU启动开销、内存带宽与核效率如何共同决定优化收益，并提出VLA-Triage堆叠优化指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型因大骨干网络与重复动作生成导致推理延迟超出机器人实时要求；现有优化（量化、剪枝、CUDA Graphs、编译等）多在单一GPU、少数模型上报告，缺乏对真实瓶颈及跨硬件泛化性的分析。

Method: 对七类推理优化方法（CUDA Graphs、编译、token剪枝、量化等）在七种SOTA VLA与四种GPU（数据中心H100至嵌入式Jetson Thor）上进行全面测量研究，拆解CPU启动开销、内存带宽、核效率对加速比的贡献，并分析方法间的交互效应，据此归纳出VLA-Triage延迟预算下的优化堆叠准则。

Result: 揭示了不同硬件上VLA的真实瓶颈来源及优化收益的差异与相互影响，形成可指导优化组合选择与部署模式验证的VLA-Triage指南（摘要未给出具体数值加速比）。

Conclusion: VLA推理优化不能靠单一方法泛化，需按硬件瓶颈识别可提供所需节省的流水阶段，并在实际部署执行模式下验证组合效果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map camera observations and language instructions to robot actions. However, their large backbones and repeated action generation can make inference take longer than the robot's real-time requirements for its next action. To reduce the load of VLA computations, researchers have proposed multiple optimization approaches, including quantization and pruning. These optimizations can be combined with other general optimizations such as better kernel compilation or using CUDA graphs instead of the eager mode. However, most of these optimization results are usually reported on a single GPU type, typically for a few VLA models, with no analysis of what the real bottlenecks are for a model on a particular hardware or how these optimizations would generalize beyond the reported results. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive measurement study of seven families of inference optimization methods, from CUDA graphs and compilation to token pruning and quantization, on seven state-of-the-art VLAs evaluated on four GPU families ranging from a datacenter H100 to an embedded Jetson Thor. We analyse how CPU launch overhead, memory bandwidth, and kernel efficiency jointly shape optimization gains, and how applying one method changes the benefit of another. From this analysis, we derive VLA-Triage, guidelines for stacking optimizations under latency budgets. The guidelines identify which stages can provide the required savings and which combinations need testing in the deployed execution mode.

</details>


### [324] [Dexterous Robotic Hands in the Foundation-Model Era: A Full-Stack Survey of Hardware, Data, Cross-Embodiment Transfer, Learning, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06724)
*Pei, Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A structured narrative survey (Jan 2024–early Nov 2026) of dexterous robotic hands organized into five interdependent layers—hardware, data collection/curation, cross-embodiment transfer, learning/foundation models, and evaluation/applications—with human hand-object understanding and tactile sensing as cross-cutting perspectives.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hardware, representation learning and evaluation for dexterous hands are often assessed in isolation; the field needs a system-level synthesis that connects design choices across layers and separates rigorous evidence from vendor/company-reported claims.

Method: Structured narrative synthesis over literature from Jan 2024 to early Nov 2026 (plus earlier seminal work for lineages); five-layer taxonomy, system-level architecture, comparative tables and five case analyses; explicit evidence stratification between within-study comparisons, corpus statistics, vendor specs and company-reported results.

Result: Three findings: (1) value of human data hinges on kinematic/visual/contact alignment rather than volume; (2) shared action representations and foundation policies do not eliminate embodiment-specific constraints on force, latency and contact; (3) task completion alone does not establish physical correctness or deployment readiness. Evidence remains thin for zero-shot contact-rich cross-morphology transfer, long-duration reliability and cross-sensor tactile generalization.

Conclusion: The survey contributes eleven open problems plus proposed reporting interfaces and controlled comparison protocols to make full-stack progress measurable; it is not a pooled performance ranking or exhaustive systematic review.

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands are increasingly coupled to large-scale human data and foundation models, yet progress in hardware, representation learning and evaluation is often assessed separately. This survey reviews work from January 2024 to early November 2026, with earlier studies included to establish technical lineages. We organize the literature into five interdependent layers: hardware; data collection and curation; cross-embodiment transfer; learning and foundation models; and evaluation and applications. Human hand-object understanding and tactile sensing provide two cross-cutting perspectives. A system-level architecture, comparative tables and five case analyses connect design choices across these layers. We distinguish within-study experimental comparisons from heterogeneous corpus statistics, vendor specifications and company-reported results. Three conclusions emerge. First, the value of human data depends on kinematic, visual and contact alignment, not recording volume alone. Second, shared action representations and foundation policies do not remove embodiment-specific constraints on force, latency and contact. Third, task completion is insufficient to establish physical correctness or deployment readiness. Evidence remains limited for zero-shot transfer of contact-rich skills between morphologically different hands, long-duration reliability, and cross-sensor tactile generalization. We identify eleven open problems and propose reporting interfaces and controlled comparisons that can make full-stack progress more measurable. The review is a structured narrative synthesis rather than a pooled performance ranking or an exhaustive systematic review.

</details>


### [325] [PyRUA-REAL: Robot Exploration and Adaptive Learning through RSI in the Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06768)
*Wang, Qiang,Bi, Jianxin,Jiang, Shulong,Si, Ruiyang,Huang, Wenbo,Chen, Kaiqi,Li, Jiaxin,Li, Liyun,Li, Xuanyi,Zhou, Daquan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PyRUA-REAL 框架，让编码智能体通过真实世界探索、代码精修与部署实现具身递归自我改进（RSI），将交互经验存为持久记忆与可复用可执行技能；8 项真机任务平均成功率 91.1%，RoboDojo 42 项任务 84.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究编码智能体能否借助物理交互反馈持续提升机器人能力，而非一次性生成程序；即回答"物理探索如何转化为可复用机器人技能"的问题。

Method: PyRUA-REAL 框架：智能体保留交互经验为持久记忆与可复用执行技能，指导后续探索与代码改进；系统性实证分析多智能体协作方式、执行反馈类型、任务规格与已获技能对鲁棒性与迁移的影响；并在不调用在线智能体的条件下评估所生成程序的效率与能力。

Result: 8 个真实世界任务平均成功率 91.1%；RoboDojo 基准 42 项任务平均成功率 84.4%；给出关于反馈形式、技能迁移与离线部署效率的实证结论。

Conclusion: 物理探索可通过记忆与技能积累转化为可复用机器人技能，支持持续自我改进及稳健、高效的离线部署，为具身 RSI 提供实证指导。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by models such as GPT-6-Astra connect spatial reasoning with executable robot behavior, raising the question of how such agents can produce lasting improvements in robot capabilities from physical interaction. In this work, we study embodied recursive self-improvement (RSI) through real-world exploration, code refinement, and deployment using PyRUA-REAL, a framework in which coding agents retain interaction experience as persistent memory and reusable executable skills that inform subsequent exploration and improvement. Our empirical study places physical manipulation at the center of the improvement process, examining how agents collaborate, what kinds of execution feedback support effective code refinement, and how task specifications and previously acquired skills influence robustness and task transfer. We further investigate the efficiency and capability of the resulting programs when deployed without involving an online agent for decision-making. Across eight real-world tasks, PyRUA-REAL achieves an average success rate of 91.1%. In complementary evaluation on 42 tasks from the RoboDojo benchmark, it achieves an average success rate of 84.4%. This study provides empirical guidance for turning physical exploration into reusable robot skills that support continued improvement and robust, efficient deployment.

</details>


### [326] [ConDR: Conservative Reachability through Neural Hamilton-Jacobi Subsolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06802)
*Givens, Kendra,Johnson, Taylor T.,Ward, Bryan C.,Beckers, Thomas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConDR learns a subsolution of the HJ-Isaacs variational inequality via a margin-shifted one-sided loss on a neural value function, guaranteeing that the learned unsafe set is a superset of the true one, with the value within ετ of the true value function.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepReach penalizes both sides of the HJI residual symmetrically, so the learned value can exceed the true one and unsafe states get misclassified as safe—an unsafe failure mode for reachability-based safety analysis.

Method: Enforce obstacle branch and terminal condition by construction; use a one-sided loss pushing the HJI residual to at least ε below zero; add a term preventing the degenerate "everything unsafe" solution; theoretically analyze the margin-shifted VI to show its exact solution is a subsolution of the original VI.

Result: On Air3D, Air6D, a 9D multi-vehicle system and a 13D quadrotor, ConDR yields the lowest unsafe-state misclassification rate and closed-loop collision rate with only small extra interventions; tolerates model mismatch scaling with ε on Air3D/Air6D; at matched false-negative rates its unsafe sets are smaller than DeepReach and MADR.

Conclusion: One-sided conservative training of neural reachability value functions gives provable over-approximation of unsafe sets without trivial inflation, improving safety certification scalability beyond grid-based solvers.

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) Reachability identifies the unsafe states of a dynamical system from which it can be forced into failure under worst-case disturbance. However, classical grid-based solvers scale exponentially with state dimension. DeepReach improves scalability by replacing the grid with a neural value function trained on the HJ-Isaacs (HJI) variational inequality (VI), but penalizes both sides of the residual equally. This allows the learned value to lie above the true one and unsafe states to be misclassified as safe. We propose Conservative DeepReach (ConDR) which aims to learn a subsolution of the HJI VI such that the learned unsafe set contains the true one. ConDR enforces the obstacle branch and terminal condition by construction, pushes the HJI residual to a safety margin $\epsilon$ below zero with a one-sided loss, and prevents the degenerate solution of classifying everything as unsafe. We prove that the exact solution of the margin-shifted VI that ConDR trains toward is a subsolution of the original VI so its unsafe set contains the true one and its value lies within $\epsilon\tau$ of the true value function. We compare against DeepReach and MADR in the established systems of Air3D, Air6D, a 9D multi-vehicle system, and a 13D quadrotor. ConDR reports the lowest rate of unsafe state misclassified and the lowest closed-loop collision rates with at most a small increase in intervention. On Air3D and Air6D, ConDR tolerates model mismatch that scales with $\epsilon$. At matched false-negative rates, ConDR's unsafe sets are smaller than the baselines showing the safety is not obtained by trivial over-approximation.

</details>


### [327] [Rethinking Self-Repair in Code-as-Policy Agents: What Drives Retry Success?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06863)
*Aphale, Siddharth,Singh, Ayushman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在模拟机器人操作中，用固定模板库按失败标签+API变体选择反馈，经冻结代码模型重试；同252个初始失败上，Qwen2.5-Coder-7B五次重试内恢复85.3%，显著高于同重试上限的重复采样68.3%，且在固定token预算下仍成立；其他episode的反馈与固定任务指导表现相当，粗粒度失败标签匹配无增益；固定引导提示首轮即用可达到与模板反馈相近的整体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码策略智能体的重试成功可能来自额外采样或任务提示，而非失败专用反馈本身；作者希望拆解各因素贡献，厘清"执行反馈"的真实价值。

Method: 模拟机器人操作任务上的受控消融：固定查找表按失败标签与API变体选取反馈模板；每次场景重置后由冻结代码模型重新生成程序；对比重复采样（resampling）、跨episode不匹配反馈、固定任务指导、首轮即用的固定引导提示，并控制总token预算。

Result: Qwen2.5-Coder-7B在252个初始失败上五轮内恢复率85.3% vs 重采样68.3%；固定token预算下优势保持；跨episode反馈与固定指导点估计相近，失败标签匹配无可见收益；固定引导提示整体成功率与模板反馈接近；在需要边缘抓取的螺母插入任务中模板反馈反而降低恢复率，与中心抓取指令不兼容一致。

Conclusion: 机器人自修复评估应纳入重采样、错配反馈与简洁且任务兼容的静态指导作为基线；反馈的主要收益可能来自任务级引导而非特定失败诊断。

Abstract: Code-as-policy agents often use execution feedback to retry failed programs. Additional sampling and task guidance can also improve success, leaving the contribution of failure-specific feedback unclear. We study this question in simulated robot manipulation. A fixed lookup selects feedback templates using failure labels and the API variant. A frozen code model generates a fresh program after each scene reset. On the same 252 initial failures, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B recovers 85.3% within five retries, compared with 68.3% for resampling with the same retry cap. The advantage persists under fixed total-token budgets. Feedback from other episodes and fixed task guidance yield similar recovery point estimates, with no detected benefit from matching the coarse failure label. In a separate study, a fixed guided prompt used from the first attempt achieves an observed overall success rate similar to that of template-based feedback. Template-based feedback also lowers recovery on a nut-insertion task requiring a rim grasp, consistent with an incompatible center-grasp instruction. Evaluations of robot self-repair should include resampling, mismatched feedback, and concise, task-compatible static guidance as baselines.

</details>


### [328] [VABAR: A Variational Automation Benchmark for Agentic Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06881)
*Xie, Shuangyu,Chen, Kaiyuan,Yoo, Uksang,Zhu, Xinyue,Shen, Meadow,Drake, Jaimyn,Solowjow, Eugen,Gulich, Lionel,Millane, Alexander,Cathomen, Rafael,Stuyck, Tuur,Subramanian, Sangeeta,Pan, Yichao,McGowan, Bruce,Benedict, Michael,Fan, Linxi,Zhu, Yuke,Huang, Spencer,Goldberg, Ken*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"变异性自动化"(VA)概念与智能体机器人基准设计准则，构建基于Newton物理引擎的开源仿真基准VABAR（六类操作任务、33个通用技能、程序化参考解），并给出GaP与Isaac Lab Arena基线评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业/工业场景中机器人需在已知的形状与位姿变化下稳定执行任务（VA），而异构的智能体机器人系统缺乏系统、可复现的评测平台，现有基准在可及性、多样性、抗饱和性与计算效率上不足。

Method: 先归纳八条智能体机器人基准设计准则（可及性、可复现性、多样性、相关性、物理可实现性、抗饱和、计算效率等），据此实例化为工具分拣/悬挂、齿轮啮合、注射器装盒、线缆插入与布线六类任务；在Newton物理引擎上实现全部开源仿真环境，含标准化任务变体、难度分级、成功指标及带人工编写程序化参考解的33技能库；用Graph-as-Policy与Isaac Lab Arena做初始基线评测。

Result: 交付VABAR开源基准及GaP、Isaac Lab Arena的初步基线结果；摘要未报告具体成功率、泛化幅度或sim-to-real数据。

Conclusion: VABAR为智能体机器人提供可复现的仿真评测基础，但缺乏真实机器人验证与定量性能证据，其价值主要在工具与评测而非方法创新。

Abstract: For robots to operate reliably in commercial and industrial applications, they must persistently perform specific tasks with known variations in object shape and pose. We call this Variational Automation (VA) to differentiate these settings from fixed automation. Inspired by recent papers on Agentic Robotics, where pretrained LLM and VLM coding agents combine interpretable robot programming with the open-world adaptability of model free policies, we propose a general set of agentic robotics benchmark design criteria that includes accessibility, repeatability, diversity, relevance to agentic robotics and practical applications, physical realizability, resistance to benchmark saturation, and computational efficiency. We instantiate these principles in six manipulation task classes spanning industrial, medical, and infrastructure applications: tool sorting, tool hanging, gear meshing, syringe packing, cable insertion, and cable routing. We introduce the Variational Automation Benchmark for Agentic Robotics (VABAR), an open, easily replicable, simulation-based manipulation benchmark to systematically evaluating agentic robotics systems. Built fully on open-source tools, VABAR features detailed implementations using the Newton physics engine, standardized task variations, difficulty tiers, success metrics, and a library of 33 generalizable skills with human-authored programmatic reference solutions. We provide initial baseline evaluations using an open agentic manipulation harness, Graph-as-Policy (GaP), and Isaac Lab Arena.

</details>


### [329] [Perceptive Humanoid Motion from Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06908)
*Hu, Siyuan,Abuduweili, Abulikemu,Meng, Yue,Liu, Shipeng,Leboutet, Quentin,Chen, Chen,Müller, Matthias,Koltun, Vladlen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO: 在1000个程序化生成的家庭中合成视觉-语言-运动数据，训练人形机器人自主系统，零样本迁移到真实世界，360次试验成功率91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人通用自主性受数据瓶颈限制：从像素与自由语言到低层关节指令需要成对监督数据，而此类数据在大规模上不存在；遥操作/穿戴捕捉采集1小时数据至少需1小时人工。

Method: 构建可随算力而非人力扩展的数据合成流水线，在约一千个程序化生成的室内家庭场景中合成视觉-语言-运动（vision-language-motion）配对数据；基于该语料习得技能库（坐椅子、导航到指定物体、侧身通过半开门、蹲伏爬行穿桌、推箱子），并通过技能编排求解复合长时程任务。

Result: 训练全程未见真实家庭；零样本迁移到物理世界，在6个未建图的真实环境中完成360次试验，成功率91%。

Conclusion: 仿真合成数据可替代人力遥操作，作为人形机器人通用技能与长时程任务的数据来源，并实现可靠的零样本sim-to-real迁移；代码与视频已公开。

Abstract: Humanoid robots have the potential to automate a wide range of physical tasks. Progress toward generalist humanoid autonomy, however, remains constrained by a data bottleneck: mapping pixels and free-form language to low-level joint commands requires paired supervision, and no corpus of such data exists at sufficiently large scale. Some approaches rely on human teleoperation and wearable capture devices, which require at least an hour of human time to produce an hour of data. We present HALO, a humanoid autonomy system powered by a pipeline that synthesizes vision-language-motion data across a thousand procedurally generated homes. The corpus scales with compute rather than human labor. HALO acquires a repertoire of physical skills, including sitting on chairs, navigating to named objects, stepping sideways through half-open doors, crouching and crawling beneath tables, and pushing boxes. It orchestrates these skills to solve composite, long-horizon tasks. Without ever seeing a real home during training, HALO transfers zero-shot to the physical world, achieving a 91% success rate across 360 trials in six unmapped real-world environments. See https://apple-aiml-research.github.io/ml-halo/ for code and videos.

</details>


### [330] [cuRoboV2-Fast: Fast Torque-Limited Collision-Free Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06913)
*Sundaralingam, Balakumar,Murali, Adithyavairavan,Birchfield, Stan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: cuRoboV2-Fast：GPU 加速的碰撞-free、负载感知、力矩受限运动规划器，采用 B-spline 轨迹优化 + 可微逆动力学 + TimeOpt 带状牛顿系统求解；3kg 负载下 RTX 5090 平均规划 4.4 ms（比 cuRoboV2 快 14.7×），Jetson Orin 上快于 RTX 5090 上的 cuRoboV1+TOPPRA，YAM 双臂立体相机部署平均抓取-放置周期 5.93 s（缩短 34.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动规划器规划延迟与执行时间偏高，且在负载变化、力矩约束下的轨迹优化计算成本高，难以在嵌入式（Jetson）平台与真实抓取-放置闭环中满足实时性需求。

Method: 1) 用 B-spline 参数化轨迹优化以减少迭代次数；2) 加速机器人运动学/动力学计算并提供可微逆动力学以降低精修成本并支持负载变化；3) TimeOpt 在固定路径下、于采样的速度/加速度/加加速度/力矩约束中优化行进速度，利用 B-spline 局部支撑在 GPU 上高效求解带状 Newton 系统。

Result: 3 kg 负载下 RTX 5090 平均规划时间 4.4 ms，比 cuRoboV2 快 14.7×；Jetson AGX Orin 上的规划速度超过 RTX 5090 上的 cuRoboV1+TOPPRA；基于立体相机的 YAM 平台部署实现 5.93 s 平均完整抓取-放置周期，较 cuRoboV2 缩短 34.6%。代码 Apache 2.0 开源。

Conclusion: 将 GPU 并行、B-spline 局部支撑与可微动力学结合，可在保持力矩与负载约束可行性的同时把规划延迟压到毫秒级，并在真实硬件闭环中显著缩短执行周期，适合作为实时操作系统的运动规划基础设施。

Abstract: We present cuRoboV2-Fast, a GPU-accelerated planner that reduces both planning latency and execution time for collision-free, payload-aware, torque-limited motions. B-spline trajectory optimization reduces the required iterations, while accelerated robot computations and differentiable inverse dynamics reduce refinement cost and accommodate changing payloads. TimeOpt optimizes traversal speed along the fixed path under sampled velocity, acceleration, jerk, and torque constraints, exploiting local spline support to solve banded Newton systems efficiently on the GPU. With a 3,kg payload, cuRoboV2-Fast achieves 4.4 ms mean planning time on RTX 5090, 14.7x faster than cuRoboV2. Our method on Jetson AGX Orin plans faster than cuRoboV1 + TOPPRA on RTX 5090. A stereo-camera-based deployment on YAM achieves 5.93 s mean completed pick-and-drop cycles, 34.6% shorter than cuRoboV2. Code (Apache 2.0): www.github.com/NVlabs/curobo , Project Page: www.research.nvidia.com/labs/lpr/curobov2-fast .

</details>


### [331] [Curvature by Design: A Support Structure for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07091)
*Justus Sagemüller,Harald Minde Hansen,Kristin Y. Pettersen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于剪叉机构(多段、过约束多关节连杆)的支撑结构，通过柔顺柔性铰链的泰勒近似几何耦合，机械地约束连续体机器人呈现预设的恒定或分段恒定曲率，无需在运动学中人为假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人运动学中常用的常曲率/分段常曲率模型并非机器人固有机械属性，实际运行中易被违背，导致形状精度差、需额外估计偏差或S形变形等非预期构型。

Method: 设计多段剪叉式支撑结构，利用泰勒近似使过约束多关节连杆将整体弯曲可预测地分配到多个柔性铰链的小角度转动上；采用柔顺柔性铰链实现几何耦合，整体可单件制造；与腱驱动、柔性骨架连续体机器人集成，实验评估抗非预设变形能力与形状精度。

Result: 实验表明该结构使机器人在外载（包括反向弯曲）下抵抗非预设曲率变形，无法形成S形，形状精度较传统柔性骨架设计显著提升，并减少了需估计的独立形状变量数目。

Conclusion: 以机械约束替代运动学假设是一种有效提升连续体机器人形状精度与建模可辨识性的途径，且可通过单件柔顺加工实现工程可行。

Abstract: Constant- and piecewise-constant-curvature models are widely used to simplify the kinematics of continuum robots, although the assumed curvature profiles are not necessarily inherent mechanical properties of the robot and may therefore be violated during operation. Rather than assuming a prescribed curvature profile in the kinematic model and estimating deviations from it, this paper presents a multi-segment scissor-based support structure that mechanically constrains a continuum robot to exhibit prescribed constant or spatially varying curvature profiles. The design uses Taylor approximations to enable an overconstrained multi-joint linkage to predictably distribute global bending among the small-angle movements of multiple flexures. The geometric coupling is realized using compliant flexure joints, enabling the support structure to be manufactured as a single piece. The structure is integrated with a tendon-actuated continuum robot with a flexible backbone. Experiments show that the structure makes the robot resistant to deformations other than the prescribed curvature, significantly enhancing shape precision compared to classical flexible-backbone designs. This constrains the robot from making S-shapes when external loads are applied in the opposite direction of the bending. By mechanically constraining the admissible deformation, the proposed structure reduces the number of independent shape variables that must be estimated.

</details>


### [332] [Spatial-UMI-Dream: Scaling World Action Models with Metric Geometry for UMI Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07229)
*Weiduo Yuan,Ze Yuan,Sihan Liu,Jionghao Wang,Liang Heng,Zhenyu Zhao,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Spatial-UMI-Dream：5B 空间世界动作模型（WAM），在 605 小时 RGB-D UMI 数据上经“先世界适配、再世界-动作联合学习”两阶段预训练，联合预测未来 RGB、鱼眼对齐的度量深度与动作；并配套 RoboTwin-UMI 基准评测 UMI 策略的跨本体/跨环境泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMI 手柄式近距离相机使相机运动与物体运动耦合、缺乏稳定外部视角，精确操作所需的度量几何只能隐含在 RGB 中；同时缺乏可控、可复现的基准来检验 UMI 数据采集的“机器人无关性”是否真能转化为跨本体策略泛化。

Method: 以立体视差推导深度，构建统一的双手柄 RGB-D 表示使尺度与空间关系显式化；5B 世界动作模型用两阶段训练（仅世界模型适配 → 世界+动作联合学习）在 605 小时 UMI RGB-D 数据上预训练，同时预测未来 RGB、鱼眼对齐度量深度和动作。另构建 RoboTwin-UMI 二十任务仿真基准，带 UMI 腕部鱼眼观测，可分别及联合施加环境与本体变化，无需额外微调即评测策略。

Result: RoboTwin-UMI 上域内成功率 79.10%，本体+环境联合偏移下 72.15%，分别超最强基线 4.65 与 10.30 个百分点；本体迁移掉点最小。匹配预训练消融中，仅 RGB 预训练域内成功率比 RGB-D 低 5.75 个百分点。六个真实任务平均成功率 85.0%，超最强基线 24.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: 在 UMI 无机器人依赖的数据范式下，显式度量深度 + 世界-动作联合预训练能显著提升精确操作与跨本体/跨环境泛化，并提供了可复现的评测基准。

Abstract: The Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) enables scalable demonstration collection independently of a specific robot. However, its moving, close-range gripper cameras entangle camera and object motion without a stable external view, leaving the metric geometry needed for precise manipulation implicit in RGB. We introduce Spatial-UMI-Dream, a 5B spatial World Action Model (WAM) pretrained on 605 hours of RGB-D UMI data through two-stage training -- world-only adaptation followed by joint world-action learning -- to jointly predict future RGB, fisheye-aligned metric depth, and actions. Stereo-derived depth makes scene scale and spatial relationships explicit within a unified dual-gripper RGB-D representation. Furthermore, evaluating UMI policies requires testing whether UMI's robot-independent demonstration collection translates into policy generalization across robot embodiments, alongside robustness to environmental changes. We introduce RoboTwin-UMI, a twenty-task simulation benchmark built on RoboTwin with UMI wrist-mounted fisheye observations, enabling controlled, repeatable evaluation of UMI policies under environment and embodiment changes separately and jointly without additional fine-tuning. On RoboTwin-UMI, Spatial-UMI-Dream achieves 79.10% in-domain success and 72.15% under combined embodiment and environment shifts, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline in each setting by 4.65 and 10.30 percentage points, respectively. It also exhibits the smallest success-rate drop under embodiment transfer among the evaluated policies. In a matched pretraining ablation, RGB-only pretraining leads to a 5.75-percentage-point decrease in in-domain success compared with RGB-D pretraining. Across six real-world tasks, Spatial-UMI-Dream achieves 85.0% average success, exceeding the strongest baseline by 24.2 percentage points. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [333] [Adaptive Hybrid Force-Vision Control with EMG-Derived References for Deformable Bottle Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07235)
*Jeffrey Lee,Geoffrey Hanks,Evangeline Bouganim,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向实验室挤压瓶液体分装的自适应混合视觉-力控控制器，按交互状态融合力与视觉反馈以应对瓶体变形与回弹；经EMG映射人示教力作为参考，12种条件下MAE 6.54 mL，优于纯力控的15.92 mL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室任务机器人化可让研究者远离危险环境与重复操作，但挤压瓶这类可变形物体因形变难以建模，是困难的操控对象；纯力控难以补偿瓶体挤压后的形变与回弹，导致分装体积精度低。

Method: 构建自适应混合视觉-力控框架：依据交互状态（接触/挤压/回弹等）切换或加权力反馈与视觉反馈，实现对可变形瓶体的闭环挤压控制以准确分装液体。以EMG信号映射为期望握力，作为人示教力参考供控制器复现；在2种目标体积×2种塑料挤压瓶×1~3次挤压共12种条件下与纯力控进行对比验证。

Result: 混合视觉-力控在12种条件下分装体积平均绝对误差为6.54 mL，纯力控为15.92 mL；同时更贴近EMG记录的人体动作与握力曲线。

Conclusion: 按交互状态融合视觉与力反馈可有效应对可变形容器的挤压-回弹特性，显著提升液体分装精度并更接近人类操作策略；该思路可推广至其他可变形物体的机器人操控与实验室自动化场景。

Abstract: Robotic automation of laboratory tasks has become a growing interest to remove researchers from hazardous environments and repetitive procedures. However, even equipment as simple as squeeze bottles poses a challenging manipulation problem due to its deformability. This paper presents an adaptive hybrid vision-force controller for robotic grippers to effectively manipulate laboratory squeeze bottles to dispense liquid. This approach integrates force and vision feedback according to the interaction state, enabling effective squeezing while accounting for bottle deformation and recovery. The proposed controller is validated by comparing its performance with a pure force controller on 12 combinations of conditions consisting of 2 target dispense volumes, 2 plastic squeeze bottles, and 1 to 3 squeezes used to dispense. Human demonstrations of these operating conditions were collected using electromyography (EMG) sensing mapped to a desired grip force, which was then used as the force reference for the controllers to squeeze the bottles. Results demonstrated that the proposed adaptive hybrid vision-force controller achieved more accurate liquid dispensing with a mean absolute error (MAE) of 6.54 mL compared to a MAE of 15.92 mL from the pure force controller, while also replicating the human action recorded from the EMG signal more closely.

</details>


### [334] [An Empirical Study of Reinforcement Learning for Keypoint Positioning of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07261)
*Vinay Rodrigues,Sara Zeynalpour,Gokhan Solak,Sebastian Hjorth,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用GPU并行MuJoCo Playground训练PPO策略，结合相对关键点观测、非对称actor-critic与鲁棒化训练，实现单端夹持可变形线性物体（DLO）平面操作的无微调零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLO操作面临高维构型、非线性接触动力学及材料/几何多样性带来的建模困难与数据密集问题，需在训练效率、观测设计、鲁棒性与零样本sim-to-real迁移上给出系统性的工程答案。

Method: 对比三种PPO训练栈（GPU并行MuJoCo Playground vs CPU方案）；采用相对关键点观测与非对称actor-critic提升样本效率；在训练中注入观测噪声、时延、域随机化与外部扰动增强鲁棒性；部署时用无标记RGB-D关键点估计构成感知闭环。

Result: 相同墙钟预算下GPU并行栈收敛更快且更稳定；仿真成功率：单点跟踪96.4%、扰动恢复98.0%、两点跟踪88.8%；真机无微调迁移成功：9/10、10/10、8/10。

Conclusion: GPU并行仿真+相对关键点观测+鲁棒化训练可实现DLO操作的零样本sim-to-real，但真机验证限于平面单端夹持场景，两点跟踪仍有明显退化。

Abstract: Deformable linear object (DLO) manipulation is challenging because high-dimensional configurations, nonlinear contact dynamics, and diverse material and geometric properties produce a broad range of object responses that are both difficult to model and data-intensive to learn. This work studies reinforcement learning for planar manipulation of a one-end-grasped DLO, focusing on training efficiency, observation design, robustness, and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. We compare three Proximal Policy Optimization training stacks and find that the GPU-parallel MuJoCo Playground stack enables faster convergence and more consistent performance than CPU-based alternatives under the same wall-clock budget. Relative keypoint observations and an asymmetric actor-critic formulation further improve learning efficiency. Policies trained with observation noise, delay, domain randomization, and disturbances achieve simulation success rates of 96.4% for single-point tracking, 98.0% for disturbance recovery, and 88.8% for two-point tracking. Using markerless RGB-D keypoint estimation, the policies transfer without real-world fine-tuning, succeeding in 9/10, 10/10, and 8/10 physical trials, respectively.

</details>


### [335] [CRISP: Constraint-Resolved Interactions between Subsystems for Predictive Whole-Body Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07268)
*Dario Valenzuela,Jeeseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层NMPC框架：将人形机器人分解为躯干单刚体(SRB)与浮动基机械臂/下肢动力学模型，用完整约束+拉格朗日乘子参数化肩部与末端交互力，在线联合优化子系统轨迹、臂力矩、GRF与交互力，并在G1机器人上完成仿真与实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维人形机器人双臂协同操作的整体优化计算量大，且臂-躯干耦合与双臂闭环运动链的交互力难以在统一模型中高效刻画；需要一种既能保持动态耦合、又能在线求解的分层建模与控制结构。

Method: 分层控制：将人形机器人分解为躯干SRB模型 + 机械臂与下肢浮动基动力学模型；臂-躯干刚性连接与双臂末端闭环链均表示为完整约束，对应拉格朗日乘子即肩部与末端传递的交互力旋量；高层NMPC联合优化子系统状态轨迹、臂关节力矩、地面反力GRF与交互力旋量，实现交互力的在线设计与优化；优化后的臂力矩直接用于双臂协同操作，GRF经映射转为下肢关节力矩实现全身协调。

Result: 数值仿真与G1人形机器人硬件实验：优化得到的交互力旋量与仿真接触测量一致，双臂协同轨迹跟踪良好，全身协调稳定，且NMPC可在线运行。摘要未给出定量误差、跟踪精度或计算耗时指标。

Conclusion: 基于完整约束与乘子的分层建模可在保持子系统动态耦合的前提下实现交互力的在线NMPC优化，为高维人形双臂协同操作提供可实机部署的统一框架；但缺乏定量指标与消融对比，证据层级属"有实机演示、缺量化验证"。

Abstract: This paper presents a layered control framework that recasts high-dimensional system dynamics as a composition of dynamically interconnected subsystems, with dynamic dual-arm manipulation on the humanoid serving as a representative application. The humanoid is decomposed into a single rigid body (SRB) representing the torso and floating-base dynamic models representing the articulated arms and lower-body. The rigid connections between arm and torso and the closed kinematic chain formed between the end-effectors during cooperative manipulation are represented as holonomic constraints. The corresponding Lagrange multipliers parametrize the interaction wrenches transmitted at the shoulders and between the end-effectors. The high-level nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) jointly optimizes the subsystem state trajectories, arm torques, ground reaction forces (GRFs), and interaction wrenches, enabling online wrench design and optimization while preserving the dynamic coupling among the independently modeled subsystems. The optimized arm torques are applied directly for cooperative dual-arm manipulation, whereas the prescribed GRFs are mapped to lower-body joint torque for whole-body coordination. We validate the proposed framework through numerical simulations and hardware experiments on a G1 humanoid robot. The results demonstrate optimal interaction wrenches consistent with simulated contact measurement, cooperative dual-arm trajectory tracking, and stable whole-body coordination while executing the NMPC in online manner.

</details>


### [336] [VREgo: Consumer VR Headsets for Learning Robot Manipulation from Egocentric Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07272)
*Liuchuan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VREgo 用消费级 VR 头显（PICO 4 Ultra，EgoKit）录制示范来训练操作策略，并对比了 PICO 与 Aria 数据下的策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示范数据采集成本高，消费级 VR 头显可能提供低门槛的自我中心（egocentric）重定向/遥操作数据源，但缺乏与专用采集设备（Project Aria）的定量比较。

Method: 基于 PICO 4 Ultra 的设备端头/手部追踪与双目物体定位；改写 HumanEgo 预处理流程：按录制自适应估计抓取阈值、释放后更新物体位姿、关键帧间传播分割掩码。在仿真中用两个桌面任务（方块放置、两阶段堆叠）评估，使用 40 条训练示范与多个训练种子。

Result: 同 41 种布局下，PICO 策略三次种子在 67/74/60 个布局放置成功；对应 Aria 为 73/78/0（不稳定）。堆叠任务基线仅 2/100 满足双放置终点判据；诊断发现重复重规划导致释放延迟超过 8 步执行窗口、张开的夹爪在抓取前推移方块。加入任务特定执行规则（利用仿真物体位姿）后达 93.8%（四噪声种子），但规则开发与评估共用同一批 100 布局。

Conclusion: 消费级 VR 头显可作为可用的示范采集途径，但策略可靠性对硬件/追踪噪声敏感，且显著依赖任务特定执行规则；评估协议存在开发-测试泄漏，结论需谨慎。

Abstract: VREgo trains manipulation policies from demonstrations recorded on consumer VR headsets; we instantiate it with EgoKit on one such headset, the PICO 4 Ultra. On-device head and hand tracking and stereo object localisation allow the operator to begin a demonstration at the table. We adapt HumanEgo's preprocessing to estimate grasp thresholds per recording, update object positions after release, and propagate segmentation masks between keyframes. We evaluate policies in simulation on two tabletop tasks recorded by one operator. For cube placement, we compare PICO and Project Aria recordings of the same 41 layouts. With 40 training demonstrations, the PICO policies placed the cube on the tray in 67, 74, and 60 of 100 evaluation layouts across three training seeds. The corresponding Aria results were 73, 78, and 0. For a two-stage stacking task recorded with the PICO alone, the baseline placed the block on the tray in 94 of 100 layouts and met the end-of-episode criterion for both placements in two. In one diagnostic episode, repeated replanning deferred release beyond the eight executed steps. Demonstration replays also showed that the open gripper could displace the cube before grasping it. After adapting the training trajectories and adding task-specific execution rules that use simulator object positions, the system met both placement criteria in 93.8% of episodes across four evaluation noise seeds. The same 100 layouts informed rule development and evaluation. VREgo is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [337] [Efficient Diffusion Policy Fine Tuning with Latent Noise Representation Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07275)
*Yuanchu Liang,Yue Yang,An-Chi He,Hanna Kurniawati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出隐噪声线性MDP（LNL-MDP）与Bridge Equation，消除微调扩散/流策略时的"噪声混叠"问题，并给出LARBRID算法仅在加噪空间学习表示以优化初始噪声分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散/流策略微调至新任务时，强化学习成本高、样本效率低、缺理论保证；已有仅微调初始噪声的方法虽避免BPTT，但噪声空间值与动作空间值缺乏自然对应（noise aliasing）。

Method: 构建LNL-MDP建模初始噪声与动作的联合结构；推导Bridge Equation，通过噪声与动作空间共享表示对齐价值函数；框架纳入FineTuneRL理论获得近最优保证；据此提出LARBRID，高效学习潜表示以优化引导预训练模型的初始分布。

Result: 在多样化机器人locomotion与manipulation控制任务上取得约2-3倍性能提升，且计算与样本效率优于BPTT类方法。

Conclusion: 将扩散/流策略的RL微调转化为潜噪声MDP问题，可同时获得理论保证与显著实证收益，为预训练生成式策略的高效适配提供可行路径。

Abstract: Pre-trained diffusion and flow policies have emerged as powerful backbones for robotics, yet adapting them to novel data via reinforcement learning (RL) remains computationally expensive, sample inefficient and lacks theoretical guarantees. Prior approaches that fine-tune the initial noise bypass costly backpropagation through time but struggle with the "noise aliasing" problem: there does not exista natural link between the noise space value and the action space value. This paper resolves these challenges by introducing the Latent Noise Linear Markov Decision Process (LNL-MDP). We derive the Bridge Equation for LNL-MDPs which eliminates the noise aliasing problem by aligning values with a shared representation between the noise and the action space. Furthermore, our LNL-MDP fine tuning framework falls under the FineTuneRL theory with near-optimal guarantee. Leveraging these insights, we propose Latent Representation Bridging for Diffusion (LARBRID), an algorithm that efficiently learns the representation to optimize the initial distribution that steers the pre-trained model to act optimally in the environment. It yields up to 2-3$\times$ performance increases on challenging control problems across a diverse suite of robotic locomotion and manipulation tasks. Code can be found at: this http URL .

</details>


### [338] [An Ordering Optimization for Fruit-Vegetable in High-Density Environment based on Genetic Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07293)
*Jaehwi Seol,Jeonghyeon Pak,Hyoung Il Son*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合RGB-D果实定位、遗传算法(GA)采摘排序与静态/动态路点的机器人采摘方法，在稠密非结构化作物环境中实现连续执行以减少Home返回动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密非结构化作物环境中，果实相互遮挡且分布随机，传统逐果"定位—往返Home—采摘"流程产生大量冗余运动并增加碰撞风险，需要兼顾可达性与避障的采摘顺序与运动规划策略。

Method: 全局RGB-D相机提供果实三维位置用于排序，末端执行器相机在执行阶段提供近距观测；GA求解采摘序列并与穷举枚举对照验证；静态与动态路点定义中间运动，并通过参数敏感性分析考察路点偏移；动态路点随剩余果实分布变化而GA序列不变。

Result: 实验室测试台及四、五、六果实田间条件下评估：连续执行减少不必要的Home往返运动；动态路点连续策略产生40次碰撞事件、目标接近成功率70.7%，静态路点连续策略为55次碰撞、65.3%。

Conclusion: 连续执行与动态路点在无需重算GA序列的情况下提升了采摘成功率并显著降低碰撞，说明路点级动态自适应是农业采摘机器人提升作业效率的可行轻量方案；但实验规模有限，需更多果园场景与真实部署验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a robotic harvesting method that combines RGB-D fruit localization, GA-based harvest ordering, and waypoint-based robot motion in dense and unstructured crop environments. The global RGB-D camera provides 3D fruit positions for ordering, while the end-effector-mounted camera provides close-range observation during execution. The GA determines the harvesting sequence, and static or dynamic waypoints define the intermediate robot motion. The method was evaluated in a controlled laboratory testbed and in four-, five-, and six-fruit field conditions. GA solutions were compared with exhaustive enumeration, and both waypoint offsets were examined by parameter sensitivity analysis. Continuous execution reduced unnecessary Home-return motion, while the dynamic waypoint changed with the remaining fruit distribution without changing the GA sequence. In the field evaluation, the dynamic-waypoint continuous strategy produced 40 collision events and a 70.7% target-approach success rate, compared with 55 collision events and 65.3% for the static-waypoint continuous strategy.

</details>


### [339] [Dynamic Watermarking and Action-Conditioned Latent Dynamics for Secure Vision-Based Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07403)
*Aditya Parameshwaran,Navid Aftabi,Abhishek Hanchate,Satish T. S. Bukkapatnam,Dan Li,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应动态水印框架检测视觉控制系统的重放攻击：向控制输入注入私有高斯水印，用控制输入条件下的隐动力学网络在图像学到的隐空间中预测响应，以隐响应与接收图像隐表示之间的偏差作为攻击证据；强化学习策略依隐状态与检测器置信度调节水印协方差，兼顾可检测性、水印能量与控制畸变，训练时加入任务安全代价的拉格朗日惩罚；在UR5视觉抓取/避障任务上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉控制系统中，被重放的旧观测仍"看起来合理"，而物理系统已沿不同轨迹运行，导致重放攻击难以被常规异常检测识别；同时固定水印会带来控制畸变与能量开销，需要自适应权衡。

Method: 1) 在名义控制输入上叠加私有高斯水印；2) 从图像观测学习紧凑隐空间，训练控制输入条件下的隐动力学网络(LDN)预测隐状态演化；3) 用预测隐响应与接收图像隐表示的不一致度作为检测统计量；4) 用RL策略根据隐状态与检测置信度调整水印协方差；5) 训练阶段以拉格朗日惩罚约束任务安全代价。

Result: 在UR5视觉引导操作任务（到达目标并避让运动人体手臂）中，于名义与重放攻击观测两种条件下评估了该框架，摘要未给出定量检测率/误报率或控制性能数据。

Conclusion: 该工作将可学习隐动力学预测与自适应水印结合，为视觉反馈控制提供了物理一致性的重放攻击检测思路，兼顾检测灵敏度与控制代价。

Abstract: Replay attacks are difficult to detect in vision-based control systems because previously recorded observations can remain plausible while the physical system follows a different trajectory. This paper develops an adaptive dynamic-watermarking framework for detecting replay attacks against vision-based controllers. A private Gaussian watermark is added to the nominal control input, and a control input-conditioned latent dynamics network (LDN) predicts the corresponding evolution in a compact latent space learned from image observations. Attack evidence is obtained from the discrepancy between the predicted latent response and the latent representation of the received image observation. A reinforcement learning policy adjusts the watermark covariance using the latent state and detector confidence, thereby balancing attack detectability, watermark energy, and control distortion. A Lagrangian penalty on task-specific safety costs is included during training to limit the adverse effect of the excitation. The framework is evaluated on a vision-guided UR5 manipulation task in which the robot reaches a target while avoiding a moving human arm under nominal and replay-attacked observations.

</details>


### [340] [Kinematic Hypothesis Context: Embodiment-Conditioned Learned Video Coding for Bandwidth-Limited Robot Policy Uplinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07411)
*Yuze Lu*
