Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"运动学假设上下文"——利用关节状态、机器人模型与相机标定在收发两端零成本构建的条件信号（自运动单应、连杆重投影、末端平面、背景记忆），将上一帧重建扭曲为假设以条件化学习式视频码片，在LIBERO上显著降码率并提升闭环策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程服务器运行大规模VLA策略时，相机上行链路成为瓶颈；现有视频编解码器把码率浪费在接收端可预测的内容（机器人自身运动）上。

Method: 零码率运动学上下文：由关节状态、机器人URDF模型与相机标定在编码解码两端构造多条扭曲假设（自运动单应、逐连杆重投影、末端执行器平面、背景记忆），据此条件化学习式视频编解码器；并给出理论证明——有限感受野下的"仅历史"与"全局位姿"条件在运动剧烈时无法获取该信息；同时论证码率增益在策略成功率悬崖处转化为任务增益。

Result: LIBERO上较容量匹配的历史条件码片节省23.9%码率，较x264/x265/SVT-AV1节省40-56%，相同动作偏差下比DCVC-RT少23.2%比特；低码率训练后在PSNR上与DCVC-RT持平。闭环同码率下OpenVLA-OFT在6kbps成功率22.8%→43.1%（低码率训练72.6%，高出DCVC-RT 23.6点），pi_0.5 55.5%→77.4%，丢包下增益更大；方法跨三种码片宿主迁移并适用于真机数据。

Conclusion: 显式利用机器人运动学先验作为零码率侧信息，是提升具身策略远程推理通信效率的有效且可迁移手段，且码率节省可实际转化为闭环任务成功率。

Abstract: Robots increasingly run large vision-language-action policies on remote servers, making the camera uplink the bottleneck. Existing video codecs spend bits on content the receiver could predict: the robot's own motion. We introduce the kinematic hypothesis context, a zero-rate conditioning signal that both ends build from joint states, the robot model and the camera calibration. Ego-motion homographies, per-link robot reprojections, an end-effector plane and a background memory warp the previous reconstruction into hypotheses that condition a learned video codec. We prove that history-only and global-pose conditioning with a limited receptive field cannot access this information under large motion, and that coding gains become task gains at the policy's success cliff. On LIBERO, the context saves 23.9% bit rate over a capacity-matched history-conditioned codec and 40-56% over x264, x265 and SVT-AV1, and needs 23.2% fewer bits than DCVC-RT at equal policy action deviation; trained to lower rates, it matches DCVC-RT in PSNR. In closed loop at equal rate, OpenVLA-OFT success nearly doubles from 22.8% to 43.1% at 6 kbps (72.6% with lower-rate training, 23.6 points above DCVC-RT), pi_0.5 success rises from 55.5% to 77.4%, and the gain grows under packet loss. The context transfers across three codec hosts and to real robot data.

</details>


### [341] [MessyNav: Zero-Shot Navigation via Manipulation in Messy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07417)
*Kenneth Llontop,Cherie Ho,Dylan Zhou,Anuva Banwasi,Priya Sundaresan,Carlota Parés-Morlans,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MessyNav：基于VLM的零样本开放世界交互式导航框架，先做显式 affordance 推理（推开/拾取/绕开），再生成高层计划，无需任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境路径常被椅子、玩具等杂物阻断，机器人需通过操作障碍物完成交互式导航；现有方法依赖大规模仿真训练或逐场景人工指令（指定避让/操作对象），难以泛化到新物体、新环境和需上下文判断的场景（如满车易碎玻璃器皿应避让、空车可推）。

Method: 将问题形式化为 open-world interactive navigation；MessyNav 以视觉语言模型为核心，在任务规划前显式进行基于VLM的affordance推理，对每个障碍物判定 swipe / pick up / avoid，并据此输出高层操作计划；整体为零样本框架。

Result: 在移动操作平台上于真实室内外环境测试，真实机器人试验成功率较最强基线提升27个百分点；在更大规模离线评测中获得统计显著增益；全程无任务特定训练。

Conclusion: 显式VLM affordance推理可作为开放世界交互式导航的有效先验，实现免训练的跨物体、跨场景泛化；局限在于仅输出高层计划、未展示底层操作鲁棒性与失败模式分析。

Abstract: Real-world environments are messy, with paths often blocked by everyday objects like chairs and toys, requiring robots to perform interactive navigation by manipulating obstacles to clear a path. Existing methods rely on extensive simulation-based learning or per-scene instructions specifying which objects to avoid or manipulate. They struggle to generalize across novel objects, diverse environments, and varied contexts such as a cart of fragile glassware that should be avoided while an empty one can be pushed. We formalize this problem as open-world interactive navigation and present MessyNav, a zero-shot framework based on vision-language models (VLMs). At its core, MessyNav performs explicit VLM-based affordance reasoning before task planning to decide whether to swipe, pick up, or avoid an object and produce a high-level plan. Evaluated on a mobile manipulator across real-world indoor and outdoor environments, MessyNav improves success rate by 27 percentage points (pp) over the strongest baseline in real-robot trials and achieves statistically significant gains in a larger-scale offline evaluation, without task-specific training.

</details>


### [342] [Closed-Loop Physics-Based Manipulation Evaluation at a Commodity Price: What 4.4 L4 GPU hours at $3.50 Buys You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07441)
*Rohun Kshirsagar,Diana Rypkema*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可复现的低成本评测框架（无服务器商用GPU，约3.5美元/38分钟复现一次，200次rollout规模），并用其对稀疏数据下的动作分块（action chunking）做部分受控负结果研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富机器人操作在统计规模上的评测目前基本只有资源充足实验室能负担，需要面向更广泛人群的低成本、可复现评测方案；同时澄清chunking在稀疏数据下是否真的有效及其失效机制。

Method: 基于前人200-rollout规模研究封装可复现评测harness（8个并行分片、共4.4 L4-GPU小时）；对比3个独立训练的chunked检查点与3个单步检查点，共780次rollout；并加入逐步重规划（per-step replanning）控制实验，以分离“chunk预测”与“开环chunk执行”两种机制。

Result: 3个chunked检查点在780次rollout中真实成功率为0，单步检查点成功率为2.6%–11.8%；逐步重规划控制表明失效可归因于训练公式与多步预测本身，而非开环执行chunk；成本约3.5美元、38分钟墙钟时间。

Conclusion: 给出了低成本可复现的物理仿真策略评测harness，并报告chunking在稀疏数据下失效的负结果与机制定位，提示训练配方而非执行方式才是瓶颈，有助于降低该方向评测门槛。

Abstract: Running evaluations of contact-rich robotic manipulation at statistical scale has largely been possible only at well-resourced labs, highlighting the need for affordable and efficient options for broader audiences. We present a reproducible evaluation harness wrapper based on a previous study at 200-rollout scale on serverless commodity GPUs, at a cost of approximately $3.50 USD and 38 minutes of wall-clock per replication (4.4 L4-GPU-hours across eight parallel shards). Using the harness, we report a partially controlled negative result on action chunking with sparse data: three independently trained chunked checkpoints produce zero genuine successes across 780 rollouts, while three single-step checkpoints range from 2.6% to 11.8% success rate. A per-step-replanning control partially isolates chunk prediction, rather than open-loop chunk execution as the mechanism, with the failure localized to the training formulation and multi-step prediction. This work further democratizes physics-based policy development and evaluation through our reusable harness.

</details>


### [343] [FREE-LIVO: Physically Decoupled Yet Tightly Coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07463)
*Reon Tabata,Kenji Koide,Shuji Oishi,Taku Okawara,Mizuho Aoki,Aoki Takanose,Masashi Yokozuka,Jun Miura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FREE-LIVO提出一种无需刚性LiDAR-相机外参标定、也无需LiDAR-相机时间同步的紧耦合LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计方法，用预训练图像特征匹配器将相机图像与由单帧LiDAR扫描生成的高密度强度图像匹配，构造跨时刻重投影因子并与点云配准、视觉跟踪、IMU约束联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIVO方法普遍假设传感器间相对位姿固定且时间同步精确，难以适用于模块化、异构机器人系统（如传感器分装于不同机器人上）；作者希望解除刚性装配与时间同步两大前提。

Method: 用生成模型从单帧LiDAR扫描合成稠密强度图像；用预训练图像特征匹配器建立相机图像与该强度图像的对应关系，得到3D点-像素对应；据此构造直接约束不同采集时刻LiDAR位姿与相机位姿的重投影因子；这些因子与点云配准约束、视觉特征跟踪、各传感器对应的IMU测量在同一因子图中联合优化。

Result: 在公开M3DGR数据集6条序列上，无需提供LiDAR-相机外参即可达到平均ATE RMSE 0.45 m；在最大0.5 s时间同步误差下精度退化极小；LiDAR与相机分装于两台独立运动机器人时，可联合估计各传感器的轨迹。

Conclusion: 免标定、免时间同步的跨传感器因子图设计可行，使LIVO可部署于模块化/异构机器人平台，并支持传感器独立运动的在线轨迹联合估计。

Abstract: LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) integrates complementary LiDAR, camera, and IMU measurements to achieve robust state estimation. However, most existing methods assume a fixed relative pose and accurate time synchronization between sensors. Toward modular and heterogeneous robotic systems, this paper presents FREE-LIVO, a tightly coupled odometry method that requires neither a rigid LiDAR-camera rig nor LiDAR-camera time synchronization. The proposed method uses a pretrained image feature matcher to establish correspondences between a camera image and a dense LiDAR intensity image generated from a single LiDAR scan using a generative model. From the resulting 3D point-to-pixel correspondences, we construct reprojection factors that directly constrain LiDAR and camera poses at different acquisition times. These factors are jointly optimized in a single factor graph together with constraints from point cloud registration, visual feature tracking, and the IMU measurements associated with each sensor. On six sequences from the public M3DGR dataset, FREE-LIVO achieves an average absolute trajectory error (ATE) RMSE of 0.45 m without provided LiDAR-camera extrinsic parameters and exhibits minimal accuracy degradation under time synchronization errors of up to 0.5 s. Furthermore, experiments with the LiDAR and camera mounted on separate robots demonstrate that the method can jointly estimate the trajectories of independently moving sensors.

</details>


### [344] [Kinematic analysis of a double-closed-loop four-bar chain-based leg for a multimodal quadruped-pentarotor robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07470)
*Prashant Raturi,Santhakumar Mohan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对三自由度、双四杆闭环腿部机构进行运动学建模与奇异位形分析，推导雅可比矩阵，并设计地面与空中构型切换的步态，在MATLAB与单腿试验台上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该腿机构是地面模式与空中模式（mid-air aerial reconfiguration）之间切换的关键部件，需要为其两套工作模式设计可行步态，因此必须先建立运动学模型并识别奇异位形。

Method: 在关节空间与任务空间分别建模，推导闭链机构的雅可比矩阵；基于雅可比做奇异分析；在MATLAB中用多种步态仿真，并在单腿试验台上实测。

Result: 得到该双四杆闭环腿的雅可比与奇异集，并在仿真和单腿样机上演示了地面与空中重构步态的可实现运动。

Conclusion: 该腿机构可在两类工作模式下完成运动，雅可比与奇异分析为步态设计提供了依据；但论文仅停留在单腿层面的运动学与步态验证。

Abstract: This paper presents the kinematic modeling and singularity analysis of a three-degree-of-freedom, double four-bar closed-loop quadruped leg mechanism. This leg is essential for transitioning between the two operational modes; therefore, it is examined in both joint and task spaces, leading to the development of its Jacobian matrix, which is vital for designing the gaits required for terrestrial mode and mid-air aerial reconfiguration (for aerial mode). Various gaits are devised for the system's motion in the MATLAB simulation, and their practical implementation on the single-leg test rig is demonstrated.

</details>


### [345] [Fast and Reliable Open-Vocabulary Object Search with Absence Detection from Robot Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07493)
*Nicholas Paul,Devson Butani,Eric Martinson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段架构，将快速候选检索与验证式选择解耦，用序列RGB+位姿观测实现在线物体搜索与"缺失拒答"，显著提升specificity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作器在家庭等日常环境中需按需定位特定物体，同时必须可靠判断目标缺失以避免无效导航或交互。现有方法要么需缓慢的穷举重处理，要么依赖昂贵的3D表示，且评测只关注召回率与精确率而忽略specificity，导致文本/视觉嵌入检索系统在目标不存在时几乎从拒答（specificity≤9%）。

Method: 两阶段架构：第一阶段基于caption/文本与视觉嵌入做快速候选检索；第二阶段加入视觉验证进行verified selection，从而在保持高召回的同时实现可靠的缺失拒答。系统自累积的序列RGB观测与相机位姿构建检索表示，无需显式3D重建。在ScanNet公寓场景上评测8种检索-选择配置及穷举搜索基线，并包含大量缺失物体样本；随后在单块消费级GPU的实体机器人上全板载实时复现。

Result: caption检索+视觉验证的配置在可靠性上达到或超过穷举搜索，同时检测器调用量减少一个数量级；显著改善absent-object拒答能力（相较现有文本/嵌入方法≤9% specificity）。ScanNet分数在实体机器人板载单GPU实时运行中复现。

Conclusion: 将快速检索与验证式选择解耦是以速度实现准确物体搜索与可靠缺失检测的有效范式；specificity应作为物体搜索系统的核心评测指标；该方案可实时部署于真实移动操作平台。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators deployed in everyday human environments, such as homes for assistive care or flexible automation, often need to locate specific objects on demand from accumulated observations. Equally important, they must reliably determine when a target is absent before committing to navigation or interaction. Existing approaches demand either slow exhaustive re-processing or expensive 3D representations, and are evaluated on recall and accuracy rather than specificity, leaving their ability to abstain on missing targets unmeasured. We find existing systems that search using text or visual embedding almost never abstain on absent objects achieving at most 9\% specificity. To support accurate search at speed, we present a two-stage architecture that decouples fast candidate retrieval from verified selection, enabling both high recall and reliable absence detection from sequential RGB observations with pose. We evaluate eight retrieval-selection configurations plus an exhaustive search baseline across ScanNet apartment scenes, including significant numbers of absent objects. Adding a visual verification step to caption-based retrieval matches or exceeds exhaustive search reliability while requiring an order of magnitude fewer detector calls. We reproduce ScanNet scores on a physical robot operating entirely on-board with a single consumer GPU running in real time.

</details>


### [346] [LMGN: A Unified Policy Learning Model for Last-Mile Grasp-Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07515)
*Xuenmin Hu,Qingxue Qian,Wei Luo,Benyi Ma,Bo Tang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LMGN统一策略学习模型，用于四足机器人的"最后一英里"抓取导航：以显式距离-方向可达表征(ED-DR)建模可抓取基座位姿，用语义对齐的分层混合专家(HMoE)策略建模分阶段行为，并联合优化导航与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有腿足-机械臂研究多独立处理全局导航或操作，忽视"最后一英里"问题——需联合决定基座放置、接近方向与抓取执行；且不同操作阶段需要差异化控制行为。

Method: 1) ED-DR：显式表征与抓取兼容的基座放置与接近方向可达性；2) HMoE策略：语义对齐的分层混合专家，按基座调整、预抓取对齐、抓取执行、物体抬升等阶段建模阶段感知行为；3) 优化方法：联合对齐导航与操作、阶段相关专家路由、动作级一致性约束。

Result: 在Habitat与Isaac Gym仿真中持续优于SOTA方法，表明抓取感知可达性与阶段感知策略学习结合能显著提升最后一英里导航与目标抓取成功率。

Conclusion: 将抓取感知的可达性表征与分阶段专家策略统一到单一策略中，是解决移动-操作最后一英里抓取问题的有效范式；但当前证据限于仿真，真机部署与跨场景泛化仍待验证。

Abstract: Legged robots equipped with manipulators have shown significant potential in the fields of daily life and industry in complex environments. However, existing methods mainly focus on global navigation or manipulation independently, while overlooking the last-mile navigation problem, where the robot must jointly determine the base placement, approach direction, and grasp execution to achieve reliable object grasping, and different manipulation stages require distinct control behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose Last-Mile Grasp-Navigation (LMGN), a unified policy learning model for Quadruped robot loco-manipulation. An Explicit Distance and Directional Reachability (ED-DR) representation is first introduced to explicitly represent the features of grasp-compatible base placement and approach direction. We then develop a semantically aligned Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts (HMoE) policy that models stage-aware behaviors across the stages of base adjustment, pre-grasp alignment and grasp execution, and object lift. Finally, a new optimization method that jointly aligns navigation and manipulation, stage-dependent expert routing, and action-level consistency is proposed to optimize the HMoE policy. Extensive experiments on the Habitat and Isaac Gym simulators demonstrate that LMGN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, denoting that integrating grasp-aware reachability with stage-aware policy learning significantly improves the success rates of last-mile navigation and target grasp for robots.

</details>


### [347] [Training-Independent Validation of a Learning-Based Controller for Stunt Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07516)
*Kiruthiga Chandra Shekar,Aliasghar Arab,Jingang Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文主张学习型控制策略的验证必须独立于训练：应改变训练中固定不变的物理参数，并采用训练目标中不存在的验收准则；并用Sim2Real2Sim回环验证特技驾驶策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型车辆机动控制器通常在训练条件与目标下验证，因而显得高度可靠；但真实道路上变化的参数往往并非模型训练时的参数，导致验证结论存在偏差（如宽松的终端航向角指标将最差设置评为最佳）。

Method: 以G-Maneuver学习型控制策略为对象：借助汽车安全分析框架通过覆盖度测试识别验证参数；构建Sim2Real2Sim回环，先用仿真筛选安全区域再实车试验；对比仿真与真车的运动学指标（航向变化、侧滑、速度归一化横摆角速度）与力/侧倾指标的一致性。

Result: G-Maneuver在训练条件下成功率达90.11%，但宽松终端航向角评分将最差设置排为最佳；实车验收率16.9%；航向变化、侧滑、速度归一化横摆角速度三类指标在仿真与真实间一致，力与侧倾指标不一致。

Conclusion: 验证须与训练解耦并采用独立参数与验收准则；运动学层面的特技驾驶指标具备仿真-现实一致性，可支撑安全部署，而力/侧倾指标不足以迁移。

Abstract: Learned controllers for vehicle maneuvers are usually validated under the conditions and objective used to train them, where they appear highly reliable, yet the parameters that change on real roads are often not those the model was trained on. We argue that validation must be independent of training: it should vary physical parameters that training kept fixed and use acceptance criteria absent from the training objective. A learning-based control policy, G-Maneuver, with 90.11% success is tested for stunt-level maneuvers. A looser terminal-heading score ranked the worst setting as the best. Because real stunt tests are safety-critical, a simulation-to-reality-to-simulation (Sim2Real2Sim) loop uses the simulation to select safe zones for real-vehicle trials with 16.9% acceptance. Automotive safety analysis frameworks are used to identify parameters for validation using coverage tests. We demonstrated that the kinematic stunt-driving indicators, namely heading change, sideslip, and speed-normalized yaw rate, match between simulation and the real world, while force and roll indicators do not, which identifies the indicators that can support safe deployment. A video of the experiments is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [348] [Embodied RAG++: Retrieval-Augmented Generation From What Exists to What Works in Embodied Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07517)
*Fan Yang,Tatsuya Kikuzuki,Jun Liang,Jun Liu,Wenkai Li,Ryosuke Kawamura,Ananya Hazarika,Prajit KrisshnaKumar,Shaunak Mehta,Yuushi Toyoda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E2W-Bench（Exists-to-Works）将动态混合场景图与真实产品说明书链接，评测“满足功能需求且机器人可实际够达”的机器人–物体指派；并提出 Embodied RAG++，把检索限定到候选物体对应说明书、按产品型号缓存能力、用确定性空间推理判定可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景图只记录物体存在与位置，缺失仅在产品手册中记载的能力信息；现有 RAG 方法虽能读手册，但很少联合推理物体能力、物理实体、机器人状态与动态连通性（可达性/移动性/垂直高度/接插件约束）。

Method: 构建 E2W-Bench：动态混合场景图 + 真实产品手册，编码可用性、移动性、垂直可达、连接器四类约束；提出 Embodied RAG++：空间接地的检索范围限定（仅检索候选物体关联手册）、按产品模型缓存抽取的能力、确定性空间推理求解机器人–物体可行性。

Result: 对象-机配对准确率 0.90（Qwen2.5-32B）/1.00（Llama3.3-70B），基线最高仅 0.30/0.55；在检索类方法中预处理时间最短，总 token 数并列最低。

Conclusion: 将产品手册知识与空间推理结合可显著提升具身任务中的机器人–物体指派可靠性，为能力接地的具身 RAG 提供基准与方法参考。

Abstract: To serve a request in a human environment, a robot system must find an object that satisfies a functional requirement and a robot that can feasibly reach it. Scene graphs record which objects exist and where, but they often omit capabilities documented only in product manuals. Retrieval-augmented generation methods can read such manuals, yet they rarely reason jointly over object capabilities, physical instances, robot states, and dynamic connectivity. We introduce E2W-Bench, the Exists-to-Works Benchmark, which links dynamic hybrid scene graphs with real product manuals to evaluate capability-grounded robot--object assignment under availability, mobility, vertical-reach, and connector constraints. We also present Embodied RAG++, a spatially grounded retrieval framework that scopes retrieval to the manuals linked to candidate objects, caches extracted capabilities per product model, and resolves robot--object feasibility through deterministic spatial reasoning. On E2W-Bench, Embodied RAG++ reaches a pair accuracy of 0.90 with Qwen2.5-32B and 1.00 with Llama3.3-70B, compared with at most 0.30 and 0.55 for the baselines, while requiring the shortest preprocessing time among the retrieval-based methods and tying for the lowest total token count.

</details>


### [349] [Semantics-to-Contact: A Stagewise Framework for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07532)
*Guanren Qiao,Ruixiang Ouyang,Sheng Xu,Yueci Deng,Yunxin Tai,Kui Jia,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S2C：先用视觉语言模型定位任务相关区域，再用人在回路残差强化学习结合力反馈精修接触行为，并辅以保留机器人与任务物体的背景随机化增强，实现视觉鲁棒的真实世界富接触操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界强化学习易过拟合训练场景的视觉外观，而富接触操作又需要在复杂接触动力学下实现精确控制并对多样视觉条件保持鲁棒，二者难以由单一端到端策略同时满足。

Method: 提出分阶段框架：第一阶段用VLM在视觉多样场景中定位任务相关区域，提供粗粒度空间引导以减轻探索难度；第二阶段在定位区域内用人在回路的残差RL策略，基于力反馈学习细粒度接触行为；并提出以物体为中心的可视化增强，仅随机化背景外观而保留机器人与任务相关物体。

Result: 在6个真实世界富接触操作任务上，成功率持续优于强基线，并在显著视觉与位置变化下保持可靠性能。

Conclusion: 分阶段的语义聚焦与接触精修是通向视觉鲁棒真实世界富接触操作的一条实用路径。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise control under complex contact dynamics while remaining robust to diverse visual conditions. However, real-world reinforcement learning often overfits to the visual appearance of training scenes. We propose Semantics-to-Contact (S2C), a stagewise framework for visually robust contact-rich manipulation. The insight is to decompose the problem into two stages: semantic focusing and contact refinement. In the first stage, a vision-language model localizes task-relevant regions across visually diverse scenes, providing coarse spatial guidance and reducing the burden of exploration. In the second stage, a real-world human-in-the-loop residual reinforcement learning policy learns fine-grained contact behaviors within the localized region using force feedback. To further improve visual robustness, we introduce an object-centric visual augmentation strategy that randomizes background appearance while preserving the robot and task-relevant objects. Experiments on six contact-rich real-world manipulation tasks exhibit that S2C consistently improves success rates over strong baselines and maintains reliable performance under significant visual and positional variations. These results demonstrate that stagewise semantic focusing and contact refinement provide a practical path toward visually robust real-world contact-rich manipulation. Our demos can be seen in this https URL .

</details>


### [350] [Unified Overview of Safety Certificate Functions: Techniques from Hamilton--Jacobi Reachability and Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07537)
*Hao Wang,Max Cohen,Zeyuan Feng,Donggeon David Oh,Oswin So,Songyuan Zhang,Javier Borquez,Chuchu Fan,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Haimin Hu,Jason J. Choi,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该教程以HJ可达性分析与控制屏障函数（CBF）两大类方法为主线，系统梳理安全证书合成的通用表述、经典与学习方法及基准对比，并给出方法选型建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全证书合成方法分散于多个研究领域，缺乏何时选用何种方法的指导，亟需统一的整体性综述与可比较的基准。

Method: 以HJ可达性与CBF两条技术路线为框架：先给出各自的一般化问题表述，再介绍古典数值方法与基于学习的证书合成工具；随后构建复杂度不同的两类车辆系统基准问题，用于评估证书质量及其在安全关键控制中的有效性。

Result: 通过统一基准对多种证书合成方法进行横向比较，展示了不同方法在证书质量与安全控制效果上的差异。

Conclusion: 提供了安全证书合成的整体视角与面向实践的选型指南，可作为自主系统安全保障的方法论参考。

Abstract: Safety is a central concern in modern autonomous systems. Safety certificates---functions whose existence guarantees that a system meets its safety specification---have long been used as a principled tool for rigorously reasoning about safety. Methods for synthesizing such certificates are scattered across research areas, with little guidance on when to use a particular approach. This tutorial provides a holistic treatment of safety certificate synthesis centered on two widely used families: Hamilton--Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis and control barrier functions (CBFs). For each family of methods, we first introduce its general formulation, along with classical and learning-based tools for certificate synthesis. Next, we introduce two benchmark problems consisting of vehicle systems of differing complexity to assess the certificate quality of different synthesis methods and their effectiveness in safety-critical control. We use these benchmarks to compare various methods and provide practical guidance for selecting an approach.

</details>


### [351] [Whole-Body Elasto-Geometrical Calibration of Wheeled Humanoid Robots Using a Shared-Waist Dual-Chain Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07560)
*Pengxin Zha,Yi Lu,Haojun Ma,Jun Zhu,Minghao Qiu,Xiaoyan Wang,Wei Shan,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轮式人形机器人“腰+双臂”一体化运动学标定方法：在估计跟踪器与靶标外参后，从两法兰靶标位置联合辨识腰部与双臂参数（含腰-基座、平行轴、分支、柔顺参数），用高斯先验与证据最大化尺度稳定估计，降低双末端误差至0.50–1.11 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式人形机器人双臂共用一条无力矩传感器的驱动腰，两个末端精度耦合于单一腰部模型；独立标定或顺序单链标定会把同一腰部误差分别归入两条链，产生两个不一致的腰模型。

Method: 两阶段：先估计光学跟踪器与靶标外参，再由两法兰靶标位置联合求解腰部与双臂参数；模型含腰-基座、平行轴、分支参数，臂柔顺由测得力矩驱动、腰柔顺由机器人质量模型重力矩驱动，采用高斯先验并以证据最大化选择尺度。理论上独立标定与顺序单链标定为该问题之特例，其腰部信息为各单链Schur补之和。

Result: 两台不同腰部机器人（各含空载与带载）标定后，留出位姿平均误差由11.3–31.1 mm降至0.50–1.11 mm；在20个位姿至全集各训练规模下，平均误差比两种已发表全身标定方法的改编低17–63%，主要收益来自腰柔顺项；单独标定得到两个腰模型，互换使用使另一臂误差升至最高3.21 mm。

Conclusion: 联合整体标定优于分链独立标定，关键在于共享腰部柔顺建模；方法在真实机器人与多种负载条件下验证了毫米级末端精度。

Abstract: Wheeled humanoid robots carry both arms on one actuated waist, usually without torque sensors, so the accuracy of both end-effectors depends on a single waist model. After a first stage that estimates the tracker and target extrinsics, we identify the waist and both arms in one problem from the target positions on both flanges, measured by an optical tracker. The kinematic model is complete, with waist-base, parallel-axis, and branch parameters; arm compliances are driven by measured torques and waist compliances by gravity torques of the robot's mass model; and Gaussian priors with evidence-maximizing scales stabilize the estimate. Independent and sequential single-chain calibration are restrictions of this problem, and its waist information is the sum of the single-chain Schur complements. On two robots with different waists, each without and with a payload, calibration reduces the mean errors on held-out poses from 11.3-31.1 mm to 0.50-1.11 mm. For every training-set size from 20 poses to all, the method has 17-63% lower mean errors than adaptations of two published whole-body calibration methods, mainly through the waist compliance. Independent calibration yields two waist models; using either for both arms raises the other arm's error to up to 3.21 mm.

</details>


### [352] [Data-Driven Configuration of Protective Separation Zones in Collaborative Robot Cells from Operator Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07621)
*Aulon Bajrami,Theo Jacobs,Werner Kraus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于相机观测的操作员占用热力图，按ISO/TS 15066速度-分离监测原则生成可变的协作机器人防护区域，在两处工业现场实现防护场缩小约17%、产能提升9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协作机器人单元的安全隔离区按最坏情况设定，忽略操作员实际作业位置，导致过度保守的空间与节拍损失。

Method: 基于姿态的感知管线叠加活动识别；跨多个生产班次累计操作员位置形成占用热力图；由此推导防护场，在可达机器人速度与所需安全距离之间做权衡（SSM原则）；输出为建议性配置，需经风险评估人工审核而非自动下发控制器。

Result: 在两个工业伙伴的两台运行单元上验证，防护场缩小约17%，实测产能提升9%；但采纳取决于技术收益之外的组织因素。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的占用感知可安全收缩防护区并带来可测的生产率提升，但落地需嵌入风险评估流程并兼顾非技术采纳障碍。

Abstract: The protective separation zone of a collaborative robot cell is commonly dimensioned for a worst case that disregards where the operator is actually present during the task. This paper presents a camera-based, data-driven method that configures this zone from observed operator occupancy. Operator positions accumulated over several production shifts are aggregated into an occupancy heatmap, from which a protective field is derived that trades off the achievable robot velocity against the required safety distance, following the speed and separation monitoring principle of ISO/TS 15066. The resulting configuration is advisory, that is, reviewed within the risk assessment rather than applied autonomously to the robot controller. The method, which builds on a pose-based perception pipeline with activity recognition, was evaluated in two operational cells at two industrial partners, where the protective field could be reduced by approximately 17% and a productivity increase of 9% was measured. However, adoption proved to depend on more than the demonstrated gain alone.

</details>


### [353] [Real-Time Event-Based LED Tracking and Pose Estimation with PWM-Encoded Markers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07630)
*Junkai Niu,Sheng Zhong,Delei Kong,Xin Wang,Kaizhen Sun,Yusen Zhang,Mingzhen Gan,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于事件相机与PWM编码LED标记的实时LED跟踪与6-DoF相对位姿估计框架，采用双率异步跟踪，并在无人机-麦轮机器人协同平台上完成闭环实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备微秒级延迟、抗运动模糊与高动态范围特性，适合高速运动与剧烈光照变化下的状态估计；但现有事件相机位姿估计多依赖人工标记的频率编码，频率资源占用大、计算开销高，且高速运动下LED身份关联易失效。

Method: 多个LED共享同一闪烁频率、以不同PWM占空比编码身份，降低频率资源与计算成本；构建双率异步跟踪框架，低频段做LED检测与身份关联，高频段用原始事件流做像素级更新；由LED身份与位置建立2D-3D对应，实时求解米制6-DoF相对位姿；在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上机载部署。

Result: 自建数据集上验证在大运动流与图像平面LED瞬态重叠下仍保持鲁棒；四旋翼+Mecanum轮移动机器人协同平台上，在时变HDR光照下完成悬停与目标跟随的闭环无人机引导实验，实现机载实时运行。

Conclusion: 以PWM占空比替代多频编码的思路有效降低了标记系统资源需求，双率异步跟踪保障了高速运动下的身份关联与像素级精度；该工作为事件相机标记跟踪与相对位姿估计提供了可复现的数据集与实现，适合近距离、高动态场景的机器人引导任务。

Abstract: Event cameras are neuromorphic vision sensors with pixels that independently and asynchronously respond to brightness changes at microsecond resolution, supporting state estimation under rapid motion, motion blur, and high dynamic range (HDR) illumination. This also enables capture of high-frequency blinking patterns from light-emitting diodes (LEDs). Based on this, we propose a real-time event-based framework for LED tracking and six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) relative pose estimation using pulse-width modulation (PWM)-encoded LED markers. Multiple LEDs share a common blinking frequency and encode identities through distinct PWM duty cycles, reducing frequency-resource requirements and computational cost. Our dual-rate asynchronous tracking framework combines low-rate LED detection with high-rate raw-event updates, maintaining robust LED identity association and accurate pixel-level tracking under rapid motion. LED identities and positions establish reliable 2D-3D correspondences for real-time metric 6-DoF relative pose estimation. Extensive evaluations on a self-collected dataset demonstrate robustness under large motion flow and transient image-plane LED overlap. Furthermore, we develop a coordinated robotic platform comprising a quadrotor and a Mecanum-wheeled mobile robot, demonstrating the proposed framework's potential for closed-loop drone guidance through hovering and target-following experiments under time-varying HDR illumination, with onboard deployment on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX. The dataset and implementation will be released to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [354] [GraspBench: Execution-Aware Cross-Robot Evaluation of 6-DoF Grasp Generation in MuJoCo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07651)
*Abdul Basit,Bibek Poudel,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspBench: 用MuJoCo受控协议端到端评测6条抓取生成管线在4种机器人、5个物体、固定位姿网格上的可执行性（含IK过滤、运动规划与固定执行序列），共3240次评测；GraspGen最优(73.1%)，GPG候选最多但仅44.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6-DoF抓取生成器多以抓取分数、数据集级指标或单一机器人演示评价，无法反映抓取能否被特定机器人实际执行；缺少在统一协议下对"生成—IK—规划—执行"完整管线进行机器人特定可执行性比较的基准。

Method: 构建MuJoCo受控评测协议：对生成候选做机器人特定IK过滤、运动规划，并执行固定的approach-close-lift序列，以二值抬升成功为判据；缺失输出保守计为失败；覆盖6条配置管线×4种机器人本体×5个诊断物体×结构化桌面位姿网格，每方法540场景，共3240次方法-场景评测；辅以按机器人与物体条件的分析以及cluster-aware配对区间。

Result: GraspGen整体成功率最高（73.1%），EconomicGrasp次之（63.5%）；GPG原生候选集最大但成功率仅44.3%，说明候选数量不等于可执行性；不同机器人本体与物体几何下成功率差异显著；配对区间支持主要方法间差异，但不刻画重复执行随机性。

Conclusion: 抓取性能由生成器、机器人本体、场景与执行协议共同决定；本工作刻画的是固定场景下限定的完整配置管线，而非模型内在质量或随机可重复性。

Abstract: Modern 6-DoF grasp generators are often evaluated using grasp scores, dataset-level metrics, or single-robot demonstrations, yet proposal quality alone does not determine whether a grasp is executable by a particular robot. We present GraspBench, a controlled MuJoCo protocol for evaluating complete grasp-generation pipelines after robot-specific inverse-kinematics filtering, motion planning, and fixed approach-close-lift execution. GraspBench evaluates six configured pipelines across four robot embodiments, five diagnostic objects, and a structured tabletop pose grid, yielding 540 scenarios per method and 3,240 method-scenario evaluations in total. Missing outputs are conservatively counted as failures, and success follows the binary lift criterion produced by each method's execution runner. GraspGen achieves the highest overall success rate (73.1%), followed by EconomicGrasp (63.5%), while robot- and object-conditioned analyses reveal substantial variation across embodiments and object geometries. GPG produces the largest native candidate sets but achieves 44.3% success, indicating that proposal volume alone does not guarantee robot-executable grasps. Cluster-aware paired intervals support the principal method differences while not estimating repeated-execution uncertainty. The results characterize complete configured pipelines on fixed scenarios rather than intrinsic model quality or stochastic repeatability, showing that grasping performance depends jointly on the generator, robot embodiment, scene, and execution protocol.

</details>


### [355] [Energy-Aware Open-Vocabulary Detection Scheduling for Low-Power Robotic Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07663)
*Abdul Basit,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向低功耗移动机器人的能量感知开放词汇检测调度器：利用里程计、地图变化与检测置信度在Jetson Orin Nano上切换YOLO-World-S的低/中/高功耗模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇检测(OVD)使机器人可运行时查询目标，但固定频率推理在静止、轨迹重复或视点稳定时浪费嵌入式算力与能耗，需要在保证任务可靠性的前提下按机器人状态调节感知开销。

Method: 构建机器人状态驱动的OVD调度器：以里程计运动量、地图变化量和检测置信度作为状态信号，触发三档YOLO-World-S推理频率/功耗模式，在Jetson Orin Nano + AgileX LIMO平台上部署。评估含40次真实导航试验（2类目标×5种摆放×4种感知策略）与60 s静止微基准。

Result: 任务成功8/10，高于各固定频率基线的7/10；相对8 Hz高频基线，Jetson平均输入功率降低5.2%，OVD实际调用率降低34.0%。静止微基准中平均功率由8.045 W降至7.301 W，能耗由484.7 J降至438.1 J。但任务窗口总能耗未必最小，长任务会抵消瞬时节能收益。

Conclusion: 机器人状态感知的OVD调度是可行的功耗管理机制，能在竞争性任务可靠性下降低瞬时功率与调用率；任务窗口能耗需结合任务进度与运行时间综合解读，且试验样本量有限。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) lets robots search for run-time object queries, but fixed-rate inference spends embedded compute on low-value frames during idle motion, map revisits, or stable viewpoints. This paper presents an energy-aware OVD scheduler for low-power mobile robots. The scheduler uses odometry, map change, and detector confidence to switch among low-, mid-, and high-power YOLO-World-S modes on a Jetson Orin Nano. We evaluate the energy-quality trade-off on an AgileX LIMO using 40 real navigation trials: two target objects, five placements per target-policy pair, and four perception policies. Across bottle and cup trials, the scheduler records 8/10 mission success compared with 7/10 for each fixed-rate baseline, while reducing mean Jetson input power (a Jetson-side power-draw metric) by 5.2% relative to an 8 Hz high-rate baseline and lowering achieved OVD call rate by 34.0%. It does not minimize mission-window Jetson input energy because longer missions can erase instantaneous power savings. A separate 60 s stationary microbenchmark isolates the perception workload: the scheduler lowers mean power from 8.045 W to 7.301 W and energy from 484.7 J to 438.1 J relative to the 8 Hz baseline. These results support robot-state-aware OVD scheduling as a promising power-management mechanism with competitive task reliability, while showing that mission-window Jetson input energy must be read together with task progress and runtime.

</details>


### [356] [PhySA: Decoupling Aesthetic and Physical Rewards for Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07679)
*Yunwan Fu,Sicheng He,Jiageng Mao,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文发现VLM的美学评分与物理平滑度方向相反（关节加速度越高评分越高，平均r=+0.66），据此提出PhySA，将稀疏异步的VLM奖励与基于模拟器状态的相位触发物理修正通道解耦；在MuJoCo Humanoid-v5上相对PPO/Text2Reward/Eureka降低关节加速度26–32%，但迁移到IsaacLab与Unitree G1失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无显式平滑目标训练的人形运动策略倾向于产生抖动、高能耗步态，加重执行器负担；VLM可作为无需特征工程的奖励来源，但作者发现美学偏好与物理平滑度存在冲突，因此需要将二者解耦处理。

Method: PhySA将奖励分为两个可独立移除的通道：(1) 稀疏、异步的VLM美学评分奖励；(2) 由模拟器状态触发、相位对齐的修正通道，用于调整物理惩罚（如关节加速度）权重。通过消融定位性能来源。

Result: MuJoCo Humanoid-v5、2.6M步、三随机种子下，较vanilla PPO、Text2Reward、Eureka降低关节加速度26–32%（代价是回合变短）；单次长程实验较Prompt2Policy降低59.5%，但三种子复现仅7.2%且不显著；迁移到IsaacLab和Unitree G1均无收益，即使重标定平滑项亦然。

Conclusion: 平滑度增益实际来自手工设计的物理修正通道而非VLM美学评分；VLM奖励在平滑性目标上存在方向性偏差，且方法泛化性弱、证据不一致，需在更多平台与统计上更充分的实验验证。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion policies trained without an explicit smoothness objective tend to produce jerky, high-energy gaits that stress actuators. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer an appealing reward source: given a rendered rollout, a VLM returns an aesthetic quality score on [0,10] without task-specific feature engineering. We find, however, that aesthetic preference and physical smoothness point in opposite directions: across four production VLMs, clips with higher joint acceleration receive higher aesthetic scores (mean Pearson r=+0.66). We study PhySA (Physical Smoothness and Aesthetics), which keeps the two on separate, independently removable reward channels: a sparse, asynchronous VLM score and a phase-triggered correction channel that adjusts physical penalty weights from simulator state. Ablations attribute the smoothness gain to the correction channel. On MuJoCo Humanoid-v5 at 2.6M steps, PhySA lowers joint acceleration by 26 to 32% relative to vanilla PPO, Text2Reward, and Eureka (three seeds), at the cost of shorter episodes. In a single extended run it lowers joint acceleration by 59.5% relative to Prompt2Policy, while a three-seed replication shows a smaller, non-significant reduction (7.2%). The gain does not transfer to IsaacLab or the Unitree G1, even after rescaling the smoothness term.

</details>


### [357] [Not Losing Your Way: Mitigating Visual-Inertial Odometry Drift through On-Demand Visual Feature Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07687)
*Simone D'Angelo,Gianmarco Corrado,Federico Esposito,Vincenzo Scognamiglio,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"主动环境改造"VIO框架：无人机主动释放地面标记或接触墙面制造视觉特征，用信息量指标+时序确认与滞回触发干预，缓解重复/弱纹理环境下的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO在航拍巡检的重复纹理或弱纹理场景中易退化；现有方案多依赖先验地图、额外定位传感器或被动鲁棒性改进，缺少仅用相机+IMU就主动"改造环境"以恢复可观测性的手段。

Method: 受"小拇指汤姆"启发，UAV通过释放地面标记或物理接触墙体标记产生按需视觉特征；设计基于信息量的干预触发指标，配合时序确认与滞回避免抖动式频繁触发；评估含3个仿真环境与900次因子实验，并在真实走廊中用OptiTrack做飞行控制、VIO标记决策在线运行。

Result: 较低巡航速度与主动标记均与发散率下降相关；非发散运行内精度取决于速度-策略交互；走廊实验（每种配置5次）显示组合框架相对基线VIO将位置RMSE降低约35%。

Conclusion: 仅凭相机与IMU、无先验地图的主动特征创建设计可有效缓解VIO漂移，为感知-行动耦合的SLAM鲁棒性提供新范式；但验证规模有限，与计算/机械代价的权衡和泛化性仍待进一步探究。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) can degrade during aerial inspection in repetitive or poorly textured environments. We propose an active environment-modification framework for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) relying on cameras and an inertial measurement unit only, without prior maps or additional localization sensors. Inspired by the fairy tale of Tom Thumb, the UAV creates visual features on demand by releasing ground markers or marking walls through physical contact. An information-based indicator triggers interventions using temporal confirmation and hysteresis. Evaluation includes three simulated environments and a 900-run factorial campaign. Lower cruise speed and active marking are associated with reduced divergence, while accuracy among non-divergent runs depends on speed-strategy interactions. Corridor experiments, with five runs per configuration, use OptiTrack for flight control while VIO-based marking decisions remain active online. The combined framework reduces the reported position root mean square error by approximately 35% relative to baseline VIO, supporting active feature creation for drift mitigation.

</details>


### [358] [AffectiveVLA: Safe and Intent-Aware VLA Control for Human-Humanoid Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07712)
*Nikita Kuzmin,Georgii Demianchuk,Mariya Lezina,Yara Mahmoud,Iana Zhura,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffectiveVLA：在Unitree G1人形+灵巧手上部署"意图门控"VLA流水线，用轻量RGB意图识别模型将握手/击拳/拥抱等社交触碰意图转为语言提示，低置信度时抑制运动，接触期用交互专用柔顺配置调节阻抗，并有语音触发的闩锁式安全停机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交触碰交互（握手、击拳、拥抱）要求人形机器人同时具备人类意图推断、接触丰富运动生成与物理安全保证，而现有VLA策略缺乏显式意图门控与安全失败机制，易产生误触发动作。

Method: 双机载RGB相机+轻量意图分类模型输出语言提示驱动VLA策略；置信度阈值门控执行；按交互类型切换臂阻抗柔顺配置；独立语音触发闩锁安全停机。VR遥操作采集约1300条多模态episode，微调GR00T、RLDX-1、Being-H0.5、UnifoLM-VLA-0四个VLA模型并于真机评测。

Result: 真机上GR00T为最佳单体模型；加入意图识别后macro-F1由0.75提升至0.90；意图模型p95推理延迟6.27ms，不影响实时性。

Conclusion: 显式意图门控可在不牺牲实时性的前提下显著提升VLA社交交互可靠性，结合柔顺控制与安全停机形成完整的人形接触交互系统。

Abstract: Social touch interactions, such as handshakes, fist bumps, and hugs, require humanoid robots to infer human intent, generate contact-rich motions, and maintain safe physical contact. We present AffectiveVLA, an intent-gated Vision-Language-Action (VLA) pipeline deployed on a Unitree G1 humanoid with dexterous hands. A lightweight RGB-based model recognizes interaction intent from two onboard cameras and converts it into a language prompt for the VLA policy. Low-confidence predictions suppress motion execution, reducing unintended activations. During physical contact, interaction-specific compliance profiles adjust arm impedance, while an independent voice-triggered mechanism initiates a latched safe-stop procedure. Using VR teleoperation, we collected approximately 1,300 multimodal interaction episodes and fine-tuned four VLA models: GR00T, RLDX-1, Being-H0.5, and UnifoLM-VLA-0. On-robot evaluation identifies GR00T as the best-performing standalone model. Integrating intent recognition increases its macro-F1 score from 0.75 to 0.90, while the intent model achieves a p95 inference latency of 6.27 ms. These results demonstrate that explicit intent gating improves the reliability of VLA-based social interaction without compromising real-time operation.

</details>


### [359] [Dual-Arm Tabletop Rearrangement with Movable Stacking and Gap-Aware Push Transfers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07726)
*Mohammadreza Firoozalizadeh,Zakiye Rostamirad,Arman Barghi,Ahmad Kalhor,Mohammadreza Nayeri,Mehdi Tale Masouleh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双臂杂乱桌面重排框架：结合可移动堆叠缓冲与Gap-Aware Push Transfer（扫描共享边界找无碰撞传递缺口、放置后单臂推入对方工作区），实现异步跨边界交接，并用MCTS联合规划双臂的缓冲与传递决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽工作区双臂重排中自由空间有限需缓冲，共享可达区附近/跨区物体须协调搬运以避免臂间碰撞；传统固定handoff构型限制交换位置与方式、强制双臂时空同步，在高密度布局下灵活性差。

Method: Gap-Aware Push Transfer：扫描两臂共享边界寻找无碰撞传递缺口，将物体置于选定穿越点附近再推入接收工作区，无需双臂同时交互；结合可移动堆叠（buffering）作为空间置换手段；构建MCTS联合规划器，同时推理缓冲放置与传递决策，规划后再用执行代价评估方案。

Result: 实验显示该方法缩短了联合执行序列；随场景密度增加，相对固定handoff基线的优势增强，在更密集场景中取得更低执行代价，在较稀疏场景中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 异步推式传递可替代固定构型交接，解除双臂时空同步约束，与堆叠缓冲联合搜索能有效提升高密度场景下的双臂重排效率。

Abstract: Object rearrangement in cluttered tabletop scenes is challenging, especially in wide workspaces where dual-arm systems improve coverage and efficiency. Limited free space often necessitates buffering, while objects near or across the shared reachability region of the manipulators must be transferred under careful coordination to avoid inter-arm collisions. Conventional inter-arm transfer typically relies on a fixed handoff configuration, which constrains where and how objects are exchanged, enforces spatiotemporal synchronization of the arms, and reduces flexibility in dense layouts. This paper presents a dual-arm rearrangement framework that combines movable stacking with Gap-Aware Push Transfer for asynchronous cross-boundary exchange. The method scans the shared boundary to identify collision-free transfer gaps, places an object near a selected crossing point, and then pushes it into the receiving workspace without requiring simultaneous interaction between the two arms. A joint Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-based planner coordinates both manipulators while reasoning over buffering and transfer decisions, and execution cost is used after planning to assess the resulting plan. Experiments show that the proposed method shortens joint execution sequences and becomes increasingly advantageous over a fixed handoff baseline as scene density increases, achieving lower execution cost in denser scenes while remaining competitive in lighter ones.

</details>


### [360] [Wedge-Jamming Mechanism for String Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07748)
*Taro Tako,Takanori Jin,Hiroshi Kaminaga,Taisuke Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出楔形卡阻（wedge-jamming）变刚度机构：在钢丝卡阻链式模块中引入楔形接触面，通过改变楔角调节对不同方向扰动的保持特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统钢丝卡阻变刚度机构仅关注绕关节轴方向的保持力矩，对其他方向扰动的抵抗能力研究不足。

Method: 将模块间接触面设计为楔形，利用钢丝张力改变模块间摩擦力实现刚度控制；通过调整楔角（外楔/内楔构型）改变各向异性保持能力，并进行实验对比验证。

Result: 外楔构型提升对垂直方向扰动的抵抗能力，内楔构型提升对扭转方向扰动的抵抗能力；实验证实保持性能的各向异性依赖于楔形几何。

Conclusion: 楔形几何为钢丝卡阻机构提供了一种可调的各向异性变刚度设计手段，可针对不同扰动方向定制保持特性。

Abstract: Variable stiffness mechanisms that achieve both flexibility and rigidity are important technologies for robotic systems. Recently, wire-based jamming mechanisms have attracted significant attention. In the wire-based jamming mechanisms, modules are chained by a wire, and the overall stiffness is controlled by changing the friction between the modules through wire tension. These mechanisms have been widely studied due to their simple structure and controllability. However, conventional wire-based jamming mechanisms mainly focus on holding torque along the joint axis, and the resistance against disturbances in other directions has not been sufficiently explored. In this study, we propose a wedge-jamming mechanism that introduces wedge-shaped contact surfaces into the modules of wire-based jamming structures. By changing the wedge angle, the holding characteristics against disturbances in different directions can be adjusted. Experimental results show that an out-wedge configuration improves resistance to vertical disturbances, while an in-wedge configuration enhances resistance to torsional disturbances. These results demonstrate that the anisotropic holding performance of wire-based jamming mechanisms depend on wedge geometry.

</details>


### [361] [Flexible Piezoelectric Tactile Sensing System for Intelligent Object Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07779)
*Razan Khalifeh,Mohamad Yaacoub,Christian Gianoglio,Moustafa Saleh,Maurizio Valle,Yahya Abbass*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 分布式压电触觉传感系统结合时空热图与2D-CNN，实现抓取初期（100ms窗口）的实时物体识别，端到端延迟约101ms、能耗63.8µJ/次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取与操作需要触觉信息，但在抓取早期阶段、强实时约束下准确识别物体仍具挑战；现有方法在传感器数量、时间窗口与实时可行性之间缺乏系统分析。

Method: 构建柔性分布式压电触觉传感系统，在6类日常物体上对比三类识别方法：SVM特征分类、1D-CNN/C-RNN原始信号分类、2D-CNN时空热图分类；系统考察传感单元数量（16/24/32）与时间窗长度的影响；最优配置部署到嵌入式边缘平台。

Result: 传感单元越多性能越好；2D-CNN时空热图方法最优，16/24/32传感器下F1分别为0.9796/0.9846/0.9903；32传感器+100ms窗口（50采样）配置在边缘平台上端到端延迟约101ms（自接触起算），每次推理能耗63.8µJ。

Conclusion: 分布式压电触觉传感可实现准确、低功耗、实时的抓取早期物体识别，具备机器人操作部署潜力。

Abstract: Tactile sensing provides information about object properties that can improve robotic grasping and manipulation. However, accurate object recognition during the early stages of grasping remains challenging, particularly under strict real-time constraints. This study presents a flexible, distributed piezoelectric tactile sensing system for early-stage object recognition. The system was experimentally evaluated using six everyday objects and three recognition approaches: feature-based classification using support vector machines (SVMs), raw-signal classification using 1D convolutional neural networks (1D-CNNs) and convolutional recurrent neural networks (C-RNNs), and spatiotemporal heatmap classification using a 2D-CNN. The effects of sensor count and temporal window size on recognition accuracy and real-time feasibility were systematically investigated. Increasing the number of sensing elements improved recognition performance across all approaches. The best-performing method, based on spatiotemporal heatmaps and a 2D-CNN, achieved F1-scores of 0.9796, 0.9846, and 0.9903 using 16, 24, and 32 sensors, respectively. The optimal configuration, comprising 32 sensors and a 100-ms temporal window (50 samples), was deployed on an embedded edge platform, achieving an end-to-end recognition latency of approximately 101 ms from contact onset and an energy consumption of 63.8 microjoules per inference. These results demonstrate the potential of distributed piezoelectric tactile sensing for accurate, energy-efficient, and real-time object recognition during robotic grasping.

</details>


### [362] [A Sequential Framework for Urban Drone Delivery: Network Design, Vehicle Sizing, and Energy and Cost Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07808)
*Hong-Su Nam,Seokhwan Lee,Jungwoo Cho,Hak-Tae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向城市无人机配送的序列化工程框架，将枢纽选址、路径规划、任务匹配的飞行器选型与能耗及运营成本评估串联起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市无人机配送中，枢纽数量、航线里程、飞行器航程与电池容量、能耗与运营成本相互耦合，但现有研究通常分别处理，缺乏从网络设计到飞行器选型与成本评估的端到端衔接。

Method: 以已知的OD聚合需求为输入：对每种枢纽配置先最小化所需最大链路距离并生成返回出发枢纽的可行航线；用混合整数线性规划确定航线频次以满足配送需求；由所得航程需求驱动电机-螺旋桨选型与电池容量优化，以最小化起飞质量；再结合飞行距离与频次、巡航性能、机体尺寸估算月度能耗、机队规模、枢纽面积、人员与运营成本。案例为韩国首尔区级配送数据，并做需求与运营假设的敏感性分析。

Result: 枢纽数从1增至4时，所需航程由30.6 km降至21.9 km，起飞质量由8.12 kg降至6.12 kg；尽管总飞行距离增加21.5%，月度飞行能耗下降13.1%（15.2→13.2 MWh）。电池更换是最大的飞行器相关运营支出；但因枢纽租金与人力成本上升，单次配送成本由USD 1.18升至USD 2.20。两枢纽与四枢纽网络中航程匹配型飞行器成本最低，三枢纽网络中长航程飞行器最经济。

Conclusion: 飞行器质量与能耗的下降并不必然带来系统运营成本的下降；枢纽数量带来的固定成本（租金、人力）会主导单次配送成本，因此网络级设计需将飞行器选型与枢纽运营成本联合权衡。

Abstract: A sequential engineering framework is presented for urban drone delivery, linking hub location, route planning, mission-specific vehicle sizing, and energy and operating cost assessment. Aggregate origin-destination demand is assumed known a priori. For each hub configuration, the maximum required link distance is minimized, and feasible routes that return to their departure hubs are generated. Route frequencies are determined using mixed-integer linear programming to satisfy delivery demand. The resulting range requirement guides motor-propeller selection and battery sizing to minimize vehicle takeoff mass. Flight distances and frequencies, cruise performance, and vehicle dimensions are combined to estimate monthly energy consumption, fleet size, hub area, staffing, and operating cost. The framework is applied to district-level Seoul delivery data. Increasing the number of hubs from one to four reduces the required range from 30.6 to 21.9 km and vehicle takeoff mass from 8.12 to 6.12 kg. Despite a 21.5% increase in total flight distance, monthly flight energy consumption decreases by 13.1%, from 15.2 to 13.2 MWh. Battery replacement is the largest vehicle-related operating expense, while operating cost per delivery increases from USD 1.18 to USD 2.20 because of higher rent and labor costs at the hubs. Among the designs evaluated, range-matched vehicles achieve the lowest operating cost in the two- and four-hub networks; a longer-range vehicle is least costly in the three-hub network. Sensitivity analyses further examine the effects of delivery demand and operating assumptions. The results demonstrate that reductions in vehicle mass and energy consumption do not necessarily lead to lower system operating cost.

</details>


### [363] [Learning Robotic World Models with Semantic and Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07850)
*Hao Wu,Yuqi Li,Yizhou Zhao,Kun Wang,Xiao Luo,Yu Cheng,Bo An,Yingli Tian,Laszlo A. Jeni,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboImagine: 通过"候选未来对比"对自回归机器人世界模型做后训练，结合冻结多模态评估器的语义偏好与像素/感知级视觉一致性反馈，用组相对优势做策略优化，提升视觉保真度与长时程预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人世界模型存在三重局限：纯视觉重建不能反映任务进展；仅语义评估对细粒度视觉误差约束不足；同质化预测缺乏可比较反馈。因此需要同时融合语义与视觉的对比式训练信号。

Method: 1) 多样性搜索：在共享视觉观测、动作序列与任务指令下生成多样候选轨迹；2) 冻结多模态评估器以双向顺序做成对视频比较，给出任务相关语义偏好；3) 像素级与感知级一致性反馈约束局部结构与外观误差；4) 两类信号转化为组相对优势驱动策略优化；5) 固定初始上下文＋分支专属滚动历史，实现有界上下文的长序列生成，并保持候选生成与策略优化间的条件一致。

Result: 在 RT-1、BridgeV2、RoboCasa（真实与仿真操作）上以独立训练模型实验。RT-1 上在相同初始化、更新步数与评测协议下，PSNR 提升 1.26 dB、LPIPS 降低 11.93%（相对继续监督微调）。消融显示多样性搜索提升预测质量；语义+视觉联合反馈的外部多模态评估得分高于仅视觉反馈且视觉保真度相当；滚动历史改善长时程预测。

Conclusion: 候选未来对比＋互补反馈的后训练范式可同时提升视觉保真度、任务相关性与长时程预测，为自回归机器人世界模型提供可扩展的优化路径。

Abstract: Robot world models predict action-conditioned future visual states to support robotic planning and decision making. However, visual reconstruction does not fully capture task progress, semantic evaluation alone provides limited constraints on fine-grained visual errors, and homogeneous predictions limit comparative feedback. We present RoboImagine, a framework for post-training autoregressive robotic world models through candidate-future comparisons with semantic and visual feedback. Diverse search promotes diversity among candidate trajectories under shared visual observations, action sequences, and task instructions. A frozen multimodal evaluator provides task-relevant semantic preferences through pairwise video comparisons in both presentation orders, complemented by pixel- and perceptual-level visual consistency feedback that constrains local structural and appearance errors. These signals drive policy optimization through group-relative advantages. Fixed initial context and branch-specific rolling histories support long-sequence generation with bounded context and consistent conditioning between candidate generation and policy optimization. Experiments with separately trained models on RT-1, BridgeV2, and RoboCasa cover real-world and simulated manipulation. On RT-1, under matched initialization, update counts, and evaluation protocols, RoboImagine improves PSNR by 1.26 dB and reduces LPIPS by 11.93% relative to continued supervised fine-tuning. Ablations show that diverse search improves prediction quality and that combined semantic and visual feedback yields higher external multimodal evaluation scores than visual feedback alone while maintaining comparable visual fidelity. Rolling histories further improve long-horizon prediction. These results support improving visual fidelity, task relevance, and long-horizon prediction through candidate comparison and complementary feedback.

</details>


### [364] [TactileLab: Sim-to-Real Dexterous Robot Manipulation with Efficient Shear-Sensitive Tactile Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07881)
*Yijiong Lin,Wenhao He,Toby Jack,Max Yang,Haoran Li,Nathan F. Lepora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TactileLab：基于IsaacLab的可扩展多模态触觉仿真框架，用刚体仿真估计接触面稠密切向运动（simulated tactile flow）替代软体物理模拟剪切形变，并配套新策略架构与real-to-sim触觉翻译管线，实现双臂与灵巧手内操作的sim-to-real部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作需要能表征剪切信息的触觉仿真，但高保真柔性/软体仿真计算代价高，无法扩展到大规模强化学习；同时真实触觉图像流与仿真触觉表征之间存在模态差距，阻碍sim-to-real迁移。

Method: 1）在IsaacLab上构建多模态触觉仿真框架，用刚体仿真高效估计接触界面稠密切向运动，生成编码剪切动力学的触觉流场，并与接触深度场的几何特征互补；2）提出联合处理长时序本体感觉信号与丰富触觉观测的策略网络架构；3）提出real-to-sim触觉翻译方法，将真实触觉图像流映射为仿真触觉流与深度场，并设计新的触觉数据采集方法，在多种触觉传感器设计上验证。

Result: 在多个接触丰富任务中，使用仿真触觉流训练的策略可大规模高效学习，并涌现接触感知行为；real-to-sim翻译管线在多种触觉传感器设计上得到验证，并在真实世界完成双臂与灵巧手内操作任务部署。

Conclusion: TactileLab提供了端到端的可扩展触觉策略学习sim-to-real框架，支持异构触觉机器人系统上的剪切敏感操作，为触觉仿真与真实部署之间搭建了高效桥梁。

Abstract: Robotic simulators that represent shear within tactile sensing are becoming increasingly important for contact-rich manipulation. However, simulating contact shear with high-fidelity deformable or soft-body simulation is computationally demanding, limiting scalability to large-scale reinforcement learning. Here we introduce \textit{TactileLab}, a scalable multimodal tactile simulation framework built on IsaacLab, with a particular focus on a new optical-flow-based tactile representation termed \textit{simulated tactile flow}. Instead of relying on soft-body physics to model shear deformation, TactileLab uses rigid-body simulation to efficiently estimate dense tangential motion at the contact interface, producing tactile flow fields that encode shear-relevant contact dynamics. This flow field complements geometric features from a contact depth field. We further introduce a novel policy architecture for jointly processing long-horizon proprioceptive signals and rich tactile observations. Across multiple contact-rich tasks, policies trained using simulated tactile flow learn efficiently at scale and exhibit emergent contact-aware behaviours. To facilitate sim-to-real policy deployment, we then propose a novel real-to-sim tactile translation method that maps real tactile image streams to simulated tactile flow and depth fields, together with a new tactile data collection method, and validate it across diverse tactile sensor designs. Using this pipeline, we demonstrate real-world deployment on tasks encompassing dual-arm and dexterous in-hand manipulation. Overall, TactileLab provides an end-to-end sim-to-real framework for scalable tactile policy learning, enabling diverse shear-sensitive manipulation across heterogeneous tactile robotic systems.

</details>


### [365] [AffordJEV: Affordance Grounding by Confirming Candidate Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07896)
*Jincheng Li,Yutian Zhang,Jianyu Zhang,Xinshun Wang,Mengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出System One风格的affordance grounding框架：冻结分割器逐级（物体→部件→子部件）提出候选区域，单向前向的判定模型一次性评估整层候选，无需文本链式推理，层间双向传递证据相互校正，仅用InstructPart训练即在域内及UMD/IIT-AFF零样本迁移上优于CoT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有affordance grounding方法沿用System Two范式：多模态大模型先写思维链，再把结论转成框/点交给分割器。这种方案推理开销大、语义决策对最终区域范围缺乏直接控制、错误难以纠正；而定位affordance本质上是感知判断，无须用语言论证。

Method: (1) 推理框架：冻结分割器产生候选区域，按物体—部件—子部件逐层确认，层间双向传递证据以相互校正；(2) 专门训练的System One判定模型：无文本输出，单次前向即读出一个决策，一次并行判定某层全部候选；训练数据直接来自框架自身产生的请求（因现有System One模型面向通用决策、一次只判一个候选，不适配该框架）。

Result: 仅在InstructPart上训练、并用与定义对齐的operation词评测，在域内及零样本迁移至UMD、IIT-AFF上均优于链式思维方法；在同框架下整体优于现成System One模型，且所需判定请求更少。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，该工作是首个将System One模型引入affordance grounding的方法，验证了"感知式单次前向判定+分层候选确认"可替代高开销CoT，具备较低推理成本与可校正性。

Abstract: Affordance grounding asks which visible part of an object performs a requested operation, a basic capability for embodied manipulation and human--robot interaction. Most existing methods follow a System Two paradigm: a multimodal language model first writes a chain of thought and then turns its conclusion into a box or points for a segmenter. This incurs considerable inference cost, gives the semantic decision little direct control over the extent of the final region, and makes mistakes hard to correct. Yet locating an affordance is essentially a perceptual judgment that need not be argued out in words. We therefore propose, in which frozen segmenters propose candidate regions and a System One model decides directly which of them perform the operation, generating no text and reading each decision from a single forward pass. Our contributions are twofold. (1) An inference framework that confirms candidate regions level by level, from object to part to sub-part, with evidence passed between the levels in both directions so that the levels correct one another. (2) A System One model trained for this framework: existing System One models are built for general-purpose decisions, judge one candidate per request and are ill-suited to the framework's region questions, so ours learns directly from the requests the framework itself produces and judges all candidates of a level at once. Trained only on InstructPart and evaluated with definition-aligned operation words, outperforms chain-of-thought methods in domain and in zero-shot transfer to UMD and IIT-AFF, is better overall than existing System One models run in the same framework, and needs fewer judge requests. To our knowledge, is the first approach to bring System One models to affordance grounding.

</details>


### [366] [Time-Indexed Skill Success Models for Latency-Aware Skill Selection and Timing in Dynamic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07964)
*Mohsen Omidi,Maria Chiara Leva,Brian Vaughan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"时间索引技能成功模型"（time-indexed skill success model），预测技能在观测后延迟Δt启动时的整体任务成功率；用滚动时域规划器联合选择技能与启动时刻，在MuJoCo与真实UR5e延迟场景中优于静态能力模型，并揭示了延迟期间暂停世界的校准失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在操作运动物体时，所选技能启动时的观测已过时，最优技能可在数百毫秒内改变；而现有"技能能力模型"只在观测瞬间预测启动成功率，与实际执行时的延迟不匹配，导致规划器在真实延迟（固定或随机）下选错技能或选错启动时刻。

Method: 定义时间索引技能成功模型 s(skill, state, Δt)，预测在延迟Δt后启动技能时整段任务的成功概率；在网格化的启动延迟处分支rollout采集训练数据；将此模型嵌入receding-horizon规划器，同时优化技能选择与启动时间，适配固定/随机延迟；在MuJoCo中UR5e用三种技能捕获滑动冰球进行评测，并与静态能力模型（点预测/采样预测两种方式评估）对比；在真实UR5e注入0.2 s延迟捕获滚动球的单技能场景中验证校准性。

Result: MuJoCo中，100–300 ms延迟下规划成功率比静态能力模型高2.8–6.2个百分点；5次完整运行（3个训练种子+2个分布偏移）中80个配对95%区间均排除零。延迟启动下时间索引模型校准良好（ECE 0.009 vs 0.239）；延迟期间暂停世界会高估成功率最多23个百分点。真实UR5e上，硬件匹配仿真训练的静态模型对35个被其判定成功率≥0.8的延迟启动预测99%，实测仅63%（95%区间46–77%），时间索引模型预测56%；该单技能可预测场景中，匹配物理预测亦校准良好。

Conclusion: 将延迟显式索引入成功模型可显著提升延迟条件下技能与启动时刻的联合规划性能与校准性；静态能力模型的时间外推在sim-to-real下严重失准，而时间索引模型更贴近实测成功率。局限性在于仅在单技能、可预测的低维场景验证，尚未覆盖多技能真实硬件与高随机性动力学。

Abstract: Robots that manipulate moving objects act on observations that are outdated when the chosen skill starts, and the best skill can change within a few hundred milliseconds. Skill competence models, however, predict success for a launch at the moment of observation. We propose time-indexed skill success models, which predict whole-task success when a skill is launched after a given delay. Trained on rollouts branched at a grid of launch delays, they let a receding-horizon planner select both the skill and its launch time under fixed or random latency. In a MuJoCo study in which a UR5e captures a sliding puck with three skills, the planner exceeded a static competence model by 2.8 to 6.2 percentage points at 100 to 300 ms of latency, including when that model was evaluated on point or sampled forecasts of the launch state. Across five complete runs (three training seeds and two distribution shifts), all 80 paired 95% intervals against four baselines excluded zero. On delayed launches, the time-indexed model was calibrated (expected calibration error 0.009 against 0.239), and pausing the world during latency overstated success by up to 23 points. On a real UR5e that captures a rolling ball after 0.2 s of injected latency, a static model trained in a hardware-matched simulator predicted 99% success on a prespecified subset of 35 delayed launches that it rated at least 0.8. Of these, 63% succeeded (95% interval 46 to 77%), and the time-indexed model predicted 56%. In this single-skill, predictable regime, a matched physics-based forecast was also well calibrated.

</details>


### [367] [A Unified Fractal Impedance Control Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08009)
*Adnan Takriti,Mahmoud Sadeq,Quentin Rouxel,Daniele Cafolla,Michael Mistry,Carlo Tiseo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Fractal Impedance Controller (FIC) 最新架构 Harmonic-Passive Motion Paradigm (H-PMP) 的机器人实现与实验验证，并提供基于 ROS2/MuJoCo 的开源部署框架，在 Kinova Gen3 上通过轨迹跟踪、突然失去接触的压墙、示教学习三项实验对比旧版 FIC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机交互要求控制器与环境的交互本质上有界（intrinsically bounded）。FIC 已被证明能在设计层面保证安全，但其最新架构 H-PMP 缺少实际机器人实现与实验验证，也缺少统一的、可复用的部署工具链。

Method: 将 H-PMP 视为 Passive Motion Paradigm 的演化版本以实现类人交互；给出其在真实机械臂上的实现，并构建整合 ROS2、MuJoCo 仿真与物理硬件的统一 FIC 部署框架（开源软件套件）。在 Kinova Gen3 上设计三项实验：轨迹跟踪、压墙并突然失去接触、学习示教，与早期 FIC 架构进行定量对比。

Result: H-PMP 在导纳与阻抗行为之间实现无缝权衡，能够以零额外计算开销在交互模式间切换，适合非结构化环境部署；在失去接触等扰动场景下表现优于早期 FIC 架构。

Conclusion: H-PMP 是一种兼具安全边界保证与类人交互特性的阻抗控制架构，配合开源软硬件栈可直接落地于真实机械臂，适用于非结构化环境下的人机交互任务。

Abstract: Physical human--robot interaction requires controllers capable of maintaining intrinsically bounded interactions with the environment. The Fractal Impedance Controller (FIC) has been proven to guarantee these safety characteristics by design. This paper introduces the robotic implementation and experimental validation of its most advanced architecture: the Harmonic-Passive Motion Paradigm (H-PMP), an evolution of the Passive Motion Paradigm designed to mimic human-like interaction. Furthermore, we present a unified framework for FIC deployment, supported by an open-source software suite featuring ROS2, MuJoCo, and physical hardware integrations. Through three experiments conducted on a Kinova Gen3 arm (trajectory tracking, a wall press with sudden loss of contact, and learning-by-demonstration) we compare the H-PMP against earlier FIC architectures. The results demonstrate that the H-PMP achieves a seamless trade-off between admittance and impedance behaviours. This makes it ideal for deployment in unstructured environments, enabling the robot to transition between interaction modes with no computational overhead.

</details>


### [368] [Symmetry-Aware Surrogate-Assisted Testing of UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08012)
*Anas Shrinah,Chris Bennett,Kerstin Eder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出对称性感知的代理模型辅助测试方法：将不同飞行任务的障碍配置变换到统一规范坐标系，使单任务训练的神经网络代理可在多任务复用，预测最小净空距离以引导生成安全关键测试用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV飞控高保真仿真测试代价高，基于学习的代理模型通常只在训练任务分布内有效；希望利用任务间的几何对称性降低仿真数据需求并跨任务迁移代理模型。

Method: 基于几何对称性的规范化坐标变换（将障碍配置对齐到共享标准系）＋神经网络代理模型预测UAV与障碍的最小净空距离＋代理引导的测试生成，在Aerialist UAV测试平台上与2024–2026 UAV Testing Competitions方法对比，并开源模型与2500+高保真仿真数据集。

Result: 平均关键测试用例命中率约42%，约束随机基线约10%；输入多样性高，基准表现具竞争力；但飞行路径特征差异较大的任务间代理迁移效果下降。

Conclusion: 几何规范化能有效复用单任务代理并提升关键场景发现效率，但受任务路径特性限制，揭示了规范化方法的适用边界；释放模型与数据集以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: We investigate a symmetry-aware surrogate-assisted testing approach for UAV autopilots. The proposed approach exploits geometric symmetries between flight missions by trans- forming obstacle configurations into a shared canonical coordi- nate frame. This enables a neural-network surrogate trained on a single reference mission to be reused across arbitrary missions, reducing the amount of high-fidelity simulation data required for training. The surrogate model is trained to predict the minimum clearance distance between a UAV and surrounding obstacles and is used to guide test generation towards safety-critical scenarios. We evaluate this approach using the Aerialist UAV test bench and benchmark its performance against techniques from the 2024 to 2026 UAV Testing Competitions. Results show that the approach achieves an average critical-test-case hit rate of 42% across the evaluated missions, compared with approximately 10% for the constrained-random baseline used to generate the training data. This approach also achieves high input diversity and competitive benchmark performance. However, our findings reveal limitations in transferring surrogate predictions across missions with differing flight-path characteristics, highlighting the boundaries of geometric canonicalization. To support reproducibility and future research, we provide the trained surrogate model and a dataset comprising more than 2,500 high-fidelity simulations.

</details>


### [369] [Development of Anthropomorphic Arm for collaborative and Home Service Robot CHARMIE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08013)
*Hafiz Adil,Tiago Ribeiro,Antonio Ribeiro,Carolina Lopes,Joana Coelho,Gil Lopes,Fernando Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A dissertation presents an open-source 3D-printed arm whose hand is controlled two ways: camera-based teleoperation with ML hand-pose estimation, and DH-parameter kinematic control for end-effector positioning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rapid growth of service robots and prosthetics motivates low-cost, open, reproducible arm/hand platforms that combine intuitive teleoperation with formal kinematic modeling.

Method: 3D-printed open-source arm; dual control front-ends: (1) teleoperation via camera + ML-based hand pose estimation mapping human hand pose to robot hand; (2) DH-method forward kinematics to define motion and compute end-effector position in 3D space.

Result: An assembled and programmed prototype achieving hand control through hand-pose teleoperation and kinematic end-effector localization; details of accuracy, latency, or task success are not reported in the abstract.

Conclusion: The work offers an accessible open-source hardware/software baseline for low-cost arm-hand systems, but provides limited methodological novelty and no quantitative real-robot evaluation.

Abstract: Service Robots are becoming a reality and are progressing by leaps and bounds. Alongside this avenue of robotics is also the field of prosthetics, which is evolving rapidly. These topics are hot cakes in the industry and are widely developed. Some have one quality, and some have others, but are making a shoreline towards success. The development of arm and hand as individual or as a package is becoming common as more and more advanced collaborative robots come to the surface and join their human counterparts in the real world. This dissertation presents an open-source 3D-printed arm which was assembled and programmed on two fronts. The first is the control of the hand by utilising teleoperation with a camera and ML-based hand pose estimation. Secondly, it is the control via kinematics utilising the Denavit-Hartenberg method to define the motion and to find the position of the end effector in 3D space.

</details>


### [370] [HITCH: Teaching a Vision-Language Model to Operate a Humanoid through a Whole-Body Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08039)
*Qingzhou Lu,Chenxin Liu,Xinming Wang,Liu Cao,Yanjiang Guo,Mengdi Xu,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HITCH 提出一套人形机器人全身控制接口：感知端把 VLM 在图像中选中的任意点返回为 3D 坐标，执行端将骨盆、躯干、手、行走、注视命令经学习式 tracker 转为全身运动；再用教师策略经 HITCH 完成五个基础任务的轨迹微调 VLM，实现跨任务的身体使用知识迁移与真机零实数据部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让 VLM 控制人形机器人需要两个条件：可靠的全身接口，以及可跨任务迁移的"如何使用身体"的知识。现有工作往往只解决其一——要么缺乏点级 3D 接地与全身执行，要么无法把操作经验抽象为可复用的身体技能。

Method: 1) HITCH harness：VLM 在图像中选点，感知模块返回该点的 3D 位置；动作命令抽象为骨盆、躯干、双手、行走、注视等高层指令，由学习式 tracker 解码为全身运动。2) 消融验证场景测量与命令执行精度对成功率的贡献。3) 用教师策略通过 HITCH 求解五个基础任务，收集轨迹微调 VLM，使其习得身体操作先验（可零样本迁移至新任务）。

Result: 无任务专用轨迹时，微调模型完成 SIMPLE 51%、HumanoidArena 42.5% 的回合；每任务仅加 5 条轨迹，SIMPLE 成功率升至 95%。在真实 G1 上无需真实训练数据即可完成搬箱并走向目标。

Conclusion: 将接口设计与跨任务身体知识学习解耦再结合，可显著提升 VLM 驱动机器人的样本效率与泛化性，真机零样本迁移初步可行。

Abstract: Enabling vision-language models (VLMs) to control humanoid robots requires both reliable whole-body interfaces and knowledge of how to use the body that carries over across tasks. We present HITCH (Harness Interfaces To Command Humanoids), a harness in which perception returns the 3D position of any point the VLM selects in its image, and execution turns commands for the pelvis, torso, hands, walking, and gaze into whole-body motion with a learned tracker. We find that both measuring the scene and executing commands accurately raise task success. We then fine-tune a VLM on trajectories in which teacher policies solve five base tasks through HITCH, so that it learns how to operate the humanoid. Without task-specific trajectories, the fine-tuned model completes 51% of SIMPLE episodes and 42.5% of HumanoidArena episodes, and adding five trajectories per SIMPLE task raises its SIMPLE success to 95%. On a real G1, it carries a box and walks to a goal without real training data.

</details>


### [371] [Terrain-Indexed Motion Priors and Geometric Support for Extreme Humanoid Parkour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08056)
*Chenghao Sun,Youhao Lyu,Zizheng Li,Shuting Zhang,Ziyang Dong,Wenhao Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三阶段渐进式模仿框架：特权模仿生成可执行机器人参考、地形索引对抗运动先验引导技能学习、几何支撑引导（低运动先验权重）实现手膝支撑接触模式适配，无人形机器人真机策略微调下完成30个障碍中29个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示可提供跑酷行为，但其接触模式不适合机器人比例与可用支撑面；需在保留技能的同时放宽演示约束，以适配机器人特定的支撑构型。

Method: 三阶段框架：1）特权模仿（privileged imitation）产生物理可执行机器人参考；2）地形索引的对抗运动先验引导技能获取；3）降低先验权重的几何支撑引导用于接触适配。采用候选点预接触吸引+实际接触评估，不预设接触序列。部署策略仅用机载深度、本体感知与速度指令，无需外部技能标签。

Result: Noetix E1 仿真混合任务成功率 96.34%；零真机策略微调迁移，30个单体障碍完成29个，5条混合赛道完成4条。消融显示完整适配阶段在测试地形上成功率更高，定性观察到接触落点变化。

Conclusion: 先由演示获取技能、再放松演示影响以适应机器人特定支撑配置的方案有效，接触模式可作为可适配而非硬约束的目标。

Abstract: We present a progressive imitation framework that learns terrain-appropriate humanoid parkour skills and subsequently adapts their hand--knee support. Human demonstrations provide traversal behaviors, but their contact patterns need not suit a robot's proportions or the available support surfaces. Our framework changes the role of demonstrations across three stages: privileged imitation produces physically executable robot references; terrain-indexed adversarial motion priors guide skill acquisition; and geometric support guidance with a reduced motion-prior weight enables contact adaptation. Candidate-based precontact attraction and actual-contact evaluation guide support without prescribing a contact sequence. The deployed policy selects behaviors from onboard depth, proprioception, and velocity commands without an external skill label. On the Noetix E1, the policy achieves 96.34\% hybrid success in simulation and transfers without real-world policy fine-tuning, completing 29 of 30 individual obstacle trials and four of five hybrid courses. Support-adaptation ablations show higher traversal success for the complete adaptation stage on the tested terrains, while qualitative observations show changes in contact placement. These results support acquiring skills from demonstrations before relaxing their influence to accommodate robot-specific support configurations.

</details>


### [372] [Leave It Better Than Found: Value-Guided Cooperation in Sequential Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08075)
*Mario Gomez Andreu,Michael Amir,Antonio Marino,Tianxu An,Marco Hutter,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人通过共享价值函数（value function）显式表达环境偏好，实现从未联合训练过的“临时顺序协作”，并用KNN不确定性过滤器防止对分布外状态过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人协作方法依赖联合训练或联合优化，假设训练/规划阶段可获取集体交互数据；随着异构本体与策略数量增长，组合爆炸使为每种机器人组合学习协作策略不可行。

Method: 将问题建模为共享工作空间中的临时顺序任务接续（ad hoc sequential continuation）。智能体以价值函数作为可传输的偏好表征进行通信，从而在无联合训练下推断前序机器人留下的环境偏好；引入基于k最近邻的不确定性过滤，用局部支撑度校准传输价值函数的可信度，抑制OOD状态下的过度自信。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界实验中，任务成功率相比朴素智能体甚至自私（selfish）智能体提升最高达48.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 价值函数不仅是内部学习工具，还可作为ad hoc多机器人协调的有效通信媒介；无需先验联合训练即可实现异构机器人间的顺序协作。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sequential task continuation, where one robot acts in a workspace that has been affected by a previous robot. Current approaches in multi-robot cooperation rely heavily on co-training or co-optimization, assumingthat the robots have access to collective interaction data during the policy training or planning process. However, as increasingly varied robotic embodiments and policies are deployed and operate in the same real-world environment, learning policies for cooperation becomes infeasible due to the combinatorial explosion of possible robot combinations that need to be accounted for. To address this challenge, this paper considers the problem of ad hoc sequential cooperation between robots that have never before operated together in a shared environment. We demonstrate that robots can explicitly communicate their environmental preferences through sharing their value functions, enabling coordination without the need for prior joint training. To ground this exchange and prevent overconfidence in out-of-distribution states, we further introduce a k-nearest neighbors (KNN)- based uncertainty filter. Our method yields an increase of up to 48.5 pp compared with naive or even selfish agents, in task success rates across simulated and real-world experiments, demonstrating that value function transmission between agents is useful not merely as an internal learning tool, but as an effective basis for ad hoc coordination. Website: this https URL

</details>


### [373] [STEP: Spatiotemporal Training with Explicit Prediction for Perceptive Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08097)
*Youhao Lyu,Chenghao Sun,Zhenxuan Ding,Shuting Zhang,Ziyang Dong,Wenhao Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STEP单阶段框架，在共享控制表征中用学习查询聚合深度与本体感觉token，并同时监督双向掩码跨模态预测与动作条件化的下一融合状态预测，实现地形感知的类人机器人运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地形感知融合工作只做特征聚合与同步观测间的跨模态预测，未明确"应学习哪些跨模态关系"，也未显式约束这些关系在动作下如何演化；而地形几何的相关性取决于机器人本体状态及二者在动作下的共同演化。

Method: 单阶段框架：多个可学习query将深度与本体感觉token聚合为紧凑策略状态；双向掩码预测用可见模态预测被遮挡模态的隐特征，显式监督地形-本体关系；动作条件预测器由当前策略状态与执行动作预测下一融合状态，目标以EMA直接定义在策略表征空间；两目标与策略学习联合优化。

Result: 仿真中相较多种融合替代方案与消融整体提升地形通过能力；深度丢失与延迟实验表明两项预测目标在视觉退化下均有收益；部署于23自由度Noetix E1（机载感知），在楼梯、40cm平台、70cm间隙、踏石及无后方地形可视的后退下楼梯任务中100次硬件试验成功98次。

Conclusion: 将显式跨模态预测与动作条件世界模型式预测并入共享控制表征，可在视觉退化下增强鲁棒性，并支撑高难度地形上的实机类人运动。

Abstract: For perceptive humanoid locomotion, the relevance of terrain geometry depends on the robot's body state and how both evolve under its actions. Existing work has advanced terrain-aware sensory fusion and cross-modal prediction, improving how visual and proprioceptive information is combined and related. However, feature aggregation alone does not specify which cross-modal relationships should be learned, while prediction between simultaneous observations does not explicitly constrain their action-dependent evolution. We present Spatiotemporal Training with Explicit Prediction (STEP), a one-stage framework that jointly supervises cross-modal relationships and action-dependent state evolution within a shared control representation. Multiple learned queries aggregate depth and proprioceptive tokens into a compact policy state. Bidirectional masked prediction uses each visible modality to predict latent features of the omitted modality, explicitly supervising terrain-body relationships. An action-conditioned predictor maps the current policy state and executed action to the next fused state, with an exponential-moving-average target defined directly in the policy representation space. Simulation comparisons show overall improvements in terrain traversal over evaluated fusion alternatives and ablations, while depth-dropout and delay experiments demonstrate the benefits of both predictive objectives under degraded visual input. Deployed on a 23-DoF Noetix E1 humanoid with onboard sensing, STEP succeeds in 98 of 100 hardware trials across stairs, a 40\,cm platform, a 70\,cm gap, stepping stones, and backward stair traversal without rearward terrain visibility.

</details>


### [374] [An Integrated Open-Source Stack for Vision-Based Autonomous Navigation of Wheeled Rovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08100)
*Iaroslav Kolomiets,Iaroslav Sheipak,Mikhail Reppo,Vsevolod Egorov,Viktor Chertopolokhov,Isaac Chairez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开源ROS 2系统集成YOLOv8n箭头检测+RGB-D SLAM+行为树，实现箭头引导行进、抵达终点锥并自主返回；Jetson Orin NX上感知时延25ms，held-out 478张mAP@0.5=0.720，完成1室内2室外实地任务但缺重复定量试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限感知与算力下的视觉移动机器人需要把感知、定位、规划与平台控制整合为一个可复用、可开放获取的自主系统；本文以标记引导往返任务为具体载体，并提供数据集、权重与日志以支持可复现研究。

Method: 标准ROS 2分层架构：YOLOv8n检测箭头并结合几何方法估计方向、距离与方位；RGB-D SLAM与轮式-惯性状态估计；运动学感知规划与比例路径跟踪插件；行为树管理任务阶段（跟随→到锥→返回）；STM32底层执行。标准接口将可复用自主层与硬件层解耦。

Result: 478张held-out图像上Ultralytics评测mAP@0.5=0.720，在统一协议下优于OpenCV基线；感知延迟25ms（Jetson Orin NX）；三个任务（1室内2室外）在无人工运动指令、无碰撞报告情况下完成标记引导导航与自主返回；现场记录集成故障与修正方案。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了低成本模块化视觉自主栈在真实平台上的可行性，开源工件完善；但物理导航误差与可靠性缺乏重复仪器化试验量化，作者自述仍需后续验证。

Abstract: Vision-based rover autonomy requires integrating perception, localization, planning and platform-specific control under limited sensing and computing resources. We present an open-source ROS 2 stack for following directional arrows, reaching a terminal cone and returning autonomously to the start. It connects YOLOv8n detection and geometric direction, range and bearing estimation with RGB-D SLAM, wheel-inertial state estimation, kinematics-aware planning, behavior-tree mission management and STM32 actuation. Standard interfaces separate reusable autonomy from the hardware layer; a proportional path-following plugin supports low-speed rover motion. Public resources include software, configuration, a labeled dataset, trained weights and evaluation logs. On 478 held-out images, the detector achieves mAP@0.5 of 0.720 under the Ultralytics evaluator and outperforms a classical OpenCV baseline under a shared evaluation protocol. The reported perception latency is 25 ms on a Jetson Orin NX. Three missions, one indoor and two outdoor, demonstrate marker-guided navigation and autonomous return without operator motion commands or reported collisions. Field results document integration failures and corrective choices; repeated instrumented trials remain necessary to quantify physical navigation errors and reliability.

</details>


### [375] [Reinforcing End-to-End Autonomous Driving Safety via Negative-Aware Planning in a Unified World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08111)
*Zihao Zhang,Xinyu Zhang,Bing Zeng,Xingyu Chen,Zhenning Li,Bin Yu,Ziyuan Pu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NegWAM: a safety post-training framework for world-action models that uses near-expert "negative" trajectories as repulsive anchors plus scene-conditioned rider tokens, discarded at inference; 40.4 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navhard (+4.1% rel.), 93.3 on navtest.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-action models rely on expert-trajectory imitation, so unsafe behaviors close to expert trajectories are weakly constrained; safety-critical robustness is limited.

Method: Construct negative trajectories geometrically near the expert but safety-risky; use them as repulsive anchors to push predictions away; encode as scene-conditioned rider tokens for compatibility learning; jointly optimize flow-matching and near-expert negative objectives; drop rider branch at inference (zero overhead).

Result: NAVSIM v2: 40.4 EPDMS on navhard (4.1% relative gain over strongest scorer-free WAM baseline), 93.3 EPDMS on navtest; zero-shot closed-loop on HUGSIM shows cross-benchmark generalization.

Conclusion: Explicit negative-sample post-training yields safety gains in critical scenarios without degrading routine driving, and generalizes across benchmarks at no inference cost.

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly model future traffic states and driving actions for end-to-end autonomous driving in intelligent transportation systems. However, their action learning remains dominated by expert-trajectory imitation, leaving unsafe alternatives close to expert trajectories insufficiently constrained.
