To improve WAM robustness in safety-critical scenarios, we propose NegWAM, a safety post-training framework for unified WAMs that learns explicit safety boundaries from near-expert failure trajectories. NegWAM constructs negative trajectories that are geometrically close to the expert trajectory yet carry safety risks, uses them as repulsive anchors to steer predictions away from dangerous behavior, and encodes them as scene-conditioned rider tokens to learn scene--trajectory compatibility. During training, NegWAM jointly optimizes the flow-matching and near-expert negative objectives, while the auxiliary rider branch is discarded at inference, incurring no additional overhead. On NAVSIM v2, NegWAM achieves 40.4 EPDMS on navhard, a 4.1% relative improvement over the strongest compared scorer-free WAM baseline, and achieves 93.3 EPDMS on navtest. These results demonstrate that NegWAM achieves strong performance in safety-critical scenarios while preserving routine-driving performance. Zero-shot closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM further demonstrates cross-benchmark generalization. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [376] [H-BRICK: Hierarchical Boundary Reachability Index with Compressed Kleene Closure for Directed Grid Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08119)
*Tolga Özaslan,Elif A. Özaslan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H-BRICK：分层边界可达性索引 + 压缩 Kleene 闭包，实现有向栅格图的精确单元间可达性查询，批处理下吞吐提升 5.0x–22.9x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态栅格图上的反复有向可达性查询（在线路径规划、任务-智能体分配、集群控制）中可达性非对称，而完整传递闭包存储代价过高，在线搜索又会遍历大量可达区域。

Method: 将图划分为 tile，在 tile 内计算局部布尔 Kleene 闭包并投影到边界端口；端口间有向摘要沿共享接口递归组合至根节点。以二元关系布尔 Kleene 代数给出精确性证明：每个组合摘要等于区域自反传递闭包的边界投影，单元对查询与原图一致。

Result: 在多种拓扑的有向栅格图上与有向搜索、基于连通分量的基线对比，并对比全传递闭包的存储；批量评估显示共享端点提升可摊薄层次遍历，k=64 时相对标量查询循环吞吐提升 5.0x–22.9x。

Conclusion: 分层边界压缩在查询密集、在线搜索易遍历大可达区域且层次边界接口保持紧凑的空间图中最具优势。

Abstract: Repeated directed reachability queries arise in online path planning, task-agent allocation, and swarm control on static grid maps, where reachability is asymmetric and full transitive closure is storage-prohibitive. We introduce H-BRICK, a Hierarchical Boundary Reachability Index with Compressed Kleene closure for exact cell-to-cell queries on directed grid graphs. H-BRICK partitions the graph into tiles, computes a local Boolean Kleene closure within each tile, and projects it onto the tile's boundary ports. The resulting directed port-to-port summaries are then composed recursively across shared interfaces up to a single root covering the graph. We prove algebraically that the construction is exact: in the Boolean Kleene algebra of binary relations, every composed summary equals the boundary projection of the regional reflexive transitive closure, and every cell-to-cell query agrees with reachability in the original graph. Experiments on directed grid graphs across diverse map topologies compare H-BRICK against directed search and component-based baselines, and compare its storage requirements against full transitive closure. A batched reachability evaluation further demonstrates that sharing endpoint lifts across many-source/many-target queries amortizes hierarchical traversal and, at k=64, yields a 5.0x-22.9x throughput improvement over the scalar query loop. The results show that hierarchical boundary compression is most advantageous in query-intensive spatial graphs where online search traverses large reachable regions and the hierarchy maintains compact boundary interfaces.

</details>


### [377] [H-TRIP: Exact Many-to-Many Distance Index for Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08139)
*Elif A. Özaslan,Tolga Özaslan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H-TRIP：基于min-plus Kleene代数的精确增量距离索引，用固定边界端口的空间层次结构，将地图新增自由空间分解为秩一边插入并仅沿祖先链传播递减的端口摘要，从而在增长的地图上快速完成多视角多对多测地距离矩阵查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿探索系统需反复计算机器人与候选视点间的测地距离并构造完整距离矩阵；逐源图搜索代价随地图规模增长，而局部刷新的距离缓存在新发现自由空间造成全局捷径时会保留过期代价。

Method: 将已知地图划分为带固定边界端口的空间层次；把每次"未知→自由"揭示分解为叶节点上的高效秩一min-plus边插入；仅沿祖先链传播递减的端口-端口摘要；通过共享的层次提升（hierarchical lift）一次性评估所有活跃查询点的稠密多对多最短距离矩阵；并给出顺序插入保持网格上精确最短路径距离的证明。

Result: 在完整建图上与参考搜索实现精确一致；几十个视点下的稠密多对多查询比串行 k×BFS 快最多两个数量级；16机器人探索中，边长256/512/1024的图上在线查询比协同调度的 k×BFS 快 2.21x–8.59x。

Conclusion: 提出一种精确、可增量维护的分层测地距离索引，兼容全局捷径更新，适用于多机器人前沿探索中的实时多对多距离查询，兼具理论精确性保证与显著加速。

Abstract: Frontier-based exploration systems repeatedly evaluate geodesic travel costs between robot poses and candidate viewpoints, and tour-based planners consume the full pairwise distance matrix built from them. Recomputing these matrices with one graph search per source scales with the explored map size, whereas distance caches that refresh only locally affected or newly created frontiers can retain stale costs between surviving pairs when newly discovered free space creates a global shortcut. We present H-TRIP (Hierarchical Tropical Rank-one Incremental Propagation), an exact distance index grounded in Kleene algebra over the min-plus semiring for monotonically growing occupancy grids. H-TRIP partitions the known map into a spatial hierarchy with fixed boundary ports. Each unknown-to-free revelation is decomposed into efficient rank-one min-plus edge insertions at the affected leaf, and only decreased port-to-port summaries are propagated along its ancestor chain. A shared hierarchical lift evaluates the dense many-to-many matrix of shortest distances for all active query points on the occupancy map. We prove that sequential insertions preserve exact shortest-path distances on the grid. Benchmark experiments confirm exact agreement with reference searches on fully built maps and show that dense many-to-many queries at a few dozen viewpoints are up to two orders of magnitude faster than sequential k x BFS. On exploration runs with sixteen robots, in-loop queries on the growing occupancy graph are 2.21x-8.59x faster than co-scheduled k x BFS on maps with side 256, 512, and 1024 cells.

</details>


### [378] [Educational robotics beyond ROS: a custom library for direct transfer of Webots controllers in the Turtlebot3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08142)
*Jaime P. Pérez-Moro,Alejandro Romero,Hector Quintian,Francisco Bellas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个模仿 Webots API 的自定义 Python 库，使同一控制器代码可在仿真与实体 TurtleBot3 Burger 上近乎无改动运行，用于无强信息学背景的硕士生入门教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 的安装、配置与分布式编程模型在 AI 系硕士课程中形成较高的入门门槛，学生缺少信息学基础，难以聚焦算法本身。

Method: 自研库镜像 Webots 的 Python API，封装电机、轮式编码器、IMU 与 LiDAR 接口；配套脚本实现向实体平台的部署与执行的自动化，从而保证仿真到实机的直接迁移路径。

Result: 在硕士级"自主载具导论"课程中使用；初步学生反馈显示该方法降低了 ROS 基础设施的感知复杂度，学生能更专注于算法逻辑与实机行为。

Conclusion: 以 API 抽象屏蔽 ROS 工程复杂度是有效的教学设计，但论文以定性教学反馈为主，缺少定量学习效果与实机性能对比，工程实用价值大于研究价值。

Abstract: Robot Operating System (ROS) is becoming a standard in robotics courses at higher education, but its installation, configuration and distributed programming model can introduce a significant entry barrier in the new landscape of AI-based master and degree studies, where many students do not have a strong informatics background. This paper presents an educational experience that enables students to program a TurtleBot3 Burger directly in Python, while preserving a direct transfer path from simulation to the physical robot. The approach is based on a custom library that mirrors the Webots API, allowing the same controller code to be executed in the simulator and on the real TurtleBot3 with minimal modification. The library provides access to the robot motors, wheel encoders, inertial sensors and LiDAR, and is complemented by scripts that automate deployment and execution on the physical platform. It was used in a master-level course on introductory autonomous vehicles, and initial student feedback suggests that the approach reduces the perceived complexity of ROS-related infrastructure and helps learners focus on algorithmic logic and real-world behaviour.

</details>


### [379] [Detecting Silent Faults at the Environment-Trainer Boundary in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08144)
*Zuo Gou,Chenhao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将机器人强化学习中"训练器—环境"边界形式化为线上协议，系统注入故障发现：学习曲线/多随机种子等结果级校验几乎无法检出故障，而基于运行本身的双侧步数账本（step ledger）和环境侧回合计数能检出并定位全部故障，且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人RL环境日益运行在训练器之外（另一进程、另一机器、另一语言），许多跨边界故障不抛异常、静默腐蚀训练数据，而现有实践缺乏对这类"静默故障"可检出性的系统研究。

Method: 把训练器与环境的交互边界抽象为wire protocol；在Pendulum、HalfCheetah、Hopper及7关节机械臂Pusher上向跨线数据注入最多23种故障（覆盖单值到全值）、向训练器日志注入2种故障；对比三类检测手段：学习曲线阈值、与10条无故障种子的比较、以及"运行本身"的校验（双侧step ledger逐步比对、环境侧episode record）；并对全部1060个最终策略做干净评估验证。

Result: 除"全部值均错误"情形外，曲线阈值最多检出1/10的运行，种子比较最多检出21%；干净评估显示，728个双重曲线校验通过的边界故障运行中有723个与无故障种子差异不超过3σ，但几乎全部收敛到不同权重、reward故障还记录了不同回报。Step ledger在单机（Pendulum/Hopper/Pusher）与跨机（Pendulum）下检出并定位所有跨线故障，无假阳性，Pendulum上训练时间中位数仅增加3%；环境侧episode record可捕获训练器日志中的故障。

Conclusion: 结果级校验对边界静默故障近乎失效，必须校验运行本身；建议在环境侧维护step ledger与episode计数，并列举了Gymnasium 1.3、自有代码及五个多智能体库中实际出现的此类故障。

Abstract: Robot reinforcement learning increasingly runs environments outside the trainer: in another process, on another machine, in another language. Many faults at this boundary raise no error. We make the boundary a wire protocol and ask what notices a fault in it. On Pendulum, HalfCheetah, Hopper and a 7-joint arm (Pusher), we injected up to 23 faults into what crosses the wire and 2 into the trainer's log, from one value to every value. Unless every value was wrong, a learning-curve threshold caught at most one run in ten, and a comparison with ten fault-free seeds at most 21%. A clean evaluation of all 1,060 final policies agreed with the curves: of the 728 boundary-fault runs both curve checks passed, 723 scored no more than three standard deviations below fault-free seeds, though nearly all ended on other weights and, for reward faults, logged other returns. Checks of the run itself, not of its outcome, saw every fault. A step ledger kept on both sides and compared step by step caught and located every fault that crossed, on Pendulum, Hopper and Pusher on one machine and on Pendulum across two, with no fault-free run and, on Pendulum, a median 3% more training time. An episode record kept at the environment caught the faults in the trainer's log. We list such faults found in practice, in Gymnasium 1.3 and our own code and, reported by others, in five multi-agent libraries, and recommend a step ledger and episode counts kept at the environment.

</details>


### [380] [La-JEPA: Compact Robot Policies with Latent World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08156)
*Mateo Clémente,George Z. Xue,Amin Abyaneh,Liheng Ma,Rui Heng Yang,Tongtong Cao,Zhanguang Zhang,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: La-JEPA:一种紧凑的世界-动作模型,策略Transformer从零训练在预训练V-JEPA 2.1编码器的特征空间上,训练时联合预测未来特征与动作、推理时仅输出动作,在无需具身预训练的策略中取得SOTA且参数量远小于竞品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有world-action模型与VLA模型都依赖为"生成"设计的大型Transformer骨干,把策略架构与推理成本绑定在生成范式上,而这些面向生成的设计对控制任务究竟造成多大影响并不清楚。

Method: 以预训练V-JEPA 2.1视觉编码器为特征空间,从零训练一个紧凑的策略Transformer(La-JEPA),沿用Fast-WAM的训练范式:训练时联合预测未来特征与动作,推理时跳过未来预测只输出动作。另做设计研究对比JEPA特征与视频VAE latent,并修正LIBERO-Plus官方评测器损坏的指令后重评基线。

Result: 在无具身预训练的策略中于RoboTwin 2.0(干净/随机化场景)与LIBERO-Plus(正确指令)取得SOTA,参数量远低于最强竞品;速度显著快于Fast-WAM;在无预训练策略骨干条件下优于LeapBot-WA与JEPA-WAM;四项真机任务平均成功率与pi0.5相当、优于无具身预训练基线。设计研究表明JEPA特征优于视频VAE latent(分布外更明显),训练时未来预测的选择即使推理时跳过也会显著影响策略。

Conclusion: 无需生成式预训练策略骨干,La-JEPA即比大得多的策略更强更快,提示机器人基础模型或应面向控制而非沿用生成架构;同时暴露并修正了LIBERO-Plus评测器的指令损坏问题。

Abstract: World-action models adapt pretrained video generators, and vision-language-action models build on pretrained vision-language backbones; both tie policy architecture and inference cost to large transformers designed for generation rather than control. It remains unclear how much these generation-oriented choices affect control. In this work, we introduce Latent Action JEPA (La-JEPA), a compact world-action model whose policy transformer is trained from scratch in the feature space of a pretrained V-JEPA 2.1 encoder. Following Fast-WAM, La-JEPA learns to predict future features jointly with actions during training and generates only actions at inference. Among policies without embodied pretraining, La-JEPA achieves state-of-the-art performance in clean and randomized RoboTwin 2.0 scenes and on LIBERO-Plus with correct instructions, while using far fewer parameters than the strongest competing policies. It runs substantially faster than Fast-WAM at comparable performance and outperforms the closest JEPA-based methods, LeapBot-WA and JEPA-WAM, on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0 without their pretrained policy backbones. On four real-robot tasks, its average success is comparable to pi0.5 and above non-embodied-pretrained baselines. Through a design study of the choices behind La-JEPA, we show that JEPA features outperform video VAE latents, most clearly out of distribution, and that training-time future prediction choices significantly impact the policy even when future prediction is skipped at inference. We correct the LIBERO-Plus instructions, as the officially released evaluator corrupts them for most variants, and re-evaluate several strong open baselines. Without a generative pretrained policy backbone, La-JEPA is better and faster than far larger policies, suggesting that robot foundation models may benefit from architectures designed for control rather than inherited from generation.

</details>


### [381] [RoboARCade: Benchmarking Agentic Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08209)
*Satvik Sharma,Hengyuan Hu,Mihika Kamat,Dorsa Sadigh,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboARCade：面向"智能体即机器人策略"（ARC）的长程操作基准，统一MuJoCo后端，含三种本体、增广初始条件，评测GPT-6 Astra（49.8%）与Claude Fable 5.1（43.6%），失败主要源于策略选择与执行精度，且智能体在24%–39%子任务上错误自报成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体的进步使其可直接充当机器人策略（agentic robot control, ARC），但缺乏能同时测试上下文适应、记忆与精细推理的长程操作评测。现有开源基准初始条件范围偏窄、难度不足、任务与接口耦合紧密，难以公平比较不同智能体与接口。

Method: 构建统一MuJoCo单一仿真后端上的长程操作基准，覆盖三种本体；在现有开源基准基础上扩大初始条件分布以提升难度。每项任务为自包含数据包，智能体通过可替换接口接入，任务、智能体、接口三者可独立扩展。采用消融式评测：对比不同视觉接口（含更丰富视觉输入）的性能与成本、在上下文中提供一次成功示范、以及让智能体自写并自我修订提示词。

Result: GPT-6 Astra 49.8%，Claude Fable 5.1 43.6%，仍有较大提升空间；更丰富的视觉接口在1.7–2.3倍成本下仅提升1.5–6.8分。失败多来自操作策略选择不当或执行不精确，而非场景理解错误；智能体在24%–39%的尝试子任务上错误声称成功。一次上下文示范使Astra在五任务子集上从53.5%升至73.0%；自写自改提示词达到72.0%。代码、视频与完整轨迹已公开。

Conclusion: RoboARCade为ARC提供可扩展、可复现的评测基础，揭示当前前沿智能体的瓶颈在策略选择与执行精度、而非感知，并量化了"虚假成功"风险；同时说明免人类输入的上下文示范与自生成提示词能显著提升性能，是低成本改进路径。

Abstract: Improvements in multi-modal capabilities have enabled agents to act directly as robot policies, a setting we refer to as agentic robot control (ARC). We introduce RoboARCade, a benchmark of long-horizon manipulation tasks across three embodiments designed to test in-context adaptation, memory, and reasoning for precise control. The tasks build on existing open-source benchmarks, which we make harder by widening the range of initial conditions. RoboARCade is easy to use and extend: all tasks run on a single simulation backend, MuJoCo, each task is a self-contained data pack, and agents connect through interchangeable interfaces, so new tasks, agents, and interfaces can be added independently. The benchmark separates leading agents: GPT-6 Astra achieves 49.8% compared with 43.6% for Claude Fable 5.1, leaving substantial room for improvement. Comparing two interfaces, we find that a richer visual interface adds only 1.5 to 6.8 points at 1.7 to 2.3 times the cost. Failures stem mostly from choosing a poor manipulation strategy or executing a good one imprecisely, rarely from misunderstanding the scene, and agents incorrectly claim success on 24% to 39% of attempted subtasks. Agents can also improve without human input: one successful demonstration in context raises Astra's score from 53.5% to 73.0% on a five-task subset, and a prompt the agent writes and revises itself reaches 72.0%. Code, videos and full traces are available at this http URL .

</details>


### [382] [Fault-Geometry-Aware Control Allocation for Emergency Quadrotor Landing under Single-Motor Loss of Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08259)
*Suhas S P,Samridhi Salwan,Arshan Ali Khan,Ujwal Pai Jakarbet,Savithramma R M,Ashwini B P*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在单电机部分失效(η=0.496)条件下,对比PINV、QP-lite与CEM调参的残差分配器,并按故障电机索引冻结"分配器路由表",在Crazyflie SITL中获得117/120与115/120安全首次触地,并量化了η50对机体参数不匹配的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分单电机失效会改变四旋翼剩余控制权限几何,使最优控制分配器随故障几何(失效电机索引)变化;现有分配器多采用单一固定方案,未考虑"按故障几何选择分配器"以及该选择对机体模型失配的敏感性。

Method: Crazyflie软件在环仿真;三类分配器:有界加权最小二乘(记为PINV)、有限候选残差分配器(QP-lite)、以及用交叉熵方法(CEM)离线调评分参数的残差分配器。以已知失效电机索引表征故障几何;匹配开发试验选出每台电机对应的分配器并冻结为路由映射;用Firth回归估计M2/PINV分支的η50并做自助法区间;单因子±10%摄动推力系数与质量,比较η50漂移及首次触地判据的违规类型。

Result: η=0.496时,CEM被选中用于M1/M3/M4,PINV用于M2(M3为单次试验微弱优势29/30 vs 28/30)。冻结路由后新鲜种子117/120安全首次触地,随机顺序集成监督器评测115/120。M2/PINV分支η50≈0.4956,自助法95%区间约[0.4955,0.4956]。±10%摄动使η50最多偏移0.05495(推力系数)与0.04974(质量);主要违规判据由多数工况下的垂向速度转为质量-10%与推力系数+10%时的角速率。固定η=0.496时,PINV在9/11机体状态下M2安全计数优于冻结CEM,2/11打平(均为0/30)。

Conclusion: 实验将"按电机条件选择分配器"的收益与对机体模型失配的敏感性区分开来:路由选择有效但边界(η50)对质量与推力系数摄动敏感,且PINV在M2上更稳健,支持采用保守/按故障几何路由的分配策略。

Abstract: Partial single-motor loss of effectiveness (LoE) changes the remaining control-authority geometry of a quadrotor and can change which control allocator performs best during emergency landing[cite: 11]. We compare three allocators in Crazyflie software-in-the-loop simulation: bounded weighted least squares (reported as PINV), a finite-candidate residual allocator (QP-lite), and the same residual allocator with scoring parameters tuned offline by the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM)[cite: 11]. For this study, fault geometry is represented by the known failed-motor index[cite: 11]. At $\eta=0.496$ remaining effectiveness, matched development trials select CEM for M1, M3, and M4 and PINV for M2; the M3 selection is a one-trial margin (29/30 versus 28/30)[cite: 11]. After freezing this routing map, the policy achieves 117/120 safe first-contact touchdowns on fresh seeds and 115/120 in a separate randomized-order integrated-supervisor evaluation[cite: 11]. For the critical M2/PINV branch, Firth regression gives a nominal $\eta_{50}\approx0.4956$ with a within-model 95% bootstrap interval of approximately [0.4955, 0.4956][cite: 11]. One-factor-at-a-time $\pm10\%$ plant perturbations shift $\eta_{50}$ by up to 0.05495 for thrust coefficient and 0.04974 for mass; the most frequent violated first-contact criterion also changes from vertical speed in many conditions to angular rate for mass -10% and thrust-coefficient +10%[cite: 11]. At fixed $\eta=0.496$, PINV has a higher M2 safe count than frozen CEM in 9/11 tested plant states and ties in 2/11; the two ties are both 0/30[cite: 11]. The experiments therefore distinguish motor-conditioned allocator selection from sensitivity to plant-model mismatch[cite: 11].

</details>


### [383] [Aligning Policy Encoders with a Shape Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08275)
*Madelon Bernardy,Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani,David M Knigge,Riccardo Valperga,Artem Moskalev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 shape-teacher alignment：训练时用预训练3D形状表征（Point-MAE，逐物体点云特征）对齐图像策略编码器的patch特征，将几何结构"蒸馏"进纯图像策略，部署时无需深度/3D输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D输入对视角、位置变化和遮挡更鲁棒，但部署时需额外传感器与处理开销；作者希望图像策略在训练中获得3D形状信息，同时保持部署时的纯图像接口与架构不变。

Method: 训练期对齐机制：对每个物体取Point-MAE提取的3D形状特征作为target，与对应图像patch的编码器特征做对齐损失；不改变策略的输入模态与网络结构，部署时可完全丢弃shape teacher和深度。

Result: 在LIBERO上，图像表征更贴合物体形状与部件结构；预训练策略在Object与Spatial套件上收敛分别加速4.7x和5.6x，成功率亦有提升。

Conclusion: 无需部署期3D感知即可把几何归纳偏置注入图像策略，是一种低成本、易插拔的训练期表征蒸馏方案；但在LIBERO仿真上验证，特征对齐依赖精确的物体-patch对应关系。

Abstract: 3D-aware representations can improve robotic manipulation by providing geometric information that is robust to changes in viewpoint, object position, and occlusion. However, using 3D inputs at deployment requires additional sensing and processing, making image-based policies more practical. We ask whether an image-based policy can acquire 3D shape information during training while retaining a purely image-based interface at deployment. We propose shape-teacher alignment, a training-time mechanism that uses a pretrained 3D shape representation as an alignment target for an image-based policy encoder. Specifically, we use Point-MAE features from individual objects as targets for corresponding image patches, transferring geometric structure to the policy without changing its deployment inputs or architecture. On LIBERO, shape-teacher alignment produces image representations that are more structured around object shape and corresponding parts, while substantially accelerating policy convergence and improving success rates. For pretrained policies, convergence is accelerated by 4.7x and 5.6x on the Object and Spatial suites, respectively, without requiring the shape teacher or depth input at deployment.

</details>


### [384] [Transformer-Based Trajectory Prescription for sEMG-Optimizing Robot-Mediated Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08295)
*Gabriel Parra,Kimia Khoshnami,Daniel S. Brown,Laura A. Hallock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于transformer（RoPE编码器-解码器）+流匹配的可个性化模型，将表面肌电激活映射为末端执行器康复机器人的接触力轨迹，在对未见任务/未见用户的预测误差<8%，支持会话级微调，并在实时康复任务中实现15%平均激活误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人康复系统多规定运动学轨迹，未显式针对被认为支撑恢复的神经运动激活（肌肉激活），且疗效不稳定；目标是让康复机器人能"优化激活"地生成处方轨迹。

Method: Transformer编码器-解码器（旋转位置编码RoPE）从sEMG构建轨迹相关与用户特异的潜表示，预测接触力轨迹；用flow matching增强预测，输出偏向训练分布的可行处方分布；16名健康被试、15个等长轨迹跟踪任务，评估跨任务/跨用户泛化与用户级微调可行性，并做在线康复任务初步验证。

Result: 未见任务与未见用户上平均轨迹预测误差<8%；确定性推理近乎即时，分布采样亚秒级；在线任务中参与者平均仅累积15%的目标激活误差。数据与代码已在SimTK的OpenRobotRehab项目开源。

Conclusion: 为数据驱动、以激活为目标的康复机器人轨迹处方提供了概念验证，并奠定未来临床人群验证的基础；模型为离线映射+在线初步测试，尚未在患者群体或大规模闭环优化中验证。

Abstract: Robot-mediated rehabilitation has the potential to deliver high-intensity, individualized therapy, but existing systems (which have not consistently improved outcomes) typically prescribe kinematic trajectories rather than explicitly targeting the neuromotor activations thought to support recovery. To address this gap, and ultimately enable activation-optimizing rehabilitation robot behavior, we present a personalizable transformer-based model mapping individuals' muscle activations (as measured via surface electromyography) to prescribed contact force trajectories when interacting with an end-effector rehabilitation robot. The model uses a transformer encoder-decoder leveraging rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) to generate a latent representation of trajectory-relevant and user-specific neuromotor factors, and augments trajectory prediction with flow matching to produce a distribution over plausible prescriptions biased toward the training distribution. Using data from 16 healthy participants completing 15 isometric trajectory tracking tasks, we evaluate generalization across held-out tasks and users, as well as the feasibility of user-specific fine-tuning within a therapy-session timescale. The model achieves mean trajectory prediction errors of <8% on held out tasks and users, with effectively instantaneous deterministic inference and sub-second sampling of trajectory distributions. An initial test using the model during live rehabilitation tasks demonstrates that, on average, participants reach the target neuromotor activations with just 15% error accumulated throughout the task. These results provide a proof of concept for data-driven, activation-targeted trajectory prescription in robot-mediated rehabilitation and establish a foundation for future validation in clinical populations. Data and code have been open-sourced as part of the OpenRobotRehab project on SimTK.

</details>


### [385] [GPWM: Modeling the World as 3D Particle Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08338)
*Robert Ren,Matthew Bronars,He Zhu,Yikai Tang,Jianing Zhang,Sergey Zakharov,Pavel Tokmakov,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPWM：在3D物理空间中以粒子/刚体轨迹生成代替视频外观预测的生成式粒子世界模型，统一覆盖刚体、可变形体、布料、颗粒材料及机器人交互，并提出在潜噪声空间规划的接触丰富操作方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型生成的是场景外观而非物理状态，难以直接支撑物理一致的长时程推演与操作规划。作者希望直接在3D物理空间（粒子/刚体运动）建模场景演化，用单一表示与单一架构覆盖多材料、被动物理与机器人交互等多种物理域。

Method: 提出GPWM：object-aware 多分辨率 transformer，将 flow matching 与 diffusion forcing 结合，自回归生成物理轨迹并支持超出训练horizon的长rollout。构建 ParticleWorlds 数据集（2550万帧，涵盖几何、材料、交互与机器人形态）。统一模型用一套权重建模异构物理域。规划层面学习关节驱动-物体运动的生成先验，在模型潜噪声空间而非动作空间搜索以实现目标导向操作。

Result: 在六个物理域上均优于最佳学习动力学baseline，预测误差平均降低31%；统一GPWM大幅优于按域baseline，并在两个域上超过专门的specialist模型。可泛化到未见几何、物理属性、物体数量与 rollout 长度。统一世界模型下，噪声空间规划在推与抓取任务上显著优于动作空间搜索，开环执行即可完成目标导向操作，无需参考轨迹或目标专用策略。

Conclusion: 以3D粒子轨迹生成为核心的世界模型可作为一个统一、可泛化、可支撑接触丰富操作规划的物理先验，是视频世界模型之外的一条可行路线。

Abstract: We introduce GPWM, a Generative Particle World Model that predicts how 3D scenes evolve by generating the motion of particles and rigid bodies over time. Unlike video world models that generate scene appearance, GPWM operates directly in 3D physical space. A single representation and architecture cover rigid, deformable, cloth, and granular materials, in both passive physics and robotic interaction. GPWM is an object-aware multi-resolution transformer that combines flow matching with diffusion forcing to generate coherent physical trajectories far beyond its training horizon. We train and evaluate GPWM on ParticleWorlds, a 25.5-million-frame corpus spanning diverse geometries, materials, interactions, and robot embodiments. GPWM outperforms the best prior learned-dynamics baseline in each of six physical domains, reducing prediction error by 31% on average. Breaking from the domain-specific setting of prior methods, a single Unified GPWM models these heterogeneous regimes with one set of weights, largely outperforming per-domain baselines and even surpassing our own specialist models in two domains. GPWM further generalizes to unseen geometries, physical properties, object counts, and rollout horizons. Beyond prediction, GPWM enables contact-rich planning by learning a generative prior over joint actuator-object motion. Instead of searching over unconstrained actions, planning searches the model's latent noise for interactions that achieve a desired object-space goal. With the same world model, noise-space planning substantially outperforms action-space search on pushing and picking, where complex contact sequences are hard to discover. Executed open-loop in simulation, the resulting plans achieve goal-directed manipulation without reference action trajectories or goal-specific policies.

</details>


### [386] [Radio-to-Pose Information for Gauge-Drift Correction in Cooperative Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08361)
*Aditya Iyer,Andy Ye,Akshay Narayan,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Radio-to-Pose Information (RPI)接口，将射频衰减地图中的功率残差及其位姿导数转换为高斯信息矩阵与向量，用于抑制多机器人协同定位中的规范漂移(gauge drift)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同定位中里程计与机器人间量测仅含相对几何，整体位姿会共同漂移（规范漂移）；现有仅用射频加权相对量测的方法无法约束这种共同位移。

Method: 利用注册在同一世界坐标系下的射频衰减地图：注意到接收功率对世界地图中的共享位移敏感，故把功率残差及对位姿的雅可比转换为高斯信息矩阵-向量对，作为接口嵌入位姿估计器，与里程计、互测数据联合优化；理论上刻画了可从地图与链路中检测到的共享运动，并在地图不确定时保持跨机器人、跨时间相关性以复现联合高斯后验。

Result: 1000次仿真高斯试验中RPI与联合估计结果一致；32组配准地图配对试验中，在50×50 m场地将公共位置RMSE由0.542 m降至0.140 m（对比仅用射频加权相对量测的基线）。

Conclusion: 射频地图可作为绝对锚定信息源，以信息矩阵形式等价地实现联合估计，有效抑制多机器人协同定位的整体漂移。

Abstract: Cooperative localization uses odometry and inter-robot measurements to estimate the motion of a robot team. These measurements describe only relative geometry, but the team's estimated position and orientation can drift together over time, i.e., gauge drift. In this work, we study correction of this gauge drift using a radio attenuation map registered in the same world coordinate frame used for robot poses. Specifically, we propose Radio-to-Pose Information (RPI), an interface that converts power residuals and their pose derivatives into a Gaussian information matrix and vector pair. RPI builds on the insight that received power is sensitive to shared displacement in the world map. The pose estimator uses the interface together with odometry and inter-robot measurements to update poses and minimize uncertainty. We characterize the shared motions detectable from the map and observed links. For an uncertain shared map, RPI keeps correlations across robots and time and reproduces joint Gaussian pose estimates and covariances. Across $1000$ simulated Gaussian trials, RPI matches the results of joint estimation. Across 32 paired trials with a registered map, RPI reduces common-position RMSE from $0.542$m to $0.140$m in a $50\times50$m arena compared with a baseline that uses radio only to weight relative measurements.

</details>


### [387] [SHORE: Stability-Hybrid On-Policy Reinforcement Learning for Online Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08364)
*Chang Shi,Ann Majewicz Fey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SHORE：将学习到的放置稳定性概率与on-policy回报估计结合，用于在线3D装箱的箱体/朝向/位置联合决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3D装箱中未来到达未知，堆叠坍塌会终止回合并损失剩余容积；现有方法依赖几何规则或硬性稳定性掩码，难以让风险影响箱体、朝向、位置的联合选择。

Method: SHORE = 3D U-Net学习放置稳定性概率（由实际执行结果+模拟备选动作提供监督，补充早期rollout中样本不足的动作结果）+ 离散on-policy actor-critic，稳定性预测同时进入动作价值与策略先验，并加入门控限制未训练/变化中模型的影响；部署时无需额外搜索或仿真。

Result: Isaac Gym中0.4M环境步达到73%空间利用率，无稳定性建模的同类学习器约需1M步；在扩充的60箱集合上，利用率0.734 vs GOPT基线0.588，但放置失败率更高（1.03% vs 0.63%）；消融显示模拟备选主要提供早期监督，执行结果可支撑后期性能；并在真实机器人上验证集成简便性。

Conclusion: 显式稳定性建模+on-policy学习可显著加速学习并提升实际装箱利用率，代价是略高风险；方法可直接嵌入真实装箱流水线。

Abstract: Online 3D bin packing with a processing buffer requires choosing which box to pack, its orientation, and its placement while keeping the stack stable under unknown future arrivals. A collapse ends the episode and forfeits the remaining volume. Existing methods use geometric rules or hard stability masks, limiting how risk can influence the joint choice of box, orientation, and placement. We propose Stability-Hybrid On-Policy Reinforcement Learning (SHORE), which combines a learned stability probability of a placement with an on-policy estimate of its return conditional on stability. A 3D U-Net learns stability from executed outcomes and simulated alternatives, revealing outcomes of actions underrepresented in early rollouts. A discrete on-policy actor--critic uses stability predictions in both its action values and policy prior, with gates that limit the effect of an untrained or changing model. In deployment, SHORE does not require additional search or simulation overhead. In Isaac Gym, SHORE reaches 73\% space utilization by 0.4M environment steps, while the same learner without stability modeling reaches that level at about 1M. On the expanded 60-box set, SHORE achieves 0.734 space utilization versus 0.588 for the GOPT state-of-the-art baseline, with a higher placement-failure frequency (1.03\% vs. 0.63\%), trading more risk for greater packed volume. The results show faster learning and higher utilization on the more demanding realistic box arrival set. Ablations suggest that simulated alternatives provide useful early supervision, while executed outcomes alone sustain later performance. We also demonstrate the method on a real robot to verify the simplicity of integrating the method to real bin packing pipelines.

</details>


### [388] [VisionSIM: Beyond Idealized Sensors - A Multi-Modal Simulator for Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08368)
*Sacha Jungerman,Kaustubh Sadekar,Avery Gump,Sriram Narayanan,Shantanu Gupta,Bhuyashi Deka,Srinivasa Narasimhan,Atul Ingle,Mohit Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VisionSIM:统一的物理驱动多模态传感器仿真平台，对LiDAR/iToF/事件/热成像等建模原始物理测量过程并复现真实失效模式，同时发布同步原始测量的室内数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人感知仿真器将传感器理想化，掩盖了事件相机、SPAD、iToF/dToF、热成像等新兴模态的关键失效模式，导致仿真到现实部署受限。

Method: 构建统一仿真平台，对各类传统与新兴模态建模原始物理测量链路，提供细粒度物理参数控制：LiDAR的pileup与glare、iToF的相位卷绕/饱和/运动伪影、事件相机的refractory drops、热相机的history-dependent emission；支持同场景同轨迹的跨模态对比。

Result: 可复现通用仿真器忽略的模态特异性失效模式，实现原理性的跨模态比较；发布平台及含同步原始测量与丰富真值标注的大规模室内数据集。

Conclusion: 将传感器真实性作为一等问题，为多模态机器人感知的仿真与评测提供物理基础，但真实机器人验证细节尚未在摘要中体现。

Abstract: Robotic perception increasingly relies on heterogeneous sensor suites combining cameras with emerging modalities (event, SPAD, iToF/dToF, and thermal). Yet existing simulators idealize these sensors, hiding critical modality-specific failure modes that limit real-world deployment. We present VisionSIM, a unified, physically grounded simulation platform that elevates sensor realism to a first-class concern by modeling the raw physical measurement process for diverse conventional and emerging modalities. We expose fine-grained control over physical sensor parameters, reproducing failure modes overlooked by general-purpose simulators: pileup and glare for LiDAR; phase wrapping, saturation, and motion artifacts for iToF; refractory drops for event cameras; and history-dependent emission for thermal cameras. This enables principled cross-modality comparison on identical scenes and trajectories. We release the platform and a large indoor dataset with synchronized raw measurements and rich ground-truth annotations.

</details>


### [389] [MOMASPLAT: Dynamic Novel-View Demonstration Augmentation for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08372)
*Sateesh Kumar,Arnav Balaji,Arpit Bahety,Skand Peri,Georgios Pavlakos,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MOMASPLAT：用3D高斯泼溅+前馈3D基础模型+运动学动作重定向，从新基座位姿合成含动态物体交互的完整移动操作演示，提升VLA对视角/基座位姿变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习的视觉运动策略对相机视角高度敏感；移动操作中因导航不确定性、定位漂移与环境约束，机器人难以复现演示时的基座位姿。现有新视角合成增广假设场景静态，无法重渲染接触后的被操作物体，导致演示中最关键的交互阶段无法增广。

Method: 观察到标准MoMa数据采集天然提供多视角观测；据此分别用3D Gaussian Splatting重建静态场景、用前馈3D基础模型重建被操作物体（刚体与铰接体），在新视角下渲染二者，并通过基于运动学的动作重定向生成几何一致的合成演示及对应变换动作。

Result: 在3个场景、5项真实移动操作任务（含刚体与铰接物体）上评估；仿真中验证合成演示的保真度；对未见基座位姿的鲁棒性大幅提升，平均成功率超过最强基线两倍，在大基座位移下所有基线失败时仍保持非平凡成功率，并减少大规模真实数据采集需求。

Conclusion: 将静态场景与动态被操作物体分离重建，可突破NVS增广的静态假设，为移动操作策略提供含动态交互的新视角演示增广，同时降低真实数据依赖。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies trained through imitation learning are highly sensitive to changes in camera viewpoint. This is particularly problematic for mobile manipulation (MoMa), where robots rarely execute manipulation from the exact same base pose used during demonstration collection due to navigation uncertainty, localization drift, and environmental constraints. Recent approaches use novel-view synthesis (NVS) to augment demonstrations with unseen viewpoints, but they assume static scenes and therefore fail to re-render the manipulated object during the post-contact interaction phase, leaving the most critical parts of the demonstration unaugmented. We present MOMASPLAT, a novel-view demonstration augmentation framework for mobile manipulation that synthesizes full manipulation trajectories from unseen robot base poses, including dynamic object interactions. Our key insight is that standard MoMa data collection naturally provides the multiview observations needed to reconstruct both the static scene and the manipulated object in 3D. MOMASPLAT combines 3D Gaussian Splatting, feed-forward 3D foundation models, and kinematics-based action retargeting to separately reconstruct the scene and manipulated object, render them from novel viewpoints, and generate geometrically consistent synthetic demonstrations paired with transformed actions. We evaluate MOMASPLAT on five real-world mobile manipulation tasks involving both rigid and articulated objects across three scenes, and validate the fidelity of its synthesized demonstrations against ground truth in simulation. MOMASPLAT substantially improves robustness to unseen base poses, more than doubling the average success rate of the strongest baseline and maintaining non-trivial success at large base displacements where all baselines fail, while reducing the need for largescale real-world data collection.

</details>


### [390] [SLA: Scaling Spatial-Language-Action Model via Continual Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08373)
*Fuhao Li,Wenxuan Song,Qizhou Huang,Ziyang Zhou,Jingbo Wang,Junwu E,Pengwei Wang,Yan Wang,Shunbo Zhou,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SLA 从预训练 pi05 策略初始化，在持续预训练与下游后训练中联合优化动作预测与"中间视觉嵌入—冻结 3D 感知模型空间表征"的隐式对齐，并用多层特征门控、部分深度/标定教师与概率状态 dropout 处理异构机器人数据；辅助模块仅训练期使用，推理架构不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 的视觉表征主要继承自 2D 视觉-语言预训练，空间监督往往只在任务适配阶段引入，缺乏可迁移的三维空间理解能力。作者主张空间理解应作为基础模型能力，从更广泛的机器人经验中学习。

Method: 1) 以预训练 pi05 为初始化，保持原推理结构与权重可直接复用；2) 持续预训练+后训练阶段联合优化动作预测损失与中间层视觉 embedding 对冻结 3D 感知模型空间特征的隐式对齐（蒸馏式空间监督）；3) 整理并统一异构机器人数据集（多本体、多任务、多场景）；4) 引入 feature-dependent gates，自适应聚合多个 VLA 层的视觉嵌入用于空间监督；5) 教师端可利用部分可得的深度与相机标定，与 RGB 共同构成几何监督；6) 概率状态 dropout 促使模型依赖视觉线索、抑制对示教轨迹的过拟合。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅描述方法设计与数据构建；可预期收益为空间表征的可迁移性提升与异构数据的高效利用，但缺乏实验证据支撑。

Conclusion: 该工作提出将隐式空间对齐扩展到持续预训练阶段、并延续至后训练的 VLA 训练范式，通过门控特征聚合、多模态几何教师与状态 dropout 解决异构数据与过拟合问题，且不改变推理架构，具备工程可复用性。但摘要未提供真实机器人评测、成功率或泛化增益等关键证据，说服力有待全文验证。

Abstract: Reliable robotic manipulation requires both semantic understanding and precise control in three-dimensional space. However, vision-language-action (VLA) foundations largely inherit visual representations from 2D vision-language pre-training, while spatial supervision is often introduced only during task-specific adaptation. Developing spatial understanding as a transferable foundation-model capability therefore calls for learning from broader robot experience. We present SLA, a Spatial-Language-Action Model that scales implicit spatial alignment to continual pre-training and maintains it during downstream post-training. Initialized from a pretrained pi05 policy, SLA jointly optimizes action prediction and alignment between intermediate visual embeddings and spatial representations from a frozen 3D perception model. We curate and unify heterogeneous robot datasets to support spatial learning across diverse embodiments, tasks, and scenes. To accommodate this diversity, we introduce feature-dependent gates that adaptively aggregate visual embeddings from multiple VLA layers for spatial supervision. The teacher further exploits partially available depth and camera calibration alongside RGB observations, enabling effective use of heterogeneous geometric annotations. Probabilistic state dropout encourages reliance on visual cues and mitigates overfitting to demonstration trajectories. All auxiliary components are used only during training, allowing direct reuse of pretrained VLA weights and preserving the original inference architecture.

</details>


### [391] [SCALE: Real-Time Latent Communication for Cyber-Physical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08390)
*Shanghao Shi,Jian Xiao,Ching-Hsiang Chan,Sanjoy Baruah,Sastry Kompella,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,William Yeoh,Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCALE: 面向信息物理智能体的实时潜在通信方法，利用时空连续性匹配token、刻画语义增益与通信成本，并在带宽与时延约束下联合调度消息与token数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在通信（latent communication）方法主要面向LLM智能体，直接交换中间表示可提升协作效率，但未考虑信息物理（cyber-physical）智能体面临的带宽约束与异构任务截止期。

Method: 受物理世界时空连续性启发，SCALE对连续潜在消息做token级相似度匹配以识别可复用信息、避免冗余传输；随后对传输不同数量潜在信息的语义增益与通信成本进行定量刻画，并基于该profile设计实时调度器，在带宽与截止期约束下联合决定发送哪些待发消息以及每条消息发送多少潜在token。

Result: 在三个信息物理基准和真实蜘蛛机器人案例上，于多种网络条件与异构截止期设置下评估；结果显示在带宽受限网络中任务性能与按时交付率均显著提升。

Conclusion: 将潜在通信引入信息物理多智能体系统时，冗余消除与增益-成本感知的实时调度是关键；SCALE提供了可行的工程方案，并附带开源代码。

Abstract: Latent communication enables agents to collaborate efficiently by directly exchanging intermediate model representations rather than raw sensor data or text messages. Existing approaches, however, are primarily designed for LLM agents and do not consider the bandwidth constraints and heterogeneous task deadlines faced by cyber-physical agents. In this work, we propose SCALE, a real-time latent communication method tailored for cyber-physical agents. Motivated by the spatio-temporal continuity of the physical world, SCALE matches tokens across consecutive latent messages using similarity scores to identify reusable information and avoid redundant transmission. It then profiles the semantic gain and communication cost of transmitting different amounts of latent information. Based on these profiles, SCALE employs a real-time scheduler that jointly determines which pending messages to transmit and how many latent tokens to send, subject to available bandwidth and task deadlines. We evaluate SCALE across three cyber-physical benchmarks and a physical spider robot case study under diverse network conditions and heterogeneous task deadlines. Our results show that SCALE significantly improves both task performance and on-time delivery in bandwidth-constrained networks. Our code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [392] [A Multimodal Haptic Interface for Simultaneous Softness and Thermal Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08398)
*Joshua Brown,Fernando Bello,Lynette Jones*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合颗粒阻塞变刚度与水循环温控的多模态触觉显示原型，机械刚度可提升300%，温控范围10–50°C，闭环误差0.2°C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉显示器多只能单独模拟单一皮肤感觉通道（软硬度或温度），缺乏能同时调控多种触觉线索的器件，而真实触觉交互是多通道并发的。

Method: 基于颗粒阻塞（particle jamming）实现可控刚度变化，通过加热/冷却水的热传导实现表面温度调控，并将两者集成于同一显示原型；分别进行机械表征（刚度）与热表征（表面温度均匀性、可达温度范围、闭环控制精度）评估。

Result: 机械评估显示刚度可受控提升约300%；热表征显示表面温度分布均匀性好，可实现10°C至50°C的温度范围，闭环控制相对设定点误差约0.2°C。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了多模态触觉显示器的设计，可应用于感知研究、医学模拟与情感触觉等场景；但仅为基础硬件原型与物性表征，缺少人体感知实验与机器人操作任务验证。

Abstract: Haptic interactions, such as touching an object in virtual reality, involve detecting a plethora of sensations through the skin. Whilst tactile displays exist to simulate and manipulate all of these cues individually, little progress has been made constructing devices that can control multiple cues simultaneously. This paper presents a tactile display technology that offers both softness and temperature change. A prototype display, based on particle jamming and the thermal transfer of heated and cooled water, is evaluated in terms of its ability to change both stiffness and temperature. Mechanical evaluations demonstrate controllable stiffness increase of 300%, whilst thermal characterisation showed strong surface uniformity and a range of achievable temperatures from 10°C up to 50°C, with closed-loop control maintaining a 0.2°C error from a setpoint. This work advances the design of multimodal tactile displays with applications to perceptual research, medical simulation, and affective haptics.

</details>


### [393] [Social Humanoid: Socially Responsive Human-Humanoid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08407)
*Vongani H. Maluleke,James Ni,Kie Horiuchi,Lea Wilken,Jiahui Lei,Evonne Ng,Roei Herzig,Jitendra Malik,Trevor Darrell,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Social Humanoid 分层系统，在 29-DoF Unitree G1 上基于人类伙伴动作生成并执行具有社交响应性的全身运动；高层用共享 tokenized 表示做序贯建模（可预测被遮挡的伙伴动作），低层为全身跟踪控制器；并提出两阶段自适应重定向与 260 小时 HHI 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人通常作为孤立智能体训练，无法与人类伙伴进行物理协同；而人类之间天然会预判并相互调整。因此亟需让机器人具备对伙伴动作的反应式生成与预测能力。

Method: 分层架构：高层策略以共享 tokenized 表示对人与机器人动作联合序贯建模，输出反应式人形运动并在伙伴被遮挡时进行运动预测；低层为全身跟踪控制器，在真机上执行生成动作。数据侧提出两阶段自适应重定向：将交互一方重定向到机器人，同时对另一方做形态变形（morphing），以保持接触动力学、可达性与相对体尺度；跨 5 个动捕数据集构建 260 小时 Human-Humanoid Interaction (HHI) 基准。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界中，Social Humanoid 在多种交互类型下产生了协调的、与人类对齐的行为，并能在遮挡情况下预测伙伴动作。

Conclusion: 将人-人交互数据经自适应重定向转化为人-人形交互数据，是实现物理社交化人形全身协同的可行路径，且分层 tokenized 序贯建模兼顾生成与跟踪；附带发布的 HHI 基准为后续研究提供数据基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are typically trained as solitary agents, leaving them unable to coordinate physically with human partners who naturally anticipate and adjust to one another. To bridge this gap, we present Social Humanoid, a hierarchical system that generates and executes socially responsive whole-body motion on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 conditioned on a human partner's motion. Its high-level policy performs flexible sequence modeling of human and humanoid motion through a shared tokenized representation, generating reactive humanoid motion and anticipating the partner's motion when they are occluded, while a low-level whole-body tracking controller executes the generated motion on the robot. To train the system, we introduce a two-phase adaptive retargeting procedure that retargets one interaction partner to the robot while morphing the other partner to preserve contact dynamics, reachability, and relative body scale. We apply this procedure across five motion-capture datasets to produce a new Human-Humanoid Interaction (HHI) benchmark of 260 hours of human-humanoid interactions. Our results demonstrate that Social Humanoid produces coordinated, human-aligned behavior across diverse interaction types in simulation and the real world. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [394] [T3R: Training-Free Two-Stage Token Refinement Towards Efficient and Robust VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08413)
*Mohammed Hassan,Zhenyang Chen,Zheng Wang,Zhixin Zhu,Tianlong Chen,Yingyan,Chaojian Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: T3R：训练无关的两阶段视觉token精炼框架，先分割引导剪枝去除空间无关视觉patch，再用显著性引导的注意力偏置重分配注意力，在LIBERO-Spatial上剪除85%场景相机token仍基本保持成功率，OOD下成功率提升最多7个百分点，高分辨率下LLM前向最高3.3x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型端到端可控制机器人但推理代价高：每次决策处理数百个视觉token（常跨多相机），且分布偏移下无关背景会干扰动作预测。作者认为这源于两种互补冗余：大量视觉token在空间上无关，而剩余token上的注意力又常常分配失当。

Method: 训练无关的两阶段token精炼。第一阶段：分割引导的token剪枝，移除无关视觉patch以降低计算并消除干扰上下文；第二阶段：显著性引导的注意力偏置，将注意力重新导向保留token中对动作关键的视觉区域和语言指令。剪枝提供更干净候选集，注意力偏置进一步强化决策相关证据，二者互补。

Result: LIBERO-Spatial上剪除85%场景相机视觉token，成功率与未剪枝基本持平，OOD物体位置扰动下成功率最高提升7个百分点；每次推理剪枝与注意力偏置开销7.8 ms；输入分辨率增大时LLM前向最高3.3x加速；在LIBERO、SIMPLER、RoboTwin与三种VLA骨干上评估，多相机设置下精度-效率收益最强。

Conclusion: 训练无关的token精炼是实用途径，可实现高效且鲁棒的VLA推理；对VLA推理效率优化问题给出了即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable end-to-end robot control, but inference is increasingly expensive. Each decision processes hundreds of visual tokens, often across multiple cameras, leading to substantial computation while allowing irrelevant background content to distract action prediction under distribution shift. We argue that these issues stem from two complementary forms of redundancy: many visual tokens are spatially irrelevant, while attention over the remaining tokens is often misallocated. Based on this observation, we propose T3R, a Training-Free Two-Stage Token Refinement framework. In the first stage, segmentation-guided token pruning removes irrelevant visual patches to reduce computation and eliminate distracting context. In the second stage, saliency-guided attention biasing reallocates attention toward action-critical visual regions and language instructions within the retained tokens. Together, pruning produces a cleaner candidate set, while attention biasing further emphasizes the most decision-relevant evidence, yielding a better accuracy-efficiency frontier than either stage alone. On LIBERO-Spatial, T3R removes 85% of scene-camera visual tokens while essentially matching the unpruned success rate, and improves success by up to 7 percentage points under out-of-distribution object-placement perturbations. The timing comparison attributes 7.8 ms per inference call to pruning and attention-bias application. As input resolution increases, LLM-forward profiling shows up to a 3.3x speedup over the unpruned baseline. Evaluations across LIBERO, SIMPLER, and RoboTwin with three VLA backbones show the strongest accuracy-efficiency benefits in multi-camera settings. These results demonstrate that training-free token refinement is a practical approach to efficient and robust VLA inference.

</details>


### [395] [Failure Prediction and Classification in Vision-Language-Action Models Via Calibrated Latent Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08425)
*Michal P. Podolinsky,Yunhao Yang,Daniel Little,Hong Lu,Rohan Siva,Olin Goudey,Neel P. Bhatt,Matthias Scheutz,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VLA-Tug，一种挂在 VLA 主干潜在层上的轻量探针，用于实时失败检测、失败模式预测与轻度干预，并发布 3,180 条标注操作轨迹数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 在分布偏移下性能脆弱，未预期失败可能损坏设备或危及人员，部署时需要及时、校准良好、信息丰富且可在边缘端运行的运行时监控器，而现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 在 VLA 主干潜在层上训练轻量探针（latent-layer probe），对轨迹的失败前片段进行监督以提升早期检测，并给出漏检率与误报率的界；同时预测失败模式以支持纠正动作；探针输出用于轻量 steering 改善任务成功率。

Result: 相同误报率下早期失败检测提升最高 30%；迁移到未见任务时漏检/误报界在最多 86% 的运行中仍成立（基线至多 21%）；未见任务上失败模式 top-3 准确率较多数类基线最高提升 44%；实时监控与 steering 提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: 潜在层探针是一种低成本、可校准、可解释的 VLA 运行时安全监控方案，兼具早期检测、失败模式归因与在线干预能力，并配套开放数据集。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) enable general-purpose embodied intelligence, but their performance is brittle under distribution shift, where unanticipated failures can damage equipment or endanger people. Deploying VLAs in real-world systems therefore requires a runtime monitor that is timely, calibrated, and informative while being efficient enough to run on the edge. Existing methods fail to fulfill these requirements. We present VLA-Tug, a lightweight probe over the latent layers of a VLA backbone that improves early failure detection and bounds the missed-detection and false-alarm rates, with minimal inference overhead. We show that supervising on the pre-failure segment of a trajectory increases early failure detection at matched false-positive rates by up to 30% over the strongest learned baseline. Under shift to unseen tasks, the bounds on missed-detection and false-alarm rates remain valid in up to 86% of runs, versus at most 21% for baselines. Unlike existing methods, VLA-Tug predicts the failure mode to enable corrective actions, raising top-3 accuracy on unseen tasks by up to 44% over a majority baseline. VLA-Tug enables real-time monitoring where the well-timed and informative probe supports lightweight steering that improves task success. We release a dataset of 3,180 annotated manipulation episodes to support future work on latent failure detection in VLAs at this https URL .

</details>


### [396] [VFlash: Fast, Accurate Visual Planning via Flash Primitive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08449)
*Yu-Cheng Chou,Shihao Wang,Jin Huang,Chi-Pin Huang,Chiao-An Yang,Xianzhe Fan,Jing Wang,Zhiqi Li,Yuanguo Kuang,Ruijie Zheng,Johan Bjorck,Sifei Liu,Hang Su,Abhishek Badki,Lei Zhang,Alan Yuille,Linxi "Jim'' Fan,Jan Kautz,Zhiding Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VFlash 提出 Flash Primitive Decoding：用轻量 drafter 一次前向联合预测视觉基元（点、框、轨迹）的全部坐标，绕过逐 token 自回归，显著提速并提升空间精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理 AI（机器人、自动驾驶）的规划往往以视觉基元（点、边界框、轨迹）表达空间目标与运动；但自回归 VLM 逐坐标 token 生成长轨迹代价高、时延大，难以支撑富结构的长序列规划。

Method: 利用视觉基元已知的结构先验，设计随基元类型、维度与长度自适应调整布局的轻量 drafter，单次前向并行输出整个基元的坐标；训练目标基于标注基元，直接接受结构合法的预测，不要求与自回归模型的逐 token 序列一致（摒弃 target-token agreement）。

Result: 在 2D 与 3D 机器人轨迹任务上预测误差均低于自回归解码；单次 rollout 初步测量显示 3D 点运动预测生成约快 25 倍；以 VFlash 计划为条件的 world action model 在四个 MolmoSpaces 任务套件上平均成功率 70.2%，基线 62.6%。

Conclusion: 结构化并行解码可替代逐 token 自回归用于视觉规划，在保持甚至提升精度的同时大幅降低延迟；VFlash 计划可直接驱动下游世界动作模型，具备面向物理 AI 的实用价值。

Abstract: Models for physical AI tasks, such as robotics and autonomous driving, need not reason solely in language. They can use visual plans--points, bounding boxes, and trajectories--to represent spatial goals and anticipate motion. However, autoregressive vision-language models generate these plans one coordinate token at a time, making richer predictions increasingly costly. We introduce VFlash, a method for fast, accurate visual planning through Flash Primitive Decoding. VFlash exploits the known structure of visual primitives to predict their coordinates jointly, using a lightweight drafter that generates an entire primitive in one forward pass. The layout adapts to the primitive's type, dimensionality, and length, supporting compact boxes and long, variable-length trajectories. Crucially, spatial accuracy need not require reproducing an autoregressive model's exact token sequence. VFlash therefore trains on annotated primitives and directly accepts structurally valid predictions without target-token agreement. Across the evaluated 2D and 3D robot trajectory tasks, VFlash consistently reduces prediction errors relative to autoregressive decoding. Preliminary single-rollout measurements indicate approximately 25x faster generation for 3D point motion forecasting. A world action model conditioned on VFlash plans achieves 70.2% average success across four MolmoSpaces task suites, compared with 62.6% for the baseline.

</details>


### [397] [Latent RL Priors for Data-Efficient Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08458)
*Yixiao Wang,Peiyi Chen,Mustafa Mukadam,Shreekant Gayaka,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Son Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"Latent RL Prior"两阶段框架：特权训练阶段将大规模RL rollout蒸馏为以本体感受+潜任务上下文为条件的flow-matching动作解码器；视觉适配阶段冻结解码器，仅用动作监督学习图像到潜上下文的视觉接口，无需特权标签或潜变量目标，并可共享解码器实现双臂协调操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限视觉演示学习灵巧操作很难，策略需同时获得感知grounding与精确控制；仿真是用特权信息大规模交互获取控制行为，但如何把这类控制知识高效迁移到视觉策略、降低示范数据需求，是核心痛点。

Method: 两阶段：(1) 特权预训练——把大规模RL轨迹蒸馏为flow-matching动作解码器，条件为本体感知与潜任务上下文；(2) 视觉适配——冻结解码器，学习一个视觉接口把图像映射到该潜上下文，仅用动作监督（无特权标签、无潜变量监督）。双臂场景下为两个手臂分别学习协调的视觉上下文以共享同一解码器。消融：直接视觉→潜上下文 vs. 经预训练状态编码器路由视觉预测。

Result: 三个仿真任务中，低数据条件下优于π0.5与GR00T N1.7的全参数微调；door lifting与双臂装配分别以10×和5×更少示范超过全数据基线。直接视觉→潜上下文条件比经预训练状态编码器路由更利于适配性能与训练稳定性；对未见质量变化具鲁棒性，解码器表征中接触力信息线性可及性更高，佐证保留了RL习得控制知识。

Conclusion: 以RL先验作为冻结动作解码器+"视觉→潜上下文"轻量接口，是数据高效视觉灵巧操作的有效范式：控制知识可复用、可跨臂共享，且潜表征承载接触力等物理信息。局限在于仅仿真任务验证，缺少真实机器人实验与真实部署证据。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation from limited visual demonstrations is challenging because policies must acquire both perceptual grounding and precise control, while reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation can acquire rich control behaviors from large-scale interaction with privileged information. We introduce \textbf{Latent RL Prior}, a two-stage framework that reuses this control knowledge for data-efficient visual policy learning. During privileged pretraining, we distill large-scale RL rollouts into a flow-matching action decoder conditioned on proprioception and latent task context. During visual adaptation, we freeze the decoder and learn a visual interface that maps images to its latent context using action supervision alone, without privileged labels or latent targets. The same decoder can further be shared across arms by learning coordinated visual contexts for bimanual manipulation. Across three simulated tasks, our method outperforms full-parameter fine-tuning of $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7 in the low-data regime; on door lifting and bimanual assembly, it surpasses full-data baselines using $10\times$ and $5\times$ fewer demonstrations, respectively. Further analysis shows that direct visual-to-latent conditioning improves adaptation performance and training stability over routing visual predictions through the pretrained state encoder. Robustness to unseen mass variation and greater linear accessibility of contact-force information in decoder representations further provide evidence of retained RL-acquired control knowledge. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [398] [OopsieRecover: Learning Transferable VLA Corrections from Semantic Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08462)
*Arpit Bahety,Skand Peri,Sriniket Ambatipudi,Chen Xu,Haruki Nishimura,Masha Itkina,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OopsieRecover 提出用溯因推理（abduction）从少量纠错演示中推断失败背后的意图，并将其编码为稠密空间表征与条件代价，用于引导 VLA 策略在未见物体与场景变化下泛化纠错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型部署时常失败，DAgger 类微调需数十至数百次交互纠错，且学到的纠正难以泛化到新场景。作者假设语义层面的失败原因与纠正理由可支撑少样本纠错学习与泛化。

Method: 以溯因推理从少量纠错演示中推断意图/失败原因，将推断知识编码为稠密空间表征（dense spatial representations）与条件代价（conditional costs），用代价刻画行为不可取区域并引导策略偏向更优动作区域，从而条件化 VLA 策略。

Result: 在 6 项任务及物体位姿、场景、相关任务变体上实验，仅用少量纠错演示即可使 VLA 克服失败并泛化到大量新情形，无需为每种变化重新采集纠错数据。

Conclusion: 将纠错知识提升到语义/意图层面并以空间代价形式注入策略，是提升 VLA 样本效率与纠错泛化性的有效途径，可作为演示增强类方法的基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can perform a wide range of robotic manipulation tasks, but often fail during deployment. Teaching them to recover from these failures requires substantial corrective data: existing approaches learn to recover from failures by directly associating corrective actions with the observations in which they were demonstrated. For instance, DAgger-like fine-tuning can require tens to hundreds of interactions, yet the resulting policy may still fail to generalize the learned corrections beyond the demonstrated scenarios. We hypothesize that semantics can capture the underlying reasons for failures and their corrections, enabling policies to learn from only a handful of corrective demonstrations and generalize those corrections to novel objects, and scene variations. We propose OopsieRecover, a framework that uses abduction to infer the intent behind a small set of corrective demonstrations and encodes the inferred knowledge as dense spatial representations and conditional costs. These costs capture when a behavior is undesirable and steer the policy toward more desirable action regions. We experiment across 6 tasks and variations in object poses, scenes, and related tasks, and find that only a handful of corrective demonstrations can enable a VLA to overcome failures across a broad range of novel situations, without requiring new corrective data for each variation. Please refer to this https URL for videos.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [399] [Representations of 3D Rotations: Mathematical Foundations and Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08086)
*Aiersilan, Aizierjiang,Liu, Haochen,Hahn, James*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 对SO(3)多种旋转表示（欧拉角、轴角、四元数、旋转矩阵、指数映射、6D连续表示、矩阵Fisher分布等）进行系统性综述与对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转表示在图形学、机器人、机器学习中至关重要，但各表示在连续性、万向锁、效率、存储、插值与复合运算上的权衡缺乏统一梳理，实践中难以选型。

Method: 综述性研究：梳理各类SO(3)表示的代数形式，从连续性、万向锁敏感性、计算效率、存储开销、插值性质、复合操作等维度进行对比分析，并结合动画、位姿估计、惯性导航、3D形状配准、神经网络等应用讨论。

Result: 指出四元数因紧凑性与计算效率占据主导地位；6D连续表示与矩阵Fisher分布等方案在连续性和不确定性建模方面更有优势。

Conclusion: 建议未来探索混合表示方法并开展大规模系统性评测，以改进旋转表示技术的基础。

Abstract: Rotation representations are foundational in fields such as computer graphics, robotics, and machine learning, where precise and efficient modeling of 3D orientations is critical. This paper comprehensively investigates diverse representations of the special orthogonal group $SO(3)$, such as Euler angles, axis-angle vectors, quaternions, rotation matrices, exponential maps, and emerging continuous and probabilistic methods, evaluating their mathematical formulations, continuity, susceptibility to gimbal lock, computational efficiency, storage requirements, interpolation properties, and composition operations, while integrating detailed algebraic insights with practical applications in fields like animation, pose estimation, inertial navigation, 3D shape registration, and neural networks. Empirical evidence highlights quaternions' dominance due to their compactness and computational efficiency, while alternatives like 6D continuous representations and matrix Fisher distributions provide enhanced continuity and uncertainty modeling. Future research could explore hybrid methods and thorough large-scale evaluations to help build a solid foundation for improving rotation representation techniques.

</details>


### [400] [Seeing Beyond the Next Step: World-Model-Guided Human-Like Navigation in Multi-Agent Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07730)
*Seong-Eun Hong,JuYeong Hwang,Jinhyun Kim,Hanyoung Jang,HyeongYeop Kang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出将世界模型仅用于训练期作为"偏好预言机"，通过组相对偏好蒸馏把前瞻能力摊薄进快速策略，部署时移除世界模型，单次前向完成社交感知导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类感知导航中，仅靠当前状态无法识别"局部可行但长期有害"的行为（偏离人行横道、切入人群流、堵塞窄通道）。在线滚动世界模型代价过高，领域普遍以牺牲前瞻性换速度。作者认为这是伪二选一。

Method: 离散视觉动作先验 + 两个任务对齐的世界模型（近域社交动力学、未来场景语义），对候选动作按其预测后果打分，再用组相对偏好蒸馏（group-relative preference distillation）精炼策略；推理时两个世界模型均被移除，策略单次前向输出动作。

Result: 在五个留出评估场景中，相较强反应式与学习式基线，导航在安全性、社会与群体一致性、目标导向性上同时更优；朴素观察者评分显示运动更接近人类。

Conclusion: 将世界模型前瞻能力"摊销"进策略（而非加速 rollout）是可部署智能体的可行路径，把前瞻成本限制在训练阶段。

Abstract: Selecting a next motion in a populated scene is not just a matter of avoiding what is currently in the way: an action can be locally feasible yet drift onto the road instead of the crosswalk, cut into a stream of crossing pedestrians, or bottleneck a narrow passage. These failure modes are invisible from the current state alone, and acting on them requires reasoning about what each candidate action will cause. World models offer exactly this, namely action-conditioned predictions of the future, but rolling them out at every decision step is a cost deployable navigation systems cannot pay, and the field has largely traded foresight for speed. We argue this is a false dichotomy. The natural place for a world model in human-aware navigation is at training time, as a preference oracle that distills foresight into a fast policy, rather than at inference time, as an online planner. We pair a discrete visual action prior with two task-aligned world models, one for nearby social dynamics and one for future scene semantics, that score candidate actions by their predicted consequences and refine the policy through group-relative preference distillation. At deployment, both world models are removed and the policy acts in a single forward pass. Across five held-out evaluation scenarios, this yields navigation that is simultaneously safer, more socially and group-coherent, and more goal-directed than strong reactive and learned baselines, with naive observers rating the resulting motion as more human-like. Taken together, our findings point to a different approach for bringing world-model foresight into deployable agents: not accelerating rollouts, but amortizing them into the policy so that the cost of looking ahead is confined to training. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [401] [EDGAR: Efficient Diffusion-Driven Gaussian Avatar Refinement via Synthetic Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07909)
*Kwan Yun,Minhyeok Bang,Colette Suhjung Koo,Junyong Noh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: EDGAR：用单步扩散+自监督Sapiens身份特征+MoE融合块修复单目/图像驱动高斯头像的伪影，再用多视图refined结果作伪目标优化单一高斯头像，实现无需逐帧推理的高质量渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频或图像驱动的高斯头部头像重建因几何与纹理约束不足，产生模糊、拉伸和时序不稳定等伪影；直接在渲染帧上做扩散式精修（如SDEdit）代价高且难保身份。

Method: 构建EDGAR-SET混合三元组数据集（真实采集+合成渲染，配对退化渲染/真值/参考图）；EDGAR为条件扩散框架：将Sapiens自监督模型提取的身份特征通过融合块注入单步扩散主干，并用Mixture-of-Experts应对多类、多严重度退化；精修后的多视图渲染作为伪目标回烘到单一底层高斯头像，使最终渲染无需逐帧跑EDGAR。

Result: 在3种高斯头像重建方法×3种设置上，感知与重建指标均优于原始输出和SDEdit式精修基线；跨视图评测显示标定一致性提升；大偏航角与张嘴极端条件下鲁棒；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 对可恢复的外观类伪影，扩散精修+伪目标回烘是一条高效可行的工作区间；但对不可恢复的几何缺陷作用有限。

Abstract: Recent advances in Gaussian splatting have enabled efficient and photorealistic head avatar reconstruction, yet monocular video-based or image-based methods still suffer from under-constrained geometry and texture, leading to artifacts such as blurring, stretching, and temporal instability. We present EDGAR (Efficient Diffusion-Driven Gaussian Avatar Refinement), a conditional diffusion framework designed to enhance the quality of Gaussian avatars by correcting artifacts while preserving identity fidelity. To train EDGAR, we construct EDGAR-SET, a hybrid triplet dataset composed of real-captured and synthetic-rendered samples, which simulates realistic Gaussian avatar failure cases by pairing degraded renderings with ground-truth and reference images. EDGAR refines degraded frames through a fusion block that injects identity features extracted by the self-supervised Sapiens model into a one-step diffusion backbone, enabling effective artifact removal without sacrificing efficiency. Our EDGAR model incorporates a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture to address diverse degradation types and severity levels. The refined multi-view renderings are subsequently used as pseudo-targets to optimize a single underlying Gaussian avatar, so that the final avatar can be rendered without applying EDGAR to every output frame. Experiments on three different Gaussian avatar reconstruction methods, each evaluated under three different settings, show improvements in both perceptual and reconstruction measures over the raw outputs and an SDEdit-style refinement baseline. In addition, cross-view evaluation demonstrates improved calibrated consistency, while tests under large yaw and open-mouth expressions verify robustness under extreme settings. These results collectively indicate a useful operating regime for recoverable appearance artifacts. The code is available at this https URL

</details>
